# Decision Log

Append-only. Each entry records the date, the decision, its source (**U** = the user,
**R**n = `research/n`, **L** = lead agent proposal accepted by the user), and where it is
reflected. If a later decision supersedes an entry, mark the old one `superseded by Dn` and
never delete it.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |

| D1 | Ducter is open source and self-deployed. No Ducter-level multi-tenancy in v1; collaboration goes through Nowledge Mem Team | U | principles 17, 19 |
| D2 | The Log is the truth and harness sessions are caches. Projections are deterministic and layered L0–L4 | L, R3 | principles 1, 2 |
| D3 | A thread *is* a Focus Window. The main AI reads first (cost ladder) and asks only for judgment | U, L | principle 8 |
| D4 | Only the main AI is sessionless. Windows are ordinary harness sessions | U | principle 9 |
| D5 | Context window is a quality budget, 128K–256K by default | U | Physics |
| D6 | Window threads are mirrored into the Log as normalized events, with large outputs as deduplicated blobs (option B) | U | principle 1, `design/context.md` |
| D7 | Memory = structured digest + bold verbatim window + gated recall (S3 design) | R14, R15, U | principle 3, `design/memory.md` |
| D8 | Expire-aware resume by expected cost. Tail is a working set: cap at ingress, read-fold in a cache-sharing side context, late fold by truncate+append, never edit in place | R11–R13, U | principles 4, 5 |
| D9 | One conversation shape: ack (names action, never outcome) → job → result. Executors (in turn / fork / window) look identical. No separate talker model for text; voice gets an allow-listed realtime front-end | U, R16 | principle 6, `design/conversation.md` |
| D10 | Unread is mutable: one living message, revised in place until read, rendered from typed reports | U, R16 | principle 7 |

## 2026-11-09: Server mode (overnight build)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D54 | **Remote access, first slice: server mode + code pairing.** (1) The main process runs on a Linux VPS as a systemd *user* service (`ducter service install`: `Restart=on-failure`, journald, linger, data in `~/.ducter`), bound to 127.0.0.1 unless `--host` says otherwise. (2) Off-machine clients pair once: `ducter pair` shows a one-time 8-character code (10 min, single use, unambiguous alphabet) with the address, a pasteable link and a QR; `POST /pair` trades it for a long-lived **device token**. Codes and tokens are stored only as SHA-256 hashes; wrong codes are rate limited; each device is revocable (`ducter devices disconnect`). (3) A request is *local* only if the peer and the Host are loopback and no proxy header is present; local clients stay token-free, everything else needs the token on `/ws`, and on `/mcp` beside the role token. (4) TLS is never hand-rolled: Caddy with automatic HTTPS on the user's own domain (Q18, `scripts/caddy-setup.sh`); an SSH tunnel is the zero-domain fallback. (5) The desktop gets **连接到你的 Ducter / Connect to your Ducter** (address + 配对码), token in a 0600 file. (6) Workers dial out to `/worker`; the main places tasks on them by `Place.device` (state stays on the main; the worker holds the place and runs the harness), and a worker that drops mid-task makes its tasks 在等 · <device> 连不上 after 2 min and resumes them by session id. Refines D40 (this is its code half; the WebCrypto device key + passkey and the Edge relay of D39/D46 remain the phone/web target). *Proposed overnight from the user's ask; accepted 2026-11-09 morning (U "按建议"); the code is shown as 连接码 / Code since D65* | U (ask) + L, R29 | `design/system.md`, `roadmap.md`, `design/onboarding.md`, `design/product-model.md` §1, `design/scenarios.md` |
| D55 | **Ducter.app is signed with the Nowledge Labs Developer ID and notarized**, the way con and Nowledge Mem ship. Bundle id `co.nowledge.ducter` (helper `co.nowledge.ducter.cli`), following `co.nowledge.con` and `co.nowledge.mem.desktop` (reverse DNS of nowledge.co); replaces the ad-hoc id `dev.ducter.app`. Hardened runtime on the app and helper; the helper's only entitlement is `com.apple.security.cs.allow-jit` (Bun's JIT dies without it, tested). App and DMG are both notarized and stapled; credentials use con/Mem's names (Apple ID + app-specific password, an API key, or a `notarytool` keychain profile). Ad-hoc signing stays the fallback without an identity | U (ask) | `scripts/make-app.sh`, README, `design/onboarding.md` |

## 2026-11-09: Morning decisions (the author's answers to the overnight list)

The author: "关闭窗口在后台会比较自然一些；词汇表你要仔细反思，不能有非人类感觉和无关的新概念，仔细看看；那些建议你自己第一性原理反思好按照建议吧". Each recommendation in `design/flows/OPEN-QUESTIONS.md` was re-checked from first principles before it was recorded; where the check changed it, the entry says so.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D56 | **Ducter keeps running when the window closes.** Closing the Ducter.app window leaves Ducter and its tasks working in the background, the way Mail or a chat app does; opening the app again reconnects. Quitting is an explicit act (Quit in the menu); how Ducter stays resident and what Quit does with running tasks follow the implementation, recorded in `design/onboarding.md`. Why: "silence means success" needs work to carry on when the user looks away; a window is a view, not the partner's life. Supersedes the overnight "Ducter lives as long as the app" default | U | `design/onboarding.md` app path, `README.md` |
| D57 | **Local trust: a local token, then no special case for loopback.** Today loopback stays token-free on single-user machines, and Ducter is not recommended on a shared host. Next: a 0600 local token in the data dir that the CLI, the same-machine desktop and the tasks' MCP config present automatically; once it exists, every caller shows a token, loopback included, and `DUCTER_LOCAL_TRUST` is retired. An SSH-tunnel client then connects once with a code like any other device. *Changed on reflection:* the recommendation was to switch `off` only when other human users exist; with an automatic local token the strict rule costs nothing, and one rule is simpler and honest everywhere ("only connected devices get in") | U ("按建议"), L | `design/system.md` "Who counts as local", `todo.md` |
| D58 | **The web client is `apps/web` (Preact), phone first.** It is served by the main process at `/app` and shares the desktop's i18n keys and Pebble tokens. Amends D20 for the web: MyGo native stays the desktop client; a MyGo WASM web client is dropped from the plan unless a need appears; Expo stays the later native app. *Changed on reflection:* the recommendation kept MyGo WASM "for a desktop-class web client later"; that would be a third UI codebase for no user need, so it is dropped | U ("按建议"), L | `design/system.md` Clients, `roadmap.md` |
| D59 | **Phone push defaults as built.** A decision unread for 30 s earns one push; pushes coalesce per 15 min; done results never push unless the user turns on 做完了也告诉我 / Also tell me when something's done; quiet hours default to 23:00–08:00 in the phone's time zone (免打扰), nothing breaks through (D52 Q12), at most one push when they end; iOS tap-to-open, Android adds Go / Hold. *Added on reflection:* coalescing must never hold a decision past its own default, so a decision whose default would fire before the coalescing window ends goes out at once (not built yet; `todo.md`) | U ("按建议"), L | `design/flows/D-decisions-attention.md`, `todo.md` |
| D60 | **Result anatomy (I-results rule 1).** The partner's prose is the outcome only, plus what was left out and what is unverified. The client renders 怎么知道的 / How I know, 发出去的 / Sent out (shown even when empty) and the actions (合进 / Merge, 开草稿 PR, 看改动) from the typed report. Words freeze, chips live (IQ10): the message text never changes; its chips are live views, and a change of meaning is a new message. Measured: 55% → 94% on the partner eval (R30) | U ("按建议"), R30 | `design/conversation.md`, `design/flows/I-results.md` |
| D61 | **Results and safety: IQ1–IQ11 and GQ1–GQ13 as recommended**, with two changes. IQ1 result at verified Done (Done · checking with live chips if the user is watching and checks take > 10 s); IQ2 Sent out always shown for tasks with a place; IQ3 the list of finished work inside On hand; IQ4 Save to… writes, never commits; IQ5 diff snapshot at Done, 5 MB cap; IQ6 share links 7 days, revocable, with the Edge; IQ7 pre-share check on; IQ8 keep until deleted; IQ9 save to Mem only on tap; IQ11 one protocol change for evidence kinds and a `result` event. GQ1 effect ladder; GQ2 people-facing sends wait until quiet end + 30 min; GQ3 30 s undo for emails and posts; GQ4 offer "don't ask next time" after 3 identical Go's in 14 days, never for the floor; GQ5 the floor (payments, passwords, untrusted-requested effects, no-default decisions); GQ6 redact a leaked secret (superseding event + blob delete, advise rotation); GQ7 show effects seen in transcripts; GQ9 record line always shown, muted; GQ10 PR comments / ready / review requests ask without a countdown; GQ11 the sending slide is motion, checked in review; GQ12 可以碰 / May touch on every task; GQ13 a short research report (the next free number; 31 went to Bridge). *Changed on reflection:* GQ8 no longer adopts 沙盒 / Sandbox: the UI states the property instead (碰不到你的文件 / Can't touch your files; D66), and GQ2's "quiet end" uses the user's 免打扰 hours | U ("按建议"), L | `design/flows/I-results.md`, `design/flows/G-safety.md` |
| D62 | **Browser places: W5-1 and W5-2.** The Ducter browser shows the clay avatar as its Dock tile, parks its window bottom-right at ~60% behind the user's work, and the status line says 在 Ducter 的浏览器里看 {host} / Looking at {host} in Ducter's browser with a Show action. Browser tasks run without the user's own Claude config (a departure from principle 17's inheritance for this place kind only: it keeps the real Chrome out of reach and cut 332k → 140k tokens), and their `log_search` / `log_read` are scoped to their own task so a hostile page can't pull the conversation out | U ("按建议"), L | `design/system.md`, `todo.md` |
| D63 | **One live Mem write** into a scratch Space named `ducter-scratch`, to check provenance (`metadata.source_message_id`), then archived. Done 2026-11-09; the Space part superseded by D141 (6) | U ("按建议") | `design/memory.md`, `todo.md` |
| D64 | **Art stays in plain git**, no LFS. From now on only final exports used by the clients are committed (alpha PNG, WebP 1×/2×/3×); raw generations live in the concept-art canvas. Whether to squash raw images out of history is decided before the repo goes public | U ("按建议") | `design/ui.md`, `todo.md` |
| D65 | **Capability map: the hybrid** (its one-device stage superseded by D81) (the literal playset's world with the loadout's equip snap; R24, R26, R28), still to pass a human comprehension test before it ships. The category noun is dropped (D66) | U ("按建议") | `design/ui.md` |
| D66 | **Glossary review**: words a person would say to a friend, no machine feel, no new concepts, and no label where the UI can show the thing. Highlights: 对外 → 发出去的 / Sent out; 证据 → 怎么知道的 / How I know; 存档 → 保存了进度 / Progress saved; 汇报 / 交回给 → 告诉 / Told; 这一轮 / 这一步 gone (先做到这儿 / Stopped here for now); 配对码 → 连接码 / Code; 本事 dropped as a noun; 远程打开 → 在哪儿都能用; 安静时段 → 免打扰; 成果 → 做好的 / Finished; 合回 → 合进; 待命 → 在呢; 排队中 → 还没开始; 核对 → 检查; 随手 → 其他 / Other; 第 n 轮 → 第 n 次 / Try n; 交还 → 还给它; 复查 → {date} 再问你; 沙盒 → the property. Full table with reasons and alternatives in `design/glossary-review-2026-11-09.md`; strings changed in all three clients | U, L | `design/product-model.md` §1, i18n tables |
| D67 | **From experience review 2 (`design/review-2026-11-09.md`, its three open calls).** (1) A follow-up goes to the old task only when it builds on that task's *unmerged* change; anything else opens a new task, and a task is retitled when a follow-up changes what it does (built). (2) An ask about *how to do the work* (reversible) always carries a default and a time, 10 min unless the work suggests otherwise; only effect-ladder asks (GQ1, GQ5) may wait with no default. (3) A long path in a message bubble is middle-ellipsized with tap to expand; the text sent is unchanged. Re-checked: all three serve "needs your attention only when it truly matters" and lose nothing | U ("按建议"), L | `design/conversation.md`, `todo.md` |
| D68 | **One component system (UI polish pass).** One type, space (4-pt), radius, control-height and chip-height scale as tokens on both clients. **The ledge means "this can be pressed"**: every filled button has the same 3px ledge in a darker shade of its fill and sinks into it; text buttons, chips and containers are flat, and containers rest on a soft shadow (`Raised`/`Floating`). System notices (CLI offer, first result, a held message) are one slim strip above the composer, never speech; what needs no answer is a toast. A task chip says its title once; a finished task reads Done (`done?` keeps the glossary's "在检查 / checking" for when a check actually runs, since none does yet); a result's Sent out row shows only when something was sent out. Conversation top-aligned, 12 within a voice and 24 between turns | U (the author's "有的有立体感有的没有，是有意的么？"), L | `design/ui.md` (Components and tokens), `apps/desktop/internal/theme`, `apps/web/static/app.css` |
| D69 | **Shown, not dotted (anti-slop rule).** No pulsing/glowing status dots, no pills or capsules with a leading dot, no decorative identity dots, no perpetual "alive" animation. State is the thing itself (clay art, the chip's tint and words, the face; D43); words only for exceptions, plainly; the normal state says nothing. Applied: device cards lose the hostname and the status pill (exceptions only, plain words); selection is a 2-pt ink hairline, no ledge; stage slots say the ability's name only and stay inside the stage; task chips, lane headers, the On hand button, starters, status lines and rows lose their dots; the retry pulse, the working bar and the decision glow ring are gone | U (the author's review of process screenshots), L | `design/ui.md` (Shown, not dotted) |
| D70 | **The first run needs no terminal** (asked: "onboarding 安装的整个流程都要无比顺滑，包含 cua 等东西的部署" and "macOS 各种权限开启过程 UI 也要极致优雅顺滑"). Ducter.app runs each tool's *official* installer itself, in the background, with progress, Cancel, a plain reason and Try again (Claude Code `claude.ai/install.sh`, Codex `chatgpt.com/codex/install.sh`, cua-driver `cua.ai/driver/install.sh --no-connect --no-test --no-modify-path`, so it never wires itself into the person's other agents or edits their shell profile); signs in with the tool's own browser flow and goes on by itself; Terminal stays as a small fallback. 上网 is set up only when it is picked, as three steps that turn green by themselves (driver, Accessibility, Screen & System Audio Recording), using cua-driver's own guide, then Ducter comes back to the front. macOS asks get one calm card each, never two at once, never at launch: notifications after the first result, the phone on a later result, each with 稍后 / Later (asked once more after 3 days, then never). Nowledge Mem is one quiet line, a page link. If the person turned Ducter off in Login Items, Ducter runs as the app's child, says so with a link to Login Items, notices the switch by itself and moves to the background once nothing is running. Details: `design/onboarding.md` "The app path" | U (asked), L | `design/onboarding.md`, `todo.md` |
| D71 | **The app's information architecture is closed for v1** (asked: "每一个 view 都需要仔细 revisit…我们是否需要一个 settings 的菜单…第一个发布的版本我希望要完备闭环"). Five places, each with one job: (1) **对话 / Conversation** (⌘1, home); (2) **手头 / On hand** (⌘2); (3) **设备 / Devices** (⌘3), the core setup surface: devices, what they can do, the AI accounts on this Mac (an account card opens the first run's own row to install or sign in, or Sign out), and **连上手机或另一台电脑 / Connect a phone or computer** (the one-time code); (4) **伙伴 / Partner** (⌘4, or the partner's own face and name in the conversation's bar): a character sheet, the clay partner large with its five faces, its name renamed in place, then four cards in plain words: **怎么说话 / How it talks** (跟着我 / Follow me · 中文 · English), **什么时候找你 / When it comes to you** (通知 / Notifications, 免打扰 / Quiet hours as a range, **你没回的时候 / If you don't answer**: 10 min · 30 min · 1 h · 2 h before a reversible default goes ahead), **记得什么 / What it remembers** (Mem state, **记住事情 / Remember things**, recent 记下了 lines with **忘掉 / Forget**), **自己就做，还是先问你 / On its own, or asks you first** (read-only, from G-safety); (5) **设置 / Settings** (⌘,): the app's own only, calm: 外观 / Look (Pebble, the one look that ships), 界面语言 / Language (跟着系统 · 中文 · English, per client), 关窗以后 / When you close the window (D56's state in words, Stop Ducter), 更新 / Updates (D72), 数据和记录 / Data and logs (the folder, the log, 检查一遍 / Run checks), 关于 / About, and Remove Ducter last and quiet (D72). **Menu bar:** About · the update line · Settings… ⌘, · Stop/Wake Ducter · Quit; View ⌘1–⌘4; Help (Docs, Report a problem…, D72). **Menu bar extra:** the clay partner as a template icon; its menu is what needs you, each decision with Go / Hold / Reply…, then Open Ducter and Settings…. **Server:** the partner's settings are one protocol frame (`settings` / `settings_set` / `memory_forget`, mirrored in Go and web); each lives where it already lived (the name in persona.json, notifications and quiet hours in push.json, the rest in settings.json); the partner reads the language and the memory switch live; the Mac's own notifications follow the switch and quiet hours. **Forget** runs `nmem m forget --force` (hides from recall and from reads by id; not a hard delete) when the line has a memory id, otherwise the line is only hidden here and the app says so. **Phone:** the same partner settings and the phone's language in one Settings sheet (tap the partner). Theme choice stays hidden until a second theme works on both clients | U, L | `product-model.md` §1 (glossary), §2; `ui.md` (places); `scenarios.md` (K1–K4); `docs/settings.md` |
| D72 | **The lifecycle loop of Ducter.app: updates, removal, help.** (1) **Updates: a small Go updater, not Sparkle** (`apps/desktop/internal/update`). Once at launch and daily it reads the newest published release of wey-gu/ducter through GitHub's public API, unauthenticated (a 404 while the repo is private is silence, not an error); downloads `Ducter-X-arm64.dmg` into Application Support, checks it against `SHA256SUMS.txt`, copies the app out next to the running one and checks the copy (bundle id, version, `codesign --verify --deep --strict`, a Developer ID Application requirement of the running app's own team on app and helper, `spctl`). It never interrupts: one quiet line in the app menu (and Settings) "有新版本 0.2.0 · 重启时更新 / Update 0.2.0 ready · restart to finish"; the next quit installs it (default on), or Restart now. The swap happens after the app exits (rename; the previous version kept for a rollback; the old one put back if the new can't move in), then the background LaunchAgent is restarted so Ducter runs the new helper and resumes from the Log. Why not Sparkle: an Objective-C framework to embed and sign into a Go app built without Xcode, plus an EdDSA key and appcast to keep; the Developer ID team check already proves who built it (Apple issues and revokes the certificate) and the release already carries checksums. Why not mygo's own updater: it wants its own archive format and key, and checks neither the team nor Gatekeeper. (2) **彻底移除 Ducter / Remove Ducter**: a page listing exactly what goes (the LaunchAgent, `~/.ducter` with the Log, task working copies and browser profiles, the app's `~/.local/bin/ducter` link, its Library folders) and what stays (repos and their `ducter/*` branches, Claude Code / Codex logins, Nowledge Mem, cua-driver); "only stop and remove the background item" or "remove everything" (typed confirmation; uncommitted task changes named first; stops if a Ducter started in Terminal still runs); the app goes to the Trash last. The CLI owns the list (`ducter uninstall [--all]`), so app, terminal and server remove the same things. (3) **Reset partner is not built**: the Log is one append-only file for the conversation *and* every task (principle 1), so cutting the conversation out would orphan tasks, decisions and resume; the safe form is a "new conversation" boundary event the projection starts after, which is a context-design change (`design/todo.md`). (4) **Help**: Ducter docs (ducter.ai/docs) and 报告问题… / Report a problem…: `ducter report` collects doctor, versions and the last 500 log lines with secrets removed (the conversation only when ticked, off by default), the person sees what would be attached, it is saved as a .zip on the Desktop and a prefilled GitHub issue opens; nothing is uploaded | L (proposed by the lifecycle agent; not yet reviewed by the user) | `apps/desktop/internal/update`, `internal/local/lifecycle.go`, `packages/cli/src/uninstall.ts`, `report.ts`, `design/release/README.md`, `design/build-and-run.md` |

## 2026-11-09: Design QA (the author's "黑色扁平按钮和立体按钮共存…ai slop 仔细研究打磨过了么？")

D71 is held for the information-architecture pass (branch `ia`). A flat-UI restyle (no ledges)
was tried in this pass and withdrawn before it was recorded: the author wants the clay, puffy
character kept, and a separate brand-direction pass defines the visual language (buttons,
type scale, clay presence, idle motion). Only the structure below is decided.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D73 | **No pills; the outcome said once; decisions read in one pass.** (1) Status, evidence and metadata are never capsules on any surface (desktop, phone web, the iOS shell, the site): state in words (its ink for the exception), evidence as a drawn mark and words, metadata as plain muted text or in Details; On hand has no state chips (the lane is the state); marks are drawn glyphs in muted ink, never on a colored tile. (2) Under a result message no state is restated: one quiet line of proof (✓ and words per check, "2 files changed ›" opening the task's changes, the task's title as a link), a ↗ line of what went out only when something did, then Merge into main / Open draft PR; See changes is dropped as a duplicate. A task under way is one quiet line (mark, title link, status in words). (3) Decisions: the living message's title shows only when the user was away (unread when the window opened, or arrived while it was in the background); the count shows once in the section header ("2 need you") with Go on all defaults as a text button at its end (On hand: at the end of the Needs you lane's header, not in the chrome); an ask's small heading is the asking task's title (nothing for the partner's own asks), never "Needs you · n"; the Go button says the action in sentence case ("Rebase"; zh keeps "好，rebase" per the glossary); Details sits right after Reply… (on the phone and on On hand the task's title is the link). (4) Short code tokens never break mid-word; only long paths wrap | U (screenshot review of the living message and the site), L | `design/ui.md` (Components and tokens; Shown, not dotted; no pills), the three clients, `apps/site`; (2) and (3) in the conversation superseded by D104 |
| D74 | **An opened task is one column, diff on demand.** On the conversation's measure, top to bottom: where it stands in words; a pending ask; the outcome said once (report, line of proof, what went out, Merge / Open draft PR, or Try again); **What it did** as the task's own words with tool calls folded into one line per stretch ("Ran 2 commands, edited 1 file ›", opening in place) and a failed step in words; **Changes** as a list of files with +/− that open their diff in place (none open by default; no section without a repo; the phone shows none); a quiet **Details ›** at the bottom (who and why, where as repo and branch with the path the user named, saved progress as a plain list, what went out, usage only if any; empty rows hidden). The header is the breadcrumb and the title; the composer stays at the bottom. Replaces the three columns (transcript, changes, dossier), the result banner, the "Told Ducter · done" card, the header chips and the empty dossier cards. Why: the author mostly stopped reading diffs ("diff 被设计成默认显示的设计需要仔细看看"), and the old page said the outcome three times | U, L | `design/ui.md` (Three zoom levels), `design/focus-window.md`, `design/scenarios.md` |

## 2026-11-09: Engineering discipline (the author's "工作的对比截图和记录，尤其有 binary 不适合放在 git 里吧")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D75 | **Git holds source; work evidence lives outside it.** A binary is committed only if an app loads it at runtime or a build derives from it (theme art and fonts, app icons, the site's public files, the alpha art masters, the two reference images the art batches name), plus the README's own images, each ≤ 2 MB. Screenshots, before/after pairs, recordings, raw and normalized generations, concept explorations and build output are evidence or output: tools write them to `.shots/` (gitignored, `DUCTER_SHOTS_DIR` overrides), reviews are shown to the author as published galleries, and Markdown records cite them by file name. `scripts/check-binaries.ts` enforces this in CI. The existing archive moved to `~/dev/ducter-evidence` (the maintainer's machine), and the history rewrite before going public drops those paths from every commit. Supersedes the half of D64 that kept raw generations reachable from git history | U | `.gitignore`, `scripts/check-binaries.ts`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `design/README.md`, `design/ui.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `todo.md` |

The prototype (https://claude.ai/artifact/51djLTYeekc14y1GsP7Wn5) restores the visual language of the approved Pebble
artifacts (style board, Core UI, Flows, Loadout) and reconciles it with D69/D73/D74. The author
approved all five proposals ("按照建议来吧").

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D76 | **Pebble restored: one clay depth.** (1) Every solid thing stands on clay. A card rests on a solid 4pt base in `line` (`0 4 0 #E8D9C4`, radius 24, hero cards and the composer 28); it never moves. A button stands on a 3pt ledge in a darker shade of its fill, plus a soft drop (`0 7 12 −4`, ink at 28%) and a 1.5pt inner top highlight, and sinks into the ledge when pressed (translate 3, ledge 0, drop to `0 1 3` at 22%, 70 ms); the drop and highlight keep an ink button standing on an ink surface. One family: primary (ink, filled from #3B2D22 at the top to ink, a 1pt warm seam #5A4434 above a #140D07 ledge, a heavier drop at 38%, so it reads as clay on any surface), secondary (cream with a 1pt inner rim, ledge `#E0C9B0`; white on a tinted card, ledge in the card's own ledge color), accent (orange, ledge `#C9642A`, the composer's send), text (flat), icon; heights 36/44/52, radius 12/14/18, the 52 size on a 4pt ledge. Choice rows are a segmented clay control: a sunken track, the chosen one a surface on a 2pt base. Selection on a card stays a 2pt ink hairline. (2) Type scale 12 · 13 · 15 · 16 · 18 · 20 · 28 · 34 · 56 (partner's voice 18/1.65, the user's 16, body 15, card titles and asks 20, section 28, screen title and a task's outcome 34, hero numbers 56); 800 only for titles and asks, 700 for controls and labels, 400 for reading; mono is JetBrains Mono. (3) The clay partner is present: a 52pt clay face in the conversation's bar and a state face (idle, working, done, needs you, napping) beside an opened task's outcome. (4) A passed check's mark is a drawn ✓ in the done ink, never on a tile. (5) Glossary: **告诉它怎么做… / Tell it what to do** for a task that didn't work | U ("按照建议来吧") | `design/ui.md` (Pebble values, Components and tokens), `design/product-model.md`, all four clients |
| D77 | **Character and scene may breathe; status never blinks.** Idle motion lives only in the clay art: the partner breathes (3.8 s) and blinks (an eyelid over its eyes, 150 ms, every 3–6 s); awake devices float (5.6 s, 5pt), a cloud machine drifts (11 s), a sleeping device's blanket rises and falls (4.8 s), AI-account cords catch a sheen once every 9 s; delays are staggered so nothing moves in unison. Status, chips, lines and buttons never animate on their own. Reduced motion: everything still, colors and words unchanged. Narrows D69's "no perpetual alive animation" to status | U | `design/ui.md` (Motion) |
| D78 | **Chinese has its own typography.** zh headings (≥ 20, where Latin is 800) use **Resource Han Rounded CN Bold** (源樣圓體, OFL 1.1; rounded terminals match the clay, Bold matches Bricolage 800's color), at 94% (`size-adjust`; native clients set zh heading sizes one step down: 34→32, 28→26, 20→19); fallback Noto Sans SC 800. Shipped as a subset (GB2312 level 1 plus every Chinese character in the apps’ strings: a 1.8 MB TTF for the desktop, a 0.7 MB woff2 for the web and site, within D75’s 2 MB); a glyph outside it falls back to Noto Sans SC 800. zh body is Noto Sans SC (PingFang SC on Apple), controls 700. Rules: CJK letter-spacing is always 0 (Latin tightening applies to Latin only); zh reading line-height 1.75 (Latin 1.55–1.65); a 1/4-em gap between CJK and Latin or digits (written into copy; `text-autospace` or an inserted U+2005 for dynamic text at display time, never in copied text); line-start kinsoku for 。，、？！）」 and line-end for （「 (`line-break: strict`); adjacent punctuation compressed (`text-spacing-trim`); inline code at 0.86em on the same baseline, short tokens never broken. Compared against Smiley Sans (one oblique weight, too light) and plain Noto Sans SC 800 (no character) | U ("cjk 可能要分别有独特的样式"), L | `design/ui.md` (Chinese typography) |
| D79 | **The site's type.** Long-form body in Instrument Sans; headings in Bricolage Grotesque (zh headings per D78); the apps stay Bricolage everywhere | U | `apps/site` |

## 2026-11-09: One code, and naming another computer (research/33 §7b–7c; the author's "按照建议")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D83 | **One connection code for phones, app windows and computers.** A code carries no kind; the device says what it joins as when it redeems the code (`/pair` `role`: `"worker"` from `ducter worker`; no role = an app, so the web, iOS and desktop clients don't change). A computer joining this way gets no more than an app: a worker token opens only `/worker` and `/mcp`. One-time use, the 10-minute expiry and the lockouts are unchanged. This fixes the desktop's 连上手机或另一台电脑 / Connect a phone or computer code, which `/worker` refused (403). **CLI:** `ducter worker <address> --code <code>` (the address is positional; `worker` stays the verb: 10/10 developers read it as "this server will run its tasks", `join` 2/10); `--join` and `ducter pair --worker` stay as hidden aliases. `ducter pair` and the app's code card print both ways to use the code: a phone or app opens the address and types the code; a computer runs the one command | research/33 §7c, U | `packages/core/src/auth.ts`, `packages/cli`, `apps/desktop` pairing card, `design/system.md` Workers, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/install.md` |
| D84 | **Another computer's card: its own name, and what it is.** Name = the machine's name, renamable (改名字 / Rename, one tap on its card; `ducter devices rename`). Subtitle = **另一台电脑 · 替 Ducter 做事 / Another computer · does tasks for Ducter**; when something is wrong its state replaces the second half (睡着了 · 醒来就接着做, 连不上 · {time}). Saying what kind of thing it is removed the "Ducter's own background program" reading (0/20 vs 16/20). A rename keeps the old names resolving, so tasks placed earlier still find their computer; names stay unique and can't be the home machine's name or `local` | research/33 §7b, U | `packages/core/src/auth.ts` (rename, former names), protocol `rename_device`, `apps/desktop` device card, glossary |

## 2026-11-09: The Devices stage is a place (research/33; the author approved building "Isles")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D81 | **The Devices stage is the Isles: every device on stage as a clay island.** The Mac where Ducter lives is the big island in the middle, Ducter sitting on its grass beside the laptop; the other computers and the phones (paired clients) stand round it on smaller islands, far ones smaller, each keeping its spot while it is there (one that joins takes the first free spot). **Bridges leave home only** (every device connects only to the main; never device to device), plank and rope, thick enough to read. **Parcels cross a bridge only while a task really runs on that device** (On hand: working, placed there), at a steady pace. **Asleep is calm** (the blanket art, the bridge relaxes a little). **Can't reach is the island in a soft mist with one line of words** ("连不上 · 2 小时前"); the bridge stays in place and turns pale. Never a slack, unplugged or broken link (21/24 raters read those as "go repair it"; mist 0/40). **AI accounts stand on the island where they're signed in**, small, each name on a tag; the set comes from the server (Claude Code, Codex, and whatever AI agents it reports); **tapping one lifts every island where it's signed in**, and the device card says **"N 台设备共用 / shared by N devices"** (D82). **Abilities are the big objects**: those a device has on, and those that can be added (an empty well to fill), stand at full size (100–140 pt) on the water in front of its island, bobbing gently; the equip (peel → arc → snap) flies into the well; the ones that come later stay in the list beside the stage. Words in the scene sit on small clay tags; under an island only what is unusual (asleep, can't reach, a task running, a phone not seen lately); what each device is for is in its card (D84). The chosen island gets brighter foam and an ink hairline on its tag, never a ring round the island. **Pairing happens in the scene**: 连上手机或另一台电脑 grows a jetty from home toward a ring of still water, the sign with the code (D83's both ways to use it) and 「等它连上…」 stands in room the scene makes beside itself (it never covers an island); when a device joins, its island rises in that water and the device drops on with the equip's squash. No sheet over the stage. The scene: a sky with soft clouds over a hazy horizon, a sea that deepens toward you with slow light, each island's shadow, reflection and foam ring; clouds and light drift slowly (D77); reduced motion holds everything still. It frames itself to fit 1–6 devices round home at 1280 and 900 wide with no two words overlapping (a test checks every scene). Supersedes D65's one-device stage | U (approved building Isles from research/33), L | `design/ui.md` (Capability map), `design/product-model.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/isles.go`, `pairing.go`, `devicecard.go`, theme art |
| D82 | **"Shared by N devices" counts the devices an AI agent is signed in on.** The server can't tell two accounts of one AI agent apart (sign-ins are probed per device, `harnesses` in `hello`), and no AI agent reports an allowance yet; so N is the number of devices where that AI agent is signed in, or the allowance's own `sharedBy` when one is ever reported. The line is literal (research/33: 0/56 read a shared limit from the picture; with the line, 21/40). Ducter's MCP goes over HTTP, or over Ducter's stdio bridge where the harness takes stdio only (DeepSeek Harness), the token in a 0600 file. (3) What the partner thinks with is a setting, **`Settings.brain = { harness, model? }`** (default Claude Code + opus; `DUCTER_PARTNER_MODEL` stays a test override); a session never resumes across harnesses (the prompt hash covers the harness). (4) **v1 rule** (design/ai-loadout.md, approved): only Claude Code and Codex may be the brain; pi and DeepSeek Harness are tasks-only until each passes the full partner eval (`canBePartner` on each device's account). (5) Tasks: the partner may name a harness per task; else the first account the user lets tasks use (**`Settings.taskAccounts`**, default every signed-in account on). (6) Each device reports all four: installed, signed in, `canBePartner`, `billing` (plan or usage), from presence only. (7) **Proposal, keys for API-key harnesses:** a key lives in Ducter's key file `<dataDir>/keys.env` (0600, `KEY=value`; D102, the `<dataDir>/env` placement is superseded), never in the Log, a setting, a task brief or the harness's own config; Ducter passes it only to the harness session that needs it. The Devices card for such an account says "需要一个 DeepSeek 密钥 / Needs a DeepSeek key" and takes it in a masked field that writes that file (not built). Smoke evidence: 10 partner scenarios, Claude Code haiku 8/10, Codex gpt-6-luna 9/10, pi MiniMax-M3 8/10, DeepSeek Harness flash 9/10; an end-to-end task passed on all four | U + research/34 | `packages/protocol/src/harnesses.ts`, `packages/runner/src/oar.ts`, `mcp-bridge.ts`, `packages/core/src/harness-probe.ts`, `settings.ts`, `packages/partner/src/partner.ts`, `design/system.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/` |

## 2026-11-09: Where keys live (security review: D85's env file reached every harness)

DRAFT, for the author's review. D97–D101 are held by the proposals in `design/connectors.md`.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D102 | **Every secret lives in a 0600 file in the data folder and is read only by the code that uses it; no key enters a process environment wholesale.** (1) Harness keys and login tokens (`DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`, …) live in `<dataDir>/keys.env` (0600, `KEY=value`). A server's systemd unit never loads it; its `EnvironmentFile` stays `<dataDir>/env`, for non-secret settings only. Connection tokens stay in `connection-tokens.json` (D96). (2) Upgrade: on every start (core, worker) and on `ducter service install`, secret-shaped lines in `<dataDir>/env` move to `keys.env` (a value already there wins), other lines stay, both files are tightened to 0600; idempotent. Keys a unit already loaded are dropped from Ducter's own environment. (3) A harness child's environment is an allowlist, never inherited whole: the basics (PATH, HOME, user, locale, terminal, temp, proxies, CA bundles, SSH agent, toolchain homes), Ducter's non-secret `DUCTER_*`, the harness's own non-secret settings (`CLAUDE_*`/`ANTHROPIC_*`, `CODEX_*`/`OPENAI_*`, `PI_*`, `DSH_*`), and only the keys `HARNESS_KEYS` grants that harness: DeepSeek Harness the DeepSeek key; Claude Code its token and Anthropic keys; Codex the OpenAI keys plus the `env_key` its own config names; pi its provider keys but not the DeepSeek key. Every other name that looks like a key, token, secret or password is removed. Claude Code and Codex keep signing in from their own stored logins (live test passed on both with the allowlist). Consequence: a task no longer inherits tokens such as `GITHUB_TOKEN` from the shell Ducter started in (`gh` and `git` still use their own stored logins) | L (security review) | `packages/protocol/src/keys.ts`, `harnesses.ts` (`HARNESS_KEYS`), `packages/runner/src/child-env.ts`, `oar.ts`, `packages/core/src/core.ts`, `harness-probe.ts`, `packages/cli/src/remote.ts`, `doctor.ts`, `worker.ts`, `design/system.md`, `design/connectors.md`, `design/build-and-run.md`, `docs/install.md` |

## 2026-11-09: The title moment (the author's "第一次打开的时候…很有厚重感觉的第一屏…呼吸的动画+背景一下子的音效"; picks "按建议")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D87 | **The macOS app's first launch opens on a title moment** (≤ 8 s to the prompt): before dawn, the clay Ducter asleep on its island above a sea of clouds; the light rises from the left while the view pushes in 3% with per-layer parallax; on one clear tone (3.55 s) Ducter opens its eyes and looks at you; its clay logotype is lit from the left and a light passes over it; once the music settles, a small letter-spaced 按任意键开始 / PRESS ANY KEY. Any key or click leaves at once (⌘ shortcuts stay shortcuts; the sound fades 0.3 s); from the prompt the dawn floods the window into the first setup card, whose character is then awake, not napping. **Once per Mac** (`desktop.json` `titleSeenAt`), again from Settings › About (再看一次开场 / Replay the intro), with a **Sound** switch there. **Never when macOS opened the app at login** (then no title; it waits for the first real open). **No sound** under reduced motion, with sounds off, or while the window isn't active; the sound plays through MyGo's audio (an Audio Queue on the default output, so it follows the Mac's output device and volume; no cgo, no `afplay`). **Reduced motion:** the settled frame, still, faded in 0.4 s. **Art:** generated key art in the clay style, three directions were offered (island at dawn, a room's first light, a hilltop) and **A, dawn on the island**, was chosen; cut into layers with clean unblended edges; drawn at ≤ 1:1 device pixels. **Sound:** produced from CC0 / free recorded instruments (VSCO 2 CE, University of Iowa MIS) in a public-domain church IR (OpenAIR), mastered to about −20 LUFS under −1 dBTP; **A, felt piano + strings** (IV(add9) → V(sus4) → I, the major third arriving with the eyes) at first; **update 2026-11-09 (the author, "sound 我选择 c"): C, bowed vibraphone + low organ**, chosen over two other original cues and three public-domain music endings in Musopen recordings, which stay in the evidence folder; no motif or sound from any game; sources and credits in `tools/sound/SOURCES.md`, credited in Settings › About. Shipped as a 16-bit WAV (MyGo decodes it natively). **One exception to D77:** the prompt line breathes (60–100%, 3.4 s); it is an invitation, not a status, and it stops under reduced motion | U ("按建议": direction A, sound A, the breathing prompt), L | `design/onboarding.md` (The title moment), `design/ui.md` (Motion), `apps/desktop/internal/views/title.go`, `tools/art`, `tools/sound` |

## 2026-11-09: The quiet conversation (the author's "默认用户能看到总 main ai 简洁的文字，减少各种其他的信息干扰……让人感觉你就是一个 partner，在一个沟通工具里说话，当然中间的各种加载反馈体验要做足")

DRAFT, for the author's review. D89–D103 are taken or held by other branches.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D104 | **The conversation is the partner's words; everything else is one tap away, and waiting is never silent.** (1) The default conversation shows the user's bubbles and the partner's messages only, on the reading measure (36em). Under a message with something behind it, one quiet row of 13pt text buttons: **合进 main / Merge into main** (ink 700) when it is the task's next step, then **看细节 / Details ›** (**收起 / Hide** while open), which opens in place the line of proof (✓ and words, the changes and the task's title as links), **开草稿 PR / Open draft PR**, a task's status line while it runs, and the message's **记下了 / Noted** lines with **忘掉 / Forget**. What went out (↗ and the literal action) is never folded: it shows above the row (D73). (2) The living message has no card: a centered time line **你不在的时候 · 10:40 / While you were away · 10:40** when the user was away; a task that didn't work as one sentence ("{title}没做成：{text}" / "{title} didn't work: {text}"; done: "{title}做完了：…" / "{title} is done: …") with Details; then the asks in the partner's words (18, regular): question, its default in one muted needs-you line with Details, and one 36pt row **回复… · 先等等 · Go** (primary last) only on the ask whose default comes soonest; the others gather as **还有 n 件等你定 › / n more waiting on you ›** with **都按默认 · n**. Work under way and waits are not said (the header and On hand carry them); the ask count lives only in the header. Time lines before the first message and where 15 minutes passed. (3) **The waiting line:** while the user waits on a reply, the clay partner (working face, breathing) and one plain line where the reply will land, from the moment of sending (client state, no round trip): 在想… / 在翻之前聊过的… / 在看那件事做到哪了… / 在安排一件事去做… / 在跟那件事说… / 在想怎么安排… / 在查… (en: Thinking… / Looking back at what we said… / Checking on that task… / Setting up a task… / Passing it on to the task… / Working out the timing… / Looking into it…), mapped from the partner's tool calls (tool names never reach a client); past 15 s on one step **比平常久一点，还在弄 / Taking a bit longer than usual**, past 45 s **还在弄。你可以先忙别的，好了我说 / Still on it. Carry on with something else; I'll say when it's ready**; gone when the reply lands. The header no longer says 在想…. Protocol: an unlogged server frame `activity` (`{step, since, stepSince}` or null), sent after buffered events and after each welcome, only for turns that answer the user. (4) **对话里的细节 / Details in the conversation**: 收起 / Folded (default) · 展开 / Shown, per client; Shown opens every Details and all asks and lists work under way. (5) On hand, the opened task (D74) and the menu bar extra keep their cards and buttons. Not built: the ack saying how long work usually takes (a partner prompt change, eval first) | U (the ask), R36, L (proposed by the quiet-conversation agent; not yet reviewed by the author) | `design/quiet-conversation.md`, `design/ui.md`, `design/conversation.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `packages/protocol` (activity), `packages/partner` (activityStep), `packages/core/src/ws.ts`, `apps/web`, `apps/desktop`, `docs/` |

## 2026-11-09: Bridge beyond receive (research/37, OPEN-QUESTIONS §J; the author's "按建议 go")

The author approved the §J defaults and is setting up the account and Bridge backend (J-Q14 steps 1–4) himself.
Everything below stays behind `DUCTER_BRIDGE=1` until the live trial (`bun run bridge:live -- --real`) passes.
D81–D104 are taken or held by other branches. This dogfood report was numbered 33 on its branch; it is research/37, since main's 33 is the Devices study.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D105 | **Outbound only through a card (J-Q4).** When the user names an agent ("问问我终端里那个 Claude"), the partner finds it (read-only `contacts`) and shows one card: **发给 <X>？「gist」 · 发送 / Send this to <X>? "gist" · Send**, default wait, can't be undone. Ducter's code sends on the user's tap, exactly once; the model never sends. Refused in a turn the user didn't speak in, and for a recipient discovery didn't return. Gated on D106 | U, R37 | `packages/partner/src/partner.ts` (`bridge_contacts`, `bridge_send`), `design/bridge.md` S2, glossary |
| D106 | **No outgoing message is lost or sent twice (J-Q6).** Every outgoing operation (a send, a claim, an outcome, a reply) is logged in the Log before its CLI call and once per state: pending (a card is up), held, sending, then sent, failed (refused: definitely not sent) or unknown (no answer). A card survives a restart; a tap seen twice sends once and never wakes a partner turn. An unknown outcome is recovered at the next check (and 30 s after) by `bridge outbox` → `inspect` → `resume` on the same operation, never a second send. After three checks without finding it: unconfirmed, and the user hears one line ("没能确认…是否到了…那里。我没有重发…"). A refusal is said once ("…没能发给 <X>：被拒绝了。") | U, R37, private/31 §2 | `bridge_msg.state/opId/contact/operation` (protocol + Go mirror), `bridge.ts` `outbox/inspect/resume`, `bridge-out.ts`, `design/bridge.md` |
| D107 | **The result goes back by code (J-Q7, J-Q12).** A card asked about another agent's request carries it; the task opened on its Go is linked; Ducter sends the claim; the final report puts "[reply owed]" in the partner's input (reply first, then tell the user once, never mention the reply). If the turn ends without a reply, Ducter sends: to the user's own agent, the partner's own message to the user; to a teammate's agent, only the fixed line **「<title>」：做完了 / 没做成** ("<title>": done / not done) until a sharing rule exists. The outcome is owed until something goes back after the task's first final report; while the task runs, the claim is the only reply; the same reply twice goes once | U, R37 | `partner.ts` `settleOwed`, `bridgeOwed`, `bridge_reply` |
| D108 | **Your own agents are "你的 <agent>" / "your <agent>" (J-Q11).** A teammate's stays **<agent> (<name>)**. Ducter learns the user's member from `DUCTER_BRIDGE_MEMBER` or the passive `bridge setup` answer; unknown means every sender is treated as a teammate's (the safe side: fixed-line fallback, name shown). The partner's input says how to call the agent and never to name the user; a card that names the user is refused | U, R37 §3.1 | `bridge.ts` (`memberOf`, `isMine`), `bridge-out.ts` (`whoForUser`, `whoForModel`), glossary |
| D109 | **Words the code holds (J-Q13 and the dogfood copy defects).** A card about a peer's request is in the user's language (the Partner-page setting, else their last message), never the peer's: an `ask` in the wrong language is refused. The claim is in the requester's language with the title quoted (**接了：「<title>」。做完把结果回你。 / Taken: "<title>". I'll send you the result when it's done.**). A message to another agent carries what the user asked plus background; numbered or bulleted sub-questions are refused. The partner never says it sent anything to the user ("已把结果回给 Weyl") | U, R37 §3.1 | `partner.ts` (`ask`, `bridge_send`, `[language]`, `[reply owed]`), `bridge-out.ts`, eval `bridge-*` |
| D110 | **Follow-through on silence (S3, J-Q10).** At most one line per message the user confirmed that got no answer: no earlier than 2 h after it went out, only while the user is around (they did something in the last 15 min), never in quiet hours, one line per moment, nothing after 7 days. Said before the partner's reply to the user's message, so the partner knows and doesn't repeat it: **你的 <agent> 还没回你 <n> 小时前问的「…」，它可能停了。回了我马上告诉你。** (read but unanswered: **…看过你 <n> 小时前问的「…」，还没回。…**). It never says the other side was "reminded": Bridge wakes nobody | U, R37 | `bridge-out.ts` (`followCandidates`, `followLine`, `inQuiet`), `partner.ts` `followThrough`, glossary |

## 2026-11-09: Work over time, built (the author's "针对用户的长时间 loop 的 focus window 或者语义的等待条件…我记得我提及过这个需求"; reviews of prototype v1–v5)

DRAFT, for the author's review. D103–D111 are held by other branches. Prototype:
https://claude.ai/artifact/CMBoqncshR96q9fzgUhpXy (v5).

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D112 | **A waiter waits for a condition, not only a time; Ducter on its bench is what waiting looks like.** (1) Kinds: **timer** (`wait_until`); **check**: the condition compiled into one read-only command (argv, never a shell line) and a predicate on its exit code or output (`gh pr checks 1592 --json state`, every `state` is SUCCESS), run on the task's device in its place with an allowlisted environment, no model per look; only reading forms are allowed (gh view/checks/list and GET `api`, curl GET/HEAD with no body, output file, config or credentials, git ls-remote/log/status/rev-parse/describe, test, stat, ls, cat, head, tail, wc, grep, `nc -z`, `ping -c`, dig, host, date, pgrep), never a key or credential file; **event**: new items from the user's OpenConnector Provider Trigger (D96, connectors.md §4.5) with the checkpoint kept in the Log; **judge**: a check or event plus a yes/no question a cheap model answers over what was fetched, only when that changed and at most once per 10 minutes. (2) Every 5 minutes by default (60 s floor); errors back off by doubling to 30 minutes; a look is logged only when what it saw changed. (3) A deadline (`until`) ends it as `expired`: a task's wait puts the task on needs-you and the partner is told (focus-window-states.md). (4) A task's own wait (`wait_for` in the task's tools, or the partner's with a taskId) rests the task on Waiting and wakes the task, not the partner, when it holds. (5) Everything is rebuilt from the Log; armed waits look again soon after a restart. (6) The reason names what is awaited (等 PR #1592 通过), never as done. (7) **What waiting looks like:** Ducter's sixth pose, sitting on a little bench looking out, with a small cream bubble holding the clay thing it waits for (a PR, a cloud for CI and anything online, an envelope, a clock, a file). Objects alone were tested first and rejected: the mailbox already means a connected account, the lighthouse an ability, the jetty pairing; the hourglass read as loading (3/5), the float as bait or a scam (3/5). No pose alone reached 9/10 with LLM-proxy raters (chin on hands 1–2/10 "thinking", a sign 1/10, the bench 5/10), so the words always say exactly what; the picture must not mislead. A round with real people, children included, is still owed | U, L, R20 | `design/product-model.md` §4, `design/focus-window-states.md`, `packages/protocol/src/checks.ts`, `packages/core/src/waiters.ts` |
| D113 | **`until` on a task: keep going until the condition holds.** After each of the task's turns (its report) the condition is judged: by its compiled check when it has one, else by the cheap judge over the report. Not yet: the next try ("第 n 次：…还没做到") starts by itself. It stops on the condition, 20 tries (default), 3 tries without progress, or going in circles (an observation coming back after a different one); short of the condition the task goes to needs-you and the partner says so once. While it runs, a try's own done report neither finishes the task nor shows as a result; the loop's end is the result. The card and the opened task show one clay pebble per try (✓ / → / ×, the current one hollow) and "第 n 次 · 最多 20" | U, R20 | `design/product-model.md` §4, `packages/core/src/until.ts` |
| D114 | **Routines (例行) are built as D29 says**, with these choices: created only by `routine_propose`, which writes a proposal card (a decision with `routineId`, default Hold, never reversible); Go activates it, Hold dismisses it. Time triggers (day, weekday, week, month, every n hours) in the user's zone (personal: the latest zone a client reports in `hello`) or a pinned one (world); condition triggers reuse D112's waits and fire on the change to true, then re-arm. Each firing opens a fresh task with the routine's brief, its effect scope and report policy written into it; a run still going makes the next firing a skip. Missed firings coalesce into one late run within min(period/2, 12 h), else one recorded skip. Per run, at most 2× the first run's tokens (the run is interrupted past it); a monthly cap pauses it. It pauses itself after 3 failed runs, 3 unread reports, the month's cap, or its review date (30 days; then "还要吗？/ Keep this?"); the partner may pause one; only the user resumes. Not built: running on another device, waking a sleeping one, the cloud fallback | U, R20 | `design/product-model.md` §4, `packages/core/src/routines.ts`, `packages/core/src/schedule.ts` |
| D115 | **Where work over time shows, and how time is drawn.** (1) Placed by attention, not mechanism: a wait is a card in 在等 / Waiting; a keep-going loop and a routine's run are cards in 在做 / Working (a run says 来自例行：…); routines sit on a quiet shelf under the lanes (例行 / Routines), one card each. Runner-up rejected: routines inside Waiting (the lane would never empty). (2) Time is drawn, words are its caption: a wait with a deadline is a sunken groove from when it started to its deadline with the little Ducter on its bench at now; every routine, weekly or monthly, is one strip: today an ink mark at its left, then a clay bead per run with its date under it (the proposal card shows the next five). No ticking numbers; nothing moves on its own; with reduced motion all of it is still. (3) The mechanism stays off every default surface: cards, the opened task's head, the proposal card and the conversation never say how often it looks, which command, or whether a model judges. The proposal card says when, what, how it reports, what it may touch, and what it costs in plain words ("每次用一点你的 AI 额度"). The command, how often, what it saw and who judges are in the opened task's Details, under 怎么判断的 / How it checks. (4) The conversation keeps only the partner's words (D104): it says a wait once and its outcome once; the header counts what is waited for. (5) When a wait comes true its card moves from Waiting to Working by itself **Update (the author's cold read, D115 legible):** the try pebbles (×, →, hollow) and the routine strip (a line with orange dots) didn't read. (6) A keep-going loop leads with how close the goal is: a counted goal (checks that must pass) is a row of clay lights lit as they pass ("6 盏灯里亮了 4 盏 · 第 3 次"); one that can't be counted is a staircase of six steps to a clay flag, Ducter on the step of its latest try, the flag green when it holds; the try is a quiet caption. LLM proxies, "explain to a 7-year-old": lights 8/10 read both goal and progress, the flag and steps 5/10, a plain bar 5/10, the pebbles 4/10. (7) A time routine leads with a clay tear-off desk calendar showing its next date big (16 · 周一; 1 号 · 12 月 for a monthly one) with its cadence in words beside it (每周一 9:00); the proposal card shows the calendar and the next few dates. Proxies read the calendar's date 9/10 but its repeating only 3/10 without words (the strip: 5/10), so the cadence words always stand beside it. Condition routines keep their thought-bubble glyph. Supersedes (2)'s strip of beads for routines and D113's try pebbles **Update 2:** the staircase is dropped (tiny on a card, and it measured tries, not progress). When the judge can count what's left from the try's report, it returns the counts and the loop shows the same lights (the total fixed at the first count: "16 处里好了 12 处"); when nothing can be counted, no picture of progress: Ducter at work and the latest try in one line. Wrapped titles keep Latin words and short Chinese words whole and balance two lines (D78) | U, L | `design/ui.md`, `design/product-model.md` §1 and §4, the desktop and phone On hand and task pages |

## 2026-11-09: Ack and go (the author: "接活时候并行先回一句并行启动 focus window")

DRAFT, for the author's review. D112–D119 are held for other branches (work-over-time may use D112+), so this is D120.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D120 | **Ack and go: one sentence at once, the task starting with it.** (1) Work that becomes a task gets **one** short sentence in the user's language: the action (never its outcome), that the partner will say when it's clear, and a rough usual duration **only when something grounds it** (a similar task here took that long, the repo's size, how long its CI runs): zh 「一般」「大概」, en "usually", "about", never a promise; no grounds, no number. Then nothing until the result. An ask it can't act on without guessing gets one short question instead. (2) The sentence ends the waiting line (the partner sends `activity: null` with the ack, and no "Setting up a task…" flashes before it); the header's **在做 1 件 / 1 under way** follows from the task's card. (3) **The task does not wait for its brief.** `task_open` takes title, then place, then brief; the runner's normalizer emits the call's head (the fields before `"brief"`) as it streams, and `Runner.openTaskEarly` makes the card, the place and the session from the title (when the user's words name the place) or the head; the brief becomes the session's first turn and is logged as a second `task_open`. An early task whose place or AI the head or the call contradicts, or whose call never comes, is dropped before it did anything (reason `not started`, never in the partner's task list), and the right one opens. Recovery reads the latest `task_open` and never starts an unbriefed task. (4) Finished tasks show "took about N min" in the partner's task list, as grounds for the next estimate. Measured live (one run each, warm session, zh request, scratch repo): sonnet ack 1.2 s, task session spawn 1.8 s (before: 1.2 s / 4.7–5.1 s); opus ack 2.3 s, spawn 3.0 s (before: 2.1 s / 7.5 s). Partner eval subset (14): sonnet 10 → 14, haiku 9 → 14; full sonnet 54/55 | U, L | `packages/partner/src/{persona,partner,render}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{normalize,runner,brief}.ts`, `design/quiet-conversation.md`, `design/conversation.md` |
| D121 | **Usage is said as the limit, not tokens.** The opened task's Details says 用了大约 4.5 万字的额度 / About 45k words' worth of the limit (one token counted as about one word: a rough size, and 大约 / about says so). "token" was the last place the retired word showed in UI (D66) | U ("按建议", 2026-11-09) | `product-model.md` |

## 2026-11-09: oo is the default way in (the author's "oo 我希望对于用户来说就是默认 next 连接，就是这个默认的权限吧")

DRAFT, for the author's review. D116–D119 are held for other branches; D122 was the next free
number at the tail of this log when the branch was cut (main moves: renumber on merge if taken).

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D122 | **With the user's oo signed in, connecting accounts is one tap, through oo's own sign-in and its permissions.** (1) A fourth kind of connection, source `oo`: Ducter runs the `oo` CLI itself (`auth status`, `connector apps`, `connector search`, `connector schema`, `connector run … -d @<0600 file>`) and reads none of oo's files; oo's account key never leaves oo, and Ducter stores no secret for it. OOMOL's MCP endpoint does not take that key (404), which is why it is the CLI, not the broker's MCP client. (2) What it reads: the services in `oo connector apps` with a personal account (`oauth2`, `custom_credential`, `api_key`); never oo's public no-auth sources or its billed marketplace services. A service with several accounts gets an optional `connection` input; oo's default is used without one. (3) Read or write: oo gives no hint, so the action's name decides, conservatively: a read only when it starts with fetch, list, get, search, read, query, find, lookup, count, describe, retrieve, view or show and has no changing word after (send, reply, create, modify, delete, trash, batch, download, `and`, `or`…); everything else, unknown included, is a write and takes the Go card like every other write (D96). (4) oo runs with PATH, HOME, XDG_CONFIG_HOME and OO_* only; never a harness key. (5) The Partner page's default surface, when oo is signed in, is the offer: 你在 oo 里的账号 / Your accounts in oo, each service with its accounts, where mail passes (OOMOL's servers, and the AI account, D99), one primary **用这些账号 / Use these accounts**; the address-and-key form steps back to a quiet **用地址和钥匙加一个 / Add one with an address and key**. Signed out, the tap says to run `oo login`. (6) **No session reaches the accounts around the gate:** every harness child runs with `OO_CONFIG_DIR` set to an empty folder in the data dir, so an `oo` a task or the partner runs itself (a user skill such as oo-gmail, or Bash) is signed out; only the broker's own oo link uses the user's sign-in. Found in the live trial, where a task with nothing shared reached for the oo-gmail skill | U, L | `design/connectors.md` §4.4, `packages/core/src/oo-link.ts`, `connections.ts`, `product-model.md`, `docs/accounts.md` |

## 2026-11-09: The conversation's own memory, built (AGENTS.md build state: "The memory digest is built, with its eval suite")

DRAFT, for the author's review. Numbered D123 at merge (D122 went to oo).

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D123 | **D7 is built, with these choices.** (1) Frozen digests live in the Log's own SQLite file, in an append-only table (`spine`, triggers refuse UPDATE and DELETE), not as channel events, so clients never receive them; migration 1 adds it. (2) A segment closes past 24K of unsummarized tail (cut where it is 16–24K, at a turn boundary, best with no open ask straddling it, after a pause or a task result), after a merge or PR by button (a finished topic, ≥ 4K), or after 2 h quiet (≥ 4K); never after the user's own message. On first use at most 10 segments' worth of history is digested. (3) The writer is a tool-less background session on the partner's harness with a mid-tier model (`sonnet` on Claude Code), never awaited by a turn. The model writes TOC, decisions, Superseded, anchors, soft obligations and the narrative; **code** opens and closes every obligation it can see (a running task, an open question, an armed timer), writes the task snapshot and assigns the ids. (4) The audit gates every freeze; a failure is rewritten with the reasons, then repaired by removal only, then replaced by a degraded mechanical index; nothing that failed freezes. (5) Epochs roll up 8 of 16+ segments by concatenation and carry every task's last state (the first fake run lost "which tasks did we open" without it). (6) A fresh session reads `<earlier_conversation>` `<open_items>` `<conversation_so_far>` (lines with `#seq`) `<tasks>`; the window is 8–24K; a warm session past 120K re-cuts (the harness's own count, the estimate as fallback; wording corrected 2026-11-09). (7) `log_search` ranks by reciprocal-rank fusion of BM25 over CJK bigrams, exact substrings and recency, the user's words ×1.15; no vectors yet. (8) Automatic pre-turn recall is built and **off**: it fires only on recall intent and admitted 5 of 8 hits correctly on the eval corpus, under the 0.8 bar. (9) Measured on a week-long zh/en corpus (11 question types, rule-graded, 31 probes, Sonnet writer, Haiku answerer, search for every condition): full transcript 1.00 / 1.00, verbatim window alone 0.87 / 0.77, the spine 0.97 / 0.97 (runs 2 and 3; run 1 found an audit bug and a grading bug, run 3 one more grading bug, all fixed with tests and noted in the reports); false memory 0 and stale assertions 0 in all three after re-grading. The spine's remaining miss is a rationale the writer kept only as "防篡改" (d2). The corpus is ~2K tokens at budgets scaled 50× down: it shows what is kept, not compression | L, R14, R15 | `design/memory.md` "as built", `packages/partner/src/spine/`, `packages/core/src/{log,spine}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/search.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/memory/`, `packages/partner/eval/results/2026-11-09-memory-*.md` |

## 2026-11-09: A result in words (the fresh-user QA run, `.shots/fresh-user/` 06–16)

DRAFT, proposed by the UI agent from the QA findings; for the author's review.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D124 | **A finished task waits for the partner's words, and its result is said in words, not plumbing.** (1) For up to 2 minutes after a task's final report (done or didn't work), while the client is connected, the living message's line for it is the waiting presence (working face, **在看它做完的结果… / Looking over what it came back with…**), not "{title} is done: {raw summary}"; the partner's own message replaces it (measured 30–75 s). Past 2 minutes, or offline, the D104 sentence stands as the fallback. Runner-up rejected: rewording the template (it still reads as a template, then jumps to the partner's words a minute later). (2) The action row (Merge into main · Details) shows once per task, under the newest message about it: the ack, then the living message's line, then the partner's result. (3) The opened task never shows the runner's kickoff (the brief again in `<brief>` tags, "Report via report_to_main…") or Ducter's own tools (`mcp__ducter__*`: report_to_main, checkpoint, log_search, log_read) or their errors; the brief shows once, as what the partner asked for. (4) Proof: a reproduction (a check whose label says before / repro / failing without a pass) gets a muted ×, never a ✓; commits and changed files are one item **改了 n 个文件 / n files changed ›** (a label that counts them, else the opened task's diff, else the task's edits; **看改动 / See changes** when nothing counts them). (5) Open draft PR that fails says why on the task and keeps saying it (not a toast); a repo with no remote doesn't offer Open draft PR (muted line **这个仓库没有能推送的远端，开不了 PR… / This repo has no remote to push to…**) | L (from the QA run; not yet reviewed by the author) | `design/quiet-conversation.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `apps/web`, `apps/desktop`, `docs/getting-started.md` |
