## 2026-11-09: The On hand ask and the opened task, redesigned (the author's v0.0.1 App Store Connect run)

DRAFT, for the author's review. Screenshots: `.shots/task-transcript/`.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D127 | **An ask on On hand reads at one glance; an opened task reads as a compact transcript, one quiet line per step.** From the author's v0.0.1 run (Codex preparing App Store Connect): the card said too much at once and the opened task "looks too dated" (raw JSON tool calls as errors, "用了 3 次工具" folds, "User says Go: …" leaking, "先做到这儿" dividers, empty Changes, a home path in Details). (1) **The On hand ask:** who and what in one row (the agent's clay coin, its hover saying where and why; the task's short title, its parenthetical details dropped, the full title on hover), ONE question, its default as a small timer (a rim and a wedge of the time left, D115's grammar) with one caption **{time}后按默认：{default} / In {time}: {default}**, then Reply… · Hold · Go. No report paragraph and no meta line on the card. The Go button is a short verb (at most 6 Chinese characters or 3 English words, no full stop), else plain **好 / Go**, on every surface; the task tool's `default` is described to the agents as that label. The forecast strip is one quiet muted line (**接下来 / Next**) and only when more than one thing is coming. (2) **What it did:** the agent's own sentences as normal text; between them each tool call is ONE line: a small drawn mark, a verb, a one-line summary, truncated: **思考 / Thinking** (a stretch of thinking, its bold headings only: the runner keeps the headings and never the text, `task_event.thinking`), **运行 / Ran** (the command's name and subcommand: `cargo test`, `xcrun altool`), **读取 / Read**, **编辑 / Edited** (a path's last two parts), **搜索 / Searched**, **浏览 / Browsed** ("在 Chrome 里打开 appstoreconnect.apple.com"), **操作 / Used** (the computer: "在 Chrome 里按 ⌘T"), **等待 / Waited**, **交给 / Handed off**, **出错 / Error** (one red-tinted line in words; what it printed opens on a click, in mono). Commands, paths and patterns read in mono on the inline-code tint (a code token, not a status capsule, D73). The same step repeated says ×n; a run of more than six steps shows its latest four behind **前面还有 n 条 / n more above ›**. The kinds are one table for every harness (`packages/protocol/src/steps.ts`, its Go port, one fixture): shell → Ran (a `cat`/`sed -n` → Read, an `rg` → Searched, `open <url>` → Browsed), file tools and patches → Read / Edited, the browser tools (cua-driver's, Claude in Chrome) and Codex's computer `js` → Browsed / Used with the app carried over from the step that named it. Ducter's own tools and a harness's bookkeeping (its plan list, a computer session's sign-off) are not steps (D124). Saved progress is a tiny flag line with its time. The user's Go relayed to the task shows as the user's own bubble (**好 / Go**), never "User says Go: …". A turn ending, a session starting and a browser's "Looking at" say nothing and are not shown; a turn that failed is an Error line. (3) **The bar over the composer:** who is doing it (the coin, the agent and the model) and where it stands for how long (**在做 · 12:34** ticking while it works; **做完了 · 用了 18 分钟 / Done · took 18 min**); the head's meta line no longer names who. The headline says what it said or saved since its latest report once it has moved on. (4) **Changes** shows only when the worktree has changed files (no "no changes yet", no Refresh to push). **Details**: where is the repo and branch, the path on hover and copied, never spelled out; usage in muted ink | U (the author's v0.0.1 run), L | `design/ui.md` (Three zoom levels), `design/focus-window.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `packages/protocol` (steps, `task_event.thinking`), `packages/runner` (normalize, tools), `apps/desktop`, `apps/web` |

## 2026-11-09: You open a task yourself (the author approved the manual-mode proposal: "全都按建议")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D128 | **开一件事 / New task: the user opens a task themselves, with their own words, and the partner reports it like any task.** (1) **Where:** a quiet text button on On hand and ⌘N (inside an opened task ⌘N prefills that task's place); the command line has `ducter task "<words>" [--repo <dir>] [--agent <name>] [--base <branch>]` (default `--repo` is the current folder when it is a git repo, else one clear line with the fix; `--repo none` = no particular place). (2) **The card has three rows:** 在哪儿做 / Where (repos, most recently used first, then the ones `ducter up` noticed, and 不用特定的地方 / No particular place; the base branch is picked in the chip, default the repo's default branch: what origin points at, else main or master, else the checkout's own; the device is inferred as today) · 谁来做 / Who does it (the agent coins from fw_candidates; default the top candidate with its one-line reason; picking another pins it for this task, reason **…，是你选的 / …; you picked it**, BodyWhy `you`) · 做什么 / What (text, with **原话会交给它 / It gets your words as they are**). (3) **Start** opens the task at once with the words verbatim as its brief (no partner rewrite), then opens that task (Esc returns). Title = the first sentence, about 24 Chinese characters or 48 letters, cut with "…"; the partner may retitle it once after the first report (task_retitle). (4) **The Log** records task_open with `origin: "you"` (the card shows `openedBy: "you"`); nothing appears in the conversation at open time (only the counters change). The partner reads a hidden note, 你自己开了「X」/ You opened "X" yourself (a user-origin `body_<task>_opened` job_status, hidden in every client, never waking it), and its task list marks the task; it reports the result like any task (D124). (5) **On hand** gains a grouping toggle 按状态 / 按项目 (By state / By project), desktop only, remembered per client; by project a row is title · branch · +adds −dels · state, and a group header's "+" is 在这儿开一件 / New task here (prefills the place). The opened task's Changes header says **改动 · 3 个文件 +42 −7 / Changes · 3 files +42 −7**. Core counts the diff (files, lines added and removed since the branch left its base, committed or not) after each turn, report and state change, caches it, and puts it on the card (`changes`); On hand never waits for git. (6) **Not in it:** no tabs, no model or effort picker, no pin or search. Attachments wait (todo, trigger: the first time a brief needs a screenshot). (7) **So a lead agent can work Ducter without the author relaying:** `ducter say "<words>"` (a user message over the client protocol, exactly as an app sends it; prints the partner's words as they arrive, ends when its turn settles, `--timeout`; prints any decision it raised with its id), `ducter tail [task]` (the conversation, or one task's steps one line each, as text; Ctrl-C stops), `ducter answer <id or part of the question> go\|hold\|"<reply>"`, and `ducter open <part of a title or id>` (the connected apps show it through a new `open_task` frame; with none, it says where the task is). All take `--port` / `--data-dir` and `--json`, zh and en. (8) Protocol: client frames `task_new_options`, `task_new` (clientId-idempotent), `open_task`; `hello.client`; server frames `task_new_options`, `task_new_result`, `open_task`, `open_result`; `Runner.openTask` takes `origin`; `BranchChanges` carries `files/adds/dels`. Mirrored in the Go client. The card and the grouping UI are not built in this pass (`design/new-task.md`) | U ("全都按建议", 2026-11-09) | `design/new-task.md`, `design/product-model.md` §3, `packages/core/src/{newtask,changes,ws,candidates,loadout,registry}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{integrate,runner}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{index,internal}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/render.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{talk,client,args,i18n,main}.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/protocol`, `design/build-and-run.md`, `docs/install.md` |

## 2026-11-09: The conversation is a messaging app (the author's v0.0.1 test: "主 AI 的回复太开发者了……希望像 Muse / Grok Bot / dots 一样面向大众，低负担，像 iMessage；排版也不好")

DRAFT, for the author's review. Screens: `.shots/conv-bubbles/` (desktop `conv-*`, phone `web-*`, zh/en, light/dark).

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D129 | **The conversation looks and talks like messaging a partner, for people who never wrote code.** (1) **Bubbles.** The partner's words sit in soft bubbles (`partner-bubble`: white on the paper, Pebble dark `#2A221B`), tail bottom-left; the user's in the accent (`user-bubble` `#FF8A3D` with ink, 6.9:1; cocoa before), tail bottom-right. One body size for both and the composer (16; the partner's 18 before), Latin 1.5, zh 1.7–1.75 (D78); a bubble is at most ~38em wide (84–88% on a phone). A run from one voice stacks 4 apart with the tail side's top corner tucked in (`bubble-join` 6); other things one voice says sit 12 apart; 16 where the turn passes. Time lines stay small and centered (D104). What stands under a bubble (the quiet row, **看细节 / Details ›**, a status line) is set in to its text. (2) **An ask** is the question in the partner's bubble and **one compact tray** under it, a white card on a 3pt clay base: **回复… / Reply…** and **先等等 / Hold** as quiet text, then Go last, named by the action (**好，创建**); the default is one small grey line under it (**{n} 分钟后你没回，我就{动作}，之后还能改回来。** · **你没回之前，我先放着。** · can't be undone: **你说好，我再做。 / I'll do it once you say so.**), never red, never "这件不会自己动，等你定". (3) **An answered ask** keeps its question and collapses the tray to one tiny line on the user's side, in their own words: **你说：好，创建 / You said: Create** · **你说：先等等** · **你说：{回复}** · by default **你没回，我照原计划做了 / No answer, so I went ahead**; never "✓ 已经说好". (4) **A task's question is said by the partner in its own words, never pasted.** A turn woken only by `needs_input` reports makes one message (≤ 2 short sentences, ~40 zh characters / 25 en words, only what the user must decide or do), logged as `ai_msg` with `decisionId`; clients show it as that question's words with the task's Go / Hold / Reply under it, and the task's wording moves to Details (**它的原话：… / In its words: …**). For up to 60 s after the question, while connected, its place shows the waiting presence (**在看它想问你什么… / Reading what it's asking…**; measured relays 3–10 s); past that, or offline, the task's wording stands in. A second line in that turn is dropped, as before. (5) **Consumer voice for everyone** (persona): short, plain, warm, one idea per message; no file or repo paths, bundle ids, branch or commit ids, tool or agent names (Claude Code, Codex, MCP) unless the user used them first or asked; an ask's question states the choice in one everyday sentence, plus one only to say what can't be undone; goLabel is the action in 2–6 words; a result leads with the outcome in everyday words, then at most one line of what's next, and things left for the user are a count and a place ("还有四样得你在苹果那边点一下"), never their technical names. The brief to a task keeps every detail. (6) The zh copy was read against the humanizer standard: the decision lines above, **在看做到哪了… · 在把你的话带过去… · 不确定成没成，你看一眼 · 说说你的想法… · 想做什么，跟我说一句就行。需要你的时候，我会来问你。** and others (`design/conversation.md` lists them). Runner-up for the user's bubble: ink (cocoa), rejected as the heaviest thing on the screen and the author's "big dark bubble"; an accent tint, rejected as it reads as the needs-you state. Eval (haiku ×2, the 16 voice-relevant scenarios, `eval/results/2026-11-09-haiku-consumer-voice-final.md`): 24/32, consumer 4/6, needs_input 2/4, ask 8/8, result 4/6, settle 0/2 (before, main's persona ×1: 10/16, consumer 0/3, needs_input 0/2, ask 4/4, result 3/3, settle 0/1; the result and settle misses are the known "repeats the test count" judge call, also in the baseline). Sonnet, 6 scenarios: 4/6 (needs_input 2/2; consumer misses: "点 Go" named in a relay, "App ID" in a question). Not yet good enough on a cheap model: open in `todo.md` | U (v0.0.1 test), L | `design/quiet-conversation.md`, `design/conversation.md`, `design/ui.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `packages/partner` (persona, `relayTarget`), `packages/protocol` (`ai_msg.decisionId`), `packages/core/src/push.ts`, `apps/desktop`, `apps/web` |

## 2026-11-09: Native browser and computer use by default (research/38 follow-up)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D130 | **Native browser and computer use are the default; cua-driver is the fallback (supersedes D126's "the default stays cua-driver").** Decided by the author (2026-11-09): "这才能最大利用 codex 优势". (1) **Codex tasks keep Codex's own Computer Use and browser use**, in every place. Ducter's clean setup for a task keeps plugins on as a feature, turns off by id every plugin the user enabled except `browser`, `chrome`, `unified-computer-use` and `computer-use` (all `@openai-bundled`), keeps the desktop app's `node_repl` server, and leaves `computer_use` / `browser_use` on; the partner's clean setup is unchanged (`features.plugins=false`). The plugin id goes in bare: `-c plugins.<id>.enabled=false` (verified against `mcpServerStatus/list`, codex-cli 0.167.0; the quoted form research/38 tried is not matched). A browser place on Codex uses that clean setup: only `cua_repl` and `node_repl` carry tools (~400 `codex_apps` tools and the user's own cua-driver left out). Codex's DOM-level browser tools (in-app browser, Chrome via the ChatGPT extension) are used when the desktop app's browser access host reaches Ducter (its three `BROWSER_USE_ACCESS_*` env vars, passed through to Codex); Ducter never reads them from another process. Otherwise Codex drives Chrome as an app through Computer Use. (2) **Claude Code tasks prefer Claude in Chrome** (`--chrome`), checked at task open: a live local bridge socket (`/tmp/claude-mcp-browser-bridge-<user>/<pid>.sock`), else one read-only `list_connected_browsers` call to `claude --claude-in-chrome-mcp` (no model call, ~2 s, reused 30 s). Not connected: cua-driver. A task that still meets "Browser extension is not connected" moves to cua-driver in place (same session resumed, a system line says so). (3) **cua-driver is the fallback** (pi, DeepSeek Harness, a harness whose own tools aren't ready); `DUCTER_BROWSER_DRIVER=cua` forces it. `DEFAULT_BROWSER_DRIVER = native`. (4) **Safety on the user's own Chrome**, after Codex Computer Use typed into the author's open Hetzner 2FA tab (research/38 B2): every native browser and computer brief says open a new window (Codex Computer Use: `open -na "Google Chrome" --args --new-window <url>`, never ⌘T in the user's window) or a tab of its own (Claude in Chrome, Codex browser tools), check the focused window/tab by URL or title before each batch of keys, never type into a page it didn't open, close what it opened; D62's Go and untrusted-page rules stay. In code: Claude in Chrome and Codex's browser tools act by tab id, so (a)-(c) hold by construction; for Codex Computer Use, which acts on the front window, a tripwire watches every `cua_repl` call and steers the task to stop the first time it sends keys before opening its own window (a system line says so). It cannot block a call before it runs. (5a) **Every session start rebuilds the browser** (recovery after a restart, reopen, resume, a switch of who does it), so a browser task never resumes without its brief, sidecar or clean setup; per harness a browser task leaves the user's own setup out (Claude Code: built-ins, denials, exclude; Codex: the clean setup keeping its own tools, on either driver; pi: clean flags; DeepSeek Harness: unchanged, its clean home is the partner's). (5) **Abilities**: Browse the web is ready without cua-driver when Codex's tools are on or Claude in Chrome is connected, and says where: 在你的 Chrome 里 / In your Chrome (`DeviceAbility.where`). Not live-tested: Computer Use and anything in the author's Chrome (rule); Codex's browser tools through the access host (Ducter does not run under the desktop app here) | U (1)-(4), L (the probe, the tripwire, the plugin list), research/38 | `packages/protocol/src/native-browser.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{browser,oar,runner,child-env}.ts`, `packages/core/src/devices.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,views,i18n}`, `design/system.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md`, `docs/faq.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `design/todo.md`, `research/38` |

The UI is 100% the phone client, so the two never drift. Release: TestFlight through CI (`design/release/ios.md`), internal testers only; the public docs keep "coming soon" until it is public. (2) What went out is still recorded where Ducter sees it: the task's report, its transcript's outside-looking commands, the ↗ line and **发出去的 / Sent out** in Details. (3) **Merge into main** and **Open draft PR** stay as the user's buttons, and merging into the user's base branch stays the user's (D26's "never merge without a rule" is unchanged). (4) The gate stays the design for effects that reach people with no way back: messages, posts, forms, payments, calls (G-safety). Supersedes, for a task's own pushes and PRs, G-safety Rule 3's push and draft-PR rows (D26's "personal auto, org ask once" as a gate, D52 Q6's 30-minute countdown on personal repos and Go on org repos) and system.md's "pushes, PRs" in Gate the effects | U | `design/system.md` Safety, `design/flows/G-safety.md`, `design/product-model.md` §3, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md` |

## 2026-11-09: The persona's Mem identity, closed (the author: "Mem identity 这个你看看把它闭环了吧")

DRAFT, for the author's review.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D141 | **A persona is bound to one Mem AI Identity once, and Ducter names it on every Mem call; nothing depends on the ambient `NMEM_AGENT_ID`.** (1) **Binding**, the first start where Mem answers with memory on (`packages/core/src/mem-identity.ts`): if Ducter itself runs with `NMEM_AGENT_ID` set, the user already chose; that identity is adopted with their `NMEM_SPACE`, and Ducter never edits it. Else the persona's own id, `ducter` (another persona `ducter-<id>`): adopted if it already exists (another Ducter of the same user; owned when its source app is `ducter`), else enrolled (`nmem agents enroll ducter --name <partner's name> --source-app ducter`, no default Space: Mem's default write Space stays the user's call). Mem records the id on every write whether a profile exists or not (verified live), so a key that may not make profiles (a Cloud team member's, 403) still binds the id, without a profile. Mem down: nothing bound, tried at the next start. Kept in `persona.json` as `mem {agentId, space?, owned, label?}` beside the name; the Log's persona id stays `default`. (2) **Named explicitly:** `memory_add` passes `--agent-id` (and `--space` only when the binding has one), `memory_search` `--space` likewise, the L1 bundle `nmem context --no-ensure --agent-id`. (3) **Tasks** get `NMEM_AGENT_ID` (and `NMEM_SPACE` when bound) in the session spec, explicitly; every other NMEM_* the host holds stays out (D102), so a skill's `nmem` writes as the persona. (4) **Rename keeps the identity** (Mem's identity is the stable binding, the name is only its profile, so memories and provenance never split): for one Ducter enrolled, the display name follows (`nmem agents set <id> --name`); an adopted one is never touched. (5) **Settings › 记得什么 / What it remembers** says, when connected and bound, **你说过的事，它记在 Nowledge Mem 的「小鸭」名下。/ What you've told it is kept in Nowledge Mem under "小鸭".** (`MemView.keptUnder`: the partner's name for its own identity, the identity's own name for an adopted one). (6) **The D63 live check, run** (`packages/partner/eval/mem-live.ts --real`, 2026-11-09, Cloud, 7/7): one `memory_add` through the real partner tool and nmem client as the scratch identity `ducter-scratch`; read back, `metadata.agent_id = ducter-scratch`, `metadata.source_app = ducter`, `source = ducter`, `metadata.source_message_id = ducter;persona=…;log=1`; then deleted. This key may create neither Spaces nor Agent profiles (403), so the write went to the user's private Personal zone instead of a `ducter-scratch` Space, and no profile was made; the rename path is covered by tests only. `nmem m show` (0.10.116) leaves metadata out; the record was read from the same REST endpoint. (7) **Bridge** keeps its approved named Agent Context (`--agent-context`), which carries its own identity; making that the persona's identity is open (`todo.md`) | U ("闭环"), L | `design/system.md`, `design/memory.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/todo.md`, `packages/core/src/{mem-identity,main,persona,settings}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{memory,partner}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/runner.ts`, `packages/protocol` (`MemIdentity`, `MemView.keptUnder`), `apps/desktop`, `apps/web`, `docs/settings.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md` |

## 2026-11-10: A folder that isn't a repo works in place and is undone with one tap (the author: "第一性原理建议就好，go")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D142 | **A task in a non-git folder (Documents, Downloads, a folder of invoices) works in the folder itself; Ducter keeps a copy from before, says what changed in plain words, and puts it all back with one tap.** Supersedes D27's draft copy, its apply Go and "trusted folders". (1) **The copy:** before a task's first turn (and again after the user kept or undid), the runner copies the folder to `<dataDir>/snapshots/<taskId>/copy` on the machine that holds it: an APFS clone on macOS (`cp -c -R -p`, verified on this Mac's data volume) or `cp -a --reflink=always` on btrfs/xfs, near-zero space and time; a plain copy only up to 256 MB (`DUCTER_SNAPSHOT_COPY_MAX_MB`) and on a different volume or file system; nothing past 100k entries. (2) **Ask first instead** (the runner holds the turn and logs a needs_input report, Hold by default, not reversible) when the folder can't be copied cheaply: cloud-synced (iCloud Drive and its Desktop & Documents xattr, `~/Library/CloudStorage` for Dropbox / Google Drive / OneDrive / Box, the old home-folder sync folders, a `.dropbox` marker), on a network or external drive, too big, or holding Ducter's own data. Go or a reply sends the held turn and it doesn't ask again for that task. (3) **Changes in plain words:** counted against the copy (added, changed, removed; a removed and an added file with the same bytes are one move, a rename when the folder is the same), cached on the card (`TaskCard.folder`) and carried on done and failed reports (`report.folder`), so the partner's result says it can be undone and until when. The opened task's Changes is one sentence (**把 42 个文件整理进 6 个新文件夹，改了 3 个文件名 / Moved 42 files into 6 new folders, renamed 3**), a quiet **随时能改回去，留到 11 月 16 日 / You can undo this until Nov 16**, then **改回去 / Undo all changes** and **就这样 / Keep the changes**; the list on request (**看看改了哪些 / See what changed**) with **改回这个 / Undo this** per file. No Merge for a folder. The mechanism is never named. (4) **Undo** makes the folder exactly what the copy holds (removes what the task added, puts back the rest with clones, times and modes) and checks it: no entry differs, and every file put back has the copy's hash; then the copy is freed. A folder changed after the task finished asks first and says what would be lost (**它做完之后，又有 1 个文件被改过（…）**, **还是改回去 / Undo anyway**). Not while the task works there. (5) **Kept 7 days** after the task last finished (done, failed or dropped), swept hourly; **就这样** frees it at once. (6) **What is enforced, what is detected:** Codex folder tasks run in its workspace-write sandbox (Ducter's `-c sandbox_mode="workspace-write"` after OAR's own wins, checked with `codex app-server` config/read; `codex sandbox` refused a write in the home folder; network on). Claude Code under bypass has no switch that keeps writes in a folder (its own sandbox wasn't verified here), so for it staying inside the folder and asking before deleting more than 10 files at once are asked in the brief, not enforced. Inside the folder everything is undoable either way, deletions included; writes outside it are not covered by the copy. (7) Protocol: `FolderSummary`/`FolderChanges`/`UndoConfirm`; `task_action` `undo` (`path`, `force`) and `keep`, answered with `action_result` (`confirm`); `folder_changes` both ways; `SessionSpec.confineWrites`; worker ops `folderPrepare/Finish/Changes/Undo/Keep` (an older worker can't copy, so its tasks ask first). CLI `ducter undo <task> [--yes]`. Git repos keep the worktree and Merge (D26) | U (2026-11-09), L | `packages/runner/src/folder.ts`, `runner.ts`, `brief.ts`, `oar.ts`, `remote.ts`, `worker-host.ts`, `tools.ts`, `packages/core/src/{changes,ws,main}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/render.ts`, `packages/protocol`, `packages/cli`, `apps/desktop`, `apps/web`, `design/product-model.md` §3, `design/flows/EC-steering-places.md` C2, `design/focus-window.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md` |
| D143 | **A server reaches the world through `ducter domain <name>`, and every printed command works in the shell it was printed in.** (1) `ducter domain` replaces copying `scripts/caddy-setup.sh` by hand: Ducter must answer locally; the name's A/AAAA records must include this server's public IP (api.ipify.org; else it prints the exact record and stops; `--no-dns-check` skips); nothing but Caddy may hold 80/443 (else `--print` for the proxy the user keeps); Caddy is installed (apt as root without sudo, sudo otherwise; Homebrew on a Mac); one marked block (`# ducter: begin` … `# ducter: end`) goes into the Caddyfile (the Debian demo site moved aside); then HTTPS is waited for (60 s), `public-url` written, and a pair code shown. Idempotent; `--remove` takes only Ducter's block out; `--json` for agents (steps, error, next). (2) `ducter pair` on a server with no address leads with the domain path and the public IP, then the SSH tunnel for a Mac alone; it no longer waits there, and `--json` carries `reachable`, `publicIp`, `tunnel`, `steps`. No user-facing text names `caddy-setup.sh`. (3) Installers: as root (or with a writable `/usr/local/bin`) link `/usr/local/bin/ducter`; otherwise one marked PATH block in the shell's profile plus the export line for this shell; commands print as `ducter` only when PATH finds this binary, else its path. (4) `uninstall --all` also removes that link, that block, the binary `install.sh` copied, and Ducter's Caddy site (Caddy stays). (5) `skills/` ships Agent Skills (ducter-deploy, -operate, -troubleshoot), served at `ducter.ai/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` | U (2026-11-10), L | `packages/cli/src/{domain,remote,uninstall,main}.ts`, `scripts/install.sh`, `apps/site/public/install.sh`, `scripts/caddy-setup.sh`, `skills/`, `docs/install.md`, `docs/for-agents.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `design/build-and-run.md` |

## 2026-11-10: A wait that ends unmet leaves a trace (the author's v0.0.3 test: a CI wait on PR #9950 vanished from On hand when a check failed)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D145 | **A wait that ends without its condition stays on On hand for the day, says why in plain words, and can be picked up again with one tap.** (1) **Ending unmet:** a check may carry `failWhen` (what shows it can no longer come true: a check FAILURE) and the wait its `unmetSays` in the user's words; when it holds the wait ends `cancelled` with `unmet`, instead of looking on in vain. The partner may also end one it sees can't happen with `wait_cancel {unmet}`. A deadline passing (`expired`) counts too. A plain 不等了 / Stop waiting, a finished task or an answered question leave no trace. (2) **On hand:** a task-less wait that ended unmet today sits in the finished area (今天做完 / Done today) as its reason plus **没等到：{why} / Didn't happen: {why}** (expired: 到时间了还没等到 / ran out of time) and a quiet **接着等 / Keep waiting** that re-arms the same wait as it was (a deadline already past is dropped). Gone the next day. A task's own wait that can't happen wakes the task with why instead. (3) **The partner** hears `[wait can't happen]` only when Ducter ended it, and says it in one short sentence: what happened and the one next step the user can take ("有一项检查挂了。推个新提交跑起 CI，我接着盯。"), no internals | L (the author's report) | `packages/protocol` (`CheckSpec.failWhen`, `waiter.unmet/unmetSays`, `OnHand.missed`, `waiter_action rearm`), `packages/core/src/{waiters,work,core}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{persona,render}.ts`, `apps/web`, `apps/desktop`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/waiting-and-routines.md` |

## 2026-11-10: The command line follows the Ducter you use (the lead: once the Mac app connected to hel.siwei.info, `ducter status` on the Mac still showed the stale local Ducter)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D147 | **`ducter` on a computer talks to the same Ducter the user uses there; only commands about the machine itself stay local.** (1) **Two kinds of command:** machine commands (`up`, `service`, `uninstall`, `worker`, `doctor`, `domain`, `report`, `update` without `--all`) always act on this computer; home commands (`status`, `say`, `task`, `tail`, `answer`, `merge`, `undo`, `open`, `pair`, `devices`, `update --all`) go to the Ducter the command line follows. (2) **What it follows:** `<dataDir>/home.json` (0600: `{address, token, name, since, deviceId?, by}`), else this computer's Ducter. The Mac app writes it when it connects elsewhere: over its own authenticated socket it asks the home for a second one-time code (the existing `pair_code` frame; no new endpoint) and redeems it as a separate device **CLI on <Mac>**, so the command line is revocable on its own and never holds the app's token. Switching the app back to this Mac removes the file. Without the app, `ducter connect <address> --code <code>` writes it and `ducter disconnect` removes it; at launch the app leaves a file the user wrote that way alone, and re-pairs only a CLI token that stopped working. If the app can't pair the CLI (an older home), the file still names the address without a token, so commands say the exact `ducter connect` line instead of quietly showing this Mac. (3) **Always visible:** every home command prints `→ hel.siwei.info` / `→ this computer` first and carries `home` in `--json`; `--local` (or an explicit `--port`) forces this computer, `--home <address>` goes elsewhere once (a token is needed: else the connect line). (4) **Transport:** the client WebSocket over `wss://…/ws` with `Authorization: Bearer dvc.…`, as the phone and the app. Failures say what to do: unreachable → check the server; the gate's 401 → it was disconnected there, connect again with a new code. `devices disconnect` stays on the home's own machine (no remote revoke frame yet). (5) **A task from the CLI** while following a server has no folder unless `--repo` names one (then on this Mac as its device, which works when this Mac does tasks for it). (6) **doctor** shows what the CLI follows and whether this `ducter` is the app's helper (updates with the app) or a copy / a checkout that won't | L (the lead's report), U (2026-11-10) | `packages/cli/src/{home,homecmds,main,args,client,status,talk,remote,doctor,i18n}.ts`, `apps/desktop/{main.go,internal/remote/home.go}`, `design/build-and-run.md`, `docs/install.md`, `skills/ducter-operate`, `design/product-model.md` |
## 2026-11-10: Every device updates in place, and its card says so (the author: "in-place update is a basic capability"; "每一个卡片也应该优雅设计")

DRAFT, for the author's review.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D148 | **Ducter's home and every other computer update in place from the official release, one tap or `ducter update`, with a check before and a rollback after; each device card says its version, renames in place and disconnects with a confirm.** (1) **Knowing.** Every device reports its version: the home in `/health` (`version` beside `build`, local callers only) and in its devices frame, a worker in its hello. The home reads `api.github.com/repos/wey-gu/ducter/releases/latest` (else the `releases/latest` redirect, which gives the version but no notes), at most every 6 h, cached in `<dataDir>/updates/latest.json`; offline or refused stays silent and keeps the last answer; `DUCTER_UPDATE_CHECK=off` never asks. Drafts and pre-releases are never offered. Each device in the devices frame carries `update {current, latest, available, notes, security, how, phase …}`; `notes` is the release notes' first two plain lines. (2) **How it updates (`how`).** `self`: a compiled Linux `ducter` in a folder it can write (the server bundle) updates itself. `app`: a Mac whose `ducter` lives inside Ducter.app updates through the app's own updater (D72; that bundle swap is the app's, not this decision's): on that Mac the button calls the app's update, elsewhere the card says to update from Ducter on that Mac. `source`: run from a checkout, updated with git. Nothing else. (3) **The staged update** (`packages/core/src/update.ts`, one code path for the home, a worker and `ducter update`): refuse a downgrade unless `--to <version>` names it; refuse when the staging disk has less than 3× the download free; download `ducter-<v>-linux-<arch>.tar.gz` and `SHA256SUMS.txt` from `github.com/wey-gu/ducter/releases/download/v<v>/` over HTTPS only (redirects only to GitHub's own hosts); the SHA-256 must match the listed sum or nothing is changed; unpack into `<dataDir>/updates/<v>/`; self-check the new binary (`--version` must name `<v>`, `doctor --json` in a scratch folder must print its checks); keep the running binary as `ducter.prev` beside it; move the new one in by rename (same folder, atomic). (4) **Safe point.** Before a restart it waits until no merge, undo, keep or archive is running (the runner's critical section; new ones are refused with a plain line meanwhile) and gives tasks mid-turn up to 2 min to end their turn; a task still mid-turn then is resumed by the restart's recovery, as after any restart. (5) **Restart and rollback.** The restart runs from a helper outside the service (`systemd-run --user` with the previous binary, `ducter update --finish`), so it survives `systemctl --user restart ducter`. It waits up to 60 s for the new process to be healthy (the home: `/health` names the new version; a worker: it reached its main and wrote `updates/alive`). Not healthy: it moves `ducter.prev` back, restarts again, and records the failure with its reason. Without systemd it swaps and says to restart. (6) **Seen.** Every step goes to the Log (`device_update` on the `devices` stream: downloading, checking, waiting, restarting, done or failed with reason and `rolledBack`), and the outcome file is read after the restart, so a client that reconnects sees **回来了，现在是 {v} / Back, now on {v}**. (7) **Workers.** The home sends `update` down the worker's socket; the worker runs the same staged update for its own binary and service, reports each phase up the same socket, comes back and says its new version in its hello. A Mac worker inside Ducter.app answers `app`. (8) **CLI.** `ducter update` (this machine), `--all` (workers first, then the home), `--check` (only report), `--json`, `--yes`, `--to <v>`. Agents run `ducter update --check --json`, then `ducter update --yes`. (9) **UI.** No nagging: a device that's behind gets one quiet dot by its name and a line in its card, **有新版本 {v} / Update to {v}** with the first lines of what's new, and one button **更新 / Update**. Progress in words: **在下载… / Downloading… · 在核对… / Checking… · 等手头的事停好… / Waiting for work to settle… · 在重启… / Restarting… · 回来了，现在是 {v} / Back, now on {v}**; failed: **没更新成，已经换回 {old} / Didn't update; back on {old}** with the reason. The partner says one line only when asked, or for a release whose notes name a security fix. Settings › About lists the home's and each computer's version. (10) **At night** (setting `autoUpdate`, off by default): inside quiet hours, when no task is working and nothing is critical, the home updates itself, then each worker. (11) **Device cards** (desktop Devices, phone Settings; *the card's shape amended by D161*): one header (the name, renamed in place by clicking it: Enter saves, Esc cancels, through `rename_device`), what it is in plain words (**云上的电脑 · {name} 住这儿 / A computer in the cloud · {name} lives here**), a short state line, its AI accounts as coins (accounts not installed fold into **+ 再登录一个 AI / + Sign in another AI** unless none is signed in), abilities, the version line with the dot, and the actions at the bottom: **断开 / Disconnect** with a confirm (`disconnect_device`, the same revoke as `ducter devices disconnect`), never a terminal line in the UI. A phone or another window gets the same card: rename, last seen, disconnect | U ("in-place update is a basic capability", "每一个卡片也应该优雅设计"), L | `design/system.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/install.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `skills/ducter-deploy`, `skills/ducter-operate`, `packages/core/src/{update,release}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/update.ts`, `apps/desktop`, `apps/web` |

## 2026-11-10: The first screen greets you by name and starts from your life (the author judged the empty conversation not up to standard)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D149 | **The empty conversation is the partner saying hello in its own name, by the time of day, with things to say that come from this person's life; worked out on the home, never from a path.** (1) **Greeting:** the client's own clock and the partner's name: **早上好呀，我是小鸭。 / Good morning, I'm 小鸭.** the first time, **早上好呀，小鸭在呢。 / Good morning. 小鸭 is here.** after (中午 / 下午 / 晚上, and 这么晚还没睡呀 late at night). Under it one line of what it can do for this person, in everyday words, then its promise on its own line (**邮件、日程、电脑上的文件，都可以交给我。需要你的时候，我再来问你。**); someone new gets **查点东西、记件事、到点提醒你，说一句就行。** The brain is never named here. (2) **Starters, 3–4, life first,** in the person's own voice, worked out on the home (`core/src/first-screen.ts`, a `first_screen` frame after each app's hello, both languages in it) from names and metadata it already has: connected accounts (mail, calendar, other apps by title), a 记下了 / Noted line from the last three weeks (**接着弄上次的「…」**), a task still going (**「…」做到哪了？**), a folder used before, the paired computer when the home is a server. Code only for someone who clearly works on code (two tasks in repos): one starter, by project name, last. One place always goes to an everyday example; someone new gets four (an email, a trip, a folder, a reminder; a plan for the week when there is no computer to tidy). Choice and wording rotate by the person's day. Nothing says how they were made; no path, no repo count. (3) **Returning to an empty conversation:** one timely line in the partner's bubble instead of the "can do" line, only when something real happened (something waits for them, a task-less wait that didn't come true today, a result from the last day), with **去看看 / Take a look**; otherwise just the greeting. (4) **The partner is alive:** it breathes and blinks (D77), waves once when the screen appears (two rocks from its feet and a small hop, 1.6 s), and leans toward the composer while there are words in it; still under reduced motion. (5) **No second way to Devices:** the header already has 设备 / Devices, so the plain-text 看看你的设备 link is gone rather than turned into a card. (6) `firstrun.json`'s repo scan no longer feeds the starters (on a server home it named the wrong machine's repos); New task still lists those repos | U (2026-11-10) | `packages/core/src/{first-screen,ws}.ts`, `packages/protocol` (`first_screen`, `FirstScreen`, `Words`, `StarterKind`), `apps/desktop/internal/views/{welcome,conversation}.go`, `apps/web/src/{views,store,i18n}.ts*`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `design/onboarding.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/build-and-run.md`, `design/ui.md`, `design/scenarios.md`; shots in `.shots/first-screen/` |

The phone shows the same sections as a list of rows under a partner card, each a pushed page with ‹ 设置 / Settings (`#settings=<section>` deep-links), its fourth section **这台手机 / This phone** (language, Details); this phone's push sits on its Notifications page. (2) **One vocabulary:** a group title on the paper, a white card on the clay base, rows of label + one-line helper with the control at the right end (switch, segmented, small secondary button, › or ↗), primary actions as clay buttons at the right end of a card's last line, quiet text buttons for cancel and the rest, Remove Ducter a quiet text button after the last group; one type scale (tab 28, group 20, label 15/700, helper 13, caption 12). (3) **Content:** D71's 关窗以后 / When you close the window becomes **在哪儿运行 / Where it runs** (this Mac or the connected host with its clay, Stop, connect elsewhere and back as link rows, data and logs as rows, Your devices and AI accounts); updates are one block (`updateSection`, a slot the update work owns); About is a clay hero with the version, links as rows, **素材与致谢 / Credits** folded; 声音 / Sound moves to Look and sound with **听一下 / Play** (the intro's sound, 5 s); Look and sound opens on a live preview (the partner, a message and its answer in this window's language, Details as set) and the partner smiles for a moment after a choice. (4) **Partner, truthfully:** one calm header (the partner, its name with Rename, "住在 {home} 上 · 用 {agent} 想事情"); the faces strip is dropped; single column. What it thinks with shows the brain core actually runs: the settings frame carries `brainNow` {harness, model?, auto, why?: "only_codex", models}, computed by one core helper (`core/src/brain.ts`) that the partner's runner uses too, so no client works it out again ("自动选的：只有 Codex 登录了"). Its models come from the home for that agent (what the account lists, else Ducter's tier table, kept to names the harness facts know; none: its own default), never a client's own list, so a Codex home never offers Opus.
