## 2026-11-10: A server browses the web, and you sign in yourself (the author's v0.0.4 goal: "server-hosted Ducter (Linux, Codex-only) can browse the web for tasks, with an elegant Muse-like credential design")

DRAFT, for the author's review.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D151 | **On a server, 上网 / Browse the web is Ducter's own browser, and the user signs in to sites themselves in a live view while the agent is blind.** (1) **The browser:** Chrome for Testing (Stable, `chrome`, linux64 and linux-arm64), started `--headless=new` by Ducter's main process with one persistent profile per persona at `<dataDir>/browser-profiles/<persona>` (the path cua-driver uses on a Mac), DevTools on 127.0.0.1 only, `--password-store=basic`, the plain Chrome user agent without "Headless", `--no-sandbox` only as root. Quit cleanly (`Browser.close`, sign-ins saved) when no task and no live view uses it, after 5 idle minutes; a stale Ducter Chrome holding the profile is quit before a start. (2) **Adding it is one tap** in Devices (or `ducter ability add browser`): Ducter downloads the build into `<dataDir>/browser`; as root it installs the missing libraries from the zip's own `deb.deps` (t64 names on Ubuntu 24.04) plus CJK fonts; as a normal user Devices says 还差一步 / One step left with the one `sudo apt-get install …` line and 再试一次 / Try again. Never 这台还装不了 on a server. (3) **Tools:** a browser place on Linux (any harness; `DUCTER_BROWSER_DRIVER=ducter` anywhere) gets Ducter's own `browser_*` tools on Ducter's MCP, listed only for that task: open, read (text with numbered refs), click, type, press, scroll, back, tabs, screenshot, ask sign-in. Chosen over Playwright MCP and chrome-devtools-mcp (both Apache-2.0, headless) because three rules must hold in code: blind during sign-in, no password values in reads and no typing into password or one-time-code fields, http(s) only (research/39 §2). D130's native-first stays on a Mac. (4) **Signing in:** `browser_ask_sign_in` opens the sign-in page in the task's tab and makes a card, 需要你登录一下 {site} / Sign in to {site}, with 去登录 / Sign in and 先不用 / Not now. Sign in opens a **live view** in the Mac app or the phone: a CDP screencast (JPEG, up to 2×, at the viewer's size) over the client's own authenticated WebSocket (so through `ducter domain`'s HTTPS), one frame in flight until the client acks it, at most 8 a second; clicks, drags-as-scroll, keys and IME text go back in. The top line says Ducter 的浏览器 · 它现在看不见 / Ducter's browser · it can't see this now. **The agent is blind** from before the first frame until the view closes: every browser tool on that profile refuses before touching the page. Nothing of a live view is logged or reaches a model. 好了 / Done answers the card with Go and the task is told the user signed in themselves; Not now leaves the card open. (5) **Signed-in sites:** Devices › 上网 lists 已登录的网站 / Signed-in sites (the sites signed into through a live view, with how many cookies remain, never their values), 退出 / Sign out per site (its cookies and stored data) and 全部清掉 / Clear all, asked once; `ducter browser sites | signout <site> | clear`. (6) **Kept:** D62's Go for submitting, posting, booking and paying; page text is data; downloads land in the task's own folder. Not built (research/25 §3): a vault that fills passwords, TOTP, per-site rate limits, session probes | U (the goal), L (the rest), research/39 | `packages/core/src/browser/*`, `packages/runner/src/browser.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{index,internal,browser-profile}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/browser-cmd.ts`, `apps/web/src/{app,views}.tsx`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{liveview,sites}.go`, `design/system.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md`, `skills/ducter-{deploy,operate}`, `design/todo.md` |

## 2026-11-10: The opened task reads as the agent tells it, and keys never render (the author's Codex run "Connect Nowledge Mem for Codex" on a Linux server home: "太丑了", a connect token in plain view)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D152 | **The opened task is the agent's voice with its steps folded under it, one quiet meta line on top, and no key ever renders.** (1) **Keys never render.** A credential in a brief, a command or its output (`nmem_ct_…`, `sk-…`, `ghp_…`, a bearer header, a JWT, a password in a URL, a long value after a field named token / key / secret / password, a PEM block) is hidden by the home in every `events`, `task_history`, On hand, New task result, action result and push it sends (`packages/core/src/ws.ts`, `push.ts`): a short head stays (`nmem_ct_`), then `••••••`, and the client says **隐藏了一段密钥 / A key is hidden** under the text. The Log keeps the key (the task was given it to use it; the Log stays on the home); clients hide again at render (`packages/protocol/src/redact.ts`, its Go port `apps/desktop/internal/redact`, one fixture). No tap-to-reveal: the user who pasted it has it. (2) **One meta line**: the state, **one clock** (since it started, ticking until it finishes, then **用了 {duration} / took {duration}**; never a wall-clock time beside a step), then the agent's coin, the agent and model, where it runs when there is a place (repo · branch, a folder, **在 {device} 上 / on {device}** when it isn't this machine; no place says nothing), and **换谁来做 / Switch who does it** as a small action. The bar over the composer and the **细节 / Details** table go: who and where are the meta line, saved progress is in the transcript once, usage and the compaction count are not shown (D121's word count of cumulative tokens, cache reads included, read as noise); what went out stays as **发出去的 / Sent out** under the transcript when anything did; a wait's **怎么判断的 / How it checks** stays when there is a wait. Header actions per state: **先停 / Pause** while it works, **放下 / Drop** until it finishes. (3) **你交代的事 / What you asked** (or **{name} 交代的事 / What {name} asked**): the brief as a quiet sunken card, three lines until **展开 / Show all**. (4) **The agent's voice is the readable layer**: its messages and its saved progress (a checkpoint said once; when it repeats the message just before, that message gets the small **保存了进度 / Progress saved** mark) in body ink at the reading measure. (5) **Steps fold per stretch**: the steps between two things the agent said are one gist line, **查看了一圈 / Looked around** (only reading, searching, probing, requesting) · **改了 n 个文件 / Edited n files** · **在 {app} 里操作 / Worked in {app}** · **跑了几条命令 / Ran commands** · **想了想 / Thought it through**, then **· n 步 / n steps** and **· n 处出错 / n failed**; folded when settled, open while it is the stretch under way, whose gist is its latest step with a spinner. A lone step is its own line. A real failure shows even folded. (6) **A step is one sentence**: the agent's own words for it when its harness carries them (Claude Code's Bash `description`, Codex's `justification`), else the summarizer: `command -v nmem` → **查找 nmem 命令 / Looked for nmem**, `curl https://cloud.nowledge.co/…` → **请求 cloud.nowledge.co / Requested cloud.nowledge.co**, `curl … \| sh` → **运行 {host} 的安装脚本**, `pipx install x` → **安装 x / Installed x**, `python3 -c` → **运行一段 python3**, `mkdir` → **新建文件夹 x**; else the command's name and subcommand in mono after **运行 / Ran**. (7) **A probe that finds nothing is not an error**: `command -v`, `which`, `type`, `test` / `[`, `ls`, `grep` / `rg` exiting non-zero say **没找到 nmem / No nmem here** · **还没有 x / No x yet** · **没搜到 x / No match for x** in muted ink; only a real failure is the red **出错 / Error** line. (8) **Waiting on you with nothing to answer** (its turn ended on a question in its own words, or a wait ran out) reads **说完了，等你回话 / Over to you** with **它停在这儿了。回它一句，它就接着做。 / It stopped here. Reply and it carries on.**, never a bare 等你 with no ask. (9) **The headline is what the task is** (its short title, in the words it was given), or where a browser or a wait stands; never the agent's latest sentence again, which is the transcript's. (10) **Known commands say what they do**: `nmem connect` → **连上 Mem / Connected Mem**, `nmem doctor` → **检查 Mem 连接 / Checked the Mem connection**, `gh pr create/checks/merge` → **开 PR / 看 PR 的状态 / 合并 PR**; a stretch of commands is named by what it got done, in at most two of its steps' words (**安装 nowledge-mem-cli，连上 Mem**), else **跑了几条命令**. (11) **A pending ask sits over the composer**, where the answer is typed; an answered one is one line in the head. While it works, a reader at the end of the page follows the newest step; one who scrolled up stays. The agent's words stay in its own language (no silent translation; a translate action is in `todo.md`). The model is one table for both surfaces (`packages/protocol/src/transcript.ts`, its Go port, `fixtures/transcript.json`). Reference: egoist/lorca's handling (research/40); its look is not taken. Runner-up for (1): redact at render only, rejected because the frames, the phone's cache and pushes would still carry the key | U (the author's screenshots, v0.0.3), L, R40 | `design/focus-window.md`, `design/ui.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md`, `packages/protocol` (redact, steps, transcript), `packages/core` (ws, push), `apps/desktop`, `apps/web` |

## 2026-11-10: Ducter's browser says which it is, and a task's English is never pasted for a Chinese user (the author's v0.0.4 use on "hel", a Codex-only Linux home: "还不能确认连上的就是你刚安装的那个浏览器"; the partner relaying "Installed standalone CLI 0.10.116…" verbatim)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D154 | **Ducter's own browser names itself everywhere and can be seen working without a task; and what a user reads of a task's result is in their language.** (1) **Which browser:** one description, `{device, name, version, profile}` (the machine as Devices names it; **Chrome for Testing** for Ducter's install, **Chrome** for `DUCTER_CHROME`, **system Chrome** for one on PATH; the version CDP answered at start). `browser_open`'s result starts with it ("Browser: Ducter's own browser on hel (Chrome for Testing 157.0.8103.38, profile "ducter"), headless on that machine; not the user's own Chrome"), so a task and then the partner can say plainly what they used. (2) **The check, no task:** start the persona's profile, open `https://example.com/` in a tab of its own, read its title, close the tab, quit the browser if the check started it and nothing else holds it; which browser and how long. Three doors: `ducter browser check [--json]`; the partner's `browser_check` tool (on a server, or with `DUCTER_BROWSER_DRIVER=ducter`), which persona rule 16 prefers to opening a task when the user asks to verify browsing, said in one plain sentence ("能用：hel 上 Ducter 自己的浏览器刚打开了一个测试网页，用了 2 秒。"); and **试一下 / Try it** on the 上网 block of the device card (Mac and phone): the sign-in live view's own path (`live_open {try: true}`, decisionId `"try"`, no card), on example.com in a tab of its own, its top line **Ducter 自己的浏览器 · 在 {device} 上 · Chrome for Testing 157**, a note **这是 {device} 上的浏览器看到的网页，不是你的 Chrome。点点看、滚一滚都行。**, one button **好了 / Done**; blind while open like any live view; closing it answers nothing and remembers no site. (3) **Results in the user's language:** a report turn names the user's language for Chinese users too (before, only English was named), and says the report is the task's own words, never to paste. A result message with no Han for a Chinese user (or Han for an English one), in a turn woken only by reports, is held back and asked for once more in the right language, as the same answer (its `replyTo`); the second try stands, never a loop. (4) **What clients say on their own** (`packages/protocol/src/say.ts`, its Go port `apps/desktop/internal/say`, one fixture): the living message's fallback line keeps the task's sentence only when it is in the reader's language, else **{title} 做完了。 / {title} is done.** · **{title} 没做成，细节里有它的原话。**; an evidence label shows as is when it is in the reader's language or reads as a name (one token, a lower-case command, a path, `#`, a version), else neutral: the page's host, the file's name, or **测试结果 / 运行记录 / 改动 …**; an English "2 files changed" is counted in the reader's words. (5) **The opened task keeps the agent's own words** (D152). Runner-up for (3): translate every report with a model at report time, rejected (a model call per report for words the partner already writes, and the opened task would no longer be the agent's) | U (the author's use, v0.0.4), L | `packages/core/src/browser/{check,host,live,tools,index}.ts`, `packages/core/src/ws.ts`, `packages/protocol` (index, say), `packages/cli/src/browser-cmd.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,persona}.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/scenarios.ts` (en-report-zh), `apps/desktop/internal/{say,views,store}`, `apps/web`, `design/system.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `skills/ducter-{deploy,operate}` |

## 2026-11-10: One computer, one card, and a Mac that does tasks for a server

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D155 | **One computer, one card; every device card in D148's language; and one tap makes a Mac do tasks for a home elsewhere.** (1) **The command line belongs to its computer** (*how the card shows it amended by D161 (5)*). The CLI token the app pairs (D147) or `ducter connect` makes stays its own revocable device; only the presentation folds. `/pair` takes `form` (`"cli"` \| `"app"`) and `computer` (the host it runs on), kept in `devices.db` and sent as `reach.clients[].form/computer` and `devices[].computer`; a CLI paired before this is known by its name `CLI on <host>`. In the app (and the phone's Settings), a CLI folds into the worker or the app window on its computer as one quiet line **命令行也连着 / The command line is connected too** with its own **⋯ › 断开** (confirm: **断开 {host} 上的命令行？**); an app window folds into a worker on its computer (**Ducter app 也连着**). A CLI alone shows as a computer named after its host (**一台电脑 · 命令行连着**), never "CLI on …". (2) **Every card speaks plainly and renames** (*the card's words amended by D161 (1)–(4)*). Phones and windows get D148's card: name renamed in place (`rename_device` already handled clients; now tested), what it is (**你的 iPhone · 用 Safari 连着**, **一台 Mac · 用 Ducter app 连着**), now or last seen, **它能为你做的** (phones: notifications, answers, talk), the folded lines, then 断开 after a hairline. The home renames too: a display name in `<dataDir>/device-name`; it still answers to its host name and `local`, and never takes a paired device's name. A default name a live device of the same kind already has gets ` · 2`, ` · 3` at pairing. (3) **Let this Mac do tasks for {home}** (*on the card, D161's first chip*). With the app connected elsewhere, this Mac's card and Settings › 在哪儿运行 carry one switch. On: a code over the app's own socket (`pair_code`), redeemed with `role: "worker"`; `<dataDir>/worker.json` (0600); the bundled helper's `ducter worker` under the LaunchAgent `<bundle>.worker` (RunAtLoad, KeepAlive on an unclean exit, `<dataDir>/worker.log`, no token in the plist). Off: booted out and removed, `worker.json` gone, the worker revoked with `disconnect_device`. A worker only does tasks (`/worker`, `/mcp`), never the conversation. `ducter service install --worker` on a Mac writes the same LaunchAgent (`status`/`uninstall --worker` too); a refused worker exits 0 under launchd (`DUCTER_SUPERVISOR=launchd`) so it isn't restarted. Browser and computer use on such a Mac stay the home's only (roadmap). (4) **The Devices page is one scroll:** the stage keeps the window's height and stays in view (sticky), the card and what can be added flow at their natural height beside it; no nested scroller, no clipped first line, ability labels wrap to three lines | U ("CLI on … 这是啥", "右边卡片很丑而且不能改名", "怎么让这台 Mac 替 hel 做事", the nested scrollers), L | `packages/core/src/{auth,devices}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{home,worker,remote,i18n}.ts`, `apps/desktop/{main.go,internal/local/{agent,worker}.go,internal/remote,internal/views/{computers,devicecard,devupdate,abilities,isles,settings_panels}.go,internal/i18n/computers.go}`, `apps/web/src/views.tsx`, `design/system.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/roadmap.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/install.md` |

## 2026-11-10: Ducter knows each AI agent's version and updates it gracefully (the author: "on a server, and every computer, Ducter should know the versions of the AI agents it uses and upgrade them gracefully and automatically")

DRAFT, for the author's review.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D156 | **Every device knows the version of each AI agent on it and where that copy came from, and updates it with the agent's own updater at a safe point, read back and rolled back where the channel can pin; at night only to versions Ducter verified.** (1) **Know.** Each device's AI agents carry `version` (its own `--version`), `how` it was installed, decided by where its binary really lives: `npm` (a global npm package; the prefix it lives under is kept), `brew` (a Homebrew cask), `installer` (the agent's own installer: Claude Code's native `~/.local/share/claude/versions`, Codex's standalone, pi's managed install), `app` (a copy inside the Codex app or the Claude app), `ducter` (pi inside Ducter from a checkout), `unknown` (a pnpm, yarn or bun global, anything else). The home reads `latest` from **that copy's own channel** (npm registry, Homebrew's cask API, the installer's own release pointer, pi.dev), one read per channel, at most every 6 h, kept in `<dataDir>/updates/agents-latest.json`, silent offline, `DUCTER_UPDATE_CHECK=off` never asks, never in tests. Workers send `version` and `how` in their hello; the home reads their channels. Each agent also carries `standing` (the installed version against harness-facts' verified range) and `latestVerified`. The per-agent facts (npm package, cask, installer mark, update and pin commands, app marks) are harness-facts' `channels`, the one registry (D135). (2) **Show** (*amended by D161 (3): in the coin's detail; the card shows one dot*). In the device card's AI account rows: **版本 {v} / Version {v}** quietly; behind: one dot and **有新版本 {v} / Update to {v}** with **更新 / Update**; outside the verified range: **Ducter 还没验证过这个版本。/ Ducter hasn't checked this version yet.** (honest, not alarming); a latest outside it: **Ducter 还没验证过 {v}，要等你点一下才装。/ Ducter hasn't checked {v} yet, so it waits for your tap.**; an app's copy: **随 Codex app 更新 / Updates with the Codex app** and no button; pi inside Ducter: **随 Ducter 更新 / Updates with Ducter**. (3) **Update** (`agent_update`, `packages/core/src/agents.ts`): wait until no task turn on that agent and no partner turn on it runs on that device (≤ 30 min, else `busy`); hold new task sessions on it on the home while the updater runs; run its channel's updater: npm `npm --prefix <p> install -g <pkg>@<v>`, Homebrew `brew upgrade --cask <token>`, an installer's own updater for the newest (`claude update`, `codex update`, `pi update --self`) or its pinned install for any other version (`claude install <v>`, Codex `install.sh --release <v>`); never an app's copy, Ducter's own pi, or an unknown copy. Judge by the version read back, never the exit code (updaters report success they didn't have). A copy that no longer answers `--version`, or whose login answered before and doesn't after, goes back to the old version where the channel pins (npm, Claude Code native, Codex standalone); a cask or pi's managed install can't, and the card says so. Steps **等它手上的事停好… / 在更新到 {v}… / 在检查 {v}… / 好了，现在是 {v} / 没更新成（，已经换回 {v}）** are `agent_update` events on the `devices` stream. (4) **Night** (D148's `autoUpdate` switch, off by default): in quiet hours, nothing working on it, one agent per look, only to a version inside the verified range: the channel's latest when verified, else, where the channel pins, the newest verified version; anything else waits for a tap. (5) **Workers** get the same `agent_update` op over their socket and send the fresh list of their agents (`agents`) after it. (6) **CLI** `ducter update --agents [--check] [--json] [--yes] [--all]`. (7) `scripts/probe-harnesses.ts` records each channel's latest beside the verified range. Rejected: one "latest" per agent from npm for every copy (a Homebrew or native user would be offered a version their updater can't install; casks lag npm by weeks, research/42 §5); auto-updating to any latest (the facts behind small windows and the partner's setup are checked per version, D135) | U (the author's goal), L, research/42 | `packages/protocol/src/{index,harness-facts}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{agents,harness-probe,devices,workers,core,ws}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{runner,worker-host}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{update,update-agents,worker,args,i18n}.ts`, `scripts/probe-harnesses.ts`, `apps/desktop`, `apps/web`, `design/system.md`, `design/harnesses.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/troubleshooting.md`, `skills/ducter-{operate,deploy}`, `design/scenarios.md` (A7), `design/todo.md`, research/42 |
## 2026-11-10: Where a task runs (the author: "AI 涉及到选择不同的环境和 harness 这部分我们设计有了么，优雅高标准顺滑自然么？"; decided with "按建议，第一性原理把事情都做好，给用户最优雅体验和情绪价值")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D157 | **Decided 2026-11-10 (every proposed default taken). Where a task runs is part of who does it: one ranking over (computer × agent), picked from what the task needs, said in one quiet line, asked only when whose sign-ins act or "now vs wait" is at stake, and never widened without the person.** (1) **Needs, not names:** the partner passes what a task needs (a place; browse; browse as you, with the site when known; a Mac app; a pinned agent; long or ongoing; continuity) and core picks the computer; "在 MacBook 上做" stays a pin by name. (2) **One ranking:** `fw_candidates` takes every computer that runs tasks; per (computer, agent, model): hard filters (the needs, then the agent's own), then can-start-now over waits (asleep or can't be reached keeps its rows, ranked below), then your pin and rules (a rule may name a computer; a pin of an agent that lives on one computer pins that computer), then the least access that does the job (Ducter's own browser over the user's Chrome), then a computer that doesn't sleep for long work, then continuity (its worktree, session, or the user's uncommitted changes), then the agent's own rank, then the home. One reason, the step that set the pick apart from the best row on another computer (**一直醒着，合上 MacBook 也不停** · **要用你登录着的 {site}** · **只有 MacBook 有 Keynote**). (3) **When it asks:** never between equal computers; never when only one computer can (it waits, said once: **得用 MacBook 上的 Keynote，它醒了我就开始。**); one line with the default **等它醒** when the best computer sleeps and another could do it with a visible difference (other sign-ins, or the user must sign in there); always when the other computer would widen access; D128's line when the user's own pick can't run. (4) **Saying where** (*drawn in D161's coin language*): with one computer, never; with two or more, on every task, in the meta line (**在 hel 上** · **用你的 Chrome** / **用 Ducter 自己的浏览器** / **用 {app}**), the reason on hover or tap; this amends D152 (2)'s "when it isn't this machine". The conversation says where only when it waits or uses the user's Chrome. (5) **Changing it** (*the sheet's subtitle and group heads per D161 (6), (9)*): in words (a pin, restated; two moves the same way propose a rule); **换谁来做** opens one sheet for who and where: on the desktop, a strip of the Devices islands with each computer's coins (tap a coin = pick that agent there), then a list grouped by computer with one reason per row and greyed rows with why; a row on another computer says what moving does (**从最近保存的进度接着做；分支会先推到 {remote}。**, and **会用你的 Chrome，你登录着的网站它都能用。** when access widens); New task's Where chips gain a computer part only when the choice is real (a repo on two computers; No folder with two or more), opening **在哪台电脑上做**, and an agent that lives only on another computer appears in Who tagged **在 {device} 上**, picking it moves the computer part too. (6) **Moving (E3):** targets come from the same ranking (a folder, a Mac app or the user's Chrome can't move to a server, greyed with why); **我要合电脑了** or **合上之前搬走** is one decision for the computer, default move, naming what stays; long or ongoing work starts on a computer that doesn't sleep, so most closes need no move. (7) **Safety:** a worker never reads the conversation (D155 (3)); sign-ins never copy between computers; nothing moves or is picked toward more access without the user's tap, rule or Go; the user's Chrome only in a window of its own (D130 (4)); uncommitted work moves only through the branch. (8) **Motion:** no new visual concept; D81's parcel lands on its island at start, waits at the home end of the bridge while its computer sleeps, and moves home and out again (never island to island); reduced motion cross-fades. (9) **First build step:** browser places on a worker (`runner.ts:924`, D155 (3)'s roadmap item), since otherwise a Mac that does tasks for hel can't use the user's Chrome. (10) **The author's six calls (2026-11-10, all defaults):** (a) public pages with both computers awake run on the home's own browser (hel's, D151); the user's Chrome only when their sign-ins are needed; D130 still decides which browser *within* one computer. (b) The where line shows on every task once two or more computers run tasks. (c) **我要合电脑了** is one decision for the computer, default **move**, moving only what doesn't widen access. (d) New task: the computer is a part of the Where chip, shown only when there is a real choice (no fourth row). (e) Long or ongoing work prefers a computer that doesn't sleep, even when an awake laptop would start sooner. (f) 换谁来做 shows the islands strip on the desktop; the phone shows the grouped list only | U, L, R43 | `design/where.md`, `design/prototypes/where.html`, `design/product-model.md` (glossary, §3), `design/new-task.md`, `design/flows/EC-steering-places.md` (E3), `design/flows/A-setup-devices.md` (A3), `design/scenarios.md`, `design/todo.md`, `design/ui.md` ; built 2026-11-10: `design/where.md` §16, `design/system.md` "Placement", `docs/devices.md` |

## 2026-11-10: OOMOL Hosted with a key, and a refusal never looks like success (the author added connector.oomol.com in Settings › 账号 and got "这个地址上没有 Ducter 能用的账号服务。" in a green, checked banner)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D158 | **OOMOL Hosted is reached where OOMOL serves it, with the key from its console; every check answer says what it is, in the right look.** (1) **The endpoint.** Any form of `connector.oomol.com` (bare, `/mcp`, `/v1`, `/v1/mcp`) is `https://connector.oomol.com/v1/mcp`, Streamable HTTP, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>` (research/44); connections saved at `/mcp` move on load. Anything at that address is source `oomol` whatever its tool list (unknown actions still count as writes). Self-hosted keeps `<base>/mcp` and its runtime token. (2) **Honest outcomes.** The SDK's HTTP status (`code`) decides: 401/403 **钥匙不对或已失效，换一把新的。** (with **去拿钥匙 / Get a key** for OOMOL), 5xx and network **连不上这个地址。看看网络，过一会儿再试。**, any other 4xx or a non-MCP answer **…没有 Ducter 能用的账号服务，看看地址对不对。**; an empty OOMOL key is never sent (`no_key`: **先把钥匙填上。** under the field, its border in the failed ink). After a check passes, `GET <base>/v1/apps?status=active` says what it can read (virtual `no_auth:`/`marketplace:` entries left out): **加上了 OOMOL，现在能看：Gmail、Discord** in the done look; none yet: **加上了 OOMOL，不过那里还没有能看的账号。** (info look) and on the row **连上了，但 OOMOL 里还没连任何账号。** with **去 OOMOL 连账号 / Connect accounts in OOMOL** (`console.oomol.com/app-connections`). `connection_result` carries `readable`; `ConnectionView.apps` holds it for OpenConnectors too. (3) **A refusal never wears the done look.** On the Mac, the tab's one line takes a tone with every message (done ✓ green, info, alert ! in the failed tokens); a refusal shows in the open form as an alert notice, with no form as an alert line. On the phone a refusal sits on the Accounts card in the failed tokens, never a toast. (4) **The OOMOL form is three plain steps, no address field:** 1. **在 OOMOL 登录，把 Gmail、Discord 这些账号连上。** (link to the console's accounts page) 2. **在 OOMOL 控制台新建一把钥匙。** (**去拿钥匙 / Get a key**, a button, `console.oomol.com/api-key`) 3. **把钥匙粘贴到下面，再点“检查并加上”。** The key is masked with **显示 / Show**, a paste is stripped of spaces and line breaks, Return checks. OpenConnector's Access page and Create Token stay in self-hosted's **怎么连** only. With oo signed in, the one tap (D122) stays first. Runner-up: MCP OAuth sign-in against `api.oomol.com` (its metadata advertises it), rejected for now: the console key is OOMOL's documented path for programs and needs no browser round trip | U (the author's screenshot), research/44 | `packages/core/src/{connections,ws}.ts`, `packages/core/test/{connections.test,fake-mcp}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,views/partner.go,views/oomol_hosted_test.go,i18n/ia.go}`, `apps/web/src/{app,views,i18n}.ts(x)`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-oomol.ts`, `design/connectors.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/accounts.md` |

## 2026-11-10: Notifications for the public iPhone app (the author: "你的 Ducter 还不能给这个 App 发通知：那台电脑上还没放 Apple 推送密钥", on the TestFlight app against a server home)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D159 | **The public iPhone app is notified through a small relay Nowledge runs, and the relay never sees what a notification says.** APNs takes a push only under the app publisher's key, which no user can or should hold (research/45), so D131's "each home sends to Apple with its own key" can't work for the TestFlight app. (1) **The relay** is one Vercel Node function next to the website, `https://ducter.ai/api/push/{register,send,revoke,health}` (`apps/site/api/push/[op].ts`; `node:http2` to Apple; no new provider). It holds the APNs key, a key of its own for handles, and an Upstash Redis store, all as Vercel environment settings (`design/release/push-relay.md`). (2) **End to end encrypted:** the app makes a 32-byte key (kept in a keychain group shared with its Notification Service Extension) and gives it to its home with the device token at subscribe time, over the paired, authenticated API. The home encrypts the title, text, where a tap goes and the decision id (AES-256-GCM, AAD `ducter-push-v1`, padded to 256-byte steps, keys redacted as in D152). Apple and the relay carry a neutral alert localized on the phone (`loc-key`: **Ducter 需要你看一下 / Ducter needs you**), `mutable-content: 1`, an allowlisted category and a collapse id that is an HMAC of the item under the phone's key. The extension (`DucterNotify`) puts the real words back and sets Go / Hold / Reply; if it can't, the neutral line shows and a tap opens the app. (3) **Abuse and state:** the app registers its device token with the relay itself and gets an opaque handle (the token encrypted under the relay's key, so the relay keeps no token database); the home only ever holds the handle. Limits: 30 sends an hour and 200 a day per handle, 600 calls an hour per caller address, 30 registrations an hour per address. The store holds revoked handle ids (400 days) and counters (an hour or a day), keyed by random ids and keyed hashes of addresses. A device's **断开 / Disconnect** (D155), turning notifications off, or connecting the app to another Ducter revokes its handle; connecting elsewhere also makes a new key. (4) **Home side:** a home with its own APNs key (`apns.json`, unchanged) sends straight to Apple, encrypted the same way, and falls back to the relay when Apple refuses that key (403); every other home uses the relay by default (`DUCTER_PUSH_RELAY`, `off` turns it off). `/api/me` says `apns`, `apnsVia` (`key` / `relay`) and `relay: "unreachable"` after a failed call; the phone says so plainly, and says when the app needs updating. (5) Web Push (D58) is unchanged: the no-app path. (6) The roadmap's "not planned: a hosted Ducter" holds: the relay forwards Apple notifications and nothing else. *Built in this change; not yet deployed: the relay's environment and the first signed build with the extension are the author's (`design/release/push-relay.md`).* | U (the author's report), L, research/45 | `apps/site/api/push/`, `packages/core/src/{push-relay,apns,web,core,devices}.ts`, `apps/ios/{DucterNotify,Shared}/`, `apps/ios/project.yml`, `apps/web/src/{push-client,views,i18n}.ts*`, `design/system.md` Clients, `design/product-model.md` glossary, `design/release/push-relay.md`, `design/release/ios.md`, `design/release/README.md`, `design/roadmap.md`, `design/todo.md`, `docs/phone.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md` |
| D160 | **Updating is calm and honest: Restart Now never leaves a blank, frozen window, and "up to date" always says when it was checked.** Two bugs on the author's Mac (0.0.5 → 0.0.6). (1) **Restart Now** turned the window white with a beach ball for a while before the relaunch. Cause, measured on a scratch signed bundle: mygo closes the windows and stops the run loop in the same main-thread turn, so the close never reached the window server; the quit hook then re-checked the staged copy (`codesign --verify --deep`, team, `spctl`: 1.0–1.8 s there, first-time and longer on a large notarized copy) with the blank window still on screen. Now: after the password (if any), the window shows **正在换上新版本，马上回来 / Putting the new version in, back in a moment** (the working partner, ≥ 0.7 s) while `Updater.Begin` checks the copy and starts the swap off the main thread; then every window is ordered out and flushed, and the quit runs with nothing slow in it (7 ms measured). A copy that fails its check keeps the app open on the update card, which says why. Every quit flushes the window server right after closing the windows, so install-on-quit and a child Ducter's stop never show a frozen window either. The first launch after a swap says **已更新到 {v} / Updated to {v}** once, and doesn't restart the LaunchAgent the swap already restarted. (2) **"已经是最新的"** on hel's card was a 6 h old answer (0.0.4, with 0.0.5 and 0.0.6 out). Now the release is read every hour; the card says **已经是最新的 · {t}查过 / Up to date · checked {t}** with a quiet **现在检查 / Check now**; opening Devices or Updates and about reads again when the answer is older than 15 min; and a device, worker or app (its hello now carries `version`) on a version newer than the latest known makes the home read again at once (once per version per 15 min) | author's bug reports 2026-11-10; scratch-bundle timings (`.shots/update-ux/`) | `design/system.md` "Updating in place", `design/product-model.md`, `design/release/README.md`, `docs/install.md`, `docs/settings.md`, `apps/desktop/{lifecycle,main,flush_darwin}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/update/update.go`, `packages/core/src/{release,updates,ws}.ts` |

## 2026-11-10: The device card as a character sheet (the author: "这些卡片设计好么，达到了我们的游戏级别的情绪价值和顺滑优雅标准了么，文字很多，layout 也很乱")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D161 | **Decided 2026-11-10 (the author: "按建议", all seven defaults) and built on both clients. Every device card is a character sheet: the device's clay big on top, its name renamed in place, one status line, its AI agents as a row of coins, its abilities as toggle chips, one quiet line of how it is connected, one Disconnect; every explanation is one tap away on the thing it explains.** (1) **Who:** the device's clay at 150 (phone 128) on a sunken oval, carrying D77's states (floats awake, blanket asleep, grey in mist when it can't be reached); the kind line goes (the art says it; the accessible name keeps it). (2) **How it is:** one line in words, not a chip (D69/D73): **{name} 住这儿 (· 在做 {n} 件事)** · **你正在用的这台** · **替 {home} 做事** · **在替 {home} 做 {n} 件事** (working ink) · **睡着了 · 醒来就接着做** (dusk) · **连不上 · 最后在线 {time}** (failed) · a phone **现在连着** / **最后在线 {time}**. (3) **What it carries:** the coins (D146) on a sunken tray with their names, grey when signed out, one static accent dot when an update exists (D148/D156), a dashed socket **再登录一个** at the end; tapping a coin opens its detail in place (never a floating layer): model, **版本 {v} · 用 npm 装的** (the channel in words), D156's "not checked" line once and only there, the update with D156's steps, **用它做事** (D89), **和 {device} 共用一个账号的额度** (replaces **N 台设备共用**), Sign out; signed out, only **登录**. (4) **What it can do:** toggle chips, the ability's clay at 30 and 2–5 words (en short forms: Browse, Use apps, Tasks for {home}, From anywhere); D155's worker switch is the first chip **替 {home} 做事**; ⓘ holds the old helper; turning one off asks in one line only when tasks would stop (**做完再停** default); a failure shows **没开成：{reason} · 看日志**, the log path only after the tap. (5) **Connected:** one line **通过 app 和命令行连着** with one ⋯ that opens the connections in place, each with its own 断开 and an inline confirm; replaces D155 (1)'s folded rows. (6) **Foot:** the version line only with an update; one **断开这台 {device}** as the destructive text button, confirm inline; the home has none. (7) **List:** one line per device, one word of state, never wraps. (8) **Phone:** the same sheet in one column. (9) **One language with D157:** the same coin row in the switch sheet (island strip and list, group heads as mini cards, subtitle **换谁，也可以换一台电脑**), New task's Who and the task's meta line; learned rules listed on Settings › 伙伴 with **你换过两次以后定的**. (10) **Motion:** chip squish on toggle (70 ms press, 240 ms `Bouncy` settle), coin pop with one sheen sweep when an update lands (420 ms), details enter 220 ms; reduced motion: none. No new visual metaphor (clay devices, coins, chips, islands are all existing). (11) **The author's seven calls, all defaults:** the status is a line of words, no capsule; no kind line (the clay says it; the accessible name keeps it); a chip acts on one tap and asks only when turning off would stop running work, default **做完再停**; version, install channel and "not checked" only in a coin's detail, the card shows one dot; Ducter's own version line only with an update (现在检查 lives in Settings › About, in the home's version row (D167); on the phone, once under the device list); the switch sheet's subtitle only with two or more computers; English chips use the short names. (12) **Only shared controls** (the author: "这种事情要杜绝"): a card or a settings page draws a button, switch, chip, coin or row only through the shared set listed in `design/ui.md` "Shared controls"; a test fails otherwise on each client (Mac `TestSharedControlsOnly`, phone `test/controls.test.ts`). Settings › About's version rows (D167's `valueRow` and `sideLink`) are in the set. (13) **As built** (`design/ui-devices-card.md` §12): the coin detail keeps a slot for more per-agent lines (the next is memory); the share line shows only when the home says an account is shared (`allowance.sharedBy`), naming the other devices; Ducter's browser's Try it and signed-in sites sit in 上网's ⓘ; the learned-rules list on Settings › 伙伴 is not built yet (todo) | U (the question; "按建议" and the shared-controls rule, 2026-11-10), L | `design/ui-devices-card.md`, `design/prototypes/device-card.html`, `design/product-model.md` (glossary), `design/ui.md`, `design/scenarios.md`, `design/todo.md`; built: `apps/desktop/internal/views/{devicecard,shared,abilities,devupdate,loadout}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/devicecard.go`, `apps/web/src/{devcard,controls,sheet}.ts(x)`, `docs/devices.md` |

| D163 | **Decided 2026-11-10 (the author: "go go go", every proposed default); X1 and X2 built, X3 behind Settings (D170). J5 (consumer AIs) stays parked as X5 until remote-MCP delegation (#8956) ships, then takes the same rules. ## 2026-11-10: Proactive help from first principles, and the accounts card (the author, after adding oo: "缺乏设计"; "基于这个的设计我们做到什么程度？精心设计各种 proactive 机制和第一性原理思考的应该有的东西了么？")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D165 | **Decided (U, 2026-11-10: "按建议", all eight defaults of `design/connectors.md` §15: all seven kinds on, Discord and Slack only @-mentions and DMs; at most 3 a day in one silent morning note at the end of quiet hours, nothing sent on a quiet day; "now" only for what costs something if it waits until tomorrow and needs the person, at night only kinds they named; watching only after yes at the first add; a 3-day first look plus a quiet 60-day lookback into the facts ledger, replacing D101's 14/60; oo read every 10 min and calendars every 15 by code, no model; the D166 card; meeting prep 10 min before, only with something open). Ducter may look often and speaks rarely: seven kinds of finds, one silent morning note of at most 3, "now" only when it costs something today, watching only after one question at the first add, and nothing it does on its own ever writes.** Audit first (`design/connectors.md` §1, VERIFIED): the broker, the oo tap and quiet hours are built; nothing proactive is (no cascade, triage, cap, morning note of finds, learning, ledger, first look; oo can't be watched, `OO_NOT_WATCHABLE`). (1) **The bar:** they likely don't know it; missing it costs something soon; there is something to do or decide, or it joins two things; it can show how it knows. (2) **Seven kinds:** 两件事对不上 (joins over a 60-day facts ledger) · 在等你回 (people only, 2 working days or asked again) · 你在等别人回 (3 working days, only when it mattered) · 你答应过的 (commitments in sent mail, 14 days, said on the due morning) · 快到期 · 要花钱 · 计划变了 · 开会前 (10 min before, only with something open). (3) **Delivery:** Left out (one line) · the morning note at the end of quiet hours (one partner message, opening line with what it read, ≤ 3 finds of ≤ 2 sentences each with one next step, the Left-out line; one silent notification) · fold-ins while in the app · **Now** (time-sensitive, within the cap) · before a meeting. Silent days send nothing; the header says **早上看过了，没有要你操心的**. Quiet hours hold everything except a per-kind exception the person said (a Rule). (4) **One chip row:** 只说急的 · **每天最多 3 件** (default) · 有用就说 · 不用主动找我. (5) **Follow-through:** one next step per find as a decision (default Hold); drafts stay in Ducter until a second Go; paying and booking are the person's; background asks expire to **过了** with the find's date. (6) **Learning:** 有用 / 这类不用告诉我 (a Rule, removable); implicit Go/Hold/ignore per kind and sender; 3 unread notes → **早上那条还要吗？** (D114's rule). (7) **Never:** send, reply, accept, pay, delete, archive, label or mark read; act on what mail says; keep mail; nag; wake the person unasked; watch without asking. (8) **Privacy:** a typed findings ledger (`findings.json`, 0600, pointers only, expires 30 days after each fact's date, dropped with its account); Mem only distilled gists with 记下了; per-app **自己留意** and **不让 Ducter 看这个** (`policy.deny`); **看了哪些** on the card. (9) **Cost:** the cascade with oo read by model-free polling (10 min; calendars 15; D137 already points oo watches to such looks), triage on the cheapest model in batches, the partner judges a few times a day; a monthly budget per source (D114's mechanism), at the budget it drops to 只说急的 and says so once. (10) **The first add:** the partner speaks (**已经能看你的 {apps} 了。要不要我现在看一眼过去 3 天，有没有在等你回的？**, default 好), answers within a minute, then asks the one cadence question with a default; no answer = nothing watches. Supersedes the proposals D97–D101 | U (the question; the decision 2026-11-10), L, research/35, research/46 | `design/connectors.md` Part A (§1.1 built), `research/46`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/scenarios.md`, `design/todo.md`, `design/prototypes/accounts.html`; built: `packages/core/src/proactive/`, `packages/core/src/{connections,settings,rules,push,apns,ws,core,judge}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{render,persona}.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/proactive/`, `docs/accounts.md` |
| D166 | **Decided (U, 2026-11-10: "按建议"). Each account source is a character sheet in D161's language.** One card per source (oo, OOMOL, your own OpenConnector, another MCP server): the source's clay mailbox (research/35's unlabeled winner) at 120, its colour saying where it runs, the lid lifting while it looks, grey in mist when refused; its name; **one status line** (**今天看了 214 封信 · 有 1 件值得说** · **你问起来才去看** · **oo 退出登录了 · 在终端里运行 oo login** in the failed ink); **a tray of app tiles** (literal rounded squares with each app's mark, D146's rule for marks), flat when not watched, grey when unreachable, a dashed **在 oo 里再连一个** at the end; **tap a tile → its detail in place** (what it reads, what asks first, last read, **自己留意**, the month's budget, **不让 Ducter 看这个**, for oo **要退出登录，去 oo 里**); the proactive chips once above the cards; one connection line (**经过 oo · 邮件会经过 OOMOL 的服务器** with ⋯ → **看了哪些** and the honest line); one destructive **不用 oo 里的账号了** confirmed inline. Five lines of default text. **加一个** steps back to a quiet text button. Phone: one column. No new metaphor (mailbox, chips, in-place detail exist; the tile is literal). Also built in this change (outright bugs): **移除** asked nothing (Mac and phone) and now confirms in its row; the added line said "加上了 oo" and now names **oo 里的账号** | U (the author's "缺乏设计"), L | `design/connectors.md` §12–§13, `design/prototypes/accounts.html`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/partner.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/ia.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/oo_connect_test.go`, `apps/web/src/{views.tsx,app.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`; built (the sheet): `apps/desktop/internal/views/{accounts,finds,shared}.go`, `apps/web/src/{accounts,finds,controls}.tsx`, `design/ui.md` (`appTile` / `AppTile`) |

## 2026-11-10: An update's outcome is judged by what runs (the author, Settings › 更新与关于 on 0.0.7: hel "0.0.7 · 没更新成", this Mac's worker "0.0.6 · 有新版本 0.0.7", and a lone "在查…")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D167 | **"Didn't update" is said only while the device still runs what it started from; the Mac's worker follows the app's updates; every state of a computer lives in its own row.** (1) **Root cause on the home (reproduced with fakes):** the restart helper writes `result.json` only after the restarted Ducter answered, but the home read it once, at its start, so it never saw its own outcome; the next update's Ducter read the previous one's (to 0.0.6) and, not running 0.0.6, called it a failure: 0.0.7 and 没更新成 at once. Any earlier failed attempt's file, an installer run or a slow start the helper gave up on did the same. **Now** the home reads the file again whenever it changes (15 min after its start, and on each card's view), a worker looks for it 10 min after it reached its main, and `judgeResult` decides against the running version: on the target → 回来了; still on `from` and not ok → 没更新成; moved on → nothing (marked reported). The card and both clients drop a failure the device has moved past (a home older than this rule). (2) **The health wait** is 180 s (was 60), the helper's journal says it's still waiting every 15 s, and readiness is told apart from the version: `alive.json` (a home writes it once it listens) counts as well as `/health` naming the version (a `/health` that doesn't say it, `DUCTER_LOCAL_TRUST=off`), an answer from another version is named in the reason, and a rollback that couldn't happen (no `ducter.prev`) is said instead of hidden. The helper is the previous binary, so (2) takes effect from the update after the one that brings it; (1) from the first start of a build that has it. (3) **This Mac's worker** (D155) kept running the old bundled helper from memory: the swap only kickstarted the home agent, and none at all when the app talks to a home elsewhere. Now the swap kickstarts `<bundle>.worker` too, and a minute after the app starts, a worker whose `alive.json` names an older version is restarted once per version (a plist naming another helper path is written again). (4) **Settings › 更新与关于 › 各台电脑上的 Ducter is built from shared pieces only** (`valueRow`, `sideStatus`, `sideLink`; a guard test fails on any `ui.*` laid out by hand there): 现在检查 and its 在查… sit at the home row's right where 已经是最新的 is; 没更新成 opens its why, what was seen, and 再试一次 under the row. The web's version block opens the why on a tap the same way, and its guard allows handlers only on shared controls. Copy: **devup.why.health** "新版本重启后三分钟里都没应答，所以换回了原来的。可以再试一次。" / "The new version didn't answer within three minutes of its restart, so the previous one runs again. You can try again." | U (the bug report), L | `packages/core/src/{update,updates,core}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{update,worker}.ts`, `apps/desktop/{main.go,lifecycle.go}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{update/update.go,local/worker.go,store/store.go,views/{devupdate,settings,settings_update}.go,i18n/devupdate.go}`, `apps/web/src/{app,format,views,i18n}.ts*`, `design/system.md`, `docs/{install,troubleshooting}.md` |

## 2026-11-10: Nowledge Mem in Ducter's tasks (the author: "充分第一性原理，拉满思辨……OK 就直接做"; then "focus window harness 需要 Nowledge Mem 呀，我们团队工作重心在这里")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D168 | **A Ducter task runs with the user's own Nowledge Mem plugin, as the partner's identity; Ducter's own memory pieces only fill in where the plugin isn't, and the one switch holds.** Decided by the author ("focus window harness 需要 Nowledge Mem 呀，我们团队工作重心在这里"), replacing the draft that turned the plugin off. (1) **Mem on (the default when Mem is connected):** a task keeps the user's setup, the Nowledge Mem plugin included, and runs as the persona's identity: `NMEM_AGENT_ID` (and `NMEM_SPACE` when bound) per D141 (3), which the plugin's Context Bundle, searches and capture use (DOCUMENTED, the plugin's README: "for multi-agent orchestrators, set `NMEM_AGENT_ID` per spawned worker … keeping `source_app=claude-code`"). Its writes therefore carry the persona's identity and the agent's own `source_app` (claude-code, codex), not `ducter`: the plugin takes no extra provenance from the environment (VERIFIED by reading its hook scripts 2026-11-10: the env it reads is `NMEM_AGENT_ID`, `NMEM_SPACE`, `NMEM_HOST_AGENT_ID`, `NMEM_AGENT_CONTEXT`, the mailbox and config ones; its capture is `nmem t save --from <agent> --session-id <native id>`). The link back to the Log is that native session id, which the Log already keeps per task. (2) **Bridge stays with the partner:** a task also gets `NMEM_NO_MAILBOX_NOTICE=1` (DOCUMENTED), so messages to the persona's mailbox never surface inside a task; `NMEM_BRIDGE_SESSION_MAILBOX` is never set (D102's allowlist keeps it out). (3) **Mem off in Ducter (Settings › 记得什么, `DUCTER_MEMORY=off`), and only then:** the plugin is turned off by id in the task session (Claude Code `--settings {"enabledPlugins":{"nowledge-mem@nowledge-community":false}}` merged with the PreCompact settings; Codex `-c plugins.nowledge-mem@nowledge-community.enabled=false` when the user's `config.toml` has it) and no NMEM identity is set, so the switch is true inside tasks. This is the one exception to D17, and only when memory is off. (4) **Where the plugin isn't installed for that agent on that computer** (Mem's own check, D169), Ducter fills in: the few memories that match the title and brief in front of the first turn (`<what_i_know>`, ids, 6 s cap, logged by id) and a read-only task `memory_search` answered by the home (≤ 5 results, 6 per task, logged by query and ids; workers hold no Mem key). Where the plugin is there, neither: it already injects the Context Bundle at session start and after compaction and nudges a search on each prompt (VERIFIED in its `hooks.json`: SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, SubagentStart, PreCompact, Stop, SubagentStop, SessionEnd), so a second recall would only double the context. (5) **Writing:** the plugin keeps the session's thread (its capture, under the persona); durable memories go one way: the task proposes `remember` (≤ 3) in its report, the partner keeps what is durable with `memory_add` and the task's provenance; the brief tells the task not to save or distill memories itself. Whether Mem distills captured threads into memories on its own, which could duplicate a `remember`, is not known (INFERRED risk; todo). (6) **Browser places get no memory** (the clean setup already leaves out every user plugin there, and the brief and the tool say nothing): the plugin injects the user's Context Bundle into the session unasked, and a page's text can steer the agent to type it into a form or a URL, a one-step leak with no search the agent chose to make; that is D62's reason, concretely. (7) Settings › 记得什么 says it: **…；替你做事时也用得上 / … and uses it in its tasks too**; off: **聊天和做事都不回想，也不记新的 / … in chat or in its tasks** | U (the author's direction), L (mechanics) | `design/memory.md` "Mem and tasks", `design/system.md`, `design/principles.md` (17), `design/product-model.md`, `packages/runner/src/{runner,brief,tools,oar}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/partner.ts` |
| D169 | **Nowledge Mem in the user's agents, per computer: Ducter shows where it is, recommends it where tasks would run without it, and installs it only by a task the user starts.** (1) **Check:** Mem's own `nmem --json plugins inspect` (local, read-only), on the home and on each worker through its `mem_plugins` op; at start, once a day, when a computer comes online, after a sign-in or an agent update (both refresh the devices), and when a setup task ends. A worker without the op, or without nmem, says nothing. The runner asks it whether a task there has the plugin (D168 (4)). (2) **One line per installed agent** in its coin detail (D161; the `coinLines` slot, its action in `coinActions`): **Nowledge Mem 已装 · 0.7.34 / Nowledge Mem 0.7.34 installed**; **Nowledge Mem 没装 / Nowledge Mem not installed** with **装上 / Install**; **Nowledge Mem 有新版 / … has an update** with **更新** (only when the check says so; today's check names no newer version); **Nowledge Mem 装了不止一份，要看一下** or **说不好 Nowledge Mem 装好没有，要看一下** with **理一下 / Sort it out**; **正在装 Nowledge Mem…** while its task runs; a computer that is away shows the line alone. "Installed" is never "working": every answer says runtime loading isn't verified, so that warning alone never turns installed into a review. (3) **装上 asks in place** (the card's own confirm block, shared controls only): **在 {device} 上给 {agent} 装上 Nowledge Mem？** and what it changes: **会在 {device} 上开一件事，照 Nowledge Mem 的官方说明来装：只改 {agent} 自己的设置，不动 Ducter。装好以后，重开 {agent} 才用得上。** Then one task on that computer, with that agent when it is signed in there (else any signed-in agent there), with the user's own setup, origin "you" (the tap is the Go): it follows https://mem.nowledge.co/SKILL.md for that host only, never guesses a URL or key and asks for a connect link when Mem isn't connected there, backs files up, verifies with `nmem --json plugins inspect --host <h>`, and says the agent must be restarted. The check runs again when the task ends. Never silent (D17). (4) **Ducter recommends it, once per computer and agent, ever:** whenever the check finds a signed-in agent without it (a computer that joined, an agent signed in, or already there): **{device} 上的 {agent} 还没装 Nowledge Mem。装上以后，我在那儿做事和你自己用 {agent} 时都记得你；会改 {agent} 自己的设置。** with **装上 / Install** as the suggested Go and **先不装 / Not now**; no timer: it changes the agent's own settings, so it waits for the tap. Go opens the same task. (5) **Memory off, or no Mem connected:** no line, no card, no install. (6) **Never a reason to buy anything** (the author): Ducter is whole without Mem (D123 remembers the conversation; tasks run the same without the plugin). Everything above shows only to someone who already has Mem connected (identity bound, D141, and Mem not "missing"). Without Mem there is one gentle mention, in Settings › 记得什么: **你说过的话它都记着。连上 Nowledge Mem（可以免费下载，在你自己的电脑上跑），它还能跨对话记住你的偏好，你的其他 AI 工具也能一起用。** with **了解 Nowledge Mem**; the empty conversation's old line ("想让 Ducter 记得更久？装上 Nowledge Mem") is removed. Copy never implies payment ("free to download, local first", DOCUMENTED, research/47 §4). | D170 | **X1 and X2 built, X3's switch built: the user's other AI agents reach the user through the partner, in existing objects only.** (1) **X1** (D164): `ask` takes `forMessage` (with `suggests`, `other`, `where`) for a question from one of the user's own agents; the decision carries `asker {messageId, agent, mine, where?, suggests?, other?}`; no default, no timer; a teammate's message is refused; at most 3 open per agent. On the user's answer Ducter's code replies **用户选了：「…」 / The user chose: «…»** (Hold: **用户说先别动，晚点再问。**), with no partner turn; after 30 min unanswered, once, **用户还没回，先别按这个动，晚点再问。** (2) **Cards:** in the conversation the asker's coin and **你的 <agent> 问你 · <where>** above the bubble, its second option as a button beside Go, **它建议「…」** and **没回就让它先等着。** under the tray; the same in On hand's Needs you (Mac and phone); the request's own quiet line is not shown again. (3) **Lock screen:** title **<name> · 你的 <agent> 问你**, body the question and the waiting line, no Go/Hold actions. (4) **Quiet lines** (J-Q5's strings, now decided): **你的 <agent> 发来请求 · <gist> / Request from your <agent> · <gist>**, **已回复 <X> / Replied to <X>**, **已发给 <X> / Sent to <X>**, and once a day **<agent> 今天又发来 <n> 条，我先放着了**. (5) **X2:** after two Go's in 14 days to the same own agent, Ducter offers once **以后你的 <agent> 发来的活，直接接？** (**直接接** · **还是先问我**); Go keeps the rule; from then on that agent's work request opens a task without a card (the brief starts **你的 <agent> 让我做的；你说过它的活直接接。**), at most 5 a day, the sixth asks. (6) **Settings › 伙伴 › 你的其他 AI / Your other AI agents** (shown once another agent has written, J6): **谁能找到 {name} / Who can reach {name}**: **只有你自己的** (default) · **也包括同事的** · **谁都不行**; the learned rules with **不再直接接**; muted senders with **恢复**. `Settings.peers {reach, trusted, muted}`. The partner's `peer_rule` tool carries "不再听 X 的" / "X 的活还是先问我" said in a user's turn. (7) **Who wakes the partner:** reach off, no check at all; own, a teammate's message is logged `muted` and never wakes it (an answer to what the user sent always comes through); muted senders likewise; over 20 a day, `set_aside`. (8) **B14, the start mark:** the first check for a persona records the handles already in the inbox without opening them (`<data>/partner/bridge.json`); they are never requests. (9) **Bridge on by default, proposed, not applied:** when `bun run bridge:live -- --real` passes S1, S2, S3 and an X1 round trip with the persona's own identity, the real `inbox`/`open`/`outbox`/`inspect` shapes replace the guesses, and a context selecting the persona's identity exists, Ducter turns Bridge on by itself for that context, with the Settings switch above as the off. ## 2026-11-10: What the author hit on real machines after 0.0.7 (hel, the MacBook, Mac app 0.0.7), and a near-real E2E rig so the next ones don't reach the author first

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D172 | **Every outcome is judged by what runs now, what's new is in the reader's language, this Mac's own copy updates here, and the Mac app decodes every frame the core sends.** (1) **AI agents (D156) follow D167's rule:** an update is judged against the version installed now (`judgeAgent`): on the target, done whatever was said; still on `from`, a failure stands; anything else is superseded and shown no more. "Nothing newer" (`no_update`) is never a failure: asked for a version it is already on, a copy is done with nothing run; a worker answers `no_update` with its fresh `agents` list; both clients drop such a failure from an older home, and an answer of `no_update` / `no_release` is never shown as a warning. **Root cause** (pi 1.5.1 on the MacBook said 没更新成 · 没有更新的版本): the worker's hello was probed once at its start and every reconnect said the old version, so the home offered 1.5.1 again and the worker reported "already on it" as a failure. Now a worker probes its agents again on every welcome and every 30 min. (2) **What's new per language:** `DeviceUpdate.notesBy {zh, en}` replaces `notes` (the release body's first English lines, cut at 140 with "…"); it is read only from a `<!-- whats-new -->` block in the CHANGELOG section (≤ 2 lines per language, ≤ 40 zh / 80 en, a longer line dropped, never cut); a card shows its own language's lines or none. A test fails a release section after 0.0.7 without both; the release job warns. (3) **This Mac's own copy:** a worker that is this Mac's app helper updates with this app (更新 when the app is behind); with the app current and the worker on an older version, **这台 Mac 上的 Ducter 已经是 {v}，替 {home} 做事的那部分还在用旧的，重启一下就好** with **重启它**; only another Mac says **在 {device} 上打开 Ducter，它会自己更新**. (4) **The live view on the Mac:** the app's frame decoder never listed `live`, `live_frame`, `browser_sites`, `browser_check`, `first_screen`, `folder_changes`, `close_result` (nor D166's `connection_reads`), so the Mac app dropped them all: 试一下 (D154) sat at 正在打开 example.com…… with "这是  上的浏览器" forever, while the phone worked. Fixed, with a test that reads the core's `ServerFrame` union. The view's top line is the window's own bar (traffic lights' room); the computer is named from the start (the home's name until the open frame); with no page in 40 s it says **{device} 上的浏览器 40 秒都没有回应，页面没打开。可以再试一次。** with **再试一次** (phone too); the core answers every failure to open as a `live` closed frame and never leaves a profile blind after a failed attach. **Found by the rig:** on Ubuntu 23.10+ as a normal user, Chrome for Testing dies at start ("No usable sandbox!": AppArmor stops unprivileged user namespaces); 上网 now stops with `browser_sandbox` and the one line that writes an AppArmor profile allowing user namespaces for Ducter's Chrome only (a glob over its versions), worded **这台的系统不让 Chrome 开它的安全沙盒。在这台上运行一次下面这行（只给 Ducter 的 Chrome 放行），再试一次。**; never `--no-sandbox` for a normal user. (5) **Guards:** drawn copy with an empty placeholder fails the view tests on both clients. (6) **The pre-release gate `bun run e2e:linux`** (`scripts/e2e/`, design/release/README.md): a colima VM like hel (Ubuntu, systemd user service), three locally built versions installed and updated twice in place against a loopback feed and mirror (`DUCTER_UPDATE_FEED`, `DUCTER_UPDATE_MIRROR`), 上网 and 试一下 with real frames decoded by the Mac app's own client, and pi updated from a loopback npm registry (`DUCTER_NPM_REGISTRY`) including the stale-worker case; each override is honoured only on loopback | U (the bug report), L | `packages/core/src/{agents,release,update,updates,ws}.ts`, `packages/core/src/browser/live.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{worker,update}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `apps/desktop/{main.go,cmd/e2eclient}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,store,i18n}`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{liveview,devupdate,devicecard}.go`, `apps/web/src/{app,format,views,devcard,i18n}.ts*`, `scripts/e2e/`, `design/system.md`, `design/release/README.md`, `.github/workflows/release.yml` |

|---|---|---|---|
| D173 | **Restraint: proactive help never says what or how much it read; it speaks only when something needs the person, and a quiet day is silent everywhere.** Amends D165 and D166. (1) **The morning note** opens with a greeting at most (**早。 / Morning.**), then the finds themselves; never "昨晚到现在看了 214 封信、今天的 5 个会和 GitHub，有 3 件值得你知道". (2) **Left out** is not in the note. Ducter still knows it (the note carries it for the partner only) and the partner says it only when asked (**还有别的吗？**); 看了哪些 under the card's ⋯ keeps the per-read lines. (3) **A quiet day sends nothing and shows nothing**: the header line **早上看过了，没有要你操心的** is gone; silence means success. (4) **The card's status line is the plain state**: **在留意 / Keeping an eye out** · **已暂停 / Paused** (the note paused itself) · **你问起来才去看** · the failed-ink lines; no read counts (only inside ⋯). (5) **The first look's answer** says only what matters: **有 2 件在等你回： / 2 things are waiting on you:** or **没有在等你回的。 / Nothing is waiting on you.**; never "看了 3 天的 386 封信". (6) **Notifications** are only for finds that passed the bar and need the person; the morning note's silent notification is the finds' own first clauses, never an "N things" summary. Guard: `packages/core/test/proactive-cost.test.ts` (no read count in any proactive text, to the person or to the partner) and the desktop's `TestMorningNoteInTheConversation`. | the author, 2026-11-10 | `design/connectors.md` §5, §6, §11, §12, §13; `design/product-model.md` (glossary); `docs/accounts.md`; `packages/core/src/proactive/{engine,words}.ts`; `apps/web/src/{accounts,finds}.tsx`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{accounts,finds,conversation}.go` |
| D174 | **The audit's fixes to proactive (P1–P5) and to Mem in tasks (M1–M4) (research/48 §2), as implementation of D165 and D168, with no behaviour the author hadn't decided.** (1) **The bar's cadence (P1):** the partner's model judges once before the morning note and, during the day, only what can't wait for tomorrow (urgent, or due today for a fold-in), at most **4 runs a day** in all (`JUDGE_RUNS_PER_DAY`); past that, or when every source a candidate comes from is over its budget, code's tests decide. A verdict is remembered by the candidate's key and a hash of its content, so nothing is judged twice. Triage stays on the registry's cheap model (`judgeModel`, `HARNESSES[h].cheapModel`); the partner's model sees only a capped set. Measured on the synthetic busy day (144 looks, a new ask every 10 min, a plan change every 30): 8 judge calls in 24 h, ≤ 4 per calendar day, against one per look before. (2) **Caps (P2):** ≤ 10 candidates per judge call (`JUDGE_MAX`, urgent first), ≤ 5 finds per note in 有用就说 (`NOTE_MAX`, the rest counted for the partner as **还有 N 件没说**), a fold-in rendered to the partner with only its new finds. (3) **Mail stays data (P3):** a Go task's brief holds Ducter's instructions only; the find, its headers and the names and asks in it travel as JSON inside `<data>` (D62); a subject or snippet is `redact`ed before triage. (4) **Budget (P4):** each source pays for its candidates' share of a judge call; cache reads count at a tenth; a source over its budget is "only urgent" alone, never the whole mode. (5) **Recovery (P5):** a look's checkpoint moves only after its items were taken in; a failed triage batch is retried on the next look; the 60-day look back is marked done only when it finished and resumes after a restart; said keys and old verdicts are pruned. (6) **Mem in tasks (M1):** if Mem's check sees the agent's plugin on that computer in any form but absent (installed, review, update), the task gets the plugin's context and Ducter adds no recall; Ducter's recall stands in where the check says absent or has nothing to say yet (no nmem there, an older worker), INFERRED to be the better miss: its recall is ≤ 4 notes of ≤ 220 chars, the plugin's bundle is unmeasured (todo). (7) **Memory off on every agent (M3):** Claude Code and Codex turn the plugin off by id (as D168); **pi runs with `--no-extensions`** (no per-package switch: the user's other pi extensions are off in that task too); **DeepSeek Harness runs under a Ducter-owned home** (Mem ships there as a profile bundle; INFERRED, DSH unverified). (8) **One registry (M4):** the plugin's host, ids, off method and context cost are `memPlugin` facts in `protocol/src/harness-facts.ts` with their evidence; the probe checks pi's flag and that Mem's check still names every host. | D176 | **The iPhone app is one universal app for iPhone, iPad and iPhone Duo, and the client lays itself out from the width it has, never from the device.** (the author: "TestFlight app 不支持 iPad 和 iPhone duo，你能支持一下么"). | D177 | **Settled 2026-11-10 (built, measured; the author confirmed the three open calls). pi and DeepSeek Harness may be what the partner thinks with, each holding the read-only partner by its own mechanism, after both passed the full partner eval above the existing partner.** (1) **Read-only, enforced, not asked** (principle 6): pi runs with an allowlist, `--tools read,grep,find,ls,mcp__*` (the pattern keeps OAR's MCP tools; without it they vanish), plus bash/edit/write excluded by name. (3) **DSH's compactor** fires at the plan's point as a fraction of the model's 1M window, with `retainTokens` a quarter of it (its default 16% retention at or above the threshold silently rejects the plugin: no compaction at all); tool-less one-shots drop every tool row; the session-log upload to DeepSeek (`dsh_session_log`) is off in Ducter-owned homes, so only the model call leaves. One home per setup (`<dataDir>/harness/dsh/<hash>`), so a session resumes where it was and a one-shot never rewrites the partner's files. A DSH task in the user's own home keeps the user's setup. (4) **pi's window** has no per-session knob; Ducter's re-cut at 120K bounds the partner (pi reports its context every turn). (5) **The eval** (74 scenarios, haiku judge, same persona): DeepSeek Harness + deepseek-flash 68/74, pi + deepseek/deepseek-flash 62/74, Claude Code + haiku 52/74 (LATEST), safety 4/4 and honesty 3/3 on all three; the eval now counts a tool call from its end event when it had no streamed head (rule_save and notice_rule were never counted on any harness before). (6) **Persona:** rule 1 gains "what a site or the web says now … is never a quick fact: it is a look-up, never said from memory" (all three answered "the latest Bun version" unseen before; DSH and pi no longer do). (7) **Models offered:** DeepSeek's `deepseek-v4-pro` (smart) and `deepseek-flash` (fast) on both, on pi only when pi lists them. (8) **The fallback brain** (amends D150 (4)): with nothing chosen and Claude Code not signed in on the home, the first signed-in of Codex, DeepSeek Harness, pi (`PARTNER_FALLBACK`; a plan before a key paid by use, then by the eval). Codex keeps **自动选的：只有 Codex 登录了**; another agent says **自动选的：Claude Code 没登录 / Picked for you: Claude Code isn't signed in** (`why: "only_signed_in"`), which stays true when several are signed in. (9) **Keys unchanged (D102):** pi on DeepSeek signs in with its own `/login`; the DeepSeek key in `keys.env` still reaches DeepSeek Harness only. A Gradle release build without the key refuses to build (Mem's postmortem: debug-signed APKs install nowhere and can't be updated). `versionCode = ((major·1000+minor)·1000+patch)·100 + (pre-release number ≤ 98, or 99)`, so every tag rises. (2) **Updating:** at launch and back in front, at most every 6 h, after the page's hello (or 3 s after a page that never says it), the app reads `api.github.com/repos/wey-gu/ducter/releases/latest` (drafts and pre-releases never), compares versions, and only when newer and not put off shows one card, in the half past a fold: **有新版本 {v} / Update to {v}**, the release's own whats-new lines in the page's language (D172's block), **更新 / Update** and **以后再说 / Later** (that version waits 3 days; a newer one comes at once). Update: download from GitHub's hosts only (https, redirects checked), progress in words (**在下载 {v}… n% · 在核对 {v}…**), the SHA-256 from the asset's `digest` else `SHA256SUMS.txt`, the size, then the APK's package, a higher versionCode and the same signing certificate as this app, else nothing is handed over and the card says why; the first time, Android's "Install unknown apps" switch, explained in one line with **去打开 / Open the setting**, and the update continues on return; then a `PackageInstaller` session asking for no extra tap where Android allows (12+). Android closes the app to replace it (**在安装 {v}… 装好后 Ducter 会关一下，再打开就是新的**); the next launch says **回来了，现在是 {v} / Back, now on {v}** once (D148's words). Not in this version: the page says **安卓 App 这一版还不能发通知；打开它就能看到要你看的事 / The Android app can't send notifications in this version yet; open it to see what needs you**; Chrome on Android keeps Web Push as today. | D183 | **Each AI agent's coin is that agent's real mark sculpted as a small clay object: Claude Code's starburst, Codex's OpenAI knot (the author's call: "codex 请用 openai icon"), DeepSeek's whale, pi's four blocks.** (1) **Why the mark, not the name:** a mark is read before a word; at the tray's 20–40 points a printed name was a smudge (D146 needed a plain body below 72 points, so three of four coins looked like coloured buttons), while a sculpted silhouette stays itself at 20 points, on light and dark. (2) **Why sculpted, not a mark on a disc:** two treatments were rendered side by side at the real sizes (`.shots/coins/`): the D146 disc with the mark raised in relief on its dome, and the mark itself as a free-standing clay toy. The relief shrank each mark to a third of the coin and made four near-identical discs; the free-standing marks read at every size, are told apart by silhouette alone, and look like the rest of Ducter's world (the character, the devices are objects, not badges). The family is held by one clay, one light, one camera and one optical size, not by one outline. (3) **One image per look:** the coin (`coin_<id>`) and the unavailable coin (`coin_<id>_off`: the same object in one grey clay, its form and light kept); no plain body by size and no shared grey body, since the object never stops reading. In the dark the grey coin sits back to 60% (it is the whole coin now, no print to protect). (4) **Motion (D77, clay only):** while that agent's task works, the coin rocks on its base 5° each way (4.2 s), replacing the rim sheen, which only fitted a round coin; an update landing pops it with one small wiggle; waiting on you, the 2-point bob (2.6 s); still under reduced motion. (5) **Pipeline:** prompts `design/art/assets/prompts/_style_coins.md` + `coin_<id>.md` with the official mark as a reference (downloaded to the gitignored `design/art/assets/marks/`, never committed), masters `design/art/assets/alpha/coin_<id>.png`, sizes by `tools/art/coins.py`; the site encodes from the masters. (1) **Who and how it starts:** the heavy multi-agent user on an iPad or an open foldable who wants every task and decision in view at once, the Mac's board, without losing the conversation beside them the rest of the time; nothing to learn: the side pane's header gets one button (arrows out) and ⌘2 does the same. (2) **The modes:** the two panes (D176, D182) unchanged; **On hand at the whole screen** (`#onhand/full`); **an opened task at the whole screen** (`#task=<id>/full`). A task opened from the whole-screen On hand opens at the whole screen; one opened from the conversation stands beside it. (3) **The Mac's, not a third design:** the whole-screen On hand is `apps/desktop/internal/views/onhand.go` and `byproject.go` in the page: the crumbs **对话 / 手头的事** with the counts beside them, **按状态 · 按项目** (remembered on this device), **开一件事** as a quiet text button; **接下来 / Next** when more than one thing is coming; the four lanes side by side with the Mac's widths (Needs you widest, then Working, Waiting, Done today; only lanes with something in them; **都按默认** in Needs you's header), the same cards (the decision card answered in place; a working card's title, where it stands, where/who/when; a waiting card's **在等 · {reason}** with its groove; Done today one card of rows with the first check); by project, groups at most 960 wide of one-line rows (face, title, branch, +/−, state) with **在这儿开一件**. The whole-screen task is the Mac's: crumbs **对话 / 手头的事 / {task}**, **先停 / 放下** at the header's end, one centered column on the conversation's 720 measure, the ask over the composer. Every word was already the Mac's (`onhand.by*`, `onhand.other`, `onhand.newhere`, `onhand.waiting.for` now in the web's strings too). (4) **In and out, the same control both ways:** the button at the right end of the level's header (arrows out **铺满 / Full screen**; arrows in **放回对话旁边 / Back beside the conversation**); ⌘2 toggles (two panes → On hand at the whole screen → two panes); the crumb **对话** goes back to the two panes; Esc goes up one level (task → On hand, at the whole screen; On hand → the two panes). The change morphs: the side pane and the whole-screen page share one view-transition name, so the pane grows into the screen and shrinks back (320 ms, the theme's ease; none under reduced motion or where the browser has no view transitions). (5) **The fold:** on a book fold On hand at the whole screen splits at the fold, what needs you on the first half and the other lanes on the second (by project, the groups in two columns at the fold); an opened task stays in its half (its column is a phone's width, which the half already gives). On a tabletop neither takes the whole screen (each half is a short screen; a whole page would lie across the crease), so the button is not shown. Under 1000 CSS px (a phone, Split View, both displays of iPhone Duo) nothing changes: a whole-screen address shows the phone's page. (6) **Three layouts weighed** before the author's direction: (a) a conductor rail (every task in a narrow list, the opened task beside it), (b) three panes (conversation · list · task), (c) the side pane growing into the Mac's board. (b) is cramped at 1024–1376; (a) would have been a third On hand the Mac doesn't have; the author chose (c), the Mac's, so a person who knows the Mac knows the iPad. *Built, headless-verified; for the author's review.* (7) **Settled 2026-11-11 (the author approved the open question's default):** beside the conversation the side pane shows **接下来 / Next** too, the Mac's one quiet grey line (13 muted, one line, cut with an ellipsis, the whole on hover), only when more than one thing is coming (D192). | U (2026-11-11) | `apps/web/src/{board.tsx,app.ts,wide.ts,fold.ts,main.tsx,views.tsx,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/test/fill.test.ts`, `design/ui.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/scenarios.md`, `docs/phone.md` |
