| D189 | **Settled 2026-11-11 (the author: 第一性原理做决定, defaults taken; built as D193, which records what changed on reflection). Ducter makes phone calls for the owner through a calling connection, Tuwa first; the partner is the only caller, every call waits for the owner's Go, the line always says it is an AI and for whom, in Ducter's voice by default or the owner's own enrolled voice, and never anyone else's.** Each item has a proposed default (按建议 takes them all). (1) **Provider:** Tuwa (tuwa.ai, chekusu Inc.) as the first calling connection: a remote MCP server with OAuth device sign-in, approval in chat built in, $0.30/min plus a destination surcharge from its line (US/UK landlines +$0, JP mobiles +$0.30), CN¥2.0/min by WeChat Pay or Alipay; the design names "a calling connection" so a self-built Twilio + real-time-model path can take the same card later. *Alt:* build on Twilio/ElevenLabs now (more control, Ducter becomes a telephony operator). (2) **Architecture:** a broker connection (`connectors.md` §18); the broker gains MCP OAuth device sign-in (a link and a code the owner opens on their phone), tokens in `connection-tokens.json`; Tuwa's `tuwa-mcp.mjs` is never installed in a task. (3) **Who calls:** the partner only; a task that needs a call says so in its report and may read the result of the call it asked for. (4) **When to ask:** every call, with one Go / Hold card from Tuwa's own summary (who, what may be said, what stays private, voice, number, estimated minutes and cost), no countdown; GQ4's "don't ask next time" never applies; revisit after 3 approvals of the same number and goal in 14 days. (5) **Mid-call questions:** the top card with push, also in quiet hours, no default; after about 90 s unanswered the agent says it will call back. (6) **Voices:** Ducter's voice by default (Tuwa's agent name = the partner's name, a Tuwa voice the owner picks); the owner's voice after enrolment on Tuwa's recording page (consent there, a short read-aloud; Ducter handles no audio and keeps only enrolled · on/off; stop and delete on the same ability card, saying the voice is stored by Tuwa's provider in China). Only the owner's own voice, never a family member's, a friend's or a late relative's. (7) **Disclosure:** every call opens with "this is {partner}, {owner}'s AI assistant" (in the owner's voice: "…speaking in {owner}'s voice"), never "this is {owner}" (FCC §64.1200(b), EU AI Act Art. 50, China's deep-synthesis and labelling rules, research/53 §5). (8) **Rails:** no calls in the owner's 免打扰 or outside 9:00–20:00 callee time; Tuwa's daily cap lowered to $10 at connect; 15-minute talk limit; a do-not-call list; never emergency numbers, marketing, calls asked for by untrusted content, more than one callee per card; money only on Tuwa's own payment pages. (9) **Results:** one outcome line exactly as Tuwa reports (`uncertain` said as such), the opened call holds summary, transcript if any, length and cost; Mem only on tap; no recordings in v1. (10) **Off:** Tuwa Friends sync and voice sampling stay off, whatever Tuwa's install guide asks of agents. (11) **Not in v1:** inbound answering, calls from the owner's own number (needs a Mac home + iPhone + Tuwa Device), listening live, Ducter calling the owner as the top escalation rung. (12) **Region:** the partner says before drafting that calls to mainland China numbers likely don't work through Tuwa (not in its zone table) | U (the request), R53 | `design/phone.md`, `research/53-phone-calls-tuwa.md` |
| D193 | **Settled 2026-11-11 (built; the author: "电话功能也做好做全…第一性原理做决定"). Phone calls as built, and what D189 changed on reflection.** (1) **The broker signs in to remote MCP servers by device code (MCP OAuth 2.1, RFC 8628/9728/8414/7591/8707), generic, not Tuwa's:** adding a server with no key that answers 401 discovers its authorization server; a device sign-in starts (a registered public client first, with a loopback redirect URI because Tuwa's registration refuses one without it, then the client-name form Tuwa's own bridge uses), the owner opens the link or scans the QR on their phone and checks the code; tokens (access, refresh, expiry) in `connection-tokens.json` 0600, refreshed a minute early and once more on a 401, revoked on remove; a refresh refused as gone shows **需要重新登录 / Sign in again** on the card. A token the owner already holds (a bridge's credentials file or a token answer, JSON) can be pasted as the key instead (the import path). Every broker request says `User-Agent: Ducter/<version>`; Cloudflare's 1010 is `blocked`, not `unauthorized`. (2) **The calling connection is recognised by its tools** (Tuwa: `tuwa_call_draft` + `tuwa_confirm`) behind a small provider interface (`phone-provider.ts`); its call-flow and settings tools are reserved to Ducter's own flow, so `connection_call` can never dial or change them. (3) **At connect Ducter sets** Tuwa's `dailyLimitCents` to $10, `voiceSampling: paused`, its phone agent's name to the partner's, and Friends `sync: off`; the phone card shows them and the owner changes the cap there ($5/$10/$20/$50; raising passes Tuwa's confirmation with the owner's tap as the words). (4) **One partner tool, `phone_call`** (draft / answer / cancel / block); core holds the rails, the provider and the polling; the Go card is a `decision` with `call` (the plan: number, what for, may say, won't say, voice, a Tuwa number, about N min and $, when, the AI opening line, Tuwa's own summary behind a fold), Go = **打过去 / Call**, Hold = **先不打 / Not now**, no default, never "don't ask next time". Ducter, not the partner, confirms on Go with the button's words (plus any words the owner added) as Tuwa's `personReply`; every change of a call is one `phone_call` snapshot in the Log. (5) **Hours, changed:** D189 said a call asked for in quiet hours "is scheduled"; Tuwa's draft takes `scheduledAt`, so the draft carries the first allowed time (outside the owner's 免打扰, inside 9:00–20:00 in every zone of the callee's country unless the partner names the zone) and the card says when; a Go given after that time waits in Ducter and dials at the next allowed one. (6) **Region, changed from "likely" to a rule:** Tuwa's schema lists the 20 countries its numbers call (no mainland China); Ducter drafts nothing elsewhere and says so. Emergency and short numbers are refused before parsing. (7) **Mid-call questions:** polled every 15 s (5 s on the line), the question is a `decision` with `call.kind = question`, pushed at once even in quiet hours and inside the coalescing window, time-sensitive, no buttons in the notification; yes/no questions answer `accept`/`decline`, others the owner's words; after 90 s Ducter sends one fixed line asking the agent to say they will call back (a rule set before the call, not an answer for the owner). The owner may answer in the conversation instead (`phone_call do=answer`). (8) **Length:** the agent is asked to wrap up by 15 minutes; Ducter hangs up at 17 on the line or 20 from dialing. (9) **The outcome** wakes the partner once (`[call ended]`), said exactly as reported (`uncertain` as 没能确认); a task that asked gets it by task_send. The owner's hang-up or Not now wakes nothing. (10) **Your voice** on the phone card: **去 Tuwa 录 / Record on Tuwa** opens Tuwa's own recording page (from `record_personal_voice`), **用我的声音打 / Call in my voice** and **删除我的声音 / Delete my voice** pass Tuwa's confirmation with the owner's tap; Ducter keeps only enrolled · on. (11) Not built: transcripts and outcome fields are read by likely names until a real call shows them; inbound, own number, live listening, Mem on tap from a result | U, R53 §8 | `packages/core/src/{mcp-oauth,phone,phone-provider,connections,push,ws}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,persona,render}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/phone.ts`, `apps/web/src/phone.tsx`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/phone.go`, `design/phone.md`, `design/connectors.md` §18.3, `docs/calls.md` |

## 2026-11-11: Starting work by hand, fast, now or later, and projects you make yourself (the author, moving daily Conductor work into Ducter: "手动创建一个 focus Window 的弹窗很缺乏设计……如果要创建 loop 类型的任务，时间类型，条件等待的任务，不从聊天触发，是不是也应该允许优雅创建？……接下来我们要让 ducter……成为我的主力工作环境")

Settled 2026-11-11 (the author: "第一性原理做决定", defaults taken; built). User first: **who** is the heavy
multi-agent developer opening ten sessions across five repos in a morning, and, second, anyone who
wants "every weekday at 9" without a conversation. **Before:** a card of three rows of chips of
uneven height, a Start button of another family, nothing for later, and every start pulls them out
of where they were. **After:** words, one row, ⌘↩, next one; later and repeating work started the
same way and visible on On hand with when it runs. **First time:** ⌘N; the card's defaults are
right; the extra choices are pills that say their value (现在 / Now), shown, not explained.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D185 | **New task is one quick card: the words first, then one row of pills, and a "when" that reuses the existing waits, loops and routines.** (1) **Layout:** 做什么 (focused, verbatim line under it) · one row of same-height pills, each opening a short list in place: the folder (recent folders tasks used and projects added, D186, each with its path as ~/…; 不用文件夹 / No folder; on the Mac 加一个文件夹… / Add a folder…), the repo's branch, the computer only when the choice is real (D157), 谁来做 (coin, name, 推荐 / Suggested on the best fit; the list has every agent with its one reason, a blocked one greyed), 什么时候开始 / When it starts. Two new shared controls: `pill` and `menuRow` (web `Pill`, `MenuRow`); `newtask.go`, `newtaskwhere.go` and `byproject.go` join `TestSharedControlsOnly`. Foot: one line (an error, what just started with 打开 / Open, or the keys **⌘↩ 开始 · ⇧⌘↩ 开完接着开下一件**), then 取消 and 开始 at one height. Runner-up rejected: keep D128's three labelled rows of chips (heights and families didn't match, the place row grows with every repo, and later starts would add a fourth and fifth row). (2) **Fast in a row:** ⌘↩ starts and opens the task (as D128); **⇧⌘↩ starts and keeps the card**, empty, with the same folder, branch, agent and when, saying **开了：「X」** with Open. The agent the user picks is remembered on that client for the next card (Suggested again when they pick it). (3) **When:** 现在 / Now (default) · 定个时间 / At a time (今天 / 明天 / 后天 + a typed time; a routine of one firing, `every: "once"`, that ends after its run) · 定期做 / On a schedule (每天 / 工作日 / 每周 + a day / 每隔几小时; a routine, active at once) · 等某件事发生 / When something happens (the task opens now with **先等到：{X} 然后：{words}** and waits through its own `wait_for`, D112 (4)) · 一直做到… / Keep going until… (opened now, then `until` on it, D113). A routine the user makes on the card (`by: "you"`) needs no proposal card (their tap is the Go), runs their words verbatim (a repeating one adds one line on which run it is) with the agent they picked, effect `local_writes`, reports every run, and keeps D114's pauses and review. Start says **定好 / Schedule** for a time or a schedule; that closes the card on On hand. A past time says **这个时间已经过了。** No new scheduler: routines, waits and untils as they are. (4) **On hand shows the kind:** by state, as D115 (a wait in Waiting with 等 …, a loop with its lights, routines on the shelf with the calendar; a one-time start says **到时候开始 / Starts then** with **不做了 / Don't do it**); by project, each row says what it waits for or **第 n 次**, and what starts later or repeats in that folder sits in its project's group with its calendar and **下次…**. Alternative: a separate "Schedule" sheet (rejected: a second place to learn, and the words are the same) | U (2026-11-11, the author's request; defaults built, not yet reviewed) | `design/new-task.md`, `design/product-model.md` §1, §3, `design/ui.md` (Shared controls), `packages/protocol` (`NewTaskWhen`, `task_new.when`, `RoutineTrigger.every "once"`, `RoutineBody.by`), `packages/core/src/{newtask,routines,schedule,ws}.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{newtask,newtaskwhere,byproject,shared,work}.go`, `apps/web/src/{newtask.tsx,app.ts,controls.tsx,work.tsx}`, `docs/faq.md`, `docs/waiting-and-routines.md` |
| D186 | **A project is a folder you work in, named after it; you make one by adding the folder.** (1) **What it is:** the glossary's Project stays a label that remembers its places, and its everyday form is one folder (a git repo, or a plain folder, D142) on the home's computer. Every task in that folder, or below it, belongs to it; its name is the folder's. Inference (a task's repo) still makes projects with no step. (2) **Making one:** 加一个文件夹 / Add a folder (By project's foot, and the card's folder list) opens macOS's folder picker; the picked folder is kept on the home (`projects.json`, user data like settings) and shows at once as a group in By project (**还没有事。** until its first task) and first in New task's folders, with its "+" ready. ## 2026-11-11: One answer to "is there a newer version" on the Mac, and updating a Mac from the phone (the author: "我的 mac app 竟然在'各电脑上的'里边说有新版本，但是 app 自身检测不出来新版本，没法更新，其次我们应该允许从手机上更新 mac 里的版本吧？")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D187 | **Settled 2026-11-11 (the author: "第一性原理做决定"; built, verified with fakes; not yet on the author's Mac). The Mac app finds every version the home knows of, and a Mac updates from any client's 更新: the Ducter on that Mac asks its app, which downloads, checks and restarts at the safe point.** (1) **Root cause (the author's Settings › 更新与关于, 00:48 on 2026-11-11):** the top card said **现在还查不到新版本** while the same page's row for this Mac said **有新版本 0.0.13**. The app reads GitHub's API anonymously (D72); its 60 requests an hour are shared by everything behind the same address, and this address had used them (`/rate_limit`: 60 of 60, read-only). The 403 was taken for "no public releases" (NoFeed, the private-repo case), and since the failed read still counted as the day's check, the app wouldn't ask again for 24 h. The home on hel reads the same release from another address and falls back to the release page's redirect (D148 (1)), so it knew. Not the cause, checked: the feed's asset names (`Ducter-0.0.13-arm64.dmg` present), the version order (numeric; 0.0.11 without a release doesn't matter to `releases/latest`), caching (no ETag is sent). (2) **The fix: one answer.** A rate limit is never NoFeed: the app falls back to the release page's redirect for the version and to the tag's download address for the files (neither is the API); the home's devices frame is a hint: when it says a newer version is out for this Mac (matched by the worker's device id in `worker.json`, which no rename changes, else the computer's name), the app checks now (once per version every 15 min) and fetches that version even while GitHub can't say. Whatever names the version, the disk image must match the tag's `SHA256SUMS.txt` and carry this app's own Developer ID team (D72), so a wrong hint can at most fetch a genuine release that doesn't check out. Only an answer counts as a check: a failed read keeps "checked {t}" where it was and is tried again within the hour; its line is **这次没查成 / Couldn't check this time** with **GitHub 没应答，一小时内再试。**, never "didn't update". (3) **This Mac's row is the way to update it**, a worker row as well as a home row: the same 更新 as the top card (D167's single flow). (4) **`ducter update --check` asks the home the CLI follows** (D147) and lists every computer there, not only this one; a home that doesn't answer is said (**连不上 {name}，下面只有这台电脑。**). (5) **Updating a Mac from the phone.** Who: the author away from the MacBook, a consumer whose Mac sits at home, a fresh user who sees 有新版本 on a card. What they feel: before, a dot they can't act on ("open the app there"); after, one tap and the card tells each step, calm that nothing mid-way is cut. First time: the same 更新 button as on a server's card, one line under it saying what will happen. The flow: the phone's 更新 goes to the home as today; a worker (or a home) whose Ducter lives in Ducter.app and says `asksApp` in its hello asks its app through two files in the shared data folder (`updates/app-ask.json` this side, `updates/app.json` the app's state, rewritten on every change and every 15 s): **get v** (the app downloads and checks it; the card shows 在下载 {v}… n% · 在核对 {v}…), then the safe point (D148 (4): no merge or undo mid-way, a turn gets its grace; 等手头的事停好), then **restart** (the app's own Restart to update: **正在换上新版本，马上回来** on the Mac's screen, D160), which swaps the bundle and restarts that Mac's Ducter on the new helper; the restarted Ducter says how it went (`result.json`, judged by D167's rule: on the target 回来了; a swap that didn't take, seen as the reopened app's install failure, 没更新成 with why). Plain refusals, nothing started: **那台 Mac 上的 Ducter 没开着，或者 Mac 睡着了** (no fresh `app.json`; a quitting app removes it at once), and **…是用管理员身份装的，这次更新要在那台 Mac 上输一次密码** (no password prompt is raised on an unattended Mac). The card's line under 更新: **{Mac} 上的 Ducter 会下载、核对，等手头的事停好再重启。** A worker from before D187 (no `asksApp`) keeps the old line, open Ducter there. The night-time auto-update (D148 (10)) still skips Macs (todo). | U (the report and its screenshot), L (`/rate_limit` on the author's address; the app's `state.json` checked 00:38:26, nothing staged; `releases/latest` = v0.0.13 with its image) | `apps/desktop/internal/update/{feed,update,asks}.go` and tests, `apps/desktop/{main,lifecycle}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{devupdate,settings,settings_update,app}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/{lifecycle,devupdate}.go`, `packages/core/src/{app-update,updates,devices,workers,core}.ts`, `packages/core/test/app-update.test.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{update,worker,home,main,i18n}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `apps/web/src/{devcard,i18n}.ts*`, `design/system.md`, `design/scenarios.md`, `docs/{install,phone,troubleshooting}.md`, `design/todo.md` |

## 2026-11-11: Autofill: sign-ins, cards and your details for errands (the author: "像 muse 里那种的 credential 管理我们做了么？比如以后我请他帮我订酒店，订机票，买东西之类的都会有各种场景需求… 很多功能我希望在手机，ipad Android 上都能优雅做到"; decided from first principles while the author slept)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D191 | **Settled 2026-11-11 (the author: "第一性原理做决定", defaults taken; built and verified on fakes and a local Chrome for Testing). 自动填充 / Autofill keeps the owner's saved sign-ins, cards and details on the home, encrypted, and Ducter's browser fills them by reference: no model, Log line or client ever holds a secret after it is saved; every payment waits for a Go that names merchant, amount and card; a saved sign-in or a private detail waits for one OK the first time on each site.** (1) **Three stores, kept apart** (research/54 §11): the session stays in Ducter's browser profile (D151); secrets in Autofill; the owner's own password manager untouched (on a Mac tasks use the agent's own browser in the owner's Chrome, D130, where it fills as always). (2) **What is stored:** sign-ins (site, user name, password), cards (number, expiry, name, postcode), details in two kinds: readable (name, email, phone, seat, meal, passport country; the partner and tasks may read them) and fill-only (passport number and expiry, birthday, address, known traveler number, loyalty numbers). **The card's security code is never stored** (PCI DSS 3.3.1.2): it is typed on each Pay card and held in memory for that payment. (3) **At rest:** `<dataDir>/saved/<persona>.enc`, AES-256-GCM, 0600; the key in the Mac's login keychain (service `Ducter Autofill`, one account per data folder) or, elsewhere, a 0600 key file; the screen says which. Never synced to a client: a secret goes up once in `saved_put` and never comes back; edit replaces, nothing reveals (alt: reveal behind Face ID / Touch ID; rejected for v1: the owner's own password manager is where to look one up, and reveal widens what a stolen phone can do). (4) **By reference:** handles (`signin:github.com`, `card:visa-4242`, `me.passport_number`); the partner's one new tool `saved_list` (listed only once something is saved); a task in Ducter's browser gets `saved_list`, `browser_fill {ref, use, part}`, `browser_ask_to_pay`; a brief naming a saved handle runs on Ducter's own browser wherever it can start. The fill types by CDP, marks the field filled and draws it as dots; every browser tool's answer is scrubbed of saved values; card numbers and named codes never render and never reach a task (D152's detector gains them, with its Go port). (5) **Asking:** every payment: a 付款 / Pay card from `browser_ask_to_pay` naming the page's host (read by code), amount, what it buys, card and last four, **第一次在这里付** when new, a security-code field, **先不付 / 付 {amount}**, no default; after Go the card fills only for that task, on that site, within 15 minutes. A saved sign-in fills only on its own site (a look-alike gets nothing) and asks **可以 / OK** the first time; a private detail asks the first time on each site. Only https. Only Ducter's tools set these cards, never a task's words; the partner doesn't ask first itself (one card, not two). (6) **2FA, 3-D Secure, passkeys:** the owner's, live, in the D151 live view; codes never stored; codes from a connected mailbox not in v1. (7) **History:** 用在了哪儿 / Where it was used: filled, said Go to {amount}, said OK, said no, with site, task and when (300 kept, inside the encrypted file). (8) **Clients:** Settings › Autofill on the Mac app and the web client (phone, iPad, Android), the same keys; the Pay and OK cards on both. (9) **Not in v1:** 1Password/Bitwarden by reference, single-use cards (Stripe Link's agent wallet, Privacy.com, US/Canada), Face ID on Pay, mailbox codes, TOTP | U (the request), R54, R25, L (the fake checkout: a page that echoes keystrokes leaked the 3-digit code to a read before the code scrub; haiku eval 58/78 with a pasted card number reaching a brief before the detector gained card numbers) | `design/autofill.md`, `packages/core/src/saved/*`, `packages/core/src/browser/{tools,reader,index}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{core,ws,decisions}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{saved,index,internal,redact}.ts`, `packages/protocol/fixtures/redact.json`, `packages/runner/src/browser.ts`, `packages/partner/src/persona.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/*`, `apps/web/src/{autofill.tsx,i18n-autofill.ts,app.ts,views.tsx}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/saved.go,views/autofill.go,i18n/autofill.go,redact}`, `design/{system,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/autofill.md` |

## 2026-11-11: The accounts card, polished (the author, Settings › 账号 with OOMOL Hosted holding two Gmail, two Slack, Discord and a WeCom bot: "我希望你能把一些忽略的 UI 细节打磨好，比如这个地方展开 openconnector 里边信息很乱很乱哈")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D192 | **A tile names the app and then whose account it is, never the account's own display name as the app; a detail reads in three bands (who, what it may do, the person's choices); the connection's facts and its two actions sit last, on one row; what passes is worded by what the card holds.** (1) **Who and how it starts:** anyone with more than one account of an app in one place (a work and a personal Gmail, two Slack workspaces), who must tell the tiles apart at a glance and see, for the one they tap, what Ducter reads, what it always asks first and whether it looks on its own. (2) **The app's name is the service's:** on OOMOL Hosted `displayName` is the account's own name ("nowledge-labs-inc", "w-nowledge-mail"), so it titled every tile of that app with the first account's name; the core now titles an app by its service (Gmail, Slack, WeCom bot), takes `displayName` only for a service it has no name for, and uses it as the account's label after `accountLabel` and `alias`. (3) **Whose it is, in a few letters:** when an app has more than one account here, the line under it is the address's name at a mail provider (weyl.gu) and the domain anywhere else (nowledge-labs); if two would read the same, the name before @; still the same, the whole address; a workspace or handle is itself, on up to two lines. The whole name (Slack · Nowledge Labs, Inc) is the tile's accessible name and its hover. (4) **A tile it doesn't read says so in words:** its second line is **不看 / Not read**, not only grey; its detail says **Ducter 不看这个账号；你问起来也不看。/ Ducter doesn't read this account, not even when you ask.** and offers **让 Ducter 看这个**. (5) **The detail, three bands:** the app's mark, its name, the whole account and, when it has looked, **最后看过：…** (never "还没看过": silence, D173); then **会看 / Reads** and **先问你 / Asks first** as a label column beside the words; then **自己留意** with its note, which says why it isn't looking when the person chose **不用主动找我** or hasn't chosen (**上面选了「不用主动找我」，现在不会自己去看** / **上面还没选怎么告诉你，现在不会自己去看**); **不让 Ducter 看这个** quiet at the foot. (6) **The connection last:** under a hairline, where what it reads passes, worded by what the card holds (**OOMOL 托管 · 邮件和消息会经过 OOMOL 的服务器 / Hosted by OOMOL · your emails and messages pass through OOMOL's servers**; 邮件、日程、消息、GitHub 通知, else 读到的内容), then one row: **看了哪些** (a labelled quiet button, no bare ⋯) at the start, **不用 {name} 了** at the end; the stop asks in place on a soft failed ground. The card spans the column like every other settings card. (7) **Marks:** Slack gets its four bars (it read as Discord); an app with no mark gets a plain app grid in ink, never the grey of off. (8) **The sweep's rule, the same class of detail elsewhere in Settings:** one control height per row (ControlSM), a section title at the group headings' size (自己发现了事，怎么告诉你 on Notifications and Accounts), no empty box adding a gap (谁来做事 with no rules), the web's one switch drawn as the Mac's pill on every browser (it was a native checkbox), a row's name at 700 like every row, the quiet hours' times a row of their own rather than loose on the paper, a link that is a button never underlined. (9) D184's open question settled with its default: 接下来 in the side On hand (D184 (7)). *Built, headless-verified on the Mac and the phone; for the author's review* | U (2026-11-11) | `packages/core/src/{connections.ts,oo-link.ts}`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{accounts.go,shared.go,partner.go,loadout.go,settings_panels.go}`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/accounts.go`, `apps/web/src/{accounts.tsx,controls.tsx,views.tsx,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-oomol.ts`, `design/connectors.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/ui.md`, `design/todo.md` |

## 2026-11-11: Watch Ducter's browser work, and take turns in it (the author: "我们应该允许看到服务器上的浏览器的操作和样子，甚至允许用户自己操作… 很多功能我希望在手机，ipad Android 上都能优雅做到")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D190 | **Built 2026-11-11, for the author's review on real devices. Ducter's own browser can be watched while a task works in it, and the user and the task take turns in it: one live view for a sign-in, Try it and watching, on the phone, iPad, Android, foldables and the Mac.** (1) **看它上网 / Watch it browse:** a task holding Ducter's own browser (On hand marks it, `TaskCard.browsing {device}`) can be watched from the opened task (**看它上网 / Watch it browse**), from its computer's 上网 block (**{task} 正在用它 / {task} is using it** · **看看 / Watch**), and from its card on the wide On hand (D184). `live_open {decisionId: "watch", taskId}` shows the task's current tab and follows it to a new one. Watching changes nothing for the task: not blind, the page keeps its own size (the screencast only scales it for the viewer), nothing the user does goes in. A task with no page yet says **它还没打开网页。一打开，这里就能看到。** and shows the first one when it comes. Watching starts nothing (a browser that isn't running stays so). (2) **What it is doing, calmly:** each browser tool notes its step (`browser/notes.ts`), and the watcher's bottom line says it for six seconds, then goes quiet: **正在打开 {host}** · **正在看这一页** · **正在点「{label}」** · **正在往「{label}」里填** · **正在按 {key}** · **正在翻页** · **正在后退** · **换到了另一个标签页** · **正在截图** (en: Opening {host} · Reading the page · Clicking "{label}" · Typing in "{label}" …); a click or a typing lands as one soft ring on the page (none with reduced motion). A label is the element's own words (a field's label or placeholder, never its value), keys hidden (D152); never what was typed. Idle, the line says **Ducter 的浏览器 · 在 {device} 上**. Presence only: never logged. (3) **换我来 / My turn · 还给它 / Hand it back:** whose turn it is belongs to the browser (the profile), and only one window holds the user's. My turn: the page takes the viewer's size, every task on that browser is blind as during a sign-in (D151's rule, one message for both: it ends its turn and waits), and a band says it plainly: **现在你在用。它看不见这个页面，也不会动它。 / It's your turn. It can't see this page or touch it.** In the user's turn: taps, drags-as-scroll, keys, IME text (committed text only), back, forward, reload, and the address line (tap the host; http and https only, checked on the client and again on the home). Hand it back (or closing the view, or the socket going away: a task is never left waiting) restores the page's own size and tells the task, only when it needs to hear it: it tried while it was the user's turn, or the page moved ("…Your tab went from A to B while they had it. browser_read before your next step"); a finished task is never woken. Another window of the same user sees **你正在另一台设备上用它** and can take the turn itself. A sign-in (D151) and Try it (D154) are the user's turn from the start and keep their Done / Not now. Sites the user signed in to in their own turn (the site has more cookies than before) join 已登录的网站. (4) **The stream:** CDP screencast JPEG, one frame in flight until the client acks it, at most 8 a second (kept from D151). Chosen over periodic screenshots: frames come only when the page changes, so a still page costs nothing, and the ack paces a slow phone link to fewer frames, never a queue. The quality steps down (q70 → 55 → 45 at ¾ size → 35 at ½) when acks come back slower than 0.7 s on average, and back up after 20 quick ones; frames are sized to the page area the client measures (rotation, a fold, the turn's band), at most 2×; a still page that sends nothing gets one picture of itself half a second after the stream starts. Watching on a small screen, a double tap zooms 2.5× where it lands (frames sharpen to match) and pans; again, back. Out of sight (the app in the background, a hidden window) the client asks for no frames (`live_pause`); a new socket reopens the view that is on screen. (5) **Safety:** frames go only to the socket that opened the view, over its own authenticated WebSocket (paired devices of this home only; a CLI is refused); nothing of a view is written to disk or logged; input goes through the home, only in the user's turn and only from the window holding it; URLs shown are redacted (D152). (6) **Autofill (D191) keeps the turns:** its `browser_fill` refuses in the user's turn like every browser tool; filling never shows a value on the watcher's line. Runner-up for (3): pause the task's turn by steering a message into it at My turn, rejected (it wakes an idle task for nothing; the refusal already tells a task that tries). ## 2026-11-11: A task's working copy you can find and open (the author, doing their own development beside Ducter's tasks; decided "按建议")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D198 | **A task's working copy is named so a person can tell it apart, and the opened task opens it in Finder, a Terminal or an editor.** *Who:* a heavy developer stepping in to edit a task's code by hand. *Feel:* before, hunting through a hidden folder of opaque ids (`~/.ducter/worktrees/t_8f3a…`), nothing in an editor's recent list saying which task it was; after, one click from the task into their editor at the right worktree, the path readable everywhere it shows. *First time:* an opened task on the Mac has **打开** at its top right; the menu says the three things in macOS's own words, nothing to learn. (1) **The path.** A new repo task's worktree is `<dataDir>/worktrees/<repo name>/<branch slug>`: the slug is the branch's part after `ducter/` (a branch named another way keeps its words, any character outside `[A-Za-z0-9._-]` as `-`), and a path already on disk, or planned in this process and not yet made, gets `-2`, `-3`. Still under `<dataDir>/worktrees`, so archive (`archiveWorktree`) removes only what Ducter made, at any depth, and then the repo's folder once it is empty; uninstall counts and warns about worktrees at both depths. **Tasks from before keep their stored path** (`worktrees/<taskId>`); nothing is moved. A branch name that is taken still gets `-<taskId>` (unchanged), so its folder does too. (2) **打开 / Open** in the opened task's header, in every state (a finished task is when one reviews it): a menu of **在 Finder 中显示 / Show in Finder** (`open -R`), **在终端打开 / Open in Terminal** (`open -a Terminal`), and **用 {editor} 打开 / Open in {editor}** (`open -a <app>`) for each of Cursor, Visual Studio Code and Zed installed (found in /Applications or ~/Applications, else by bundle id through Spotlight; the one Spotlight says was used last comes first, else that order). A folder task (D142) opens the folder itself. Shown only while the working copy is **on this Mac**: the place is the home's and the home runs here (the same `machine` check as the diff, system.md "Whose paths"), or the place's computer is this Mac's own worker (its device id in `worker.json`, else the computer it named, D187); and only while the folder is there (an archived task's worktree is gone). The app does the opening through an injected `OpenFolder`, so the views stay pure. A failure is one toast, **没能打开：{why} / Couldn't open it: {why}**. (3) **The path over the changes**: `~/…`, mono, small, muted and selectable, on every repo or folder task, remote ones too (it is useful to copy, e.g. over ssh). The phone and web show no menu (they aren't on that computer) and, for now, no path. Alternative considered: a stable per-task symlink tree (`~/Ducter/<project>/<title>`) beside id-named folders; rejected: two names for one folder, and editors resolve symlinks to the opaque path anyway. | U (2026-11-11) | `packages/runner/src/{places,integrate}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/uninstall.ts`, `packages/soak/src/run.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/openin.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/local/editors.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/openin.go`, `apps/desktop/main.go`, `design/{product-model,system,ui}.md`, `docs/{getting-started,faq}.md` |

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D199 | **Built 2026-11-11. 打电话 / Calls is a chip on the home's character sheet again, and real; 用电脑 and 用手机 come back as ghosts that say 以后会有.** Since D161 the clients hid every `later` ability, so calls (built by D193, but only reachable from Settings › 账号) and the abilities still to come disappeared from 你的设备 (D43: an ability is shown where it lives). (1) **Where calls live:** a call runs on Tuwa's line, not on a computer, so 打电话 is the home's only (it stands for Ducter itself); workers no longer carry `call` at all. (2) **Its state is the calling connection's** (`callAbility`, core `devices.ts`): `on` while one is connected, `on` + `signed_out` when its refresh is refused as gone (D193's 需要重新登录; the broker now tells its listeners, so the card changes at once), `off` before one; phone.ts tells the devices view when the calling connection comes or goes. (3) **No second flow:** **+ 打电话** opens Settings › 账号 and sends the same `connection_add` to Tuwa as the offer there (the device sign-in shows in place); on, a tap opens 账号 at the phone card; signed out, the failed tint says **需要重新登录 / Sign in again** and goes to the card that does it. The core refuses an `ability` request for `call`. The isles' dock routes its call slot the same way. (4) **ⓘ** says what and where without claiming it is free: **替你打电话，走 Tuwa 的线路。每一通都先给你看要说什么，你点了打过去才打；Tuwa 按分钟收费。 / Makes calls for you over Tuwa's line. Each call shows you what will be said first and goes out only when you tap Call. Tuwa charges by the minute.** plus **在设置 › 账号里连上和管理。/ Connected and managed in Settings › Accounts.** (5) **Ghosts:** on the home's sheet only, after the live chips, each `later` ability is a flat sunken chip with its existing `_later` clay (`ab_computer_later`, `ab_phone_later`; the web gets them as WebP from the same art, no new metaphor), its name and a faint **以后会有 / coming later**; it never sinks, squishes or adds, a tap opens its ⓘ. Another computer's card shows neither calls nor ghosts (less noise where they don't apply). *Alt:* a separate "coming later" row under the chips (clearer separation, one more line on every home card). | U | `packages/core/src/{devices,phone,connections,core}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{index,phone}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{sheet.ts,devcard.tsx,controls.tsx,app.ts,phone.tsx}`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{devicecard,abilities}.go`, `design/ui-devices-card.md` §3.4, `design/phone.md` §1, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/calls.md` |

## 2026-11-11: An app newer than its home says so (the author's iPad: the app was 0.0.9 or later, its home older, so it showed the phone layout and the new things were missing with nothing saying why; decided from first principles)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D200 | **Built 2026-11-11, for the author's review on real devices. When the iPhone/iPad or Android app is paired with a home that runs an older Ducter than the app, the app itself says so in one quiet native line, with 去更新 / Update it and 知道了 / Got it.** (1) **Who, what they feel, how it starts.** Who: anyone whose phone app updated itself (TestFlight, the Android self-update, D181) before their home did; the heavy developer with a VPS home first, the consumer user with a Mac home next. Before: an iPad that looks like a phone and promised features missing, with no reason given, so the app feels broken. After: one sentence names the cause and the one tap that fixes it, so the gap reads as a known, small thing (放心). First time: it simply appears at the bottom over the page after the page has loaded; nothing to set up or learn. (2) **Why native (D178 (5)):** the shell shows the home's own page, so an older home means an older page, which can't know what it lacks. Only the shell knows both versions. (3) **Where the version comes from:** `GET <home>/api/me` with the paired device token (the token the page already hands the app in `hello`; iOS keeps it in the Keychain as before, Android holds it in memory only), which now carries `version`. A home that answers 200 with no `version` is from before D200 and so older than any app that asks; the line then leaves the number out. Not `/health`: off-machine it says only `{ok}`, by design. (4) **The rule** (`HomeVersion`, the same in both apps and checked against the same cases): compare major.minor.patch numerically, ignoring a pre-release (an App Store build is digits only: 0.2.0-beta.1 ships as 0.2.0); shown only when the home's is lower; never for an app that isn't a real version (0.0.0, CI; a local Xcode build stays quiet unless launched with `-appVersion`). A home newer than the app is not this decision (TestFlight and D181 handle it). (5) **Words:** **{home} 上的 Ducter 还是 {v}，比这个 App 旧，有些新东西要等它更新。/ Ducter on {home} is still {v}, older than this app; some new things wait for it to update.** (without a version: **{home} 上的 Ducter 比这个 App 旧，有些新东西要等它更新。/ Ducter on {home} is older than this app; some new things wait for it to update.**) · **去更新 / Update it** · **知道了 / Got it**. {home} is the home's address, as the can't-reach screen names it. (6) **去更新** opens the page at `app#settings=devices` (Settings › Your devices, where the home's own card has 更新: a server updates itself, D148/D167; a Mac through D187). A hash route rather than a new bridge message, because the page has understood it since D150 (v0.0.4), so even an older page goes there; an even older page stays on the conversation, harmless. (7) **知道了** holds for that pair of versions on that home (the shell's own storage: UserDefaults / SharedPreferences); a new pair (the home updated but still behind, or the app updated again) shows it once more. The check runs after the page's `hello` and when the app comes back to the foreground; any failure is silent (can't reach is its own screen). (8) **Look:** the Android update card's form (Screens.kt `UpdateCard`, Pebble tokens) at the bottom, one phone-wide column on an iPad; on Android the app's own update card speaks first and this line waits behind it. | U (2026-11-11, decided from first principles) | system.md (Updating in place), android.md, ui.md, product-model.md (glossary), scenarios.md (H4), docs/phone.md, `apps/ios/Ducter/HomeVersion.swift` + `HomeOlder.swift`, `apps/android` `Core.kt` HomeVersion + MainActivity, `packages/core/src/web.ts` |

## 2026-11-11: A folder on your Mac, for a home on a server (the author's setup: the home on hel, "Hetzner Server"; the MacBook "Macbook Pro M3" does tasks for it, D155; the code lives on the Mac, and the New task card could neither add a Mac folder nor offer its branches)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D197 | **加一个文件夹 adds a folder on this Mac when the home is elsewhere and this Mac does tasks for it; the home reads the folder through the Mac's worker; a repo on a worker offers its branches; the list names the computer only when its places span two.** (1) **Who and how it starts:** the heavy multi-agent developer whose Ducter lives on a server and whose repos live on the laptop (the author); nothing new to learn: the same 加一个文件夹… opens the same macOS picker. Before: the row was missing and a Mac repo had no branch pill, so the Conductor move (D186: add each repo once) was impossible on a server home. After: one pick, and the card is at that repo with its branch. (2) **When it shows:** the window's Ducter runs on this Mac: as D186. It runs elsewhere and this Mac does tasks for it (`MacWorker.On()`, the worker's device id in `worker.json`, as D187 matches this Mac): the row works and `project_add` carries `device: <that id>`. It runs elsewhere and this Mac doesn't: the row shows greyed with the one step, in the setting's own words: **在设置 › 住在哪儿打开「让这台 Mac 也替 {home} 做事」，就能加这台 Mac 上的文件夹。/ To add a folder on this Mac, turn on "Let this Mac do tasks for {home}" in Settings › Where it lives.** (greyed, not hidden: the reason the folder can't be added is the thing to fix, and saying it once beats a missing row). The phone and the browser list these places but still don't add (no picker). (3) **The home checks the folder there:** a read-only worker op `folderInfo {path}` → `{exists, kind: repo\|folder, root, name, defaultBranch?, branches?}`; a folder inside a repo is the repo (D186). The git reads are one module shared by the home and the worker (`packages/protocol/src/folder-info.ts`). The project is kept with `place.device` = the worker's id; the same path on two computers is two projects. The worker away: **那台设备现在连不上。/ That device can't be reached right now.** (the existing `newtask.err.offline`); a worker too old to answer says to update it. (4) **Branches for a worker's repo:** the card asks that worker's `folderInfo` for each repo place on it, in parallel with the home's own reads, and waits at most 3 s; away or slow, the place is listed without branches, as before. (5) **Which computer:** only when the listed places span more than one computer, each row's second line is its path then **· {computer}** (the device's name as Devices shows it; the home's own name for its places). Chosen over group headers: the list stays in one order, most recent first, so the folder used a minute ago stays on top whichever computer it is on, and a list of 3–8 rows gains no chrome; one computer, nothing added. Paths: **~** is the home folder of the computer the path is on, read from the path itself (`/Users/<name>`, `/home/<name>`), never from this Mac's own home; the Mac app and the web sheet use the one rule. Alternative: a path field on the server with its folders suggested (D186's todo default; kept for a home whose folders are on the server itself, the trigger unchanged). | U (2026-11-11), L | `packages/protocol/src/{index,folder-info}.ts` (`project_add.device`, `project_add_result` reason `offline`, `PlaceChoice.computer`), `packages/runner/src/worker-host.ts` (`folderInfo`), `packages/core/src/{projects,newtask,core,ws}.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/protocol.go,views/newtask.go,i18n/starts.go}`, `apps/web/src/{newtask.tsx,i18n-starts.ts}`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/new-task.md`, `docs/faq.md`, `design/todo.md` |

## 2026-11-11: A task's folder and branch in its own words, and the terminals you use (the author: a Chinese title gave `task`, `task-2`; 「在终端打开」 offered only macOS Terminal)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D201 | **Built 2026-11-11. A task's branch and folder keep its title's own words in their own script, and 打开 / Open offers every terminal installed, as it offers editors.** *Who:* the heavy developer who writes briefs in Chinese (the author) and lives in iTerm2, Ghostty, Warp or con, not Terminal. *Feel:* before, a column of `task`, `task-2`, `task-3` in Finder and the editor's recents, and a Terminal window they never use; after, `修复登录页的闪退` in every place the folder shows, and the task opens in their own terminal. *First time:* nothing to learn; the folder simply reads as what they wrote, and the menu lists what is on their Mac. (1) **The slug (one function, `nameSegment` in `packages/runner/src/places.ts`; `slugify` for a title, the folder's `segment` for a branch).** The folder exists so a person recognises it on their own disk, and the task's words in the language they were written are the most recognisable; APFS, ext4 and git all take Unicode. Letters, marks and digits of any script stay (`\p{L}\p{M}\p{N}`: marks too, or Devanagari and Thai lose their vowel signs); every run of anything else is one `-` (a folder segment also keeps `.` and `_`, as D198). NFC first (how macOS stores names); never starts with `-`, `.` or a mark, never ends with `-` or `.`; at most 40 characters for a title (80 for a branch someone named), cut between graphemes (never a split surrogate pair or accent) and within 200 UTF-8 bytes (a file name holds 255); only ASCII letters are lowercased (`Починить-вход` keeps its case: lowercasing another script changes how it reads); `task` only when nothing is left (an emoji-only or punctuation-only title). An ASCII title gives exactly the slug it gave before. **The branch `ducter/<slug>` is the same slug**: every slug of letters, digits and `-` is a valid ref (tested with `git check-ref-format` over CJK, kana, Hangul, Cyrillic, accented Latin, Devanagari, CJK Extension B, emoji). A taken folder is compared NFC too (APFS folds case and form by itself; ext4 doesn't), so two spellings of one title never get two folders that look alike. The branch and the folder reach git, `git push` and `gh pr create` only as their own arguments, never through a shell (tested with a Chinese branch pushed to a remote and handed to gh). Tasks from before keep their stored branch and path. (2) **Terminals, found like editors** (`apps/desktop/internal/local/editors.go`: in /Applications, ~/Applications, /Applications/Utilities or /System/Applications/Utilities, else by bundle id through Spotlight; the one Spotlight says was used last first, else the order below). Each, with how it opens a new window at the folder and how sure: **Terminal** `com.apple.Terminal`, `open -a Terminal <dir>`, VERIFIED (a shell started in a scratch folder named with a space and Chinese, on this Mac); **iTerm2** `com.googlecode.iterm2` (`iTerm.app`), `open -a <app> <dir>`, VERIFIED the same way; **Ghostty** `com.mitchellh.ghostty`, `open -a <app> <dir>` (its Info.plist claims `public.directory`), VERIFIED the same way; **Warp** `dev.warp.Warp-Stable`, its URI `warp://action/new_window?path=<dir>` (percent-encoded, slashes kept) through `open -a <app>`, DOCUMENTED (docs.warp.dev, URI scheme; not installed here, so not run); **Rex** by Superlogical, `com.superlogical.rex` (INFERRED from its Application Support and Preferences paths in a hands-on review; Rex for Mac is a tester beta, `Rex.app` or `Rex Beta.app`), and **con** (`co.nowledge.con`, `.beta`, `.dev`; `con.app`, `con Beta.app`, `con Dev.app`; VERIFIED from the con-terminal repo's `scripts/macos/common.sh` and the installed con Beta's Info.plist): **neither has a way to open a folder on macOS today** (con: no `CFBundleDocumentTypes`, and its `--working-directory` startup flag is parsed on Linux only; Rex: its docs give `rex new --cwd` for a session, nothing for the Mac app's window). So both are offered only while the installed app's Info.plist claims folders, through `open -a`: they appear by themselves the day they do, and until then the menu never has an item that opens the app somewhere else (放心: every item does what it says). macOS's Terminal is offered even when detection finds nothing. (3) **The menu**, calm, in two groups: **在 Finder 中显示 / Show in Finder** · — · **用 {editor} 打开 / Open in {editor}** for each editor · — · **在 {terminal} 里打开 / Open in {terminal}** for each terminal, Terminal itself **在终端里打开 / Open in Terminal**; every one installed, the one used last first in each group; a terminal's name is its .app's (iTerm2 by its product name). Alternative considered: one "Open in Terminal" item opening the default terminal (macOS has no default-terminal setting to read; picking one for the person is a guess). | U (2026-11-11, decided "按建议") | `packages/runner/src/places.ts`, `packages/runner/test/{slug,integrate}.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/local/editors.go` (+ test), `apps/desktop/internal/views/{openin,app}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/openin.go`, `apps/desktop/main.go`, `design/{product-model,system,ui,todo}.md`, `docs/{getting-started,faq}.md` |

## 2026-11-11: Who does it, on every computer; its model and how hard it thinks (the author: a home on the server hel with Codex only, Claude Code on the Mac that works for it, and New task offered neither Claude Code nor a model nor an effort)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D202 | **Built 2026-11-11. 谁来做 / Who does it lists every AI agent on every computer, one row each; an agent on another computer than the folder's is offered there; under the chosen agent, its 模型 / Model and 思考深度 / Effort.** *Who:* the heavy multi-agent developer whose home is a server and whose Mac does tasks for it (the author); also anyone with two computers. *Feel:* before, Claude Code on their Mac silently missing from the list when the folder was on the server, and no way to say "Sonnet, think hard" without the partner; after, every agent they have is there, the one on the Mac says **在 Macbook Pro M3 上**, and the pill reads **Claude Code · Opus 5.5 · 高**. *First time:* nothing to learn: the defaults are already right (Ducter's suggestion, the model's own default effort), and the rows only show under the agent they opened. (1) **Every agent, one row each** (`fwCandidates` with `every`: no caps; the clients keep each agent's best row per computer): those that can at the chosen computer first, then each agent that can't there but can on another computer (core sends it `blocked: "elsewhere"` for a folder; for No folder it is simply another computer's), tagged **在 {computer} 上 / on {computer}**, then the ones that can't anywhere, greyed with why as before (an agent available elsewhere replaces its greyed row here). The top candidate and **推荐 / Suggested** stay D157's (never an agent on another computer by itself). (2) **Picking one on another computer** brings its computer along: No folder moves there (D157); a repo switches to **its copy on that computer** when the places list one, and keeps the branch when that copy has it (the row's helper line: **会换到 {computer} 上的 {folder} / Switches to {folder} on {computer}**); else the folder list opens on that computer's places only, headed **文件夹要在 {computer} 上 / The folder must be on {computer}**, with **不用文件夹** on that computer and, where this client can add one there (D197), **加一个文件夹…**; picking one there picks the agent. **Same repo = same origin**: `PlaceChoice.remote` and `folderInfo.remote` carry `git remote get-url origin` normalized to host/owner/repo (scheme, user, port, `.git` and case dropped), and two places match when both know an origin and they are equal; when **neither** knows one, the exact folder name matches; one known and one not never matches (a fork or a different repo of the same name must not be mistaken for it). A plain folder is never matched by name. (3) **Model**: the account's own live list on that computer, read token-free through OAR `listModels` (Claude Code's `list_models` control request, Codex's `model/list`) in Ducter's own folder, never a project's; the home's through the runner's harness port (`HarnessPort.models`), a worker's through its new `models` op; kept 10 minutes; the card waits at most 3 s (alongside the branches) and otherwise shows Ducter's tier table without effort levels, the list arriving for the next card. Claude Code's row is its named models (`opus`, `fable`, `sonnet`, `haiku`; its pinned versions and its `default` alias stay out: todo); every other agent's is its list, at most 8. **Ducter's suggestion** is the tier table's model (it stays the ranking source), else the model the agent runs by default (Codex's `default`), tagged **推荐**. (4) **Effort**: the chosen model's own levels, the row hidden when it lists none; the model's own default tagged **默认 / Default** (Codex lists one; Claude Code doesn't, so its row starts with a **默认** choice that sends nothing). Words: **none 不思考 / None · minimal 最少 / Minimal · low 低 / Low · medium 中 / Medium · high 高 / High · xhigh 很高 / Extra high · max 最高 / Max · ultra 极限 / Ultra**; a level Ducter has no word for shows as the agent says it. The last model and effort are remembered **per agent, per client** (the web's localStorage `ducter.newtask.tune`, the Mac's prefs `newTaskTune`), like the last agent (D185). (5) **The wire**: `task_new_options` adds `models: AgentModels[]` (`{harness, device?, from: live|tiers, models: ModelChoice[]}`); `task_new` adds `effort`; the client sends `model` and `effort` only when they differ from the suggestion and the model's default (and then `harness` too). Core checks the effort against the chosen model **on the computer the task goes to** (the loadout's pick, waiting up to 10 s for a list it hasn't read); one the model doesn't offer, or that can't be checked, is **dropped, never sent**, with one line in the task (`effort x was left out: …`) and the server's log. Recorded on `task_open`, `body_selected`, the card (`effort`) and a routine of the user's own; passed to OAR `SessionOptions.effort` on open and **on every resume** (Claude Code keeps no effort per session); a switch to another agent or model drops it, a move keeps it with the same agent and model. Shown wherever the model is: the opened task's who line and its footer (**Claude Code · Sonnet · 高**). CLI: `ducter task --agent claude-code/opus --effort high`. (6) Unchanged: the partner's tools and persona (a task the partner opens keeps its current pick, D175's eval untouched), the switch sheet (换谁来做) and its caps. Alternative considered: a flat list of every (agent, model) row as before (D185): with live lists that is 14 rows for Claude Code alone, and the model is a property of who, not another who. | U (2026-11-11, "按建议 做全") | `packages/protocol/src/{index,internal,loadout,folder-info,harness-facts}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{models,newtask,loadout,candidates,registry,routines,ws,core}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{oar,runner,worker-host}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{args,main,talk,i18n}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{app,newtask,controls,store,views,transcript,i18n}.ts(x)`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,store,hints,i18n}`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{newtask,newtasktune,newtaskwhere,shared,app}.go`, tests in `packages/{core,runner,cli}/test`, `apps/web/test/tune.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/newtasktune_test.go`, `design/{new-task,where,product-model,ui,todo}.md`, `docs/{faq,devices}.md` |
| D203 | **Built 2026-11-11. Every computer says which repos it has, and New task can find a folder on any computer of the home, from the phone too.** *Who:* the author on their phone, whose home is the server hel and whose Mac does tasks for it; anyone whose home isn't the computer they code on. *Feel:* before, picking Claude Code on the Mac for hel's ducter opened the Mac's folder list with nothing in it but 不用文件夹, a dead end (the home only knew the Mac's folders added from the Mac app or used before, D197); after, the card switches to the Mac's ducter by itself, and when the Mac has no copy, its list shows the repos it does have and **找一个文件夹… / Find a folder…**. *First time:* nothing to set up: the Mac's repos are simply there, each saying **· Macbook Pro M3**. (1) **A computer says which repos it has.** One scan (`protocol/src/repos.ts` `findRepos`, moved from the CLI's first-run hints, which keep using it): git checkouts up to two levels under `~/dev`, `~/code`, `~/src`, `~/Projects`, `~/projects`, `~/work`, `~/repos`, `~/git`, hidden folders and build folders skipped, most recently touched first, **at most 20** (`REPOS_REPORTED`); with each one's origin (normalized as D202) and default branch read **from its files** (`.git/config`, `.git/HEAD`, the origin's HEAD, `refs/heads`, `packed-refs`), no git process. A worker answers the home's new **`repos`** op with it; the home scans its own (main.ts; core takes it as `homeRepos`). Kept **10 minutes** per computer (`REPOS_KEEP_MS`); the card waits for it at most WORKER_BRANCHES_MS (3 s, alongside the branches and models) and a slower answer lands for the next card; a worker away, or too old to know the op, reports none. (2) **The card lists them** after the folders tasks used and the projects added (the first-run hints too), deduped by computer and path, as `PlaceChoice.found`, with `computer`, `remote` and `defaultBranch`; their branches are read only for the one asked about (`task_new_options` with that place), so picking one reads its branches then. The clients show the used and added folders and then the found ones **up to 12 rows**, and **all** of one computer's while an agent waits for a folder there. D202's `ntCopyOn` now finds the Mac's reported copy of a hel repo by its origin; the branch picked is kept once that copy says it has it. (3) **找一个文件夹… / Find a folder…**: a row at the foot of the folder list, one per computer it can look through (**在 {computer} 上 / on {computer}** under it when there are two): the home and each reachable worker on the phone and in the browser; on the Mac every one but this Mac, which keeps macOS's own **加一个文件夹…**. It opens a small browser in place of the list: **{computer} 上的文件夹 / Folders on {computer}** and the path as ~/…, **上一层 / Up one level**, the subfolders with repos first (the branch glyph) then by name, tap to go in; **用这个文件夹 / Use this folder** (not at ~ itself) sends `project_add` with that computer (D186, D197), so it is remembered and listed next time, makes it the card's place and picks the agent that waited for a folder there; **返回 / Back** returns to the list. Lines: **这里面没有文件夹。/ No folders in here.**, **只列出了前 {n} 个。/ Showing the first {n}.**, **{computer} 现在连不上。/ Can't reach {computer} right now.**, **只能选主文件夹（~）里面的。/ Only folders inside the home folder (~).**, **这个文件夹不在了。/ That folder isn't there anymore.**, **在加… / Adding…**. (4) **Safety of the listing**: the new worker op **`browse`** and the home's own run one function (`listFolders`): only under that computer's home folder, the asked path resolved (`..` and links) before it is checked, a link that leads out of home left out, hidden folders skipped, **folders only, never a file name**, at most **200** (`more` past that). Read-only. (5) **The dead end**: while an agent waits for a folder on a computer with none listed, the list says **还没有 {computer} 上的文件夹。/ No folders on {computer} yet.** and offers Find a folder… above 不用文件夹, never 不用文件夹 alone. (6) **Wire**: `{t:"folder_browse", device?, path?, clientId?}` → `{t:"folder_browse_result", ok, clientId, device, path, home, parent?, folders: {name, path, repo?}[], more?}` or `{ok:false, reason: outside\|not_found\|offline\|failed, message}`; `PlaceChoice.found`. Worker ops `repos` and `browse`. Unchanged: who is suggested (D157, D202), the partner's tools. Alternatives considered: the worker pushing its repos in its hello (stale for a laptop that lives for weeks, and a hello is not the place for a list of 20); a full folder picker on the phone (a file system tree on a phone is the wrong shape; one level at a time with repos first is what the user needs to find a checkout); scanning every folder under ~ (slow on a big disk and reads places the user didn't mean). | U (2026-11-11, "按建议 做全") | `packages/protocol/src/{repos,index}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/firstrun.ts`, `packages/runner/src/worker-host.ts`, `packages/core/src/{newtask,core,ws,main}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{app,newtask,i18n-starts}.ts(x)`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-find.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,store,i18n}`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{newtask,newtasktune,newtaskfind}.go`, tests `packages/runner/test/worker-repos.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/found-repos.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/find-folder.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/newtaskfind_test.go`, `design/{new-task,where,product-model,todo}.md`, `docs/devices.md` |

## 2026-11-11: One Ducter folder per computer, and projects from a Git address (the author: "路径不能假设 dev code src 这种吧，我觉得最好是 ducter 这种的 一个就好 类似 conductor")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D204 | **Built 2026-11-11. Each computer has one visible Ducter folder, `~/ducter`, like Conductor's `~/conductor`: the projects Ducter cloned in `projects/<repo>`, new tasks' working copies in `worktrees/<repo>/<branch slug>`. Nothing guesses where people keep code; 从 Git 地址加… / Add from a Git address… clones onto any computer, from any client.** *Who:* every developer, and the author first: their code lives under folder names Ducter can't know (D203 guessed `~/dev`, `~/code`, …), and their phone had no way to put a repo on the Mac. *Feel:* before, a list of whatever sat under the folders Ducter guessed, and hidden worktrees in `~/.ducter`; after, one folder they can open in Finder that holds everything Ducter made, and a repo on any computer is one pasted address away. *First time:* the empty list says **还没有文件夹。/ No folders yet.** and offers the ways to get one (this Mac's own picker, 找一个文件夹…, 从 Git 地址加…, then 不用文件夹); nothing to set up. (1) **The folder** (`protocol/src/ducter-folder.ts` `ducterFolder`, one rule for the home, a worker, the runner and uninstall), in order: **`$DUCTER_FOLDER`** when set (`~/…` allowed), exactly that; else a Ducter with **a data folder of its own** (not the default `~/.ducter`: tests, the soak, a second Ducter on one computer) keeps it inside that data folder, `<dataDir>/ducter`, so it never touches the user's `~/ducter`; else the first of **`~/ducter`, `~/ducter-work`, `~/ducter-work-2` … `-9`** that carries Ducter's marker **`.ducter-folder`**, and when none does, the first that is missing or holds nothing but `projects/` and `worktrees/` (empty counts). **A `~/ducter` that holds anything else and no marker is someone else's** (a clone of the ducter repo, say) and is never touched: Ducter uses `~/ducter-work`, and keeps using the folder it marked even if `~/ducter` goes later. Resolving only reads; the folder and its marker (a two-line note saying what it is) are made on first use (a clone, a task's worktree). The real path is what the clients show (the field's **会放在 {path} / It goes in {path}**). A settings field for it: not now (todo); the env is enough until someone asks. (2) **No scanning anywhere.** D203's scan of `~/dev`, `~/code`, `~/src`, `~/Projects`, `~/projects`, `~/work`, `~/repos`, `~/git` is gone (`findRepos`, `REPO_ROOTS`), and so are `ducter up`'s first-run repo hints (`firstrun.json`: nothing read them but New task; `ducter up` now removes a stale one; the Mac app's `hints` package no longer reads it). A computer's projects are **the git repos directly in its `<Ducter folder>/projects`** (`projectRepos`, most recently touched first, at most 50, origin and default branch read from their files as D203 did); the worker op keeps its name `repos` and answers `{folder, repos}` (D203's bare list is still read). New task's folder list is exactly: the folders tasks used and the projects added (D186, D197: the Mac picker, 找一个文件夹…), then those projects; 找一个文件夹… stays (D203). (3) **从 Git 地址加… / Add from a Git address…**: a row under 找一个文件夹… for each computer that can do it (the home and each reachable worker; on the Mac this Mac too, since it is git, not a picker; **在 {computer} 上** under it when there are two), opening a field in place of the list: **从 Git 地址加到 {computer} / Add from a Git address on {computer}**, the address (placeholder **https://…、git@…:主人/仓库，或 主人/仓库 / https://…, git@…:owner/repo, or owner/repo**), **会放在 {path}，用 {computer} 自己的 git 登录克隆。/ It goes in {path}, cloned with {computer}'s own git sign-in.**, **返回 / Back** and **克隆 / Clone**; while it runs **在克隆…大的仓库要几分钟。/ Cloning… A big repository takes a few minutes.** with a spinner and the button **在克隆… / Cloning…**. Wire `{t:"project_clone", address, device?, clientId}` → `{t:"project_clone_result", ok, clientId, device?, place?, existed?, reason?, message?}`; a worker answers a new op **`clone {address}`**; the home waits up to 11 minutes. That computer clones into `<folder>/projects/<repo>`; **the same origin already there is simply that project** (`existed`, no second clone); a folder of that name holding another repo makes `<repo>-2`, `-3`. Done, the home adds it (`project_add`, D186) and the card picks it, and the agent that waited for a folder there (D202, D203). A resend with the same clientId (the phone's socket came back) waits for the same clone. **Safety** (`protocol/src/clone.ts`): only the address comes from a client, checked strictly before git sees it on the computer that clones (`parseGitAddress`, again on the worker): `https://host[:port]/path`, `ssh://[user@]host[:port]/path`, scp-like `user@host:path` (a user required, so it never reads as a local path), `host.tld/owner/repo` (made https) and GitHub's `owner/repo`; printable ASCII, no spaces or newlines, at most 300 characters, never starting with `-`, no `::` (`ext::`), no `..`, no credentials in the URL, no `file://`, `git://`, plain `http://` or local paths. git runs as `git clone -- <url> <target>` with no other argument, the user's own git settings and sign-in (git's environment as D102 gives it, plus gh's token for a gh credential helper), **`GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`**, **`GIT_ALLOW_PROTOCOL=https:ssh`** (redirects and submodules too) and `ssh -o BatchMode=yes` unless the user set their own `GIT_SSH_COMMAND`, so it fails instead of waiting on a prompt; **10 minutes** at most (`CLONE_TIMEOUT_MS`), then stopped; a failed or stopped clone leaves nothing behind. **Failure lines** (git's words sorted, `cloneReason`): **这不像 Git 地址。试试 https://…、git@…:主人/仓库 或 主人/仓库。/ That doesn't look like a Git address. Try https://…, git@…:owner/repo or owner/repo.** · **找不到这个仓库，或者 {computer} 没有它的权限。/ That repository wasn't found, or {computer} has no access to it.** (GitHub answers "not found" for a private repo it won't show) · **{computer} 没有这个仓库的权限：先在 {computer} 上登录 git，再试一次。/ {computer} has no access to it: sign in to git on {computer}, then try again.** · **{computer} 连不上那个地址。/ {computer} can't reach that address.** · **克隆超过 10 分钟，停下了。/ Cloning took over 10 minutes and was stopped.** · **{computer} 现在连不上。** (away) · **没能克隆：{git's line} / Couldn't clone it: {git's line}**. (4) **Worktrees move for new tasks only**: `worktreeFor` puts a new task's working copy in `<Ducter folder>/worktrees/<repo>/<branch slug>` (D198's naming and D201's slug unchanged; the folder is marked when the first is made). Tasks already running keep their stored path. Archive (`archiveWorktree`) removes a worktree under `<dataDir>/worktrees` (before D204) or under the `worktrees/` of a folder that carries the marker (so a task keeps archiving after `DUCTER_FOLDER` changes), never anything else. **`ducter uninstall --all`** removes `<Ducter folder>/worktrees` (counted, an unsaved one warned, their repos pruned) as well as the data folder, or the whole Ducter folder when nothing but Ducter's is in it, and **never `projects/`**: its listing keeps **Ducter 替你克隆的 {n} 个项目：{path}，是你的：卸载从不删它们 / The {n} projects Ducter cloned for you: {path}, yours: uninstall never removes them**; inside a data folder that goes (a data folder of its own), the projects are spared from the removal. (5) **An older worker says so, in the user's words**: a worker without the new ops answers `repos`, `browse`, `clone` and `folderInfo` with "unknown op"; the home now says **old** (`DucterFolderView.old` on `task_new_options.ducterFolders`, reason `old` on `folder_browse_result`, `project_add_result` and `project_clone_result`) instead of D203's English sentence, and both cards show one greyed row in place of its Find and clone rows: **{computer} 上的 Ducter 需要更新 / Ducter on {computer} needs an update** with **到「设备」里更新它，就能在那儿找文件夹、从 Git 地址加。/ Update it in Devices to find folders or add from a Git address there.**; a failed look or add there says **{computer} 上的 Ducter 需要更新，才能这样做。/ Ducter on {computer} needs an update for this.** (6) **The phone and the Mac card behave the same** in the situations reviewed (table in `design/new-task.md`): hel + the Mac, this Mac alone, a worker away, an older worker, a first run with no folders. Alternatives considered: keep scanning, with a setting for which folders (still a guess the user must correct, and it reads folders they didn't offer); the hidden `~/.ducter/worktrees` with a visible link folder (two names for one folder, as D198 found); cloning from the client (a phone has no git, and the credentials belong on the computer that holds the code). | U (2026-11-11, "按建议") | `packages/protocol/src/{ducter-folder,clone,repos,index}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{places,integrate,worker-host}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{newtask,core,ws,projects,main}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{firstrun,main,uninstall,i18n}.ts`, `packages/soak/src/run.ts`, `apps/web/src/{app,newtask,i18n-starts}.ts(x)`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-clone.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,store,hints,i18n}`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{newtask,newtaskfind,newtaskclone,app}.go`, tests `packages/protocol/test/ducter-folder.test.ts`, `packages/runner/test/{worker-repos,integrate,slug,runner}.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/{found-repos,new-task}.test.ts`, `packages/cli/test/{uninstall,firstrun}.test.ts`, `apps/web/test/clone-folder.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/newtaskclone_test.go`, `design/{new-task,where,product-model,system,build-and-run,onboarding,todo,scenarios}.md`, `design/flows/A-setup-devices.md`, `docs/{getting-started,faq,devices}.md` |

## 2026-11-11: The persona connects itself, with one tap (Mem PR #9996, "existing-role connection"; the author: "bridge 是有开关的对吧？在 ducter 里")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D205 | **Built 2026-11-11, behind a capability check: with nmem 0.10.118 or later on the home, Settings › 伙伴 › 你的其他 AI connects the persona to the user's other AI agents in one tap, and that connection turns Bridge on without `DUCTER_BRIDGE=1`.** *Who:* the heavy multi-agent developer (the author first, D171's refusal); the consumer and fresh user never see it until Mem is in and connectable. *Feel:* before, a 403 and three commands only the author could run; after, one tap that says exactly what it does, and a plain line with the step in Mem when Mem says no. (1) **What changed in Mem** (DOCUMENTED, nowledge-co/mem#9996, tag v0.10.118-rc2, Cloud side deployed; not in a public nmem yet): an ordinary Mem login may represent an existing identity it holds, `nmem bridge connections connect --space-id <id> --agent-id <agent> [--context <name>]`, with `connections list --space-id <id> [--agent-id]` and `connections revoke <connection id>`, and four registered 403 codes for own-role prerequisites. Interfaces only in `research/private/37b` (closed source). (2) **Ducter finds the persona's context itself** (closes D170 (9)'s first criterion): `nmem --version` ≥ 0.10.118 (a pre-release counts) or `bridge connections --help` exits 0, cached per version; passive `bridge setup` (no context, no Space, never `--enable`) lists the user's places, and exactly one with `kind: "personal"` ("Only me", D171) is used by its exact id, none or two: nothing is offered (**no_space**). The identity is the persona's Mem identity (D141, `ducter`); the context is that name (`DUCTER_BRIDGE_CONTEXT` overrides it). (3) **On the user's tap only**, Ducter runs `nmem --json bridge connections connect --space-id <that id> --agent-id <identity> --context <ctx>`, checks the answer (`state: connected`, the same `agent_id`, `state: active`, the same Space), then passive `--agent-context <ctx> bridge setup` to confirm it acts as that identity, and keeps `<dataDir>/bridge-connection.json`. Unconfirmed: the row says so and Bridge stays off. Once per start, `connections list` drops a connection revoked elsewhere. (4) **Refusals, never a fallback:** `cloud_bridge_setup_role_authority_missing`, `_role_custody_birth_missing`, `_role_lifetime_inactive`, `_role_attachment_mismatch` (the four), plus `_role_control_denied`, `_connection_revoked`, `_binding_conflict`, each a plain zh/en line naming the step the user takes in Mem (ask the workspace owner; no new identity or key) and **再试一次 / Try again**; no other identity, place, key, `select-cloud` or `--enable` is tried. (5) **The words** (glossary): the row **{name} 跟你的其他 AI 连上 / Connect {name} with your other AI agents**; ready **用你在这台电脑上的 Nowledge Mem 登录，以「ducter」的身份收发，只在「Only me」里，只有你能看到。** with the literal `ducter · Only me · <id>` under it; **连上 / Connect** · **在连… / Connecting…** · connected **连上了：以「ducter」的身份，在「Only me」里。断开后它不再收，也不再发。** · **断开 / Disconnect**, which asks once (**断开后要再连上，可能得先在 Nowledge Mem 里处理。确定断开？** · **先不 / Not now**: Mem keys a CLI connection by login and identity, so a revoked one may not reconnect by itself, INFERRED) · an older Mem: only **要先在 {home} 上更新 Nowledge Mem，才能跟你的其他 AI 连上。** Never "Bridge", "Space", "mailbox", "contact". (6) **Two levels, both always findable** (the author's follow-up): the section shows whenever the home's Mem can connect the persona or needs updating to, not only after another agent wrote (amends D170 (6)'s J6 gate for Mem users; a user without Mem still never meets it). **断开 / Disconnect** is the whole thing: no checks, nothing sent, the tools gone. **谁能找到 {name} › 谁都不行** is the reach: no other agent reaches the partner, while what the user asks it to send on a card still goes and its answer comes back (checks run only while such a message waits for an answer, at most 7 days; every other message is logged muted; amends D170 (7)'s "no check at all"). Its note says so: **谁都不行：别的 AI 找不到 {name}；你让它发的消息照样能发，回复也会带回来。** (7) **No restart:** the partner's Bridge tools are listed only while it is on, `task_open`/`ask` show `forMessage` only then, and a change starts a fresh session at the next turn. `DUCTER_BRIDGE=1` + `DUCTER_BRIDGE_CONTEXT` still forces it on (tests, the fake, a context made by hand; the row shows it pinned, no 断开); `DUCTER_BRIDGE=0` forces it off. (8) **The live trial:** `bun run bridge:live -- --connected` is a dry, read-only preflight (version, the one personal place, the connection in Mem's list, passive setup); with `--real` it binds the peer's own `claude -p --session-id <id>` natively (`nmem bridge session --host claude-code --session-id <id> prepare`) instead of a second identity with a key, and runs S1, S2, S3 and an X1 round trip, raw output kept as before. (9) **Not decided here:** D170 (9)'s "on by default" still waits for that live run; until then nothing turns on without the user's tap | U (the request; the follow-up "bridge 是有开关的对吧？"), L (Mem #9996, research/37 §8, private/37b) | `packages/core/src/bridge-link.ts`, `packages/core/src/{core,ws,settings,mcp}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{bridge,partner}.ts`, `packages/protocol`, `packages/partner/eval/{fake-bridge-nmem,bridge-live}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{views.tsx,app.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/demo-bridge.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/peers.go,protocol,store,i18n}`, tests, `design/bridge.md`, glossary, `design/todo.md` |
| D209 | **Built 2026-11-11, measured before and after; for the author's review on a real iPad against hel. The apps open on the last screen at once and catch up from the home in the background; a layout change never waits on a frame.** (the author, iPad app, home on hel: "第二栏的全屏点击之后好几秒才全屏", "app 每次一打开的时候数据加载都过几秒，闪烁一下没有数据的界面", and whether a pure web client is good enough engineering). (1) **Who, what they feel, how it starts.** Every user of the phone, iPad and Android apps, most of all the heavy developer whose home is a server abroad: before, each open was a loading screen, then an empty conversation and "Nothing on hand", then the data (a messaging app that forgets your chats every time feels broken, not slow); after, the conversation and On hand are the first frame, exactly as left, and what's new slides in a second later. Nothing to learn or set up. (2) **Full screen took 4.0–4.1 s, all of it a timeout:** D184's morph waited for a frame inside the view transition's update callback, and WebKit holds frames there until its 4 s timeout (headless Chromium, where D184 was checked, gives them). The callback now waits for a timer after Preact's microtask render: the new layout paints in 20–60 ms, the 320 ms morph is done in 0.35–0.41 s. (3) **Opening waited on five round trips in series** (the page, its code and styles revalidated, `/api/me`, the socket, hello → replay) with nothing of its own to show.
