Now: (a) **the last screen is kept on the device** (`glance.ts`: the channel's newest 400 events, On hand, the empty conversation's words, who the device is; localStorage, read synchronously; saved a moment after each change and when the page hides; ≤ 1.5 MB) and painted before the socket opens; hello carries **the cursor it holds**, so the home sends only what's newer (the reconnect replay); a replay as long as the home sends at once (5000) starts over fresh; a revoked device or a Log that started over forgets it; it is kept per device, so another pairing never reads it; until the home answers, the header keeps the line it showed rather than "connecting"; (b) **nothing waits on `/api/me`**: a paired device starts the socket and paints at once, who it is arrives alongside (a 401 still goes to pairing); (c) **code and styles are named by content** (`app.js?v=<hash>`, `immutable`), so a launch never revalidates them; (d) **the apps open the page from their cache** (iOS `returnCacheDataElseLoad`; Android `LOAD_CACHE_ELSE_NETWORK` for that load), only for a home whose page names its code by content; once caught up the page asks for the page again in the background and reloads when it names other code, under the stale-version rule (once per build, nothing lost); the page tells the app whether the home answered (`reach`), so a home it can't reach still brings up the can't-reach screen; (e) on iOS the web view stays laid out under the loading screen and is uncovered once its safe-area insets have arrived (a cached page painted before them and jumped); (f) messages are turned into HTML once, not on every frame. (4) **Measured** (research/57; a scratch home with 160 exchanges and 7 tasks, 200 ms RTT through a proxy): WebKit, the conversation visible at 1487–1722 ms on a warm open → 260–265 ms (the first frame), the empty screen 850–870 ms → none, caught up 1489–1725 → 1032–1039 ms; a first ever open unchanged (~2.3 s). iPad Pro 13" simulator, a relaunch: loading screen to 2.4 s, empty page to 3.5 s, then the data → the data at ~1.3 s after the launch animation, no empty page. (5) **Not a service worker, not a bundle inside the app:** WKWebView runs service workers only for App-Bound Domains fixed in the app (a home is any address); a bundled page would sit on another origin (storage, CSP, pairing all rebuilt) and help only the first open. (6) **Native vs web:** both faults were engineering inside the hybrid. It stays, held to a measured bar: the conversation in the first frame on open, a layout change painted within 100 ms, every interaction under 100 ms to its first visible response; next if a real device misses it: start the web view at app launch, split the bundle, virtualize a long transcript; native is reconsidered only if a measured interaction on a real device still misses the bar after those. | U (2026-11-11), R56 | `apps/web/src/{glance,app,main,markdown,native}.ts(x)`, `packages/core/src/web.ts`, `apps/ios/Ducter/{WebShell,DucterApp}.swift`, `apps/android` (`MainActivity.kt`, `Store.kt`), `apps/web/test/glance.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/web.test.ts`, `design/system.md`, `design/scenarios.md`, `design/todo.md`, `docs/phone.md`, research/57 |

## 2026-11-11: Pictures in the conversation and in a task (the author: "做最优雅实现吧"; "要优雅一些，极度顺滑方便用户浏览查看，体验拉满")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D206 | **Built 2026-11-11 (phase 1). Pictures go both ways: the user sends them to the partner, to an opened task and with a New task's words; a task shows the user what it made (a screenshot, a picture it generated, a file) and the partner relays it under its words; "画一张… / Draw…" is a task only an agent that makes pictures takes.** *Who:* all three; the consumer first (a messaging app that can't take a photo feels broken), the developer next (⌘V a screenshot into a task). *Feel:* before, a picture described in words and a path in Details; after, the picture itself, up before Send, opening big with a tap. *First time:* the picture button where every messaging app has it; paste and drop just work. (1) **Sending.** Composer, task composer and the New task card's What row: **加图片 / Add images** (the phone's own sheet: library, camera, files; the Mac's open panel), paste and drop (**松手就加上 / Drop to add**); up to 10 (**一次最多 10 张 / Up to 10 at a time**); pictures only for now (**现在只能发图片 / Only images can be sent for now**). The sending device scales to 2048 px, re-encodes (JPEG ~0.85; a screenshot stays PNG, a GIF stays moving; HEIC becomes JPEG), which drops EXIF and so the location, and makes the 720 px tile and a ~16 px preview. The upload starts when a picture is picked (a thin ring on its thumbnail), so Send is instant; a message waits for its pictures, and one that didn't go up keeps its words and picture with **没发出去 · 重试 / Didn't send · Retry**. (2) **Store and wire.** Content-addressed `<dataDir>/media/<sha256>.<ext>` plus a small `.json`; the home never decodes or re-encodes a picture (no pure, fast image library for Bun; `sharp` breaks `bun build --compile`; every sender has a canvas or Go's `image`): type and pixel size from the header, EXIF orientation honoured. `POST /api/media` behind the gate (25 MB, same origin); `GET /media/<id>?k=` before the gate, the key an HMAC of the id with the home's secret, immutable; `&dl=1` downloads. Frames carry ids (`send`, `task_send`, `task_new` `.media`), the Log carries `MediaRef`s (`user_msg`, `ai_msg`, `task_open`, `task_event`, `report`), never bytes. (3) **To the models.** OAR `images` (files on the home; a worker's session gets the bytes): the partner sees the user's pictures and up to 4 a task showed, named in its line by a 12-hex id; `task_open`/`task_send` `.media` hand them on (none named: the latest message's go with the brief). An agent that can't see pictures (DeepSeek Harness, OAR `capabilities.images` false; `HarnessInfo.images`) gets the words and a line; with pictures on the New task card it shows **看不了图片 / Can't see images**. The partner's image generation stays off. (4) **From tasks.** `report_to_main.show` (≤ 6 paths): only from the task's folder, its evidence folder or `$CODEX_HOME/generated_images` (research/56), links resolved; read where it runs, copied in, on the report as `media`; what can't be shown is a line back to the task. The reports that woke a turn put their media under the partner's first words (`ai_msg.media`), once. Browser briefs say `show` for screenshots. (5) **Drawing.** `needs.draw`: only an agent with `draws` (Codex, `image_generation` kept on for tasks only) takes it; the others are blocked with **画不了图 / Can't make pictures**; none anywhere and the open fails, and the partner says drawing needs Codex signed in on one of their computers. (6) **Showing** (`gridLayout`, the same in TS and Go): one at its own shape (21:9…1:3) within ~70% of the column and ~a third of the screen; 2 side by side; 3 as one tall and two stacked; 4 as 2×2; more as four with **+n**. Space kept from the ref's size; the blurred preview fades to the picture. Files: one line, icon, name, size, **打开 / Open**. The viewer grows out of the tile onto the theme's paper (a fade under reduced motion): pinch and double-tap zoom, swipe through the conversation's (or the task's) pictures, swipe down or Esc back into the tile; **保存 / Save** (the share sheet on a phone), **分享 / Share**, **拷贝 / Copy**, **来自 {task} / From {task}**. Mac: Space, ←/→, Esc, ⌘C, drag out to Finder. (7) **Shells.** iPhone: Info.plist names the camera, the library and adding to Photos. Android: a chooser (several at once, the camera first when allowed) and Save to Downloads. (8) **Later** (todo): files and PDFs from the user and a share sheet into Ducter (phase 2); video, reply to a message, link previews (phase 3). *Defaults the author may revisit:* 2048 px / JPEG 0.85; 25 MB; 10 per message, 6 per report; the viewer on the theme's paper, not black; the user's latest pictures go to a task the partner opens when it names none. | The author's brief (2026-11-11); research/56 | `design/media.md`, product-model glossary, `design/ui.md` (tokens), `design/quiet-conversation.md`, scenarios C7, `docs/pictures.md`, todo |

## 2026-11-11: The opened task's bugs from a hel run, and its words (the author: 「我们的很多文案怪怪的」)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D207 | **Built 2026-11-11. No task ever works in Ducter's own data folder; the opened task says the runner's lines in the user's words, keeps quiet when nothing is worth saying, and its actions and labels are words a fresh user reads at a glance.** *Who:* all three; the run was the author's (a hel home, Codex, 「排查电话只播放开场白的问题」). *Feel:* before, a task rewrote the partner's own `state.json`, a sent message stayed in the box, English plumbing and a wrong 在浏览器里操作 sat in the transcript, and 先停 / 放下 / 它收到了，接着做 read oddly; after, it works in a folder of its own and the page reads like a person wrote it. (1) **Ducter's data folder is never a place** (root cause: the partner passed its own working directory, `<dataDir>/partner`, as `place.folder`; nothing checked it, and the folder copy then kept and could undo Ducter's own files). `isDucterData(path, dataDir)` (`protocol/src/ducter-folder.ts`, symlinks resolved) is the data folder or anything in it, except the Ducter folder when it lives there (`<dataDir>/ducter`, a test's or a second Ducter's) and the old `<dataDir>/worktrees`. The runner's `planPlace` refuses such a folder, repo or worktree before the task exists, on every computer (`DucterDataError`); the partner's `task_open` refuses it first, with no ack, job line or task, in one line that points to `{"kind":"none"}`, so it opens again with no place and gets `<dataDir>/scratch/<taskId>` (the one way into the data folder, made by the runner); a path there in the user's words is never taken as the place. New task says **这是 Ducter 自己放数据的文件夹，任务不在这里做。换一个文件夹，或者选「不用文件夹」。/ That is where Ducter keeps its own data, so no task works there. Pick another folder, or No folder.** (`reason: ducter_data`). The folder copy (D142) is never kept for such a folder (`holds_ducter`), and Undo refuses to put its files back (a task opened before this). No persona or tool-description change: the refusal is the tool's answer, so no eval was run (D175). (2) **The composer empties on send** (Mac: Enter ran after the frame had stored its draft, so the next frame put the words back; the stored draft is emptied too). The web reads what is typed now, not the render's copy. A held message (it looks like a key) stays until it is sent. (3) **The runner's lines in words** (root cause: the transcript showed every system line's English text, "no report after one nudge; leaving the task idle (stall rule)" among them). `noteOf` (`transcript.ts`, its Go port, one fixture) maps a line to a key by its `raw` fields, the clients word `note.<say>`: the stall, a computer gone and back (`raw.name` now logged), waiting for one, a fresh session, a switch, an effort left out, the browser guard and fallback, and four failures under **出错 / Error** (the line as logged on hover only); everything else (session started, memory recalled, retries, archiving, a restart, a harness exit) says nothing. The blocked reason "Claude in Chrome isn't connected" is a key too (`browser:native_missing`). (4) **在浏览器里操作 only for a browser** (root cause: a stretch with any `browsed` step was browse, and `curl` is `browsed · request`): a request or a fetch is a command that reads a page; a stretch of shell commands with a curl is **跑了几条命令 / Ran commands**. (5) **Changes for a folder** were right for an ordinary folder; with (1) Ducter's own never get there. (6) **The words, old → new** (zh / en; reasons): header 先停 / Pause → **暂停 / Pause** (先停 asks "stop first, then what?"; 暂停 is everyone's word and the routines'); 放下 / Drop → **不做了 / Cancel task** (放下 reads as setting it aside for later, but it ends the task; Drop is jargon), so the state 放下了 / Dropped → **不做了 / Cancelled** and the toast 放下了「X」/ Dropped "X" → **「X」不做了 / Cancelled "X"**; the receipts 它收到了，接着做 / Got it; it's on it and 它正在做，已经告诉它了 / Told it while it works → **nothing** (proactive restraint: the live line already shows it at work), 等它忙完手上的就告诉它 / It'll get this once it finishes what it's doing → **它忙完手上的就看 / It'll read this once it's done with what it's doing** (what the user needs: when it will see it), 正在送过去… → **在发…** (Sending… kept); the composer 直接跟这件事说 / Talk to this task → **直接跟它说… / Message it directly…** (you talk to the one doing it, not to a 事); the brief 你交代的事 · {name} 交代的事 (and 你交代的 · {name} 交代的) → **你交给它的事 · {name} 交给它的事** (交代 is a boss instructing; partners hand work over, as D196 dropped 交代一件事; en kept); 说完了，等你回话 → **该你了** (Over to you kept); while a folder task works 等它做完，随时能改回去 / You can undo this once it's done → **它做完后，可以全部改回去 / You can undo all of it once it's done** (two clauses that fought); the stall line → **它停下了，没交回结果 / It stopped without reporting back**; the rest of the runner's lines in the glossary. Checked and kept: the meta line 在做 · 5:36 · Codex · … · 在 {computer} 上 · 换谁来做 › (its "partner" was the folder's name, `<dataDir>/partner`, gone with (1)), 出错, n 处出错, 运行 pwd 出错, 改动, 改回去 / 就这样. Runner-up for (1): move scratch out of the data folder into the Ducter folder, deferred (`todo.md`): the per-task scratch is Ducter-made and empty, and moving it touches every place-less task's recovery | U (the author's screenshots, 2026-11-11) | `packages/protocol/src/{ducter-folder,transcript,index}.ts`, `packages/protocol/fixtures/transcript*`, `packages/runner/src/{places,folder,runner}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,place}.ts`, `packages/core/src/newtask.ts`, `apps/web/src/{transcript.tsx,views.tsx,app.ts,where.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views,i18n}`, tests, `design/{product-model,focus-window,system,ui,todo}.md`, `docs/privacy-and-safety.md` |

## 2026-11-12: Is Ducter's Nowledge Mem integration done the way Mem intends? (the author)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D229 | **Mem the way Mem says: recall reads the identity's own lane, the home's `nmem` goes in with pipx, a copy past the system's mark moves there in one tap, and every Mem switch says plainly what happens. Built 2026-11-12 with fakes in nmem 0.10.118's real shapes; not yet run on hel. (D228 is taken by the From-anywhere branch.)** Audit in research/69 (nmem 0.10.118 help and `--json` shapes, plugin 0.7.36 skills and hooks, mem.nowledge.co docs, hel read-only). (1) **Recall reads the identity's lane (M1, M2):** an AI Identity carries "a default space for memory reads and writes"; Mem's plugin reads it for an `NMEM_AGENT_ID` (`agents show` → `defaultSpaceId`, then `--space-id`). The persona's identity keeps its lane (`MemIdentity.lane`, from `agents show`, camelCase as 0.10.118 answers), read again on each start. With no Space the user chose, the partner's search runs twice in parallel: where saves land (no Space: Only me in a team workspace) and `--space-id <lane>`, merged by id and Mem's score; one failing still gives the other's hits. A Space the user chose stays the only one. Writes are unchanged: `--agent-id`, Mem decides where it lands. Measured on the author's workspace: the two reads shared no hit. (2) **Install pipx first (M3):** pipx → uv → pipx from the system's own package manager when that needs no password (Homebrew; apt or dnf as root or with `sudo -n`, never a password prompt) → pip into the user's site only when that Python isn't marked externally managed (asked once with a short Python probe). (3) **The move (M4):** a pip copy in the user's site under a marked Python (hel) is moved to pipx: get pipx if needed, the old copy out (`pip uninstall`, past the mark: it is the copy that was put there so), `pipx install --force nmem-cli` in; if that fails, the old copy goes back. The sign-in (`~/.nowledge-mem/config.json`) is never touched. It is the update when the copy is too old for Bridge, and a quiet offer under any working state otherwise. Without any pipx route, the old in-place update stays the fallback. A copy under an unmarked Python stays. (4) **Mem's own "newer version" (M9):** `status.update.update_available` offers a quiet **更新 / Update** from the copy's own source. (5) **Tasks (M6):** a task also gets `NMEM_CLI_PATH` when Ducter has one (the plugin's own rule); the lane is left to the plugin. (6) **nmem's environment (M7, M8):** the three places that spawned nmem with Ducter's whole environment use D102's allowlist; installers get the basics plus their own `PIP_/PIPX_/UV_/HOMEBREW_`; the identity bundle is read with `--source-app ducter`. (7) **Copy (the author's North star for every Mem switch):** one calm line per state with its next action; no pipx, PEP 668 or key on the main line, the how behind **细节 / Details** or **怎么装 / How**; progress in place (**正在换… / Moving…**); a failure says it plainly, whether the old copy still works (**原来那份还在，照常能用，登录也还在。**) and offers **再试一次 / Try again**; success is a short warm line in the done colour (**{home} 上换好了。登录还在，什么都没丢。 / Moved on {home}. Still signed in, nothing lost.**); turning memory off says what stops and what stays (**…已经记下的都还在 Nowledge Mem 里，一条没删；再打开，马上接着用。**). Web (phone and wide) and the Mac app the same; zh and en together. Kept on purpose: tasks never save memories themselves (D168 (5)) although the plugin's startup text invites it; no thread search in partner recall yet (todo). | U (the author's question and the North-star brief), L, research/69 | `packages/core/src/{mem-cli,bridge-link,mem-identity,persona,main}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/memory.ts`, `packages/runner/src/runner.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{index,internal}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{views.tsx,app.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-bridge.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/protocol.go,i18n/i18n.go,views/peers.go}`, tests (`core/test/{bridge-link,mem-identity,persona}.test.ts`, `partner/test/memory.test.ts`, `runner/test/memory.test.ts`, Go `bridge_test.go`), `design/{bridge,memory,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/faq.md` |

## Superseded
- **在哪儿都能用 on as soon as any device is paired** (devices.ts since D54) and the onboarding's "先给这台 Mac 一个从外面也能打开的地址（比如你自己的域名）" dead end. Superseded by D228: on means an address from outside (set up another way, or the person's own Cloudflare tunnel), and the onboarding offers the guided setup in one tap.
- **D152 (2)'s header actions 先停 / Pause and 放下 / Drop, (3)'s 你交代的事 / {name} 交代的事, (8)'s 说完了，等你回话; the attention state 放下了 / Dropped; the receipts 它正在做，已经告诉它了 / Told it while it works and 它收到了，接着做 / Got it; it's on it; the composer's 直接跟这件事说 / Talk to this task; and the transcript's note that showed any system line as logged.** Superseded by D207: 暂停 / Pause · 不做了 / Cancel task · 不做了 / Cancelled · 你交给它的事 · 该你了; no receipt when steered in or started; 直接跟它说… / Message it directly…; the runner's lines worded from `note.<say>` or not shown.

## 2026-11-11: The opened task as a coding agent's conversation (the author: "你看我们这个 focus window 对话框的 ai 聊天实现（这里应该像 coding agent 那样了）非常不优雅，我建议你参考 lorca 的设计和实现")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D210 | **Built 2026-11-11 (web: phone and wide; Mac), for the author's review. The opened task reads as a conversation with the agent, turn by turn, the way a coding agent's chat does: what was said to it, then its answer in order, its words and every step as one compact line between them, an edit with its diff and a failure with its error in place, and one line over the composer that says where it stands.** *Who:* the heavy developer first (they read a task the way they read Claude Code or Codex: which commands ran, which files changed, what failed), and the fresh user, who reads the agent's words, sees a red line only for a real failure and never needs to open anything. *Feel:* before, loose paragraphs and one gist line per stretch ("在浏览器里操作 · 15 步 › 运行 pwd 出错") that hid the commands and edits and said failures that were already fixed; after, a page that reads like the agent's own run, calm, dense where it is work and roomy where it is words, and that says at the bottom what it is doing now. *First time:* nothing to learn; the brief is the first thing in it, and every line that holds more has a chevron. (1) **Turns.** A turn opens with what someone said to the agent: the brief (你交给它的事 / {name} 交给它的事, the quiet sunken card, three lines until 展开, D207), your words (your bubble), or what the partner passed on ({name} 对它说, the same card); everything after it until the next is the agent's answer. Shared model: `turnsOf(blocksOf(…))` (`packages/protocol/src/transcript.ts`, its Go port, `fixtures/transcript.json` now holds turns). (2) **Every step is its own line** (after egoist/lorca, research/58): its drawn mark, its sentence (D152's: the agent's own words, else a verb and a summary), **the command beside the agent's words in mono and muted ink** (`Run the glance cache tests  bun test apps/web/test/glance.test.ts`; the words keep their room, the command is cut first), an edit's **+n −n** in the done and failed inks, ×n for a repeat, a chevron only when it opens. **Only consecutive looks fold** (reads, searches, probes, a request that reads a page): **看了 3 个文件，搜了 2 次 / Read 3 files, 2 searches**, open on a tap; a lone look is its own line. Consecutive steps sit tight; words and steps 12 apart; turns 24 apart. (3) **An edit's diff, inline, from the edit itself**: the edit tool's input (Claude Code's Edit / MultiEdit / Write, Codex's apply_patch and fileChange, any old/new strings or patch) as lines, put in on the done tint, taken out on the failed tint, two lines of context, "@@ path" when it touched several files, at most 60 lines then **还有 n 行 / n more lines** (`editdiff.ts`, `editdiff.go`); no git, so it works for a folder, a server or another computer. The task's 改动 / Changes (D74, D128) stay under the transcript as the whole run's result. (4) **A failure in place**: the row in the failed ink with **出错 / Error**, and under it the line of its output that names the error (`✗ glance > paints On hand…`, `error TS2339 …`, else its last line; `failLine`); its output on a tap. A probe that finds nothing stays neutral (D152). (5) **Output** opens under a step: `$ command`, then the first 12 lines, **还有 n 行 / n more lines** for the rest (the model keeps 40). (6) **The foot over the composer** says where it stands: while it works, a spinner, **在做 / Working**, the clock (D152's one clock moves here while it works; the meta line says **用了 {duration}** once finished) and its latest step in words, with **暂停 / Pause** at the end; when it's over to you, **该你了 · 它停在这儿了。回它一句，它就接着做。** (D152, D207); silent otherwise (the outcome is at the head). Pause leaves the head (the Mac's header, the phone's row) for the foot; 不做了 / Cancel task stays. (7) **Esc twice pauses** a task at work (in its composer too): the first Esc turns the foot into **再按一次 Esc 就暂停 / Press Esc again to pause** with **接着做 / Keep going** and **暂停 / Pause**, for 3 s; otherwise Esc still goes up a level. **⌘↩ sends** everywhere (a keyboard on an iPad included), Enter as before. (8) **Queued**: your words sent while it is busy read **排队中 · 它忙完手上的就看 / Queued · It'll read this once it's done with what it's doing**, lighter, until it answers; then nothing (D207's restraint). (9) **Scrolling**: a task that works or waits on you opens at its latest, a finished one at its head (the outcome); while it works a reader at the end follows the newest; one who scrolled up stays, with **回到最新 / Jump to latest**, a round raised button (no pill, D73). (10) **Long tasks**: blocks are built once per change of the task's events (web: a cache per task; Mac: per frame, measured 7 ms a frame with 423 events in the test renderer), each turn redrawn only when its own blocks change (web), settled turns `content-visibility: auto`; the latest turns up to 240 lines are drawn and older ones wait behind **显示更早的 n 步 / Show n earlier steps** (the last turn always whole). D209's glance cache is unchanged (it keeps events; the page builds from them). (11) **Pictures (D206) stay**, small in a turn (about 15 rem / 240 pt wide, a tap opens the viewer); the outcome's as before. (12) **English step words start on a capital** now that each is a line ("Opened appstoreconnect.apple.com in Chrome", "Pressed ⌘T in Chrome"; the open and fetch words gained their verbs). *Taken from Lorca* (research/58): one line per step with only looks folded, turns from what was said to it, queued messages at the end, bounded output with "+N", chevrons only where something opens, the hover row, follow-the-end with a small threshold, bounding data at the source. *Not taken:* its look (symbols, colours, sizes, cards, the sheet), tense-shifting verbs, polling, red for every non-zero exit. *Defaults the author may revisit:* the 240-line window; Esc twice (vs. once) because Esc also goes up a level; the command shown beside the agent's own words; the outcome's picture size. Runner-up for (2): keep D152's gist and open it by default, rejected because a gist line is still a summary that can be wrong and the author asked for a coding agent's page | U (2026-11-11), R58, R40 | `packages/protocol/src/{transcript,editdiff}.ts`, `packages/protocol/fixtures/transcript*`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{transcript,editdiff,taskturns,tasksteps,task,app,media}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/{turns,i18n}.go`, `apps/web/src/{transcript.tsx,views.tsx,wide.ts,app.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-transcript.ts`, tests, `design/{focus-window,ui,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/tasks.md` |

## Superseded
- **D152 (5)'s steps folded per stretch under one gist line (查看了一圈 / 改了 n 个文件 / 在 {app} 里操作 / 跑了几条命令 / 想了想 · n 步 · n 处出错, open while live, a real failure shown even folded), (10)'s stretch named by what it got done, (2)'s clock ticking on the meta line while it works, (6)'s row as the agent's words alone when its harness gives them, the brief as its own card above the transcript, Pause in the opened task's header (D207's 暂停), and D152 (11)'s "a reader at the end follows" without Jump to latest, opening at the head.** Superseded by D210: turns, one line per step with only looks folded, the command beside the agent's words, inline diffs, the error in place, the foot with the clock and Pause, Esc twice, Jump to latest, opening at the latest while it works.

## 2026-11-11: Phone calls, after the first real test (the author's test from Japan: "马上" was held to the morning, a call that reached Google Voice was called "refused")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D208 | **Settled 2026-11-11 (built; the author's first real calls, defaults taken overnight). "Now" means now when the owner asks; the callee's hours are a courtesy the owner may pass, never a hard limit's; outcomes say what really happened.** *Who:* the author testing calls to their own Google Voice number while the person it stood for was at an expo in Japan; then any owner who says "现在打". *Before:* told "明天 8:00" twice after saying 马上, then that "the calling service forced it to tomorrow" (false: it was Ducter's rule), and "没打出去，服务商拒绝了" for a call that connected. *After:* the card says why it waits in the callee's clock and offers 现在就打; the outcome line says it got through and Google Voice's screening answered, and how to fix that. (1) **The owner's 免打扰 holds only calls nobody just asked for:** a call the owner asks for in the conversation (the partner's turn holds their message, or their reply on a card), or a Go they are tapping, may start now in their quiet hours; a time Ducter picks later (a task's call, a Go that waits) still respects them (`nextAllowed` `present`). (2) **The callee's 9:00–20:00 is a courtesy:** when it alone moves a call the owner just asked for, the card says why in their clock (**对方那边现在 20:03，我排到了对方的 9:00。 / It's 20:03 where they are, so I set it for 9:00 their time.**), Go reads **到点再打 / Call then**, and **现在就打 / Call now** sits beside the reason (frame `call_now`; `decision_resolved.now`). Such a draft carries no `scheduledAt`: Ducter holds the hour, so Call now can dial at once. (3) **8:00–21:00 where they are is never crossed**, even on Call now (the TCPA's calling hours, 47 CFR 64.1200(c)(1), are for solicitations, which Ducter never makes; Ducter keeps them for every call as its own floor). Past 21:00 the card says **太晚了 / too late to call** and offers no Call now. (4) **The owner's own number has no callee hours:** the partner passes `own` when the owner says the number is theirs, and their saved phone in Your details (D191) counts too. (5) **Zones:** a +1 number is read by its area code (a table of ~400 NANP codes, two zones where a code spans a line; unknown and toll-free codes fall back to the four continental zones); the partner passes `calleeZone` only when it knows where the person is now. The partner's tool answer says the time is Ducter's own rule and never the provider's; the persona says so in one clause. (6) **Outcomes from `tuwa_call_result`'s words, not its status alone:** a machine that answered (voicemail, call screening, "the subscriber you have called is not available") is a new ended state, **转到了自动语音 / Got an automated message**; nobody picking up is **没人接 / No answer**; a dialed call that failed is **没打通 / Didn't connect** with the provider's words (reason `call_failed`; "Tuwa 没说原因 / Tuwa gave no reason" when it gave none); **没打出去 / Didn't go through** stays for calls never placed, and "refused" only for a real refusal, with its words. A cancel at the provider (not the owner's) is a failure with "cancelled at the provider". (7) **Google Voice's call screening** (it announces "call from …" to its owner and says "not available" unless they press 1) is recognised from the words and said with the fix: **接起来按 1，或者在 Google Voice 设置里关掉「过滤来电」 / answer and press 1, or turn off Screen calls in Google Voice settings** (`hint: screening`). (8) **A confirm that errors may have placed the call:** Ducter reads the call's status by the draft's id before anything else; placed or held → it goes on (never a second yes); still waiting for its yes → the existing re-approval (same summary only); unreadable → failed as **lost**, "不确定打出去没有，先去 Tuwa 里看一眼再重打 / Not sure it went out: check Tuwa before trying again". (9) **The card and the phone card, polished:** the plan gains **不能做 / Won't do** (the owner's own don'ts); Tuwa's approval text folds under **Tuwa 的原话（英文） / Tuwa's own wording**, as label/value lines with labels in the owner's words and values as Tuwa wrote them; no status dots or pulses on any call surface (D69); On hand's ended rows open in place only when there is more to read; Settings › 账号's card: today in words (**今天还没打 / No calls today**), the cap's help (**到了就不再打；Tuwa 那边也按这个封顶。**), and **Ducter 在 Tuwa 上替你关了 / Ducter turned these off on Tuwa** with one ✓ line each (**Tuwa 不采集你的声音样本**, **不向 Tuwa 好友同步你的近况**) instead of "声音采样已暂停 · 好友同步已关". *Alt considered:* let the owner's words ("now") skip every hour rule. Rejected: a stranger called at 23:00 by an AI is the one outcome no "now" should buy; 8:00–21:00 is the line. *Verified:* unit tests with a fake Tuwa (`phone.test.ts`), the partner eval (`call-ended-screening-zh` added). *Inferred:* Tuwa's real outcome words (only the author's one call, as summarised to us), whether Tuwa itself refuses a call past 20:00 in the callee's zone, and Google Voice's menu name | U (the author's test call, 2026-11-11) | `packages/core/src/{phone,phone-provider,nanp,core,ws}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{phone,index}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,persona}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{phone.tsx,i18n-phone.ts,app.ts}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,views,i18n}/phone*.go`, `design/phone.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `docs/calls.md` |

## Superseded
- **D193 (5)'s "the draft carries the first allowed time (outside the owner's 免打扰, inside 9:00–20:00 in every zone of the callee's country unless the partner names the zone)" and D189 (8)'s "no calls in the owner's 免打扰 or outside 9:00–20:00 callee time" as hard rules for every call.** Superseded by D208: the owner's quiet hours hold only calls nobody just asked for; 9:00–20:00 is a courtesy the owner's Call now passes; 8:00–21:00 is the hard line; a +1 number's area code names its zone; a card offering Call now keeps the hour in Ducter.
- **D193 (9)'s outcome "said exactly as reported" read from the status word alone (a failed status was "didn't go through", the fallback "the provider refused"), and D193 (4)'s Go label 打过去 / Call on every card.** Superseded by D208 (6)–(8): the words decide (got an automated message, no answer, didn't connect with the provider's words), "refused" only when it was; a card that waits says 到点再打 / Call then.
- **D170 (9)'s "Until then `DUCTER_BRIDGE=1`" as the only way on, D170 (6)'s "shown once another agent has written" for a user whose Mem can connect, D170 (7)'s "reach off, no check at all", and D171 (3)'s path (Mem grants authority, then `select-cloud`, `bridge setup --enable`, a second identity with its own key for the trial).** Superseded by D205: with nmem 0.10.118+ the user's one tap in Settings › 伙伴 connects the persona (`bridge connections connect`) and turns Bridge on; the section is always findable there; 谁都不行 still lets the answer to what the user sent come back; the trial's other side is a native Claude Code session binding.
- **D203 (1)'s scan ("git checkouts up to two levels under `~/dev`, `~/code`, `~/src`, `~/Projects`, `~/projects`, `~/work`, `~/repos`, `~/git`", `findRepos`, `REPO_ROOTS`, at most 20) and its first-run hints ("moved from the CLI's first-run hints, which keep using it"; D149 (6)'s "New task still lists those repos"), and (2)'s "the first-run hints too" among the places; D203 (3)'s English "update it first" for a worker too old to look.** Superseded by D204: no scanning anywhere; a computer's projects are those in its Ducter folder (`<folder>/projects`); `ducter up` writes no repo hints; an older worker is said in both languages.
- **D198 (1)'s "A new repo task's worktree is `<dataDir>/worktrees/<repo name>/<branch slug>` … Still under `<dataDir>/worktrees`, so archive removes only what Ducter made".** Superseded by D204 (4): new tasks' worktrees are in the visible Ducter folder, `~/ducter/worktrees/<repo>/<branch slug>` (naming unchanged); archive and uninstall handle both places.- **D186 and D197's "The phone and the browser list these places but don't add one (no picker)", and the Mac's folder list for an agent on another computer holding only the folders added from the Mac app or used before (D202 (2)).** Superseded by D203: every computer reports its repos, listed after the used and added ones; 找一个文件夹… / Find a folder… looks through any computer of the home, from the phone and the browser too, and adds the folder there.
- **D185's 谁来做 / Who does it list "every agent at that place, best first", one row per agent and model (Claude Code · Opus, Claude Code · Sonnet), and D157 §6.3's "agents that live only on another computer" (an agent greyed here because it isn't installed hid the same agent on another computer; for a folder, agents on another computer were dropped).** Superseded by D202: every agent on every computer, one row each, the model and effort picked under the chosen one; an agent on another computer than the folder's brings its copy of the repo, or the folder list on that computer.
- **D198 (1)'s slug "any character outside `[A-Za-z0-9._-]` as `-`" and the title's slug keeping only ASCII letters and digits (a Chinese title gave `task`), and D198 (2)'s one terminal item, 在终端打开 / Open in Terminal (`open -a Terminal`), placed second after Show in Finder.** Superseded by D201: letters, marks and digits of any script stay, NFC, cut by grapheme; and every terminal installed is offered in its own group after the editors, **在 {terminal} 里打开 / Open in {terminal}**, Terminal itself **在终端里打开**.

  column, diff on demand, Details.
- **D68's result sheet and task chip** (a two-column How I know / Sent out sheet with evidence
  chips, and a state chip under every result). Superseded by D73: one quiet line of proof and
  no restated state.
- **D64's "raw generations live in the concept-art canvas" with raw images still in git
  history.** Superseded by D75: evidence and explorations are out of git, history included.
- **D68's container depth** (containers rest on a soft `Raised` shadow; "nothing else has a
  ledge"). Superseded by D76: cards stand on a solid 4pt base; buttons keep the ledge plus a
  drop and a highlight.
- **D69's "no perpetual alive animation"** as a rule for art. Narrowed by D77: the clay
  partner and scene may breathe; status never animates.
- **D23's 手头 as the overview's zh label.** Superseded by D80: 手头的事.
- **D65's one-device stage** (the chosen device alone on a pedestal with four ability slots
  round it, the AI accounts as cards under it with dotted cords, the pairing code in a sheet
  over the stage). Superseded by D81: every device on stage as a clay island, bridges from
  home, abilities at full size in front of the island, pairing in the scene.
- **"The partner runs on Claude Code only"** and system.md's "if Codex is ever the partner …
  (not built)". Superseded by D85: the partner on any partner-capable harness, each with its
  own clean setup.
- **D85 (7)'s key file `<dataDir>/env`** ("a key lives in Ducter's env file `<dataDir>/env`
  (0600, `KEY=value`; the file a server's systemd unit already reads)"). Superseded by D102:
  on a server that file is the unit's `EnvironmentFile`, so its keys reached every harness
  child. Keys now live in `<dataDir>/keys.env`, which nothing loads into an environment.
- **D73 (2) and (3) in the conversation** (under every result a proof line, a ↗ line, then
  Merge into main / Open draft PR as clay buttons; the living message as a card with "While you
  were away" as its heading, "2 need you" with Go on all defaults, a tinted card per ask with
  the asking task's title, a countdown and Reply… · Details · Hold · Go; status rows for work
  under way). Superseded by D104: words by default, one quiet row with Merge into main and
  Details, one ask at a time. D73 still governs On hand, the opened task and the rule of no pills.
- **"Thinking…" in the header line** as the only sign the partner is answering. Superseded by
  D104's waiting line.
- **Persona rule 2c's "write nothing more this turn: no 'I'll tell you when it's done'",
  the ack as "one short line that names the action", and the task opened only when the full
  task_open call arrives.** Superseded by D120: one sentence that may say it will report back
  and, when grounded, how long it usually takes; the task opens from the call's streamed head.
- **D115 (2)'s strip of beads for routines** (a line, today an ink mark, a clay bead per run) and
  **D113's try pebbles** (✓ / → / ×, the current one hollow). Superseded by D115's update (6) and
  (7): the goal's clay lights or a flag to climb to, and a tear-off clay calendar for the next run.
- **D115 update (6)'s staircase to a clay flag** for goals that can't be counted. Superseded by D115 update 2: judged counts
  show the lights; otherwise Ducter at work and the latest try in words.
- **D96's "the oo one tap reads only a self-hosted address and token … never the OOMOL account
  key"** and §4.4's "the OOMOL account key in auth.toml is never used; make a token for Ducter in
  the OOMOL console instead". Superseded by D122: with oo signed in, the one tap reads through
  oo itself, with oo's own sign-in and permissions; Ducter still never reads or holds that key.
  The self-hosted address-and-token tap stays only for an oo that isn't signed in.
- **product-model "Archive and disk": "Archiving commits leftovers, removes the worktree, and
  keeps the branch."** Superseded by D125 (3): leftovers are never committed or force-removed
  for the user; the worktree that holds them is kept and the task says why.
- **product-model §3 "+ New task in On hand opens a picker: project → repo(s)/folder → device →
  base branch → model. It is the power path, never required."** Superseded by D128: a three-row
  card (where, who, what), the device inferred, no model picker; the words go to the task as
  they are.
- **D104 (3)'s waiting-line copy** (在想… / 在翻之前聊过的… / 在看那件事做到哪了… / 在跟那件事说… …).
  Superseded by D129 (6): the humanized lines (在看做到哪了… · 在把你的话带过去… and the others
  `design/conversation.md` lists). The waiting line itself stands.
- **G-safety Rule 3's rows for a task's own push and draft PR as gated effects** (D26 "branch
  pushed as `ducter/*` at checkpoints: personal auto, org ask once" read as a gate; D52 Q6 "draft
  PR auto-opens on personal repos after a countdown, waits for Go on org repos"), and system.md's
  "pushes, PRs" under Gate the effects. Superseded by D132: a task may push its branch and open a

- **The roadmap's "Native mobile app (Expo)" and system.md's "Native mobile later: Expo".**
  Superseded by D131: a native SwiftUI shell around the phone web client.
- **ui.md "Volt, Paper, Signal, and Midnight ship as alternative themes".** Superseded by D134:
  they are designed; only Pebble ships, and Midnight is planned as the Mac's dark mode.
- **context.md "the harness's own compactor is bounded for the partner only (tasks keep their

  window with standard-window models; the partner's compactor is capped at its window, tasks
  compact at 160K with a save before and the brief again after.
- **onboarding.md step 5's starters from `firstrun.json`** ("Hi, I'm {name}." and three starters about

  看看你的设备 under them). Superseded by D149: a greeting by name and time of day, life-first
  starters worked out on the home, no path, no note, no second link to Devices.
- **D71's two places for settings** (伙伴 / Partner as its own page, a character sheet with the

- **D148 (11)'s card text**: "one header (the name …), what it is in plain words (**云上的电脑 ·
  {name} 住这儿**), a short state line, its AI accounts as coins (accounts not installed fold into

  Superseded by D161: the clay, the name, one status line, the coin tray with its socket
  **再登录一个**, the chips, one **通过 … 连着** line, the version line only with an update, one

- **D155 (1)–(2)'s card presentation**: the folded rows **命令行也连着 / Ducter app 也连着** each with

## 2026-11-12: No emoji, and a quality pass over every screen (the author, on 0.0.23: "新建 focus window 的界面居然有 emoji?? 这也太低质量了吧？…不会还有别的这种低水准的设计吧？")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D211 | **Built 2026-11-12, for the author's review. Nothing a person reads is ever an emoji or a symbol the phone can't draw as text: marks are drawn glyphs, keys and states are words or symbols seen drawn as text on iOS, and a guard in every test run keeps it so. The same pass looked at every screen on the iOS Simulator and fixed what was clearly below the bar.** *Who:* all three; the author saw it on their own device in the New task sheet. *Felt:* a template's emoji in a product that is otherwise drawn by hand reads as cheap and careless; afterwards the sheet is words, drawn marks and clay only. (1) **Root cause, verified on the iOS 26.3 Simulator** (Mobile Safari, the app's own stylesheet, a probe page of every symbol the clients use; `.shots/quality-audit/ios-glyph-probe-*.png`): the New task foot's **⌘↩ 开始 · ⇧⌘↩ 开始并接着新建**. No text font in the stack (Bricolage Grotesque, PingFang SC, the system's) has U+21A9 ↩, so WebKit on iOS takes it from the emoji font: a colour tile among the words on a device (the Simulator, which has no emoji font in WebKit, draws the same fallback as a ? box, as it does 😀 and ✅; `newtask-keys-iphone-zh-before.png`). The line shows wherever a fine pointer exists (an iPad with a keyboard or trackpad; the Mac), so a touch-only iPhone never shows it. U+FE0E does not fix it: with it, every probed symbol (↩ ✓ ↗) became the missing-glyph box, even in JetBrains Mono, so text presentation is not something the app can ask for. The same probe found ⌄ (the find card's **怎么知道的 ⌄**) and ⓘ missing too; ✓ ✗ × → ← ↔ ↗ ⇧ ⌘ ⌥ ⌃ ⏎ ⋯ − ≤ ≥ ≈ ▾ ① ② draw as text. (2) **Keys:** the foot says **⌘⏎ 开始 · ⇧⌘⏎ 开始并接着新建 / ⌘⏎ Start · ⇧⌘⏎ and start another** (U+23CE, drawn as text on iOS and on the Mac), still only where a hardware keyboard is plausible (not `(pointer: coarse)` without `(any-pointer: fine)`). (3) **Marks:** a mark is a drawn glyph (views.tsx, components.go), never a font's symbol: the started line's ✓ is the drawn check; a text button that opens something in place ends in a drawn chevron that turns down while open (`Btn chev`: 怎么知道的, a call's summary and transcript, a connection's reads list, which had no chevron at all); a routine's last run is words (**上次周二做完了 / Last Tue: done**, **上次周二没成**). (4) **The guard** (`scripts/check-glyphs.ts`, run by `bun test` as `scripts/glyphs.test.ts`): it reads the web client, the Mac app, the iOS and Android shells, the site, the user docs and the core's words (comments, tests and fixtures excepted) and fails on any emoji (Extended_Pictographic, Emoji_Presentation, regional indicators), any variation selector, and any symbol from the arrow, maths, technical, enclosed, shape, miscellaneous-symbol or dingbat blocks that is not on the list seen drawn as text in (1). A symbol joins the list only after the same check on iOS. (5) **The pass** (iPhone 17 Pro and iPad Pro 11 Simulators, light and dark, zh and en, against the demo homes; the Mac's New task shots; `.shots/quality-audit/`, named by screen, before and after): the table below; what is a design call is a recommendation, not a change. Not covered this pass: the picture viewer, an empty first conversation, the Android emulator (no Android code changed; its strings are under the guard) | U (the report), L (the cause, the rule, the fixes) | `scripts/check-glyphs.ts`, `scripts/glyphs.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/{i18n-starts,i18n,controls,newtask,views,finds,phone,accounts,autofill,format}.ts(x)`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/desktop/internal/{i18n/starts.go,i18n/i18n.go,views/newtask.go}`, `apps/site/src/pages/index.astro`, `design/ui.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/new-task.md`, `docs/faq.md` |

**The pass, screen by screen** (fixed: built in this change on every client it applies to; recommended: the author's call).

| Screen | Finding | Fix / recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| New task, keyboard foot (iPad with a keyboard, Mac web) | ↩ from the emoji font: a tile in **⌘↩ 开始** | Fixed: **⌘⏎** (2); the guard (4) |
| New task, who pill and list | **推荐 / Suggested** on a yellow capsule: a tag pill (D73) | Fixed, web and Mac: the word in the highlight's ink, no fill |
| New task, after ⇧⌘⏎ | **✓ 开始了：「X」** with a font check | Fixed: the drawn check |
| New task, who pill; opened task meta; On hand card headings | The agent's clay coin at 16–22 points beside text reads like an emoji, the likeliest "emoji" on an iPhone, where the key line never shows | Recommended: the coin only where it stands as an object (28 points and up: the who list, the device tray, Settings); in a pill or a meta line, the agent's name alone (a change to D183's sizes) |
| Opened task, phone (every state) | The meta line wraps and its second line starts with **·** before the coin | Fixed: no dot before the coin; the coin starts the group |
| Opened task, phone | **不做了 / Cancel task** stands alone under the header, a stranded item | Recommended: in the foot beside **暂停 / Pause** (D210 moved Pause there) |
| Opened task (D210), zh | The agent's own step titles are English inside zh chrome ("Run the glance cache tests") | Recommended: the step's verb in the reader's language before the agent's words (运行 · …), as the read and edit rows already do |
| On hand and the task's decision card, zh | **fix-ci 落后 main 12 / 个提交，有 2** : `word-break: keep-all` with `text-wrap: balance` turned a sentence into short ragged lines | Fixed: a question wraps as a sentence (keep-all stays for short titles) |
| Settings › Notifications | A stray **0** under the quiet hours (`(a \|\| list.length) && …` rendered the count) | Fixed |
| Settings › Notifications, on a phone | **发通知到这台 Mac 和你连上的手机** read on the phone | Fixed on the web client: **通知你连上的手机和电脑 / on the phones and computers you connected**; the Mac keeps **这台 Mac** |
| Settings › Autofill | Add rows: a typed **+** on a peach disc (a mark on a tile, D73) | Fixed: a drawn ＋ in the row's ink |
| Conversation, the find card | **怎么知道的 ⌄**: ⌄ is a missing glyph on iOS | Fixed: the drawn chevron (3) |
| Conversation, the find card's sources | **220 小时前** | Fixed: days from a day and a half on (**9 天前**), the Mac's rule |
| Conversation, offline | **连不上 Ducter，正在重试** said twice: the header line and a yellow strip | Fixed: once, in the header under the sleeping face (already a live status) |
| Phone call card | **Tuwa 的原话（英文） ›** and **‹** as font toggles | Fixed: the drawn chevron |
| Settings › Partner, dark | Codex's charcoal coin nearly disappears on the dark ground | Recommended: a dark-theme coin with a lighter key light (tools/art/coins.py) |
| Settings › Your devices | An accent dot after a name when an update exists (D161's one static dot; a D69 tell, and it says nothing to a screen reader) | Recommended: the words **有更新 / Update** in the row's word instead |
| New task, branch pill | The branch in mono beside the folder in the sans | Recommended: keep (a code token in mono, D78); noted only |
| Text buttons | **换谁来做 ›**, **用地址和钥匙加一个 ›** use a font ›; **看细节** has the drawn chevron | Recommended: one drawn chevron for every "opens more" text button |
| The site's task mock-up | **⌄ crates/storage/…** | Fixed: ▾ |
| Demo homes | `apps/web/demo.ts` reads the machine's own `oo`, so Settings › Accounts showed the author's real accounts in shots | Recommended: a stub `oo` as `demo-phone.ts` does (those shots were deleted) |

## Superseded
- **D185's and the glossary's key notation ⌘↩ / ⇧⌘↩** in the New task foot. Superseded by D211 (2): ⌘⏎ / ⇧⌘⏎.
- **The 推荐 / Suggested tag on the highlight fill** (`design/new-task.md`, both clients). Superseded by D211: the word in the highlight's ink, no capsule.
- **The conversation's offline strip** (a yellow strip under the header repeating the header's line). Superseded by D211: said once, in the header.
- **"Last %s ✓ done" / "上次%s ✓ 做完了" and "× 没成"** for a routine's last run. Superseded by D211 (3): words only.

## 2026-11-12: The audit's recommendations, decided (the author, away: "尽量不用我来做决策，第一性原理把这里的标准拉满")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D213 | **Built 2026-11-12, decided from first principles while the author was away, for their review. D211's recommendations, taken: the agent's coin only where it stands as an object; a dark-theme Codex coin; Cancel task in the task's foot; an update said in words; one drawn chevron everywhere; demos that never read this machine's accounts.** *Who:* all three; the author first, who saw a coin in the New task pill as an emoji on their iPhone. *Felt:* before, small sculpted marks sat in lines of text like emoji, a stray button stood alone under a header, a dot meant "update" only to those who knew; after, a line is words and drawn marks, the clay stands only where it is an object, and every control sits where its kind sits. (1) **The coin at 28 points and up only** (D183's art unchanged). In a pill (New task's who), a meta line (the opened task's head), an ask's heading (On hand and the conversation), a quiet line (a peer's line, the live view's sub-line, a device's browsing row, Settings › Partner's trusted and muted senders), the agent's name alone says who, in the line's own ink; where the line had no name (an ask's heading: the coin beside the task's title), nothing stands in for it: the title says what, the hover (Mac) and the task say who. Kept: the who and switch lists (28, 36, 40), the device tray (52) and its missing list (28), Settings › Partner (40), the loadout and the account sheet (44 and up). Since every coin that moved stood for a running task and was small, no coin moves now; the motion stays in the code for a large one that ever does. (2) **Codex on the dark theme:** a third look `coin_codex_dark`, the same sculpted object re-cast in bone clay (#DCD4C8) by `tools/art/coins.py` (`DARK`, `recast`: lightness re-centred, its spread kept, never inverted, so the top light and the over-and-under weave read as before), the way the mark itself turns light on a dark page; chosen over a pale plate (a plate is a tile behind a mark, D73) and over a new generation (the form is already right; only the clay is wrong for the ground). Web only: the theme shows one of the two images (`.coin-art.has-dark`), so a theme switch needs no re-render; the Mac app has no dark theme yet, so it ships no dark coin. Seen on the iOS Simulator, dark, iPhone and iPad (`.shots/quality-audit-2/`). (3) **不做了 / Cancel task in the foot** (D210's), before **暂停 / Pause**; with Esc armed, only Esc's words, 接着做 and 暂停; over to you, after 该你了's hint; otherwise, until the task finishes, the foot holds only 不做了 at its end. Still undone from its toast (D207). Gone from the opened task's header on the phone (where it stood alone), the whole-screen web header and the Mac's title bar. (4) **An update in words:** the device list says **有更新 / Update available** after the state word, in the highlight's ink and weight 700, and its accessible name says it; the dot is gone there, and from the Mac's version rows, where the words beside it already said so. The coin tray's dot (D161 (3)) stays for now: it marks an object, with the coin's label saying it, and changing the tray is a D161 call. (5) **One drawn chevron** for every link, text button or row that opens more: 12 pt, a 1.8 stroke, the text's ink (a row's faint), 3 pt after the text, turned down while open, pointing left for the way back. Web: `Chev` in `controls.tsx` (`Btn chev` draws the same; the views' own copy is gone); Mac: `drawChevron` (`quietLink`, the task line, the ask's heading, a file row). Replaced: every font › ‹ in the web client (换谁来做, an ask's heading, a task line, proof links, folder changes, the accounts sheet's links, a call row's toggle, the device list, Settings' back) and the Mac (`quietLink`, the task line, the ask's heading, a changed file's row); "看了 3 次，变了 1 次 ›" was not a control and lost its ›. A path in words (设置 › 账号) is prose and keeps it. A wrapped group in the task's meta line never starts with "·": the dot sits outside its group and is clipped at a line's start. (6) **Demos never read this machine's oo:** `apps/web/demo-oo.ts` (`noOo`) is passed to every demo core that had none; demos that show accounts keep their own fakes. Not changed: the agent's English step titles in zh views (its own words) and a branch in mono (D78) | U (the request and its seven calls), L (the reasons above) | `apps/web/src/{coin,controls,newtask,transcript,views,devcard,live,phone,accounts,i18n}.ts(x)`, `apps/web/static/{app.css,app.js,art/coin_codex_dark.webp}`, `apps/web/demo*.ts`, `packages/core/src/web.ts`, `tools/art/coins.py`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/{components,quiet,onhand,tasksteps,taskglyphs,liveview,peers,sites,newtask,task,taskturns,abilities,devupdate,devicecard,conversation}.go,i18n/devupdate.go}`, `design/ui.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/ui-devices-card.md`, `design/where.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `docs/install.md` |

## Superseded
- **D183's and D161 (9)'s coin beside text** (New task's who pill, the opened task's meta line, an ask's heading, the peer line, the live view, a device's browsing row, Settings' trusted and muted rows; 16–22 points) and the coin's motion while its task works or waits (`design/ui.md` motion table). Superseded by D213 (1): the coin only at 28 points and up; the name alone in a line.
- **D148 (9)'s and D161's "one quiet dot by its name"** in the device list (and the dot beside the Mac's version line). Superseded by D213 (4): **有更新 / Update available** in words.
- **D207's 不做了 in the opened task's header** (the phone's own row under the head, the whole-screen header, the Mac's title bar). Superseded by D213 (3): in the foot, before 暂停.
- **The font chevrons › ‹ ⌄ as affordances, and D211's "`Btn chev` for an opens-in-place text button" as the only drawn one.** Superseded by D213 (5): one drawn chevron for everything that opens more.

## 2026-11-12: The opened task's workbench (the author: "focus window 界面（mobile 宽屏也包括）还是不能满足所有的生产需要，像我们在用 lorca 或者 conductor, t3 code 这类项目时候，拥有掌控感模式下还会想要（默认不展示）看和操作其他的东西，比如右边多出一栏的 artifact、网站预览、文件树，单个文件预览编辑等等…估计 computer use, phone use 也要一起结合着设计")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D214 | **Built 2026-11-12 (web: wide and phone, which is also the iPhone, iPad and Android apps; Mac: Changes, Files with the editor, Preview, Browser), decided from first principles while the author rested, for their review. The opened task has a workbench, 工作台 / Workbench: its control surface beside the conversation, hidden until asked for, its pages only when they have something.** *Who:* the heavy developer first (they want the diff, the files and the page the agent builds beside it, as in Conductor, Codex or t3code, and their task often runs on a server); the consumer user meets a small drawn panel mark that appears only when there is something; the fresh user sees nothing new. *Feel:* before, counts and per-edit diffs but nothing to open, fix or look at, least of all a page served on hel; after, one key and the work stands beside the conversation, a line fixed by hand reloads the page and the agent is told. *First time:* zero setup; Preview finds the page by itself. (1) **Shape.** Wide (the D184 whole screen), a column at the right, 42% by default (380–760 px), resizable and kept on the device; from the two panes, opening it takes the task to the whole screen and closing gives it back. Phone and narrow: a sheet over the task with a strip of pages that swipe. Mac: the same column, 520 pt, MyGo's editor and diff view. ⌥⌘B shows or hides it (the right panel's key in VS Code, Zed, Codex and t3code, research/59); open/closed and the page are the device's own. (2) **Pages**: **改动 / Changes** (against the base branch or the D142 copy; a file's diff; **改回去 / Undo** one file, asked first, not while it works), **文件 / Files** (the working copy as a lazy, windowed tree; a file with its colours; **改 / Edit**, **存 / Save**), **预览 / Preview** (the page it serves, in a frame; 手机 / 平板 / 铺满 widths; **在浏览器里打开 / Open in browser**), **浏览器 / Browser** (the D190 live view, inside the workbench), **手机 / Phone** (the phone-use view registers itself), **成果 / Results** (its pictures and links). Browser, Phone and later Computer share one **Screen** frame with who is driving: **它在用 / It's driving** · **你在用 / You're driving**. (3) **Editing**: a save names the version opened; a file the agent wrote since is refused with **你打开以后，它又改了这个文件。** and **看它的版本 / 用我的覆盖**; atomic, mode kept, 1 MB cap. **The agent hears it with its next turn** (one line before the next thing said to it: which files the user edited or put back, read them again, keep their changes), said once, never in the user's words in the Log; an idle task is not woken for it. (4) **Confinement**: every op runs on the computer holding the working copy (a worker through its socket), paths resolved with links and refused outside the task's worktree, folder or own scratch folder, or in Ducter's data folder (D207); caps on every op. (5) **Preview** finds pages from listening ports whose process works in the working copy (lsof, `/proc`) and local addresses the task's output named, each checked to answer; each previewed page gets **a port of the home's own** (an origin of its own: absolute paths, its storage and hot reload kept, never Ducter's origin), forwarded to where it runs (loopback; a worker's socket, HTTP only for now), behind the preview's token traded for a cookie of that port; Ducter's CSP frames only its own host's ports. Where the device can't reach that port (one HTTPS address, an SSH tunnel), **用 Ducter 的浏览器看 / View in Ducter's browser** opens it in Ducter's browser on the task's computer and streams it (the D190 live view; t3code's approach). (6) **Restraint and speed**: nothing is read while it is closed but one look for pages when a task opens; lists windowed, diffs paged, a small highlighter (no editor library on the web). *Taken from:* t3code (per-task panel, the screencast for a far page, ports only from the task's own processes, ⌥⌘B), Codex (per-file revert), Claude Code desktop (Override on conflict), Zed (tell the agent the user edited), Conductor (what a right panel holds). *Ducter's own:* pages only when they have something, the preview origin per page through the home with the browser fallback, the same workbench on a phone as a swipeable sheet, one Screen frame with who is driving, the agent told once with its next turn. *Not in this pass (todo):* a terminal, comments on diff lines as context, a worker page's hot reload, per-hunk revert, Mac width resizing and Results/Phone pages on the Mac. *Runner-up for (5):* the screencast for every preview (works everywhere, as t3code does), rejected as the default because a real frame keeps the page's own fidelity, scrolling and hot reload where it can be reached | U (2026-11-12), R59 | `packages/protocol/src/workbench.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{workbench,brief,remote,worker-host,runner}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{workbench,preview,ws,web,core}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{workbench.tsx,workbench-screens.ts,hl.ts,i18n-workbench.ts,live.tsx,main.tsx,views.tsx,app.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,views,i18n}/workbench*.go`, tests, `design/{workbench,product-model,ui,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/getting-started.md` |

## 2026-11-12: The Chinese copy, to native product quality (the author: 「我之前注意到那个'不做了'的表达非常不 Native speaker 哈，类似的表达请你仔细审视杜绝一下…」)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D212 | **Built 2026-11-12 (the author asleep; defaults taken). Every Chinese string was read against the best zh-Hans product copy (Apple's system apps, WeChat, Feishu, Notion), and about 400 keys changed on web, Mac, iOS, Android, the CLI and core's fixed lines.** *Who:* all three, the fresh user most: a button that reads like a remark (不做了, 算了, 先不搬) makes the whole product feel unfinished. *Feel:* before, warm but loose, the same act said four ways, half the buttons spoken asides; after, it reads like a product a careful team wrote, and the partner's own voice stays warm. *The rules applied* (they refine D66, which tested each word by "would a person say it to a friend?"; that test stays for the partner's sentences and status lines, not for controls): (1) **A button is a verb phrase that makes sense out of context**, in the words every zh app uses: 取消 / 删除 / 移除 / 保存 / 编辑 / 添加 / 安装 / 连接 / 关闭 / 完成 / 继续 / 停止 / 重启 / 查看 / 重命名 / 清除 / 停用 / 保留 / 试听 / 拨打; never 不X了, 先不X, 算了, 就这样, 好了 or a verb plus 上 (装上, 加上, 连上, 存好). (2) **A dialog's dismiss is 取消**; "not now" on an offer is **以后再说**, or **暂不 + verb** when the act needs naming (暂不安装, 暂不付款, 暂不拨打, 暂不登录, 暂不转移). (3) **One verb per act everywhere**: 继续 resumes (a routine, a paused foot, a wait: 继续等), 停止 stops, 还原 undoes a folder, 安装 installs, 连接 connects, 添加 adds, 密钥 is a key. (4) **A status says the fact**: 已取消, 已连接, 已复制, 已更新到 X, 未安装, 未登录, 已暂停；a failure is **没能 + verb** (没能更新, 没能打开, 没能保存, 没能装好), never a clipped 没X成. (5) **Progress is 正在 + verb + …** for system work (正在连接…, 正在安装…, 正在检查…); the partner's own 在想… / 在查… stay, they are its voice. (6) **The partner stays warm, not cute**: no 呀 in its greetings, no 弄 / 干活 / 歇着 in settings or status; it still says 你, 在呢, 好，现在就打. (7) **Glossary concept words stay** (事, 手头的事, 等你 / 在做 / 在等 / 做完了 / 没成, 好 / 先等等, 伙伴, 例行, 换谁来做, 怎么知道的), except where clearly wrong. *The words, old → new (zh; en kept unless noted):* the opened task: 不做了 → **取消任务** (the state → **已取消**, the toast 「X」不做了 → **已取消「X」**), 该你了 → **等你回复** (with 它停在这儿了。回它一句，它就接着做。 → **它停在这里了，回复后会接着做。**), the foot's 接着做 → **继续**, 它忙完手上的就看 → **它忙完手上的事就会看到**, 交代给它了 → **交给它了** (D207 had retired 交代 elsewhere), 开工了 → **开始了**, 用了 {d} → **用时 {d}**, 细节收起来了 → **细节已收起**; New task: 定个时间 / 定期做 / 等某件事发生 → **定时 / 定期 / 等条件满足** (helps 到时间自动开始 · 每次到时间新建一件 · 现在新建，条件满足后再做 · 没做到就再试一次; 马上开始 → 立即开始), 定好 → **安排**, 开了：「X」 / 定好了： → **已开始：「X」 / 已安排：**, 加一个文件夹 / 加进来 → **添加文件夹 / 添加**, 找一个文件夹… → **选择文件夹…**, 从 Git 地址加… → **从 Git 地址添加…**, a one-time start's 不做了 → **取消**; calls: 打过去 / 现在就打 / 到点再打 / 先不打 / 不打了 → **拨打 / 立即拨打 / 到时拨打 / 暂不拨打 / 取消通话**, 挂了 → **已挂断**, 转到了自动语音 → **接通的是自动语音**, 等你点头 → **等你确认**, 不说 → **不透露**, 连上 Tuwa → **连接 Tuwa**, 不用 Tuwa 打电话了 → **断开 Tuwa**; folders (D142): 改回去 / 就这样 / 改回这个 → **全部还原 / 保留改动 / 还原这项**, 随时能改回去，留到 {date} → **{date}前都能还原**; waits and loops: 不等了 / 现在看一眼 / 接着等 / 停下 → **停止等待 / 立即查看 / 继续等 / 停止**; routines: 接着来 → **继续**, 停着： → **已暂停：**, 留着 → **保留**; decision cards: 先不用 → **暂不使用 / 以后再说 / 暂不登录** by card, 还是先问我 → **仍然先问我**, 以后你的 X 发来的活，直接接？ / 直接接 → **以后你的 X 发来的事，直接接下？ / 直接接下**, 照样发送 / 不发了 → **仍然发送 / 取消**, 搬走 / 先不搬 / 合上之前搬走 → **转移 / 暂不转移 / 合盖前转移**; accounts: 钥匙 → **密钥** (OpenConnector's page is English; every zh product says 密钥, and the glossary already kept 密钥 / Key), 加一个 → **添加**, 去拿钥匙 → **获取密钥**, 算了 → **取消**, 不用{name}了 → **停用 {name}**; devices and abilities: 连上 (button) → **连接**, 取下 → **移除**, 装上 → **安装**, 改名字 → **重命名**, 就叫这个 → **保存** (the partner's own naming keeps 就叫这个), 没装 / 没登录 → **未安装 / 未登录**, 在 Finder 中显示 → **在访达中显示** (macOS's own words, as D198 meant); Settings: 让 Ducter 歇着 / 歇着了 → **让 Ducter 休息 / 在休息**, 跟着系统 → **跟随系统**, 听一下 → **试听**, 都好 / 缺 → **一切正常 / 缺失**, 留着它 / 停掉它 → **保留 / 停用**, 配对码 (left in one line) → **连接码**; pictures: 加图片 / 拿掉 / 存到下载了 → **添加图片 / 移除 / 已存到「下载」**; the browser: 换我来 → **接手** (the glossary's word), 好了 / 关掉 → **完成 / 关闭**, 全部清掉 → **全部清除**; autofill: 存一张卡 / 存一个登录 / 存好 / 删掉 / 改 → **添加卡片 / 添加登录信息 / 保存 / 删除 / 编辑**; the transcript's steps: 在 Mem 里搜了搜 / 连上 Mem / 翻了翻 → **搜索 Mem / 连接 Mem / 滚动了页面**. The whole table (every key, old → new, where) is `design/zh-copy-review-2026-11-12.md`. *Not changed:* the partner's persona and tool descriptions (no eval needed, D175); its one mention of 接着等 waits for the next persona change (`todo.md`). Runner-up: change only the buttons the author named, rejected because the same pattern (不打了, 不等了, 不发了, 先不搬, 算了) sat on every screen | U (2026-11-12, the author's message), `design/glossary-review-2026-11-09.md` (D66), `~/.claude/skills/humanizer-zh` | `apps/web/src/i18n*.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/i18n/*.go`, `packages/cli/src/i18n.ts`, `apps/ios/Ducter/zh-Hans.lproj/Localizable.strings`, `apps/android/app/src/main/res/values-zh/strings.xml`, `packages/core/src/{phone,closing,mem-plugins,routines,push}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/partner.ts`, tests, `design/{product-model,ui,new-task,phone,connectors,autofill,focus-window,where,ai-loadout,bridge,todo,…}.md` |

## Superseded
- **D207 (6)'s 不做了 / Cancel task, the state 不做了 / Cancelled and the toast 「X」不做了, 该你了, the hint 它停在这儿了。回它一句，它就接着做。, and 它忙完手上的就看** (also D210 (6), (8)'s foot words and 接着做 / Keep going, and D213's 不做了 in the foot). Superseded by D212: 取消任务 · 已取消 · 已取消「X」· 等你回复 · 它停在这里了，回复后会接着做。· 它忙完手上的事就会看到 · 继续.
- **D185's 什么时候开始 options 定个时间 / 定期做 / 等某件事发生 with their help lines, 定好, 开了：「X」, 定好了：, and the one-time start's 不做了 / Don't do it.** Superseded by D212: 定时 · 定期 · 等条件满足 · 安排 · 已开始：「X」· 已安排： · 取消.
- **D186's 加一个文件夹 / 加进来, D203's 找一个文件夹… / 在加…, D204's 从 Git 地址加….** Superseded by D212: 添加文件夹 · 添加 · 选择文件夹… · 正在添加… · 从 Git 地址添加….

- **D142's 改回去 / 就这样 / 改回这个 / 随时能改回去，留到 {date}.** Superseded by D212: 全部还原 · 保留改动 · 还原这项 · {date}前都能还原.
- **D112's and D145's 不等了 / 现在看一眼 / 接着等, D113's 停下, D114's 接着来, 停着： and 留着.** Superseded by D212: 停止等待 · 立即查看 · 继续等 · 停止 · 继续 · 已暂停： · 保留.

## 2026-11-12: A number no line calls gets a call sheet you call yourself (research/60; the author: "不考虑中国了", generalized from a +86-only first cut)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D215 | **Built 2026-11-12, for the author's review (the author asleep; decided per "decide and ship overnight"). When the number is in a country no calling line Ducter has calls (Tuwa's 20 countries are the coverage; mainland China is one of the uncovered places, research/60 §4 option D), Ducter dials nothing and the partner makes a call sheet (通话单) instead: what the owner needs to make the call themselves, with one tap to dial on a phone.** *Who:* the consumer and the fresh user who asked "call the restaurant for me" for a place Tuwa doesn't reach; before, a dead end ("Tuwa's numbers don't call that country", D193 (6)); after, a ready script and one tap, then the outcome kept like any call (好用, 放心: nothing is faked). (1) **Detection is coverage, nothing country-specific:** `reaches(provider, number)` in `core/src/phone.ts`: with no calling connection, or with Tuwa, the number's country code must be in `TUWA_REACHES`; another provider decides for itself. Emergency and short numbers are refused first, as before. A sheet needs no connection (connecting Tuwa wouldn't help). (2) **The sheet** is a `phone_call` snapshot with `plan.from = "self"` and `plan.sheet` (`why: uncovered`, `open` the first thing to say, `ask`, `dont` = the owner's don'ts plus what stays private, `note`), no provider, no cost, no hours; its card is a `decision` with `call.kind = "sheet"`: **打给 {name} 的通话单 / Call sheet: {name}**, the line **这个国家或地区我还打不过去。照着这张单子，你自己打就行。 / I can't call numbers in that country yet. Here's what to say, so you can call it yourself.** (On hand; in the conversation the partner has just said it), **号码 / Number** with **复制号码 / Copy number** (→ **已复制 / Copied**), **要办的事 / What for**, **先说 / Open with** set apart on the sunken ground as the line to read out, **要问 / Ask**, **可以说 / May say**, **别说 / Don't share**, **记下 / Note down**; buttons **先不打 / Not now** · **打完了 / Done calling** · **自己打 / Call it yourself** (a `tel:` link; the iPhone and Android apps hand it to the dialer; primary until tapped, then 打完了 is). The Mac has no dialer: Copy number, and Go is 打完了. The sheet stays readable after it is answered. (3) **After:** 打完了 resolves the card (the snapshot becomes `done`, no outcome yet); the answered card wakes the partner, which asks in one short question how it went and records the user's words with `phone_call do=result` (`reached: false` → `no_answer`); said in the conversation before tapping, the partner records it and the card is answered by the partner (no second wake). On hand: **等你自己打 · X / For you to call · X**, then **打完了 · X / Called X** with their words, or **你自己打的 / You made this call** until they say. A sheet snapshot never wakes the partner on its own. (4) **The partner:** the tool's draft takes `open`, `ask`, `note`; a draft for an uncovered number without `open` is answered "phone_call again with open, ask, note"; after a sheet the partner says plainly in one line that it can't call there yet (that line is kept, unlike the dropped line after a Go card). Persona §Phone calls gains one bullet; partner eval `call-sheet-zh` and `call-sheet-done-en`. (5) **Later, not built:** a second calling provider that does reach the number (the owner's own Android phone and SIM, built concurrently: research/60 §4 A) is found by `reaches` before the sheet, and its Go card replaces the sheet with no change to this flow; the Mac could hand `tel:` to an iPhone through Continuity. *Alt:* refuse with one line as before (no new card; leaves the user with nothing). | U (the coordinator for the author), R60, R53 §8 | `packages/core/src/phone.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{phone,index,internal}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,persona,render}.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/{scenarios,run}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{phone.tsx,i18n-phone.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/phone.go,i18n/phone.go,views/{phone,components,bubbles,quiet}.go}`, `design/phone.md` §3a, `design/product-model.md`, `design/scenarios.md` B6, `docs/calls.md` |

## Superseded
- **D193 (6)'s "Ducter drafts nothing elsewhere and says so"** for countries Tuwa doesn't call, and the Go card's note **打到中国大陆的号码，多半接不通。 / Calls to mainland China numbers likely won't connect.** Superseded by D215: a call sheet the owner calls themselves.

## 2026-11-12: On hand as a workspace (the author: "t3 code 的 conductor-like 的 uiux 很完备优雅…我们参考它和 lorca 让 ducter 也能拥有一样级别的体验哈 … 当用户进入 on hands 时候，我们不能完全抄袭，但是核心的能做的事情可以充分参考他们补齐")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D216 | **Built 2026-11-12 (web: phone, beside the conversation, the whole screen and the rail; the Mac: the list, Find, the keys, hover actions, ⌘O), decided from first principles while the author was away; for review. On hand is one list in every room. A row says what the task is doing now, in words. Repo facts show only on repo tasks. Keys, a switcher, bulk actions and a rail beside an opened task appear when there is a keyboard, a pointer and room.** *Who:* the heavy developer with 20 to 40 tasks across repos, who wants Conductor's sense of control without reading four columns of cards; the consumer with a call, a booking and a routine, who wants "what needs me, what is happening" in plain words; the fresh user with one errand, who must see one friendly row and nothing else. *Feel:* before, the whole screen was four side-by-side lanes of cards (D184 (3)). They were fine with 8 tasks and a wall with 40. Nothing could be walked with keys, merging meant opening each task, and nothing on a card said what an agent was doing right now. After, one calm list: what needs you is answered in place at the top, every other task is one two-line row, and the second line moves as the agent works. *First time:* nothing to learn. One errand is one row ("订 12 月 6 号京都的酒店 / 在 Chrome 里打开 booking.com"); Find appears at ten tasks, By project at two projects, and keys only where a keyboard is. (1) **One list, four rooms:** the phone's page, the pane beside the conversation, the whole screen (a 960 measure), and a 340-wide **rail** beside a task opened at the whole screen (from 1360 CSS px, or 1720 with the task's workbench, D214, open). The sections are 等你 / 在做 / 在等 / 今天做完 (Needs you / Working / Waiting / Done today), or by project. Each section's tasks are one clay card of rows parted by a soft line; a call and a task-less wait keep their own cards. In the rail what needs you is a row too: its answer is in the task beside it. (2) **The row:** line one is the title, then **+a −d** (mono, done and failed inks, a zero side left out), **草稿 PR / 已合并** (Draft PR / Merged), the state word (only by project, where the section doesn't say it), and the time (how long it has worked: **12 分钟**; else when it changed: **3 分钟前**). Line two is the story: for working, its **latest step in D210's words with that step's mark** (**运行 bun test**, the command in mono); for needs you, the question in the needs ink; for waiting, what it waits for (with the groove of a check); for done, the first line of the result; for didn't work, the result in the failed ink. On wide screens it is followed by the branch in mono, the computer (with two or more, D157) and the agent. Finished rows recede (ink-soft, 600); what needs you never does. (3) **The card carries what the row says** (`TaskCard.latest`: the latest step or words, long strings cut to 400 and outputs to 300 characters, read once per task from the stream's tail and then kept live; `TaskCard.integrated`: a merge or a draft PR the user made, from the `job_status` a Merge or an Open draft PR logs, now with `act` and `ref`). A row is therefore right on the first frame (D209's glance, a fresh device) before the client holds the task's events. A client uses a newer event it holds. (4) **Hover offers the one next step** in the time's place (pointer, wide): **合进 main / 开草稿 PR** (Merge into main / Open draft PR) for finished work on a branch (D125's actions, unchanged), **暂停 / Pause** for work under way. (5) **Keys** (where a keyboard is): j/k or the arrows walk the list, Enter opens, `/` goes to Find, Esc lets go of a selection, then of Find, then goes up a level (D184). In the rail, j/k open the next and the previous task. **⌘O 打开一件事 / Open a task** opens the switcher everywhere (the Mac's menu and key, the iPad's menu, the web): a field (**打开哪件事？打几个字… / Which task? Type a few words…**), every task the client knows, a title match first, then what needs you, what works, what waits, the newest; words that match nothing offer **新建：「…」 / New task: "…"**, which opens New task (D185) with them. ⌘K stays "talk to the partner" and ⌘1 to ⌘4 the levels (they are Ducter's; T3 and Conductor give those keys to the palette and the jumps). (6) **Find** (**找一件事… / Find a task…**, from ten tasks): every word typed must appear in the title, project, folder or branch, agent, computer or story; **没有对得上的。 / Nothing matches.** (7) **Many at once** (the whole screen, pointer or keys): ⌘-click, ⇧-click or `x` selects; a raised bar at the list's foot says **选了 3 件 / 3 selected** and offers only what fits each task, counted: **合并 n 件 · 开 n 个草稿 PR · 暂停 n 件 · n 件不做了…** (Merge n · Open n draft PRs · Pause n · Cancel n…). Cancel asks once (**这 n 件都不做了？ 都不做了 / 再想想**); **不选了 / Deselect** (Esc). (8) **By project** is offered from two projects (one project, or errands only, would be a toggle that does nothing), and its rows are the same rows with the state word. (9) **Hundreds stay smooth:** rows are memoized on what they draw, rows off screen skip layout and paint (`content-visibility`), and the latest step is parsed once per event. Measured headless (Chromium, 1440 wide, 413 rows, a loaded machine): j to a painted frame in two frames (33 ms), Find narrowing 413 to 10 in two frames. The Mac draws the same rows in immediate mode. (10) **Not taken:** status dots (D73), settle and snooze (Ducter's finished tasks already leave On hand after a day and are archived by the runner; a verb waits for someone to ask), ⌘1 to ⌘9 jumps, a `>` action palette, CI on the row (needs a PR watcher; todo), cost on the row (todo), split compare. *Taken:* from T3 Code, the row's status slot with time and repo facts, receding rows, mod/shift selection with counted bulk actions, palette ranking by title, and hover actions in the time's place; from Conductor, PR state on the row and the list beside the opened task; from Lorca, a line that never shows a tool call yet says what happened, and attention answered at the top, apart from the list (research/61). *Ducter's own:* the latest step in words on every row, from D210's model; repo facts only where there is a repo; answers in place; density that grows with use. Runner-up for (1): keep the four lanes at the whole screen and add only the rail. Rejected because the lanes can't be walked with keys or selected in bulk, and two layouts of one list are two things to learn. *Defaults the author may revisit:* ⌘O for the switcher; the rail's 1360 and 1720 thresholds; Find from ten tasks; the time on working rows as how long it has worked. | U (2026-11-12), R61, R58 | `packages/protocol/src/index.ts` (`TaskCard.latest`, `integrated`, `job_status.act/ref`), `packages/core/src/{registry,ws}.ts`, `packages/core/test/onhand-latest.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/{onhand.tsx,board.tsx,views.tsx,main.tsx,wide.ts,app.ts,transcript.tsx,i18n-onhand.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-onhand.ts`, `apps/web/test/onhand.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{onhandlist,onhand,byproject,app}.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/protocol.go,i18n/onhand.go}`, `apps/desktop/main.go`, `apps/ios/Ducter/{DucterApp.swift,*.lproj/Localizable.strings}`, `design/{product-model,ui,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/on-hand.md` |
