## 2026-11-12: 用手机 / Use a phone (the author: "请你看看把我们的 phone use 给做了，这样 ducter 就自带手机，可以做更多有意思的事情了，看看之前我让你调研的 pinky 是否适合，可以的话，我们先在 macOS 上支持一下")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D217 | **Built 2026-11-12 on a scratch home (defaults taken; for the author's review on a real Mac). Ducter has its own phone: an Android 16 phone with Google Play that Ducter installs on a Mac, starts when something needs it and stops when nothing does; the user installs apps and signs in on it themselves, in a live view where the task can't see; tasks then use those apps through Ducter's own phone tools, and every tap that sends, posts, pays, installs or deletes waits for the user's Go on a card.** *Who:* the consumer personal-AI user first (their life is in apps with no web version: 小红书, 美团, a bank's app they never let Ducter near), then the heavy developer (testing an app, reading an app-only dashboard), and the fresh user who meets it as one more chip on their Mac's card. *Feel:* before, "it can only do things on the web"; after, "it has a phone of its own, with my apps on it, and it asks me before it sends anything" (好用, 放心: the same Go as payments; 惊喜: a phone appears on a Mac with no Android Studio). *How it starts:* the faded **用手机 / Use a phone** ghost (D199) on the Mac's card becomes **+ 用手机**; or the user asks for something in an app and the partner says in one line where to add it. (1) **Pinky is not the gateway (research/62 §1).** Its approvals and even an agent's first connection need its own window (`agent.go:877-935`, `control.go:160-166`; remote approval still its M4), it makes no phone (Android Studio's AVDs, started from its window), and it ships 15+ releases a day with telemetry and updates on by default. Ducter drives the phone itself over adb, as it drives its own browser (D151); Pinky stays the candidate for the user's own physical phones and iPhones once it can be approved from outside its window. Its ideas are kept, not its code: the gate in code at the effect, commit verbs in several languages, money and key apps out of reach, credential fields refused, the screen re-checked before an approved tap. (2) **The phone:** Google's platform-tools 37.0.1, emulator 37.2.13 and the Android 16 (API 36) Google Play arm64 image r07, pinned by version, size and SHA-1 from Google's repository (2.32 GB), downloaded at install time into `<dataDir>/phone` (never `~/Library/Android`, `~/.android` or adb's 5037: every tool runs with `ANDROID_*` inside that folder and adb on port 5137); no Java or sdkmanager (the zips unpack into the SDK layout, Ducter writes the AVD: 720×1600, 3 GB RAM, Play on, quick boot). Phase 1: Apple Silicon Macs, the home only; another computer shows the ghost. (3) **Adding it is one sheet:** what it is, **下载 约 2.3 GB · 占用空间 约 10 GB · 装什么 · 什么时候开着**, Google's two licenses by name with their full text one tap away (read from Google's own XML at that moment), one switch **我读过，也同意这些许可协议 / I've read and agree to these licenses**, then **加上 / Add it**. The home installs only with the texts as they read now (their SHA-256 recorded), refuses without room, checks every download and installs nothing that doesn't match. The card shows **正在下载手机 n% / 正在解开… / 手机快好了…** with a bar, and **可以先去做别的，装好了这里会变。** (4) **Running only when needed:** started by a task's first phone tool or an opened live view, held while a task that needs it works or a view is open, stopped five minutes after the last use (`emu kill` saves a quick-boot snapshot: 6 s to start again, vs 17 s cold and about a minute the very first time); the card says **Ducter 的手机开着。没事的时候它会自己关上。** or **…关着，要用的时候自己会开。** Animations are off on it. (5) **Where a task runs:** needs gain `phone` (`task_open` needs {phone:true}); only a computer with 用手机 on takes it (`no_phone`, **要用手机，{device} 上没加**; where line **只有 {device} 上有 Ducter 的手机**). The task's brief gains a phone section and its MCP lists ten tools only while it needs the phone: `phone_look` (the screen as numbered things to tap, then its text, labelled as data; a picture when asked or when the screen won't read), `phone_tap`, `phone_type`, `phone_swipe`, `phone_key` (back, home, recents, enter, delete), `phone_apps`, `phone_open_app`, `phone_screenshot`, `phone_ask_go`, `phone_ask_user`. Text adb can't type (中文…) goes through the emulator's clipboard (its own gRPC, token from its discovery file) and a paste, then the clipboard is emptied; the Mac's clipboard is never touched. (6) **The gate at the effect, in code:** a tap whose button says pay, buy, send, post, delete, install, call, confirm or sign out (zh, en, ja) is refused until the user says Go on a card the task asks with `phone_ask_go` {what, app, ref}: the card says what in the task's words and **会在{app}里点「{button}」 / It will tap "{button}" in {app}**, with **看看手机 / See the phone · 先别 / Not yet · 点吧 / Tap it** (no default; answered **点了 / Tapped** or **先没点 / Not tapped**); the Go covers that one tap (same app, same button, 15 minutes), re-checked against the screen first. Banks, wallets, payment apps, password managers and authenticators are invisible and untouchable; password fields are never read or typed. The partner doesn't ask first for a send on the phone (persona rule 9, as for payments): the phone asks at the tap. (7) **The user's turns:** `phone_ask_user` {what} (sign in to an app, a code, a CAPTCHA) is a card **去手机上 / Open the phone · 先不用**, with **你在手机上做的时候，它看不见。**; the live view opens in the user's turn, **好了 / Done** answers it. While it is the user's turn every phone tool refuses before touching the screen and the tasks that tried are told on hand-back. (8) **The live view is D190's, for the phone:** the same bars, band, calm step line and turns (**换我来 / 还给它**), the screen in its phone shape on the sunken page, Android's keys drawn under it in the user's turn (返回 · 主屏幕 · 最近用过), taps, drags as swipes, scroll, keys and text (IME text committed once). Frames are the device's own JPEG (`screencap -j`, 83 ms), one in flight until acked, at most 6 a second, none while the screen is still. Three ways in: **打开手机 / Open the phone** on the card (the user's turn), **看看 / Watch** beside a task using it on the card, **看它用手机 / Watch it use the phone** in the opened task and **看看手机** on a Go card; steps read **正在看屏幕 · 正在点「{label}」 · 正在往「{label}」里打字 · 正在滑动 · 正在按「{key}」 · 正在打开 {app} · 正在截图 · 等你用完**. It is one self-contained component (web `PhoneLivePanel`, Mac `phoneView`) with a small interface (computer, task or none, mode watch / takeover / signin; turn changes out), mounted full screen now and in the opened task's side panel once that exists. (9) **Its data and wiping it:** apps, sign-ins and data stay in the phone (`<dataDir>/phone/home/avd`); in 用手机's ⓘ, **抹掉这台手机 / Erase this phone** asks in place (**手机上的 app、登录和数据都会删掉，之后是一台新手机。** · 留着 · 抹掉) and leaves a fresh phone. (10) **Art:** the existing 用手机 clay (`ab_phone`, the D199 ghost's live sibling) on the chip and the sheet; no new metaphor. *Measured (research/62 §5):* install 3 min 25 s here, 3.5 GB after install and about 10 GB once the phone has run (its storage and the snapshot), ~5 GB RAM while it runs, a few % CPU idle; a real task through the scratch home (read the Android version in Settings, two screenshots) took 1 min 41 s, and a send in Messages went out once after Go. *Alt:* Pinky's desktop app beside Ducter (best for physical phones; a second app, window and approval surface, unusable from the phone); scrcpy video for the live view (smoother, a decoder in every client) | U (the author), L, R59 | `packages/core/src/android/*`, `packages/core/src/{core,ws,devices,candidates,loadout,decisions,mcp,web}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{phone-use,index,loadout}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{brief,runner}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{persona,partner}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{phone-use.tsx,i18n-phoneuse.ts,devcard.tsx,sheet.ts,views.tsx,main.tsx,app.ts,where.ts}`, `apps/web/static/{app.css,art/ab_phone.webp}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,store,views,i18n}/phoneuse.go`, `design/phone-use.md`, `design/product-model.md`, `design/scenarios.md`, `design/todo.md`, `docs/devices.md`, `research/62-phone-use.md` |

## 2026-11-12: Calls from your own number (the author: "做一下 物理手机+ sim 卡方式，用它打电话，这个适用于全世界的场景"; decided overnight from first principles, defaults taken)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D218 | **Built 2026-11-12, verified only with fakes and the Android emulator (no phone, cable or key here); for the author's review. A second calling provider: 用你自己的号码打 / Call from your own number. The owner's own Android phone and SIM, plugged into a computer of theirs, places the call; Ducter speaks through a real-time voice model on that computer. It works wherever the SIM can call, mainland China included, behind the same Go card and every D189/D193/D208 rail.** *Who:* the heavy developer abroad calling China, or wanting their own number shown; the mainland user, for whom no AI calling service is open to individuals (research 60); the fresh user, who meets one card that ticks itself off. *Feel:* before, "it can't call there" or "they won't pick up an unknown number"; after, the call goes out as them, said honestly as their AI assistant. (1) **Hardware:** an Android phone with USB debugging on, plugged into the computer by USB (adb), and a wired cable that makes the phone think a headset is in: a USB-C headset adapter if needed, a CTIA splitter, a USB sound card, a −25 dB attenuator (about ¥100 / $15; the card's parts list and a drawn diagram). Bluetooth HFP (Linux only) is later. (2) **Setup, Settings › 账号 / Accounts, both clients:** one card, **用你自己的号码打 / Call from your own number**, five steps that tick themselves off as the home reads them, only the next one unfolded: **手机 / Phone** (adb found, else the one install line; the phone listed; **在手机上点「允许」 / Tap Allow on the phone**), **SIM 卡 / SIM** (state and carrier from two properties; the number from Your details or typed: the phone's own number isn't readable), **连线 / Cable** (the phone sees a headset; the computer's sound card found by name, the owner can pick another), **实时语音 / Live voice** (an API key: a Claude or ChatGPT plan has no live voice, said plainly), **试打一通 / Test call** (to a number the owner picks: a chime and one sentence from the computer's own voice, no AI, then **听到了 / Heard it** · **没听到 / Didn't**; Ducter checks it heard the line through the cable). The home looks for the phone every few seconds while Accounts is open, every minute once set up. (3) **The voice engine (research/63):** OpenAI Realtime `gpt-realtime-2.1` by default (one speech-to-speech model: sub-second turns, semantic VAD, barge-in by truncate, tools; about $0.20 a talking minute on the card, the cost measured from the usage it reports); **通义千问 / Qwen** (Qwen-Omni-Realtime, the same event protocol) for mainland China, where OpenAI isn't offered. The model gets the approved plan, Ducter's rails and the opening line, and three tools: ask the owner (the call's question card, D189 (5)), press keypad digits (phone menus; never to pay or agree to a charge), end the call with the outcome in its words. Voicemail or screening: no message, outcome automated. (4) **Which line:** `phone_call from=own` when the owner asks for their own number; else Ducter picks: their phone when they turned on **每通电话都用我的号码 / Use my number for every call**, or when Tuwa isn't connected or can't reach the country (+86 first); Tuwa otherwise. The card's **从 / From** says **你的号码 +86 139… (Pixel 7) / Your number … (Pixel 7)**; **大约 / About** counts the voice only, **话费走你的套餐 / the call on your plan**. Own-number calls speak in Ducter's voice only (no cloned voice here). (5) **Rails added for the owner's real phone:** adb is wrapped so it can do only six things: list phones, read five named properties, read the call state (only its lines are kept), read whether a headset is in, dial one E.164 number, hang up, press digits; never messages, contacts, files, the screen or another app, and never an incoming call answered; it runs on the adb server Ducter's own phone uses (D217), and finds adb in that SDK too. One call at a time; a Go waits while the owner is on their own call (up to 10 minutes, then **你的手机一直在通话 / Your phone stayed on another call**). At most 10 calls a day from the SIM (a person's errands, not a call centre's pattern). Nothing recorded: the transcript is text, as for Tuwa. Caller ID never hidden. The call runs only on the computer the phone is plugged into, never driven from far away. (6) **Live:** **正在用你的手机打给{name} / On a call from your phone to {name}** with **挂断 / Hang up** on the card; On hand's row adds **· 用你的手机 / · from your phone**; hang up from any client. (7) **Where (D157):** the home only in this build; a worker computer beside the phone is in todo. (8) **Mac:** the app asks for the microphone once (ffmpeg hears the sound card); `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription` and the helper's `audio-input` entitlement, to be checked in a signed build. (9) **Not verified, needs a phone, a cable and a key (research/63 §6):** real-network call states, keypad digits mid-call, headset detection words, levels through the cable, both engines' live session shapes and Qwen's price, microphone permission in the signed app. (10) **China:** the PRC lawyer's questions in research 60 §6 (反诈法 Art. 14, the disclosure wording) stay open before this is offered to mainland users at large. (11) **Partner:** `phone_call from=own` and one persona line; the eval's `call-from-own-number-zh` passes (haiku 75/91, calls 8/9; tool descriptions trimmed to stay under the ceiling after the merge with D217) | U, research 60, R63 | `packages/core/src/phone-own/{adb,audio,voice,index}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{phone,phone-provider,core,ws}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{phone,index}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,persona}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{own-phone,phone,app,accounts,views,i18n-phone}.tsx`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/ownphone.go,views/phone.go,protocol/phone.go,i18n/own.go,store/store.go}`, `scripts/make-app.sh`, `design/phone.md` §10, `docs/calls.md`, `research/63-own-number-calls.md` |

The workbench: 改回去 → **还原** (D212's verb for undoing changes), 改 / 存 / 在存… / 算了 / 没存 / 存好了 → **编辑 / 保存 / 正在保存… / 取消 / 未保存 / 已保存**, 删掉了 → **已删除**, 和 X 比 → **与 X 对比**, 看整个文件 / 看它的版本 / 看它上网 → **查看完整文件 / 查看它的版本 / 查看浏览器**, 铺满 → **全宽**, 没成功 → **没能完成**, 它正在做，改之前想一想：… → **它还在做，可能也在改这个文件。** On hand: 找一件事… / 没有对得上的。 → **搜索… / 没有匹配的结果。**, 选了 n 件 → **已选 n 件**, 开 n 个草稿 PR → **创建 n 个草稿 PR**, n 件不做了… / 这 n 件都不做了？ / 都不做了 / 再想想 → **取消 n 件… / 取消这 n 件事？ / 全部取消 / 保留** (the dismiss is 保留, not 取消, so the two buttons never read the same), 不选了 → **取消选择**, 打几个字 → **输入几个字**. Kept: 工作台, 打开一件事, 抹掉 (Apple's own), 现在你在用。它看不见这台手机，也不会动它。 (the same line D212 kept for the browser), 它看不见 (D212's live.blind). ## 2026-11-12: The workbench's phone and the working copy at the task's top (the author, with a T3 Code screenshot: a live iPhone simulator beside the thread and a thread info popover; "按建议", decide the details from first principles)

| D220 | **Built 2026-11-12 (web: wide and phone; the Mac app), decided from first principles while the author was away; for review. (1) **The name stays 手机 / Phone** (the glossary's page, D214): an iPhone simulator is a phone to every reader; 设备 / Device is taken by computers (D84) and would mislead. (2) **Ducter's phone in the page** (todo D217): the page mounts the live view watching the task (web `PhoneLivePanel` through `registerScreen("phone")`; Mac `phoneBody`, the full view's body), streaming only while its page is shown; the task head's **查看手机 / Watch it use the phone** opens the workbench at the page (the full-screen sheet stays for the device card, a card's sign-in turn, and a task without a workbench). Turns, the band and Hand it back are D190's, unchanged. (3) **The simulator, only what `simctl` does (research/64)**: frames by `simctl io <udid> screenshot` made small by `sips` on the task's computer, asked one after another while the page is in sight (≈250 ms and ≈35–70 KB a frame, so 3–4 a second); the rail, drawn marks with their names on hover and to screen readers: **主屏幕 / Home** (`simctl launch <udid> com.apple.springboard`, verified to keep the front app running), **切换到深色模式 / 切换到浅色模式 · Switch to dark / light mode**, **文字大小 / Text size** with four steps **小 · 默认 · 大 · 特大 / Small · Default · Large · Extra large** (iOS's `small`, `large`, `extra-extra-large`, `accessibility-large`, T3 Code's steps), **打开链接 / Open a link** (a field: **输入链接，如 https:// 或 myapp:// / A link, like https:// or myapp://**; never `file:`), **截图 / Screenshot** (the full PNG into the task's composer as a picture, D206: **截图已添加，会随下一条消息发给它。 / Screenshot added; it goes with your next message.**). **No taps, said plainly** under the screen: **这里只能看。要点按，请在 {computer} 上的模拟器里操作。 / View only. To tap, use Simulator on {computer}.** simctl has no input; every tool that taps a simulator loads Xcode's private frameworks (T3 Code installs npm tools for it after a consent step), which is a decision of its own (todo). **No rotate:** nothing reliable exists (T3 Code refuses it too). (4) **When it is the task's business:** a booted iPhone or iPad simulator on the task's computer, and either the task's own output touched one (`simctl`, `xcodebuild … Simulator`, "iOS Simulator", the home reads its Log) or its working copy holds an iOS app (an `.xcodeproj` / `.xcworkspace` or an `ios/` folder at its top or one level down, or under `apps/*` / `packages/*`). Off a Mac, without Xcode, or with none booted: no page, nothing said. (5) **Where it runs (D157):** three new `wb` ops (`sims`, `sim_frame`, `sim_act`) run on the computer that holds the task's working copy, so a worker Mac serves its own simulator through its socket with no new plumbing; an older worker answers "can't do this yet". Each udid is checked against the booted list, nothing goes through a shell. (6) **工作副本 / Working copy at the top:** a button in the task's header (the crumbs at the whole screen, the narrow header, the Mac's title bar) showing the branch (a repo task) or the folder's name (a folder task) in mono, cut in the middle when long, with a drawn branch or folder mark; on a phone only the mark. ## 2026-11-12: A release creates and revokes no Apple certificate (the author: every release brought Apple's mail that a certificate of "null null" was revoked)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D221 | **The iPhone release signs with one long-lived, cloud-managed Apple Distribution certificate and touches no other: the archive is built unsigned and ad-hoc signed (`codesign -s -`, no identity) with each bundle's entitlements resolved from the project, and only the export signs for real, with Xcode's automatic signing and the API key.** *Who:* the author, who got a revocation mail from Apple on every release and had to wonder whether something was wrong with the team's account (放心). *Cause, verified:* `xcodebuild archive` with `CODE_SIGN_STYLE=Automatic -allowProvisioningUpdates` signs with an Apple Development identity; a fresh GitHub runner has none, so each run made a new "Created via API" Development certificate owned by the API key (no person, hence "null null"), and `scripts/ci/prune-dev-certs.ts` (added at v0.0.11 when the account filled) revoked the previous run's one before archiving. The v0.0.28 run's log says "revoked 1 development certificate(s) earlier CI runs left", and the team's list then held one Development certificate created that day. *Why this way:* the archive's signature is discarded at export, so it needs no identity; signing the archive with "Apple Distribution" instead would need that certificate's private key on the runner, which the cloud-managed one never gives, so Xcode would make a new Distribution certificate each run, the same churn on a scarcer kind. An unsigned archive alone loses the entitlements (push, the shared keychain group, D159), hence the ad-hoc signature that carries them. *Alternative kept:* a fixed Apple Distribution `.p12` and App Store profile in secrets, as the Mac's Developer ID; more to rotate, used only if the cloud route fails. `prune-dev-certs.ts` is removed: nothing makes such certificates any more, and the one left from v0.0.28 expires on its own (revoking it would send the very mail this ends). `ios.yml` gains a manual run (`testflight=true`) that uploads for internal testers only, to check signing without a release. | the author; the v0.0.28 release log; the App Store Connect certificate list | `design/release/README.md`; code: `scripts/ci/testflight.sh`, `.github/workflows/ios.yml` |

## 2026-11-12: Calendars (the author: "我的 oomol 只有 gmail 没有 calendar，所以 ducter 没办法管理我的日程…我们也忘记在 Accounts 里有 calendar 的设置和支持了，同时也没有设计日历相关的东西如何在 UI 里渲染…我刚刚在 oomol 里给两个账号都加上了 google calendar"; decided while the author was away, defaults taken)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D222 | **Built 2026-11-12 with fakes (read-only checks against the author's two real accounts; no write ever run on them); for the author's review. A person has one day, not N calendars: Ducter reads every calendar account its sources hold and every calendar inside each that the person lets it read, merges them in the person's zone, and changes anything only through a structured card it runs once on Go.** *Who:* the heavy developer with a personal and a work Google account (their own zones differ from where they live, research/65), the consumer who wants "put it in my calendar" to be trustworthy, the fresh user who meets one line under a Gmail tile. *Feel:* before, two calendars that can't see each other and a guessed watcher; after, one sentence and one card for a day, the cross-account clash said once, a change card that names the calendar, both zones and who gets an email. (1) **Reading (research/65):** the real oo actions: `list_calendars`, then `list_events` per calendar (the main one as `primary`), `singleEvents`, the person's zone; the watcher reads each chosen calendar every 15 minutes (it read only the default calendar with guessed names); `free_busy_query` is a read (a checked list beside oo's name rule). (2) **Which calendars:** Google's own `selected` by default; holidays and birthdays are day names, never busy time or a clash; a declined or free-shown event is never a meeting or a clash; one invitation in both accounts is one event (`iCalUID` + start). The person changes it per calendar in Accounts (`calendars.json`, 0600). (3) **Accounts (D192):** a calendar tile's detail reads **你选的日历里的日程：时间、标题、地点和参加的人** and asks first for **添加、更改、取消日程，回复邀请**, then **看哪些日历 / Calendars it reads** (dot, name or **主日历**, holidays and birthdays **只显示名字**, **共享**, a switch); the first time, one line with **试试 / Try it**; Gmail without a calendar, **加上 Google 日历，它就能看日程、帮你约时间。** with **在 oo 中添加** (OOMOL's page) and **以后再说**. (4) **The partner:** one tool, `calendar` (agenda with an optional day card under its words, free, propose), listed only while a calendar account is connected; a persona line sends calendar asks there instead of `connection_call`. (5) **Free time:** worked out by Ducter over the merged busy times, 09:00–18:00 on weekdays unless told, up to 5 options spread over the days, from half an hour from now (oo's `find_free_slots` ignores working hours). (6) **Changes:** create, move, cancel, answer; core picks the account (named, else the one whose domain the guests share, else the default), the calendar, the clash at that time and who hears; one write is staged and core runs it once on Go (D189's rails: no default, no "don't ask next time"); the partner isn't woken by a calendar card's Go or Hold, only by a failure. Cancelling someone else's meeting is declining it; moving it is refused (only the organizer can). The card's words follow D212: Go **添加到日历 / 更改时间 / 取消日程 / 接受 / 拒绝 / 可能参加**, Hold **暂不添加 / 暂不更改 / 保留日程 / 暂不回复**. (7) **How it looks (`design/calendar.md` §6):** literal, no metaphor (five candidates weighed): the day card (a time column, each calendar's colour as a 3 px rail, now as a line, past rows faded, **时间重叠 / Overlaps** chips and a bracket, **未回复 / Not answered**, the other zone only when it really differs, never UTC); the change card (before and after for a move, both zones, In, who hears, a clash line), settling from its own run (**已添加到日历 · 在 Google 日历中打开**, **没能添加** and a reason); the greeting's **今天还有 3 件事，下一件 14:00「…」** from the last read only (a background read when today was not read in the last half hour, after the greeting is on screen). (8) **Proactive:** **两件事对不上** gains the clash of two busy meetings today or tomorrow in any calendars (urgent today, the morning note for tomorrow); no calendar-only morning line. (9) **Not built:** Outlook and other calendar services stay on `connection_call`; the Meet link on create is unverified (oo exposes no `conferenceDataVersion`); recurring moves are one occurrence; a guest's zone only when named. Runner-up: keep calendars inside `connection_call` with a better persona line, rejected because the model would merge accounts, compute zones and write the card's facts itself, which is where trust breaks | U (2026-11-12), research/65, D96, D122, D149, D165, D173, D189, D192, D212 | `packages/core/src/{calendar,core,ws,connections,oo-link}.ts`, `packages/core/src/proactive/{watch,items,triage,detect,engine}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{calendar,index,proactive}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/{partner,persona,render}.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/{scenarios,run}.ts`, `apps/web/src/*`, `apps/desktop/internal/*`, `design/{calendar,product-model,connectors,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/calendar.md`, `research/65-calendar.md` |

## Superseded
- **The calendar tile's 会看 / Reads: 今天和明天的日程 / Today's and tomorrow's events, and 先问你: 建、改、接受或拒绝日程.** Superseded by D222 (3): **你选的日历里的日程：时间、标题、地点和参加的人** · **添加、更改、取消日程，回复邀请**.
- **D149 (2)'s "only names and metadata" for the empty conversation, as far as the calendar goes.** Superseded by D222 (7): one line of what is still on today, from the calendars' last read (titles and times), never a new read before the greeting is shown.
- **D205 (5)'s older-Mem line as the only thing shown ("要先在 {home} 上更新 Nowledge Mem…", no button), D205 (4)'s seven codes as the whole list (anything else shown as Mem's own detail), and D169 (6)'s "only to someone who already has Mem connected" for Settings › 伙伴 › 你的其他 AI.** Superseded by D225: one tap installs or updates the home's `nmem` with Mem's official route (the app's copy keeps the line), a pasted link signs it in, all sixteen registered codes are plain lines, and that one row shows whenever memory is on.

## 2026-11-12: Calendars over OOMOL Hosted (hel, Ducter 0.0.29: no day card, no change card; the partner fell back to a task and generic Go cards)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D223 | **Built 2026-11-12 with fakes; not yet re-checked on hel. Follow-up to D222. (1) An account is addressed by its app of the action's own service.** Through OOMOL Hosted or an OpenConnector, the broker turns an account label into an `appId` from `/v1/apps`. It matched the label alone, and one address is both a Gmail and a Google Calendar app there (Gmail listed first), so every calendar read carried the Gmail app's id and OOMOL refused it: `app service does not match requested service` (hel's Log, every look from 01:49 to 02:01 UTC). The label now matches only rows of the action's service. **(2) A calendar it can't read says so, and a change goes only through its card.** `do=agenda` that read nothing and `do=free` with anything unread answer that it couldn't read (an empty day or a free slot would be a guess); `do=propose` stages nothing and says why; the partner says so in one plain line and stops. A Google Calendar write through `connection_call` is refused while the calendar tool is listed. On hel the writes had still waited for the person (three generic Go cards, **创建安排**, each resolved Go before its `create_event` ran; the task only read), so nothing ran unasked, but without the change card's facts (calendar, zones, who hears). **(3) An action a catalog can't describe is missing, not a write.** OOMOL has no `slack.list_mentions` or `discord.list_mentions` (`oo connector schema` answers 404, checked 2026-11-12). Its guide failed, the broker counted that as a write, refused the watcher's read as "a write outside the Go path", and the look stopped there every ten minutes. A read of such an action now fails as "No action …", and the watcher moves on to its next action; staging still treats it as a write. No persona or tool-description change; a new eval scenario, `calendar-unreadable-zh`, passed 3/3 on haiku (with `calendar-today-zh` and `calendar-add-zh`). Runner-up for (2): keep the generic Go card as the fallback, rejected because the person then approves a write whose account, calendar and zone the model wrote itself (D222's reason for the card) | hel's Log (read-only), `oo connector apps` / `schema` (read-only), D189, D222 | `packages/core/src/{connections,calendar}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/partner.ts`, `packages/core/test/{calendar-hosted,calendar,proactive-watch}.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/{fake-mcp,fake-hosted-calendar,fake-calendar,fake-oo-calendar}.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/{scenarios,run}.ts`, `design/calendar.md` |

## 2026-11-12: Update all (the author: "现在更新我们的 App 和我们管理的 harness 都要手动一个个点进去，而且更新的顺序也要考虑……怎么改进 UX，让想升级的用户一键完成？"; decided while the author was away, from first principles)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D224 | **Built 2026-11-12 with fakes, for the author's review. 全部更新 / Update all: one tap updates everything that is behind, in the one order that is always safe, never cutting running work off; the home runs the plan and keeps it on disk, so it outlives the home's own restart; `ducter update --all` is the same plan; 自动更新 / Update automatically runs the same plan at night.** *Who:* the author (a VPS home, a MacBook and a mini doing tasks, Claude Code, Codex and pi on each: after a release, seven taps across cards and the order to keep in mind); the consumer with a Mac home who sees 有更新 on Ducter and on a coin and doesn't know what an agent's updater is; the fresh user who has never thought about versions. *Feel:* before, a chore with a worry (which first? will this kill the task that's running?); after, one tap and a short list that ticks itself off, each line saying where it is in plain words, nothing cut. *First time:* the first time anything is behind, Settings › 你的设备 opens with one card on top (the Settings list's row already says **有 n 项更新** in the highlight's ink, D213): what and from → to, the order in one line, one button. (1) **Where:** one card at the top of Settings › 你的设备 (phone and wide page) and first on the Mac's Settings › 更新与关于; shown only when something is behind or a plan is under way or just over (30 min; a day with a failure); nothing in the conversation or On hand (D148 (9): no nagging; the partner's one line for a security release stays). Before: **有 {n} 项更新 / {n} updates**, up to four lines **{device} 上的 Ducter / Ducter on {device}** and **{device} 上的 {agent} / {agent} on {device}** with **{from} → {to}**, **还有 {n} 项**, the order line **先更新 {home}，再更新其他电脑，最后是各台电脑上的 AI。正在做的事不会被打断。**, what can't take part (**{device} 现在连不上，醒来后再更新。**, a Mac app too old to ask, a Ducter from source), what the app itself does at the end, and **全部更新 / Update all** (**更新 / Update** for one). (2) **Order, and why.** **The home first:** everything else talks to it, and every release is built and tested (the e2e rig's stale worker) for a newer home serving older computers and apps, never the reverse, so a home must never be behind its peers; it also brings the newest verified agent versions (harness-facts). **Then the other computers' Ducter side by side**, each at its own safe point (D148 (4)); a computer never goes past a home that didn't update (**{home} 没能更新，这台保持原版本**). **Then each computer's AI agents**, once that computer's Ducter step is over (its restart would cut an updater off, and its new Ducter knows the newer verified range), one updater at a time on a computer, computers side by side; an agent whose tasks are mid-turn **waits** (**等用它的 {n} 件事停下**), with no limit and nothing stopped. **The apps last, each by itself:** the phone page reloads when the plan is over and the home serves newer code (back on 你的设备), the Android app checks its own update (`update.check`, D181), a Mac app that is only a client of a home elsewhere downloads at the tap and restarts into the new version once no step is mid-update (D160's putting-in screen); a Mac app that is the home or does tasks is already a step (D187's `asksApp`). Nowledge Mem's plugin is not part of it (D169: only a task the user starts). (3) **Failures stay apart:** a step that fails rolls back as its single update does (D148 (5), D156 (3)) and is said with D167/D172's judgement (by what runs now; "nothing newer" is done, never a failure); independent steps go on; dependents are skipped with why; a step that never says how it ended is called after 45 min (**开始更新以后，一直没有收到它的结果。**); the end says **已全部更新 / 已更新 {n} 项，{m} 项没能更新** with each why and **再试一次** (a new plan of what is still behind). **停止剩下的 / Stop the rest** skips what hasn't started; what runs finishes. **完成 / Done** puts a finished plan away. (4) **Survives the home's restart:** `<dataDir>/updates/update-all.json`, written before each step starts; the restarted home reads it, judges its own step by the version it runs (or the helper's outcome), and goes on; every client follows the same plan in its devices frame (`updateAll {offer, notNow, plan}`; op `update_all {stop?, dismiss?}`, `update_all_result`; `update_all` events started/finished/stopped on the `devices` stream). (5) **自动更新 / Update automatically** (D148's `autoUpdate`, still off by default: Ducter ships often in this preview and a regression the health check can't see should land while someone is watching): every 10 min, inside quiet hours, else 03:00–05:00 on the home's clock, with nothing working: the same plan, with Ducter only where it updates itself and agents only to versions Ducter verified (D156 (4)); a step that failed at night isn't tried again that night. Offered once where it means most: on the finished card, **以后自动更新** with its one line, when it is off. (6) **CLI:** `ducter update --all [--check] [--json] [--yes]` lists the plan in its order, asks once, starts it on the home, and follows the list through the home's restart (it reconnects); a home from before D224 gets the old per-device loop. | U (the request), L | `packages/core/src/{update-all,core,devices,ws}.ts`, `packages/core/test/update-all.test.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{update,i18n}.ts`, `packages/cli/test/update.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/{updateall.tsx,i18n-updateall.ts,app.ts,devcard.tsx,views.tsx,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/test/updateall.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,i18n,views,mock}`, `scripts/e2e/linux.ts`, `design/{system,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/{devices,install}.md`, `skills/{ducter-deploy,ducter-operate}/SKILL.md` |

## Superseded
- **D148 (8)'s `--all`: "workers first, then the home", and D148 (10)'s night: "the home updates itself, then each worker".** Superseded by D224: the home first, then the other computers, then their AI agents, as one plan the home keeps through its restart.
- **D156 (4)'s separate night look for agents ("one agent per look")** and D148 (10)'s "inside quiet hours" with no quiet hours set meaning never. Superseded by D224 (5): the night runs Update all's plan (agents still only to verified versions), and without quiet hours the night is 03:00–05:00 on the home's clock.
- **夜里自动更新 / Update at night, "在免打扰的时段里、没有事在做的时候，悄悄更新到最新版本。"** Superseded by D224 (5): **自动更新 / Update automatically**, "在免打扰时段里（没设就是夜里 3 点到 5 点）、没有事在做的时候，按同样的顺序更新；AI 只更新到 Ducter 验证过的版本。"

## 2026-11-12: The released Bridge, live (nmem 0.10.118 public; the author: "continue the Bridge work"; and "installing Nowledge Mem on a home that lacks it must be started from inside Ducter")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D225 | **Built 2026-11-12; the Connect step itself is still unproven live: Mem's Cloud answers 503 `cloud_bridge_setup_unavailable` for this workspace (research/66 §3). Follow-up to D205.** *Who:* the heavy multi-agent developer whose home is a headless server (the author's hel: nmem 0.10.116 by pip, too old), and anyone whose home has no `nmem`. *Feel:* before, a line that said "update Mem on hel" and nothing to tap, then Mem's agent-facing words ("stop, don't mint another key") on a Connect that Mem's side wasn't ready for; after, one row that walks them through: **装上 / 更新**, a pasted link, **连接**, each a tap, with plain words when Mem's side isn't ready. (1) **The released Bridge** (research/66 §1–2): D205's commands, flags and JSON hold; 0.10.118 registers nine more setup codes; the plugin now prepares a native-session inbox in every Claude Code or Codex session on its own. (2) **Every registered code is a plain line, never Mem's agent-facing detail:** `authority_denied` → control_denied; `authority_retired` → lifetime_inactive; `unavailable`, `busy`, `capacity` → **unavailable**: **Nowledge Mem 那边现在还接不了这个连接，不是你这边的问题，你的记忆照常能用。过一阵再点「再试一次」。**; `outcome_unknown` → **unknown**: **没等到 Nowledge Mem 的回答，不知道连上了没有。再点一次「再试一次」就知道了，不会多出一个连接。** (the same connect; INFERRED idempotent per login and role); any other code shows as **没连上：Nowledge Mem: <code>**. Nothing is retried by itself (the skill: stop on unavailable). (3) **The home's own Mem command line, on the same row, before Connect** (a dedicated step in core, not a task: one deterministic command, like D156's agent updates; D169's task stays for an agent's plugin, which needs judgment): **no `nmem` here** → **install_mem**: **要先在 {home} 上装 Nowledge Mem 的命令行工具（免费）。点「装上」，Ducter 照 Nowledge Mem 官方的方式来装，不动别的。** · **装上 / Install**, which runs the first official route that is here (`uv tool install nmem-cli`, `pipx install nmem-cli`, `python3 -m pip install --user nmem-cli`), never past the system's PEP 668 mark for a fresh install; none here: **{home} 上还没有 Nowledge Mem，也没有能装它的 pipx。先在那台电脑上装 pipx（Ubuntu：sudo apt install pipx），再点「再试一次」。**. **Too old** → **update_mem**: **{home} 上的 Nowledge Mem 是 {v}，要 0.10.118 以上才能跟你的其他 AI 连上。点「更新」，照它当初装的方式更新。** · **更新 / Update**, from the same source the copy came from (uv, pipx, or pip in the user's own site or a venv; a user-site copy under a marked python is updated past the mark, since it was put there so); the app's copy keeps D205's line (update the app); a copy Ducter can't place: **…请照你当初装它的方式更新一下。** While it runs: **正在 {home} 上装 / 更新 Nowledge Mem…**; a failure shows the installer's last line. Afterwards the version is read back and the new `nmem`'s folder joins the process's PATH and `NMEM_CLI_PATH` (memory, the partner's Bridge, tasks and the Mem plugin's hooks all find it), so no restart. (4) **Signing in a home**: `nmem` here but not signed in (`status` not healthy) → **sign_in**: **Nowledge Mem 已经在 {home} 上了，还要登录你的 Nowledge Mem。在 Nowledge Mem 网页的「Connectors › AI Tools」里拿一个连接链接，贴在这里。链接只能用一次，用完就不留。** with a link field and **登录 / Sign in**: `nmem connect --skip-tools <link>` (the sign-in only; no AI tool's settings change, D169 does that per agent); a non-link is refused before anything runs (**这不像 Nowledge Mem 的连接链接…**), a used or expired one says so (**这个链接用过或过期了。再拿一个新的贴进来。**). The link goes from the client to nmem only: never logged, kept or sent back. Then Mem is connected: the persona's identity is bound as on a start (D141), and the row goes on to Connect. Mem answering from the computer itself (`backend: local`) → **local_mem**: one line that other AI agents need a Nowledge Mem cloud workspace (INFERRED from "Cloud Agent communication"). (5) **Who sees it** (amends D169 (6) and D205 (6) for this one row): the row shows in Settings › 伙伴 › 你的其他 AI whenever memory is on, even before Mem is on the home: it is a place the user opened on purpose, it never pushes a card into the conversation, and its copy says the tool is free. Memory off: nothing. (6) **No device code**: nmem 0.10.118 has none; a pasted one-time link from the phone is the headless sign-in until Mem adds one (B16, research/66 §6). (7) **Still off by default** (D170 (9)): the live trial stops at Connect while Mem's side answers unavailable; `bun run bridge:live -- --connected` now says so and stops, as the skill does. Docs keep "coming soon" with the requirements stated | U (the author: "continue the Bridge work"; the added scope: Mem installed from inside Ducter on a home without it, a headless Linux server first), L (research/66: the release, the live 503, hel's copy, the container run) | `packages/core/src/{mem-cli,bridge-link,core,ws,main,persona,mem-identity}.ts`, `packages/partner/src/memory.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{index,mem-plugins}.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/{fake-bridge-nmem,bridge-live}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{views.tsx,app.ts,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/demo-bridge.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/peers.go,views/app.go,protocol,i18n}`, `scripts/e2e/mem-cli-home.ts`, tests, `design/bridge.md`, glossary, `docs/faq.md`, `design/todo.md` |

## 2026-11-12: Why nothing ever reached the author (hel, Ducter 0.0.32: two days of Gmail, two calendars and Slack watched, not one heads-up; "dots has already reminded me of several things")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D226 | **Built 2026-11-12, decided from first principles while the author was away; for review. Implementation fixes to D165/D174 plus three small changes of behaviour, and a quiet place to see why it stayed quiet.** *Evidence (hel, read-only, counts only):* `proactive.json` had no finds, no verdicts, `judgeRuns.day=""` and `month.tokens={}` (no triage call ever succeeded); `findings.json` did not exist (the facts ledger was empty); the journal had ~60 `[proactive] triage: harness claude-code is not installed` over two days; 152 mails were looked at today (seen lists held 162 ids), every one dropped; the partner runs on **Codex** (D150's fallback: Claude Code is not installed there) while triage, the bar and the cheap judge (D112) asked `settings.brain`, whose unset default is Claude Code. `settings.proactive` was `urgent`, set from Settings, so the first look (D165 §11) and the 60-day look back never ran (`backfill={}`, `offered={}`). Quiet hours 23:00–08:00 Asia/Tokyo; two phones subscribed; push on. *Who:* first the fresh user and the consumer user, whose first days decide whether Ducter is a partner or a mailbox reader (the "aha" must come in days, as dots' first users report: a talk on one date in the calendar and another in the confirmation mail; a warranty mail that needed an answer, research cited in `design/connectors.md` §5.1); then the heavy developer, who must be able to see in one place why it was quiet. *Feel:* before, "it says it is keeping an eye out, and in two days it has said nothing; is it even working?"; after, either a heads-up worth having, or, one tap away, "看了 152 封信、3 个日程 · 2 件不急，没说（你选了只说急的）", and if reading is broken, the partner says so once, in plain words (放心: silence means success only when it is success). *First time:* nothing to set: choosing the mode in Settings now does what the first-add question did (the quiet look back), and the day view needs no setup. (1) **Root cause, fixed: one brain.** Triage, the bar and the cheap judge run on what the partner really thinks with (`brainInUse`: D150's fallback when it applies, else the setting), never the unset default; a Codex-only home triages on Codex's registered cheap model. (2) **A failing read is not silence.** Each failed triage is counted (`no_agent` · `not_installed` · `signed_out` · `failed`); after 3 failures in a row over at least 30 minutes the partner says it once in the conversation (**我没法细读你的信：这台电脑上没装 Claude Code。现在只看得到标题，所以什么都没跟你说。**), and the line stands in Settings until reading works again, then goes without a word. Items that waited are read on the next look (P5, unchanged); with no model at all they now wait too, instead of being dropped. (3) **A mode set from Settings catches up.** When watching is on and a source never had its look back (set from the chips, a "Not now" to the first look, or a home that watched before this), the quiet look back runs once: the last 3 days in full and 60 days of subjects that may hold a booking, a renewal, a payment or a promise. The long look back now asks the mail service only for those subjects (one read returns at most 50, the newest; an unfiltered 60-day read of the author's inbox saw a few hours), and calendars a month ahead. Nothing is said for it. (4) **Calendars are read two weeks ahead, not three days**, so a calendar entry and a confirmation can meet. (5) **A new join in 两件事对不上 / Two things don't match:** a confirmation (triage's `booking`, "other": an appointment, a reservation, a talk) that names the same thing as a coming meeting on another day, within two weeks: **日历上「AI Builders Meetup」是 11/18，Mia Chen 的确认信写的是 11/19。** with the step **核对哪个对 / Check which is right** (a read-only task). (6) **只说急的 / Only urgent also has 开会前 / Before a meeting** (10 minutes before, only with something open): it is a "now" by nature; still no morning note. (7) **今天看了什么 / What it looked at today**, under the switch in Settings › 通知 / Notifications, on the phone and the Mac: a button that opens counts only (looked at, left by rules, read closely, noted, told you) and what is held now under its one reason (**不急，没说（你选了只说急的）** · **留到早上 {time} 那条** · **不值得打扰你** · **今天已经说满 3 件** · **你说过这类不用告诉你的** · **这个月的额度用完了** · **在免打扰时间里**), whether notifications reach a phone, and whether it is still catching up; the reading problem shows without opening. Counts appear only here, on request, as D173 allows for 看了哪些. *Considered and not done:* loosening the bar itself (people only for 在等你回, 2 working days) before any find has ever been judged live: the gates never ran on hel, so they are not yet evidence of being too strict; the triage eval and the first live week are (todo). Lowering 只说急的's meaning further: it was the author's choice; the day view now says plainly what it held. *Follow-up (2026-11-12, after 0.0.33/0.0.34; decided while the author was away, for review):* triage worked on hel and still nothing was noted (`today.read=3`, `noted=0`, `backfill` done, no `findings.json`). Root cause (research/68): through OOMOL, `gmail.search_threads` answers `{threadId, snippet}` only, so the catch-up look back's 157 mails were undated and had no subject; its filter (a facty subject, or the last 3 days by date) kept none, and it marked itself done; any fact from such mail would have been dated 1970 and swept, and a thread id hid each later reply. Separately, items whose triage failed had been marked seen, and the in-memory retry died with each restart. (8) **Mail is read with `gmail.fetch_emails`** (`detail: "summary"`, 100 a page, up to 5 pages), `search_threads` the fallback; actions a source lacks are remembered and skipped. (9) **Seen means read**: an item is seen only once triage read it or a rule left it; one whose reading failed waits in `watch.json` (id and date only, ≤ 3 days), the checkpoint stays before it, and the next look brings it again, across restarts. (10) **A look back from before this runs once more** (`lookBackRev` 2) as a catch-up, without the mode being touched; one that no source answered stays pending. (11) The look back's subject search is a few short searches (≤ 14 terms); a refused one falls back to the newest mail (logged); each look back logs `[proactive] look back: read N, kept M, noted K`. *Considered and not done:* keeping the snippets of failed items on disk to retry without asking again (mail stays out of Ducter's files; asking again costs one read). Tests: `proactive-lookback.test.ts` (OOMOL-shaped fakes: hel's state re-runs and 只说急的 says the flight change and the invoice; a failed reading waits across a restart; a refused subject search). | U (2026-11-12: "I have never received a single proactive heads-up from Ducter about mail or calendar, while dots has already reminded me of several things"); hel read-only evidence (above); public reports of dots (connectors.md §5.1) | `packages/core/src/{core,ws}.ts`, `packages/core/src/proactive/{engine,watch,stage1,detect,triage,words}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{proactive,index}.ts`; web `accounts.tsx`, `views.tsx`, `i18n.ts`, `app.ts`, `static/app.css`; Mac `internal/{protocol,store,i18n,views}`; tests `proactive-quiet.test.ts`, `proactive-deliver.test.ts`, web `quietday.test.ts`, Mac `TestQuietDayUnderTheSwitch`; `design/connectors.md` §1.2, §5, §5.1, §10, §11; `design/product-model.md`; `design/scenarios.md` K10; `docs/accounts.md`, `docs/settings.md`; `design/todo.md`; follow-up: `packages/core/src/proactive/{watch,engine}.ts`, tests `proactive-lookback.test.ts`, `fake-accounts.ts`, `proactive-rig.ts`; `research/68-oomol-gmail-reads.md`; `design/connectors.md` §1.2 |

## 2026-11-12: Update all stuck on a step whose computer restarted under it (the author's MacBook: 正在更新到 0.167.2 for 20+ min)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D227 | **Built 2026-11-12 with fakes and real sockets, for the author's review; not yet run on the author's machines. Follow-up to D224.** *What happened:* hel (0.0.32) had just updated itself; 更新 on the MacBook's card (05:01:30Z) started that Mac's Ducter update through its app (D187); 80 s later the author tapped Update all. Its plan held only Codex on the MacBook (its Ducter was under way, so not offered), and since the plan's own wait looked only at Ducter steps *in the plan*, Codex's npm started at once. 4 s later the worker reached its safe point, which counted merges and turns but not its own agent updater, and asked the app to restart (app.log: "restarting into 0.0.32, asked from elsewhere"); the swap restarted the worker, npm died with it, the new worker knew nothing of it, and the home kept the update "updating" (no answer ever comes) until the 45-min no-answer limit. Not D72's daily check (it only downloads; it installs on quit) and not a tap on 重新启动. *Who:* the heavy developer (a home, a Mac doing tasks, agents on each) first; anyone who taps a computer's 更新 and then Update all. *Feel:* before, a row that says it is updating while nothing is, for most of an hour; after, a row that says what it waits on, and a step that finishes by itself, or says honestly why not, within seconds of the computer coming back (好用, 放心). (1) **A computer's agents wait for its Ducter, in the plan or not:** an agent step on a computer whose Ducter is mid-update (any phase but done or failed) waits, **等 {device} 上的 Ducter 更新完 / Waiting for Ducter on {device} to finish updating** (`wait: "ducter"`), and is judged afresh once it is back. (2) **No restart cuts an agent's updater off:** a worker's own Ducter update waits at its safe point while an agent's updater runs there (past its own safe point; up to the same 15 min as a merge, else `busy`), and an agent's updater waits before it starts while that worker's own Ducter update is under way; the home's own restart counts an agent updating on the home the same way. Belt and braces on the Mac: the worker keeps `<dataDir>/updates/agent-updates.json` (pid and what it updates) while an updater runs, and the app holds a restart asked of it (`app-ask.json` "restart") while a live process is named there; its own last-step restart for a plan (D224) also waits while any step on this Mac is still to do or that file names one. A tap on 重新启动 stays the person's call. (3) **Re-judged on reconnect, by what runs now (D167, D172):** the worker's hello carries a `boot` id (one per process) and the agents it updates now (`agentUpdates`); a hello from another process (another boot; for a worker from before D227, another version) ends every update the home thinks runs there and this process doesn't: on its target, **done**; else failed **interrupted** (a new AgentReason: **更新时那台电脑上的 Ducter 重启了，把它打断了。 / Ducter on that computer restarted during the update and cut it off.**). A plain reconnect (same boot) judges nothing; frames the worker couldn't send while away are kept and sent on the next welcome, and the next process says once what its predecessor left in that file. A step from disk that a restarted home has no update state for, its computer awake and still on the old version, is judged interrupted too. Only news counts: an interruption older than the step's current attempt is ignored. (4) **Started again once, said on the row:** an interrupted step goes back to queued with `retried`, is started as soon as nothing holds it, and reads **刚才被打断了，正在重新更新到 {v} / Cut off; updating to {v} again**; interrupted again, it fails with **更新时 {device} 上的 Ducter 重启了，把它打断了；重新试了一次，还是没更新完。/ Ducter on {device} restarted during the update and cut it off; tried once more, and it still didn't finish.** The 45-min no-answer limit stays for a computer that never comes back. (5) **A running step whose computer is away says so:** **等 {device} 重新连上 / Waiting for {device} to reconnect** (`wait: "reconnect"`), for a Ducter step too (a worker restarting into its new version). (6) **The plan stuck on hel** (0.0.32, before this) ends by itself with the 45-min limit (about 05:48Z, `no_answer`); **再试一次** then makes a new plan. A home running this that finds such a step on disk after its own restart (no update state, the computer awake, the old version) starts it once more instead of waiting. | U (the author's report and evidence: hel's plan file, the Mac's app.log), L | `packages/core/src/{update-all,agents,workers,core}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{worker,update,i18n}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/update/asks.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{updateall.go,app.go}`, `apps/desktop/main.go`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol,i18n}`, `apps/web/src/{updateall.tsx,i18n-updateall.ts,i18n.ts}`, tests (`core/test/{update-all,update-all-restart,agents,workers,app-update}.test.ts`, `cli/test/update.test.ts`, `web/test/updateall.test.ts`, Go `asks_test.go`, `updateall_test.go`), `design/{system,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/devices.md` |

## 2026-11-12: A home with no public address, reached from anywhere at the person's own domain (the author: "our Linux home has a public IP, but a Mac has none; use Cloudflare Tunnel so any home is reachable"; then, after the quick-tunnel draft: "Don't do temporary tunnels — the experience is bad. Guide the user to use an agent and Cloudflare's CLI to bind their own domain", and no dependency of the user's devices on a relay the project runs)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D228 | **Built 2026-11-12 with fakes (cloudflared, Cloudflare, the setup task's agent) and on the Linux VM with the real cloudflared (download, checksum, sign-in link) and a fake tunnel; for the author's review. Not run against a real Cloudflare account. 在哪儿都能用 / From anywhere reaches a home with no public address at the person's own domain, through their own Cloudflare account and a named tunnel that Ducter runs; a task the person starts sets it up with Cloudflare's own program, and the card shows each step ticking.** A first draft of the same number (a free quick tunnel on `trycloudflare.com`, and an encrypted address relay on ducter.ai so devices could follow its changing address) was merged and reverted before any release; it is not kept (research/67 §7). *Who:* the consumer who wants the phone to work outside and has (or will buy) a domain; the developer with a Mac or a box behind NAT; a fresh user meets it on the home's card or in the onboarding ask 在手机上也能找到我. *Feel:* before, a dead end ("set up a domain or an SSH tunnel"); after, one line that says what is needed, one tap, and a partner that does the steps, asks only to sign in and to say go, and ends with an address that never changes (好用, 放心); nothing of theirs depends on a service the project runs. *First time:* the chip's ⓘ: **要一个放在 Cloudflare 上的域名，Cloudflare 账号是免费的。还没有域名？在 Cloudflare 里就能买，按成本价。 / You need a domain on Cloudflare; the Cloudflare account is free. No domain yet? You can buy one right in Cloudflare, sold at cost.** and **让 Ducter 帮我设好 / Set it up for me**. (1) **One path, the person's own:** their Cloudflare account and their domain, a named tunnel; no quick tunnels (their address changes with every start of cloudflared, and Cloudflare positions them for testing: research/67 §1a), no address relay, no project-run hostname. (2) **The setup is a task the person starts** (D169's pattern: the tap is the Go for the task; origin you; on the home, with an agent signed in there): its agent runs `ducter anywhere step login | create | run --json`; the home does each step with cloudflared and keeps the state, so the card's list ticks by itself, whatever the agent says: **备好 Cloudflare 的程序 · 登录 Cloudflare，选你的域名 · 定下地址 {host} · 在你的账号里建通道 · 把地址指到这台电脑 · 在这台电脑上一直开着 · 从外面打开试试 · 连新设备时用这个地址** (en: Get Cloudflare's program · Sign in to Cloudflare and pick your domain · Choose the address {host} · Create the tunnel in your account · Point the address at this computer · Keep it running on this computer · Try it from outside · Use it to connect new devices). *login:* cloudflared (on PATH, else downloaded, 2026.10.5 pinned, SHA-256 checked) runs `tunnel login` with HOME in `<dataDir>/anywhere`, so `cert.pem` never touches the person's own `~/.cloudflared`; its link shows on the card with a QR to open on a phone (a headless home), or cloudflared opens the browser itself on a Mac (**浏览器里打开了 Cloudflare：登录，选你的域名，点 Authorize。**); the step waits for them (about 10 minutes, cloudflared's own). The zone is read with the certificate's own token (`GET /zones/<id>`; INFERRED to work) and **ducter.<zone>** suggested. *create:* outward, so it waits for a **Go card** of the task, asked by the home in fixed words: **在你的账号里建一条通道，把 {host} 指到 {device}？… 想用别的名字，直接回我。** with **建好它 / Set it up** suggested, no timer; a reply with another name asks again for that name; a name outside the zone is refused. After Go: `tunnel create --output json --credentials-file <dataDir>/anywhere/tunnel.json` (0400), then `tunnel route dns <id> <host>`; a name that already has a record fails as **taken** and is never overwritten (the agent asks for another). *run:* Ducter writes its own config (that hostname to `http://127.0.0.1:<port>` only, then 404), starts cloudflared beside Ducter (detached, or its own systemd user unit `ducter-anywhere-<port>`, adopted by a restarted or updated Ducter, started again at boot), waits for `/ready`, for public DNS over HTTPS, and for `https://<host>/.well-known/ducter-anywhere` to answer the home's own check string over HTTPS and as a WebSocket's first message; only then it writes `https://<host>` to `<dataDir>/public-url` (where `ducter domain` writes it), so pairing codes carry it. A step that fails says why in a word (download · unsupported · login · taken · cloudflare · unreachable) with **再试一次** (a new task; what is done stays done). (3) **No agent, or no wish for one:** `ducter anywhere on --token <t> --hostname <h>` for a tunnel made in Cloudflare's dashboard (the token in `<dataDir>/anywhere/token.env`, 0600, given to cloudflared by environment or an EnvironmentFile, never on a command line; the same check before the address is used); `ducter domain` (D143) on a server with a public IP; `ducter anywhere on` in a terminal starts the same task and follows its steps there, the sign-in link and a QR included. (4) **Off** stops the tunnel and takes the address out of `public-url`; the tunnel and its DNS record stay in the person's account, said in the confirm (**通道和地址还留在你的 Cloudflare 账号里，随时可以再打开；不要了就在 Cloudflare 里删掉。**); the card then reads **关着。{host} 的通道还在你的 Cloudflare 账号里，打开就能用。** with **打开**, and on again needs no setup. (5) **Security as before:** requests through the tunnel arrive from loopback with the outside Host and `cf-connecting-ip`/`x-forwarded-for`, so they are remote and need a device token (auth.ts, D57). Cloudflare ends TLS at its edge, said on the card: **数据在 Cloudflare 那里解开再转到这台电脑，和所有用 Cloudflare 的网站一样。** Credentials stay in `<dataDir>/anywhere` (0700) and are never logged; the agent is told never to read or copy them, nor to run cloudflared or change Cloudflare another way. (6) **The address doesn't move,** so nothing follows it: no relay, no service-worker redirect, no `moved` in the apps. | U (the request; then the author's reversal of the quick-tunnel draft, 2026-11-12), L, R67 | `packages/core/src/{anywhere,core,devices,decisions,ws}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/index.ts`, `packages/cli/src/{anywhere-cmd,args,main,remote,i18n,uninstall}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{devcard.tsx,app.ts,i18n.ts,updateall.tsx,phone.tsx}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/demo-anywhere.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/anywhere.go,views/devicecard.go,views/asks.go,views/pairing.go,views/abilities.go,views/devupdate.go,protocol,store,mock,i18n}`, tests (`core/test/{anywhere,devices}.test.ts`, `cli/test/anywhere-cmd.test.ts`, Go `TestAbilitiesReachFromAnywhere`), `scripts/e2e/linux.ts` step 8, `design/{system,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/{devices,phone,troubleshooting}.md`, `research/67` |
| D230 | **The look back waits for the source's apps: one that ran before they were listed is not done. Built 2026-11-12 from hel on 0.0.37.** On hel the catch-up look back (D226) ran in the same second Ducter started, before OOMOL had answered the list of apps: `apps()` was empty, so no app was asked, nothing was read, and "no source answered" didn't hold (it asked only of listed apps), so the source was marked done with `read 0`. (1) A look back with no app answering stays pending whether the apps are unlisted or unreachable; only a source whose listed apps hold nothing to look at is done with nothing read. (2) A pending look back goes when the connections change (the apps listed, a source back), as it already did at a start and when the models change; it is queued at most once per source, so a burst of changes reads once. (3) `LOOK_BACK_REV` 3: a home whose look back was done before this runs it once more, quietly, the last 3 days in full (the ledger keeps one fact per item).  (4) **Slack reaches the watcher:** OOMOL's `slack.search_messages` answers `matches` ({channelId, ts, userId, username, text}) with no mention or DM flag, so all of them were dropped (110 looks on hel, 0 items; the same `to:me` query live gives 255 since 11-05). What a `to:me` search answers is addressed to the person by the query: it counts as a mention unless it is a DM (a `D…` channel). The window goes in the query by day (`after:YYYY-MM-DD`, a day early; seen keeps each once) with `count` and `sort: timestamp`, the action's own fields; the ts (epoch seconds with a fraction) is parsed. (5) **今天看了什么 counts once:** a look asks from the last checkpoint on and reads the newest mail again each time (388 "looked at" against 16 new on hel); a mail or mention counts the first time it is seen, a calendar its window once a day (the largest). A day keeps 1,500 read lines, not 400 (about 10 hours at hel's rate). (6) **Quiet hours off hold nothing:** off (null) no longer fell back to 23:00–08:00 for holding finds; the morning note still goes at 08:00. Found with it, not changed here (in the proactive redesign): the next two weeks of hel's calendar were 4 all-day, free events with a place (stays and trips, one made by Gmail from a booking mail), dropped as "not a meeting", so the ledger never held a fact. | L (hel's journal, `proactive.json` and `watch.json`, counts only; live OOMOL shapes, types only) | `packages/core/src/proactive/{engine,watch,items}.ts`, `packages/core/test/{proactive-lookback,proactive-slack}.test.ts`, `packages/core/test/fake-accounts.ts` |

A second ask for the same variable waits on the open card; `again: true` (the key didn't work) asks anew and the card says it replaces the kept one. (2) **The card:** what (the name), the variable (mono), why (the task's words); a masked field, paste allowed; **记住，以后直接用 / Remember it** (the default) or **只这一次 / Just this once**; **不给 / Don't give** · **保存，继续 / Save and continue** (**交给它，继续 / Give it and continue** when once). After: **已交给 {task} / Given to {task}** · **你没给 / You didn't give it**. A message to the task never answers it (a key never goes through the conversation). Don't give tells the task plainly to carry on and say in its report what is left undone; the task doesn't go to Waiting. (3) **How the task uses it without the model seeing it:** a per-task key file on the home, `<dataDir>/task-keys/<taskId>.env` (the folder 0700, the file 0600, `export ENV='…'` lines, rewritten from the store on every change and at start), loaded only inside the command that needs it: `. '<file>' && <command>`. It goes when the task ends (done, dropped, archived), and at start for a task that ended while Ducter was off. Rejected: a `ducter key run ENV -- cmd` wrapper (it needs Ducter's CLI on every harness's PATH and a credential in the task's environment to ask the home with, which the model reads as easily as the key); the key in the session's env (a key given mid-run would need a new session). (4) **What is guaranteed, and said on the card:** the value is never in the conversation, the Log, a client frame, a push, a Ducter tool's answer or a model call Ducter makes: the Log scrubs every key value (kept or once) from every event before it is written, so a command that prints it shows `••••••` in the transcript, On hand and the partner's reads; the browser tools' scrub includes keys too. Not guaranteed: the task's own agent sees what its own commands print, and its harness keeps that in its own transcript; a task that prints the key on purpose sees it. (5) **The store:** Autofill's encrypted file (D191) gets **密钥 / Keys** {name, variable, value, added, used} and per-task grants (a kept key by id, so an edit or a removal reaches the task's file; a once value held only in its grant, gone with the task). ## 2026-11-12: A worker Mac's own phone, and tasks in the person's Mac apps (the author's setup: hel, a Linux server, is the home; the MacBook Pro M3 does tasks for it with 上网 on, and its card showed only 替…做事 and 上网; "both tonight", 按建议)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D234 | **用手机 / Use a phone on a worker Mac: the phone is that Mac's own; the tools, the gate and the live view stay on the home (supersedes D217's "Phase 1: the home only"). Built 2026-11-12 with fakes.** Who: the person whose home is a server (the author's hel) and whose Mac does tasks for it: the most common shape, and the one where 用手机 was missing. Before: a phone ability that existed only when the home itself was a Mac; after: **+ 用手机** on the Mac's card, the same sheet (download, space, Google's licenses by name), the phone there. (1) **Where it lives.** A worker on an Apple Silicon Mac makes a `PhoneHost` (`android/host.ts`, the home's own code: the pinned SDK in `<worker dataDir>/phone`, the AVD, installs, erase) and says 用手机 in its hello and abilities frames (install progress at most once a second). The home shows it on that Mac's card; a Ducter there from before says nothing, so its card keeps no chip until it is updated (never a dead + ). (2) **Adding it.** `phone_terms` / `phone_install` / `phone_erase` with that Mac's id go over the worker's socket (`link.call`): Google's texts are fetched there and the SHA-256s checked there, as on the home. (3) **Which computer a task runs on (D157).** `needs.phone` already ranks by the computer's 用手机 (`no_phone` elsewhere); a worker Mac with it on takes the task, as the home did. (4) **The tools run on the home and drive that Mac's phone over the socket**, not on the worker: the gate (`gate.ts`, the Go per tap, the blocked apps), the blind rule and the live view's turns are the home's code and must not be duplicated where the Mac could disagree. `remotePhoneDevice` is a `PhoneDevice` whose methods are one `phone_dev` request each; the worker answers only a fixed set (`state, ensure, touch, hold, release, stop, jpeg, screen, tap, swipe, key, text, front, apps, launch, size`), checks key codes and app ids, and never runs a shell or adb for the home. The home picks the phone by the task's place (`place.device`); each worker phone has its own live view (its own turns: the person's turn on the MacBook's phone blinds only the tasks on it). Frames are the device's JPEGs over the same socket, one in flight until acked. The home's socket gone, the worker lets go of every hold (the phone stops when idle). (5) **Cards and words**: the opened task's **查看手机** and a card's own turn open the phone the task uses (`phoning.device`), never the home's by default. The home-Mac path is unchanged. Not yet: the real 2.3 GB install and a real errand on the author's MacBook through hel (the 2.3 GB SDK was not installed for this; tests use a fake phone); a Linux server's phone (KVM) | U (the author: the setup, "both tonight"), L (tools on the home, the fixed op set) | `packages/core/src/android/{host,remote,index,tools}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{worker-browse,devices,ws,core}.ts`, `packages/cli/src/worker.ts`, `apps/web/src/phone-use.tsx`, `apps/desktop/internal/views/{devicecard,phoneuse}.go`, tests (`core/test/worker-mac-abilities.test.ts`, `computer-use.test.ts`, Go `computeruse_test.go`), `design/{phone-use,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/devices.md` |
| D235 | **用电脑 / Use the computer: a task whose work is in a Mac app uses that Mac's apps through Ducter's own app tools over cua-driver, gated at the effect, on the home or a worker Mac. Built 2026-11-12 with fakes.** Who: the consumer who asks "在 Keynote 里把 Q3 导出成 PDF" or "用邮件回张三", and the developer whose work is in an app with no command line. Before: tasks could use files, a shell and the web, never an app's window; after: **+ 用电脑** on the Mac's card, macOS's two switches turned on once (Accessibility, Screen Recording), and app work done in the background with the person's Go before anything that commits. (1) **Turning it on.** On a Mac with cua-driver (上网 already put it there), **+ 用电脑** saves the choice on that Mac (`abilities.json` `computer`). cua-driver's own read-only `permissions status --json` says the two switches; missing, the chip fails and its ⓘ lists both with where each stands, how to turn them on, and **在 {Mac} 上打开指引 / Open the guide on {Mac}**, which runs cua-driver's own guide (`permissions grant`) on that Mac's screen. Ducter never flips them itself. On without both switches the chip says so until they are on; cua-driver missing, it says to install it. Linux and Windows: 以后会有. (2) **Which computer.** `needs.app` (already in the task tool, "A Mac app it works in") ranks a Mac with 用电脑 on first (D157's least-access step); a Mac without it still takes work that needs only the app's files or command line, and the brief says how to ask for 用电脑 when clicking is needed. No partner tool or persona changed. (3) **How a task gets it: Ducter's own tools, not cua-driver's MCP handed to the task, and not the harness's own computer use (an exception to D130's "native first", for apps only).** Ten tools on Ducter's MCP, listed only for a task with `needs.app` whose Mac has 用电脑 on: `computer_apps`, `computer_open_app`, `computer_look` (one window's elements numbered, a picture on request), `computer_click`, `computer_type`, `computer_key`, `computer_scroll`, `computer_menu`, `computer_ask_go`, `computer_ask_user`. Why: a task drives the person's real apps, and the gate must hold in code before an action runs; D130's own finding is that a native computer use can only be watched by a tripwire after the fact (research/38 B2). Codex's Computer Use stays for browsing (D130). On the home they run cua-driver's CLI (`cua-driver call`); for a task on a worker the home sends one `computer_call` over the socket and the worker runs it, refusing while 用电脑 is off there and anything outside eleven read/act tools (no `run_script`, `kill_app`, clipboard, shell). (4) **Safety.** Never seen, opened or acted on: password managers, Keychain and Passwords, authenticators, banks, wallets and payment apps, System Settings, cua-driver and Ducter themselves (`computer/gate.ts`). Password fields are numbered but never read or typed; signing in is the person's (`computer_ask_user`, Go = **好了 / Done**). A click, menu item or key whose words send, pay, buy, post, delete, install, call, confirm or sign out waits for **Go** on a decision card that says what will happen; the Go covers that one action in that app for 15 minutes; Return in a mail or chat app and ⌘Return anywhere count as a send, ⌘Delete as a delete. What an app shows is data, never instructions. (5) **What the person sees.** cua-driver acts in the background (no stolen mouse or focus; its own pointer shows where). On hand marks the task **正在用 {Mac} 上的 {app} / On {Mac}, in {app}** on the opened task and under that Mac's chips, each with **停下 / Stop**: from any window it ends app use for that task at once (every tool refuses from then), interrupts its turn and tells it to report. (6) **Name.** zh stays **用电脑**; en **Use apps** becomes **Use the computer**, the clean pair research/70 asked for (parallel to 用手机 / Use a phone); the ⓘ states the literal thing (its apps, the way you do). Verified for real on this Mac: `cua-driver permissions status --json` (both on) and `list_apps` / `get_screen_size`, read-only; nothing was driven. Not yet: a real task in a real app on the author's Mac, the agent cursor seen, the guide walked; `computer_look` on Electron apps (cua-driver's tree can be thin there) **2026-11-13: the author approved the exception to D130 for apps ("按建议") and added 用电脑 and 用手机 on their MacBook.** | U (the author: "both tonight", "do NOT drive the author's real apps"), L (Ducter's tools over native for apps, the gate, the name), research/23, research/38, research/70 | `packages/core/src/computer/{ability,driver,gate,tools,index}.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/{computer-use,index,abilities}.ts`, `packages/core/src/{devices,candidates,decisions,worker-browse,ws,core}.ts`, `packages/runner/src/{brief,runner,worker-host}.ts`, `apps/web/src/{computer-use.tsx,i18n-computeruse.ts,devcard.tsx,sheet.ts,views.tsx,i18n.ts}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/computeruse.go,i18n/computeruse.go,views/computeruse.go,views/devicecard.go,views/task.go,views/components.go}`, tests (`core/test/computer-use.test.ts`, `worker-mac-abilities.test.ts`, `runner/test/phone-brief.test.ts`, `web/test/sheet.test.ts`, Go `computeruse_test.go`), `design/{computer-use,product-model,scenarios,todo,ui-devices-card}.md`, `docs/{devices,privacy-and-safety}.md` |

## 2026-11-12: Reply to one message (GitHub issue #3: "You can reply to a specific conversation, much like in Dots or Muse… to address multiple issues simultaneously"; the author: "the visual expression maxed out, our style's mechanism, emotional value and distinctive stylization, with matching animation; the context design designed and reflected on carefully, to the max; reference T3 Code and Lorca where apt")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D236 | **回复 / Reply to one message: the imprint, a quote that travels with the reply, and routing by what was replied to. Built 2026-11-12 on the web (phone and wide), the iPhone shell and the Mac app; for the author's review.** (1) **Gestures:** swipe a bubble right on the phone (starts at 14pt, arms at 56pt with a light haptic, a drawn reply mark easing in and settling; never within 28pt of the left edge), long press or right click for **回复 / Reply · 复制 / Copy**, hover Reply beside the bubble with a mouse, and on a wide screen a selected part of the partner's words gives **回复这段 / Reply to this part**. Any bubble can be replied to, including each find of a note. (2) **The look is the imprint** (research/72: seven candidates, mapping tables, rubric, 33 haiku rater calls; 34/35): the message replied to as a small copy of its own bubble (its voice's colour and tail) pressed into the reply like a pebble into clay, its first line literal (**Ducter** or **你 / You**), two lines of quote. In the composer while writing (placeholder **回复 {who}… / Reply to {who}…**, × **不回复了 / Cancel reply**, Esc), pressed into the top of the sent bubble after, and pressed into the top of a partner answer only when that answer is not next to its reply (another message of the user came between: several replies in flight); an answer right after its reply shows no quote, adjacency says it. The imprint is debossed, the reverse of a raised clay button (its shadow under the top and left rims, a lit lower edge, its floor a little below the bubble's own colour, no border), its quote one step smaller (12) and quieter than the words, its who-line smaller still (11), so the person's words lead (the polish pass after review, 2026-11-12). Tapping it scrolls the original to the centre and deepens its own tint once (never a glow ring); one no longer on the screen says **那条在更早的对话里，这里翻不到了。/ That message is further back than this screen goes.** Reduced motion: nothing moves, the tint still changes. (3) **Context:** the client names only the message (`ReplyWant`: seq, a find, a part); core logs a `ReplyRef` inside the `user_msg` from the Log (whose words, kind, a quote of at most 200 characters, `clipped`, the tasks it was about, the question or find, its pictures), so the reference survives the window, re-cuts, digests and restarts and costs the same however long the message was (T3 Code: 300 characters and a pointer; Lorca: 280). The partner reads `[user, replying to your message of … (#n): "…"]`, a line per task with where it stands and where more work goes, the pictures again, and a `log_read` pointer when cut. (4) **Routing:** a reply to an open question (a task's, or the partner's own ask) is its answer, resolved by core as Reply (`decision_resolved.msgSeq`), and the partner may not send it again (its task_send is refused); a reply about a task's work goes to that task (task_send) unless its work was merged (rule 2d); a reply to a find is about that find; anything else is conversation about that message. (5) **No cross-talk:** replies to different messages are separate turns (`turnLength`), a turn's history leaves out what is still queued, and each answer replies to its own message. A reply never forks or branches anything. (6) Persona: a "Replies" section; eval: 5 new scenarios (an older task's result, two replies at once, a find, out of the window, an open question), 5/5 on haiku; the whole suite 71/103 (the last full run, D221, 75/97; a recheck of the 32 failures passed 18 of them: haiku's run-to-run noise, none about replies). Its look is a new token-only state beside D199's: full clay (not desaturated), flat on the page with a 1.2 hairline in `line` and no base (it is not a switch here), the where words in `xs` 600 `ink-muted`, and a drawn chevron; it never sinks or squishes. What you hand it here uses that phone when it needs one.** and **任务能像你一样用 {device} 上的应用：看窗口、点按钮、打字。在这里交给 Ducter 的事，要用应用时就去那台做。发送、付款、删除这类事先问你。/ Tasks can use {device}'s apps the way you do: read a window, click, type. (2) **No screen, no 用电脑 ghost.** A home of kind `server`, or any Linux home (core reports every Linux home as a server), shows no 用电脑 chip at all unless another computer has it (then (1)).
