## 2026-11-13: Use this phone (the author: "Go ahead as recommended… the user must know how to set it up without thinking; the setup animation must elegantly show how this ability is turned on and how it's used… For iPhone, iphone-use may be a reference")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D238 | **用这台手机 / Use this phone: a task borrows the person's own Android phone through the Ducter app on it, asking first, showing it the whole time, gated at the effect. Built 2026-11-13 on the home, the Android app, the web client and the Mac app; checked through the real core with a fake phone link and on an Android 16 emulator (the bridge, the pill, CJK typing); for the author's review. Not yet on the author's Pixel.** *Who:* the consumer whose apps (美团, 微信, 小红书) are signed in on their own phone; the developer checking their app on a real device; the fresh user who meets one chip. *Feel:* before, "it can only do the web", or Ducter's phone with every app to install again; after, the task borrows the phone they use, and gives it back when they say. (1) **On the phone:** an Android accessibility service in the Ducter app, switched on once in Android's settings, idle outside a **loan**: a fixed set of calls (state, start, doing, stop, off, screen, jpeg, tap, swipe, key, text, front, apps, launch, size), each refused outside the loan it names. The screen in uiautomator's XML shape, read on the home by the same parser as Ducter's phone; a password field's text never leaves the phone; typing by `ACTION_SET_TEXT` (any language, no keyboard); five keys only; pictures by the accessibility screenshot (Android 11+), never MediaProjection. Not an accessibility tool (`isAccessibilityTool` false: views apps mark sensitive stay out of reach). (2) **The link** is the app's existing connection: its page relays the home's calls to the app's bridge and back (`handset_hello`, `handset_req`, `handset_res`); while lent, the web view's renderer stays important. A paired app that dials in over loopback is still that device when it offers its token. (3) **Which phone:** `needs.phone` work uses the person's own phone when one is added (its apps and sign-ins are theirs), else Ducter's phone (D217); bound at first use; with one added, phone work may run on any computer. Saying which phone in the request (`needs.phone: "mine" \| "ducter"`) is in todo (a partner-tool change, with its eval). (4) **The loan:** a task's first phone tool posts one card, **「{task}」想借你的 {phone} 用一会儿 / "{task}" wants to borrow your {phone}**, **不借 / Not now** · **借给它 / Lend it**, no default ever; Go starts the loan on the phone (or when its app next connects); Not now refuses the phone for that task. One loan per phone. It ends when the task stops working, on **停下 / Stop** from any window or the phone's pill (the turn interrupted, the task told, its phone tools refusing from then on), or when the phone comes back without it. (5) **The gate** is D217's, unchanged: a tap that sends, pays, buys, posts, deletes, installs, calls, confirms or signs out waits for Go (the card says **在你的 {phone} 上**); banks, wallets, payment apps, password managers and authenticators out of reach; the person's own steps (`phone_ask_user`) blind the task until **好了**. Found and fixed on the way: `phone_look` on a blocked app fell through to a picture of it (both phones). (6) **The indicator** (research/74 §8.1, seven candidates, 21 naive-rater answers): a clay pill in the status bar over every app, the partner's face, **{name} 正在用这台手机 / {name} is using this phone**, what it is doing now, **停下 / Stop**, and a soft ring where each tap lands; **轮到你了** with an accent border in the person's turn. An accessibility overlay: no extra permission, never in what it reads. (7) **Turning it on** (research/74 §8.2): on the phone's own card, **+ 用这台手机** opens **让任务能借用这台手机 / Let tasks borrow this phone**: each step a clay miniature of Android's own page in Android's own words with a fingertip where to tap, **去设置** opening the right page, the step ticking itself; on Android 13+ for a browser-downloaded APK three steps (tap the switch once, App info › ⋮ › 允许受限制的设置, then 使用 Ducter › 允许), else one; the app comes back to the front by itself when the service connects; then a looping demo of a loan (the pill, a ring, a Go card, Stop) and **知道了**. (8) **Removing it** also turns Android's switch off on the phone (a service can turn itself off, never on), for banking apps that refuse to run beside one. (9) **iPhone: not offered.** iOS lets no app use other apps; `leeguooooo/iphone-use` (PolyForm Shield 1.0.0: source-available, no competing use, so no code taken) drives an XCTest runner from a Mac and needs full Xcode, a signing team (7-day re-signing on a free one), Developer Mode and the phone unlocked; iPhone Mirroring gives only pixels, needs the phone locked and one Mac, and isn't offered in the EU. An iPhone's card shows nothing, not a "coming later". Recorded in todo with what would change it. (10) **Not verified:** the restricted-settings path and Pixel's own strings, the allowance surviving a self-update, the page keeping its socket in the background on a real phone, tree-read times on a real device, a real errand. | U (the author's brief), research/74 | `apps/android/app/src/main/java/co/nowledge/ducter/android/{Handset,HandsetPill,HandsetTree,MainActivity}.kt`, `apps/android/app/src/main/{AndroidManifest.xml,res/xml/handset_service.xml,res/values*/strings.xml}`, `apps/android/app/src/debug/` (the probe), `apps/android/app/src/test/…/HandsetTreeTest.kt`, `packages/protocol/src/{handset.ts,phone-use.ts,index.ts}`, `packages/core/src/handset/index.ts`, `packages/core/src/{android/index.ts,android/tools.ts,ws.ts,devices.ts,decisions.ts,loadout.ts,candidates.ts,core.ts,auth.ts}`, `packages/core/test/handset.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/{handset.tsx,i18n-handset.ts,app.ts,main.tsx,devcard.tsx,views.tsx,phone-use.tsx,i18n.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/handset.go,protocol/protocol.go,protocol/phoneuse.go,store/phoneuse.go,views/handset.go,views/components.go,views/task.go,views/devicecard.go,i18n/handset.go}`, `design/{use-this-phone.md,phone-use.md,product-model.md,scenarios.md,ui.md,todo.md,prototypes/use-this-phone.html}`, `docs/phone.md`, `research/74-own-phone-use.md` |

## 2026-11-13: A file a task made opens inside Ducter (the author, with a screenshot of `investigation.md · 5 KB · 打开`: "让我点开一个文件居然是 hel 上的链接 … 应该能在 ducter 里合理设计 ui 查看"; T3 Code as the bar)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D239 | **A file a task showed opens inside Ducter, in a viewer that suits it; Open never sends the person to the home's address. Built 2026-11-13 on the web (phone and wide) and the Mac app; for the author's review.** *Who:* the developer reading a task's `investigation.md` or a script it wrote; the consumer handed "the report" in a messaging app; the fresh user who taps a card and expects to read it. *Feel:* before, a tap threw them into a browser at a server's address and a page of raw text (and on a phone, a new tab); after, the page of the report grows out of its card in the conversation, laid out, and closes back into it. (1) **The bug** (research/75): the Mac opened `<home>/media/<id>?k=<key>&dl=1` in the default browser, the web a new tab; no device token, but the file's key (never expires) went into (synced) browser history with the home's address; and the home answered `inline` for every non-picture even with `&dl=1`. Fixed: clients fetch the bytes themselves, as pictures already are, and `&dl=1` is always a download. (2) **The viewer**, by kind (`fileView` in `@ducter/protocol`, mirrored in Go): Markdown laid out as a document (every heading level, tables, quotes, task and nested lists, front matter shown quietly, code coloured by its fence's language; pictures in it show their words); CSV/TSV as a table (header row stays, numbers on the right, 2000 rows); text, JSON, HTML (its source, never run) and code with the workbench's colours and line numbers (`hl.ts` on the web, MyGo's editor read only on the Mac); on the web an SVG as a picture and a video playing; anything else (PDF, Office, zip; on the Mac also SVG source and video) said plainly: **这种文件在 Ducter 里还打不开。存一份，用你自己的应用看。/ Ducter can't open this kind of file yet. Save a copy and open it in your own app.** (the Mac: **…可以用这台 Mac 上的应用看，或者存一份。/ …Open it in an app on this Mac, or save a copy.** with **在 Mac 上打开 / Open on this Mac**, from a copy in the user's own temporary folder, 0600). Markdown and tables have **看原文 / Show source** ↔ **看排版 / Show formatted**. (3) **Shape:** a sheet of the theme's paper over the dimmed page (wide: up to 48 rem, the reading measure inside; a phone: the whole screen, the head pulled down closes it); a head with the file's drawn mark on a sunken tile (lines for a document, a grid for a table), its name, **{KIND} · {size} · {n} 行 / {n} lines**, ×; a foot with **来自 {task} / From {task}**, **拷贝 / Copy** (the text) and **存一份 / Save a copy** (the whole file: Downloads on the Mac, **已存到「下载」**; the share sheet on a phone; a download from the bytes elsewhere). Esc, × or the scrim closes it. (4) **Motion:** the sheet grows out of the card (a shared element: the card's box to the sheet's, 320 ms) and shrinks back into it when the card is on screen; under reduced motion a fade. The card itself is now a clay button (raised on its ledge, pressed in), its drawn mark telling the kind. (5) **Bounded:** the first 1 MB of a text is read, cut at a whole line, and the foot of the page says **这里只显示了前 1.0 MB。存一份就能看到全部。/ Showing the first 1.0 MB here. Save a copy to see all of it.**; long texts are drawn in blocks the browser skips while out of view. (6) **What may arrive:** a task may now show text and source (`.ts`, `.go`, `.sh`, `.log`, `.yaml`, … `TEXT_EXTS`) as `text/plain`, served as text and never run; never `.env`; text has no NUL in its first 8 KB. (7) **Reuse, not a second viewer:** the workbench (D214) reads the live working copy; a shown file is a snapshot in the home's store that outlives it, so the viewer reuses the workbench's renderers, not its data path; the picture viewer keeps pictures. Not built: PDF pages drawn inside Ducter (pdf.js on the web; Chromium won't draw a PDF under the media CSP's `sandbox`), expiring media keys, find in a long file, a real iPhone pass (the phone shots are Chromium at 390 px with an iPhone agent, the conversation in the iOS Simulator). | U (the author, 2026-11-13), research/75 | `packages/protocol/src/media.ts`, `packages/protocol/test/fileview.test.ts`, `packages/core/src/media.ts`, `packages/core/test/media.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/{fileview.tsx,markdown-doc.ts,markdown.ts,media.tsx,views.tsx,app.ts,i18n.ts,i18n-fileview.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/test/markdown-doc.test.ts`, `apps/web/demo-file.ts`, `apps/desktop/{mediahands.go,internal/protocol/fileview.go,internal/media/upload.go,internal/views/{fileview.go,media.go,app.go},internal/i18n/fileview.go}`, `design/{media.md,workbench.md,product-model.md,scenarios.md,todo.md}`, `docs/pictures.md`, `research/75-file-viewer.md` |

## 2026-11-13: A task keeps its harness's own tools (the author worried Ducter makes tasks weaker than the harness run by hand; "第一性原理仔细搞清楚吧，然后可以做需要做的")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D240 | **A browser place changes which of the person's own config loads, never which of the harness's own tools exist; the only built-ins it takes away are the ones that would start a model without the task's brief. Built 2026-11-13; checked by tests and by measurement (research/76), not yet in a live browser task.** *Who:* the developer who expects a task to be at least the Claude Code they run by hand; the consumer who asks "look this up and write it down" and gets a task that can't edit its own file or search the web; the fresh user, who never sees the difference except in results. *Feel:* before, a task that touches a web page quietly loses Edit, WebSearch, WebFetch and the rest; after, the same harness as everywhere else, with the browser's rules still around it. (1) **What was found (research/76):** every task's brief is appended on all four harnesses (Claude Code `--append-system-prompt-file`, Codex `developerInstructions`, pi `--append-system-prompt`, DeepSeek Harness a first-message preamble); no task's prompt is replaced. The one real weakening: Claude Code browser tasks ran with `--tools Bash,Read,Write,ToolSearch`. (2) **Claude Code, every browser place (cua-driver, Claude in Chrome, Ducter's browser):** no `--tools` allowlist; denied are Ducter's seven for every task (plan mode, Cron ×3, ScheduleWakeup, AskUserQuestion), **Agent** and **Workflow**, and on cua-driver the driver's tools outside the ten. Why those two: a subagent gets the parent's MCP tools but not its appended brief (measured, 2.1.322), so it would drive the browser with none of D62's Go and untrusted-page rules or D130's own-window rules. `userConfiguration: "exclude"` stays: it keeps the person's MCP servers, plugins, hooks and CLAUDE.md out of a session that reads hostile pages. (3) **Cost, measured** (haiku, isolated, per request): the old four 6.6K tokens, D240's set 12.2K (+5.5K), an ordinary task 17.0K; on a repeated call in a session the tool block is a cache read (a tenth of fresh input), and 5.5K is 3.4% of a task's 160K compaction point. The old 332k → 140k came from three changes at once (user config, cua-driver denials, built-ins) and can't be split; the built-ins were roughly half (INFERRED). (4) **Codex:** unchanged in code. Its clean setup for a browser task already keeps its own browser, Computer Use, plugins as a feature and image generation; `multi_agent` and `goals` stay off, now on purpose and written down (a Codex subagent's brief is unchecked; Ducter owns when a turn ends). `system.md` said only the partner dropped them; corrected. (5) **pi:** unchanged. Its clean flags (`--no-skills --no-context-files --no-prompt-templates --no-extensions --no-themes`) remove only the person's own resources; no built-in tool. (6) **DeepSeek Harness:** unchanged; its browser task keeps the person's own setup (no capability lost; the missing clean home is open in todo). (7) **Name what to deny, not what to keep:** an allowlist rots as a harness renames tools (`TodoWrite` no longer exists in 2.1.322). (8) **Open:** a live rerun of the HN lookup; whether an ordinary Codex task's subagents see the brief while Computer Use is on; `PushNotification`, `RemoteTrigger` and `Workflow` (8.2K tokens) in every ordinary Claude Code task; pi's `--no-context-files` also drops a repo's own AGENTS.md under `isolateUserConfig`. | U (the question), L (the call), research/76 | `packages/runner/src/{browser.ts,runner.ts,oar.ts}`, `packages/runner/test/{browser.test.ts,ducter-browser.test.ts}`, `design/{system.md,onboarding.md,todo.md}`, `research/76-task-tool-set.md` |
| D241 | **A task leaves out of its harness only what competes with Ducter or would act without the brief; everything else, the project's own instructions included, stays. Built 2026-11-13; checked by tests and by reading the harnesses' source (research/76 §6), not yet in a live task.** *Who:* the developer whose task should behave like their own Claude Code, Codex, pi or DSH; the consumer whose task must never push to their phone or start agents on their account behind Ducter's back; the fresh user, who sees only that tasks report through Ducter. *Feel:* before, a task could send its own push notification, schedule a remote agent, fan out a hidden workflow, lose its repo's AGENTS.md on pi, or read pages in the person's own DSH setup; after, the same harness, with Ducter the one voice. (1) **Claude Code, every task and the partner:** PushNotification (a push to the person's terminal and phone), RemoteTrigger (scheduled remote agents on the person's claude.ai account, outliving the task) and Workflow (a scripted fan-out of agents that never see the brief) join Ducter's seven denials (`oar.ts` DISALLOWED). Measured in an ordinary task: 17,042 → 15,048 tokens a request (Workflow ~2.0K; D240's 8.2K was its cost over the four only, corrected here; the other two are deferred, ~10 tokens). (2) **Agent stays in ordinary tasks:** a subagent's result returns to the task, which holds the brief, and the tools that act for the person outside a browser (the phone, the person's apps) are Ducter's own, their Go gate enforced by the tool and stated in its description; only a browser place, whose tools enforce none of D62's rules, denies it (D240). (3) **Codex subagents inherit the brief:** a spawned agent's `developer_instructions` are the parent turn's (codex 0.167.2 `child_config.rs`; upstream test `spawned_multi_agent_v2_child_inherits_parent_developer_context`), unless an agent role with its own instructions replaces them; roles are the person's config (`[agents]`, `~/.codex/agents/`). So an ordinary Codex task keeps the person's `multi_agent` (a role they wrote is their own Codex as by hand); a browser task keeps it off, since `-c` can't leave their roles out. No code change. (4) **pi, a clean task (opt-in `isolateUserConfig`, a browser place):** pi has no flag that drops only the global context file (`--no-context-files` drops all; the finer seam is SDK-only), so Ducter keeps the flag and appends the project's own AGENTS.md / CLAUDE.md after the brief in pi's own form, read from the task's folder up to its repository's top (never pi's agent dir, never folders above the repository). The partner, whose prompt is its own and eval'd (D175), gets nothing added. (5) **DeepSeek Harness, a browser place:** a home of Ducter's for the task (`userConfiguration: "exclude"` on DSH), keyed by its folder so a changed brief resumes in the same home, the brief in that home's AGENTS.md, DSH's own tools all kept (they come from its built-in `acp` profile). A home of Ducter's has no credential file, so when neither the env nor `keys.env` has the key, the key from the person's own DSH credential file rides in the launch env (never the file); this also closes the gap for the partner's and memory-off homes. (6) **Left open (todo):** the live HN rerun (needs the author's Chrome and running instance); Claude Code's `EnterWorktree` against Ducter's own worktrees; pi's global `SYSTEM.md`, which replaces pi's prompt in a task and has no off switch. | U ("按建议，剩下的待办也都做了吧"), research/76 §6 | `packages/runner/src/{oar.ts,browser.ts,runner.ts}`, `packages/protocol/src/internal.ts`, `packages/runner/test/task-tools.test.ts`, `design/{system.md,todo.md}`, `research/76-task-tool-set.md` |

## 2026-11-13: On hand beside the opened task, everywhere there is room (the author approved research/78's items 1, 1a, 2 and 2a: "按建议")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D244 | **Opening a task keeps On hand beside it wherever there is room, on the Mac as on the web: rail · task · workbench, with Lody's two-slot rule when only two fit. A rail row has one status place, the partner's own small face; a task that finished unseen keeps its done face until opened. Built 2026-11-13; checked by view tests and headless shots, not yet on a real iPad or Fold.** *Who:* the heavy developer with 20 tasks who opens one to answer it and wants the next one a key away, without losing the list (Conductor's sense of control); the consumer on an 11-inch iPad held across, who opens the hotel errand and still sees the call and the routine beside it; the fresh user with one errand, who still sees one calm row (nothing opened, nothing beside). *Feel:* before, on the Mac opening a task left On hand: the window became the task, and getting to the next one meant Esc, j, Enter; on the web the rail appeared only at the whole screen from 1360 px, so no iPad ever saw it. After, the list stays where it was, the open task is marked in it, ⌘⇧] walks to the next, and a task that finished while you looked elsewhere greets you with the partner's done face. *First time:* nothing to learn: opening a task on a wide window simply keeps the list at the left. (1) **The Mac (`task.go`, `onhandrail.go`):** an opened task is **On hand's rail \| the task \| the workbench (520)**, under the window's header (crumbs and the task's actions stay full width). The rail is `clamp(22% of the window, 300, 340)` wide on the deeper ground (`bg-deep`), no hairline. Rail and task from **1100 pt**; all three from **1560 pt**; narrower with the workbench open, the workbench takes the rail's place (**task \| workbench**, Lody's two-slot rule); under 1100, the task alone. The rail shows for any opened task when there is room (from On hand, the conversation, ⌘O). With no task open, On hand stays the 960 list. The rail and the workbench slide their width in and out (260 ms, Pebble's ease); under reduced motion they snap. (2) **Keys:** **⌘⇧] / ⌘⇧[** open the next and the previous task in the rail's order from anywhere, the composer included (Lody's keys; the Mac's View menu **下一件事 / Next Task** · **上一件事 / Previous Task**; the web the same with Ctrl elsewhere); j/k do it when no field has the keys. (3) **The rail row (both platforms):** the D216 two-line row, narrower. Line one ends in **one status place**, in priority **needs you > working > new result**: the partner's own clay face at 24 pt (needs you: the raised flag; working: reading; new result: the done face, or the flag for one that didn't work), still in a row (no breathing). An active row shows only `[title][face]`: its `+a −d` and time go (they return when it rests). A resting row shows its `+a −d` and time (Draft PR and Merged stay off the narrow rail; the task beside it says them). In the 960 list, beside the conversation and on the phone, only **new result** takes the place (the sections already say needs you and working). (4) **New result:** a finished task (done, done as claimed, didn't work) whose card changed after this device last had it open, counting only finishes after this device started keeping track, so a fresh install shows none. Kept per device (the Mac's data folder hints, the web's localStorage); opening the task clears it; its row stays in full ink until then. Its label is **新结果 / New result**. **The handover:** a row this device saw working that turns into a new result plays once: the reading face sinks and fades as the done face pops up with Pebble's bouncy spring (about 520 ms). It replaces Lody's grid collapsing into a dot; D73 holds (a face, never a dot). Under reduced motion the faces cross-fade. (5) **A folded group keeps its most urgent mark:** By project's groups fold from their name (a drawn chevron), remembered per device. A folded group's header carries the face of its most urgent task (needs you > working > new result) and, on hover and to a screen reader, the counts: **1 件等你 · 2 件在做 · 3 个新结果 / 1 needs you · 2 working · 3 new results**. A folded Needs you is never hidden. (6) **The web (`wide.ts`, `main.tsx`, `app.css`):** at the whole screen the rail comes from **1120 px** (ROOMY, was 1360) at `clamp(300px, 22vw, 340px)`, so an 11-inch iPad held across (1180–1194) gets **On hand \| task**. All three from **1560** (ROOMY_BENCH, was 1720); under it the workbench swaps the rail out (two slots), never a sheet. **On a book fold** (a Pixel Fold's inner screen, D182) a task opened from the whole-screen On hand stands in the second half with the rail in the first, nothing across the fold (it was the task alone in its half). Phones and the two-pane default (conversation \| side pane) are unchanged: no third pane beside the conversation. The rail's arrival and the swap are a view transition (320 ms; none under reduced motion). (7) **The workbench's panel (both):** its edge paints nothing at rest (the deeper ground is the edge); hovered, a 4 × 40 grip in `line-2`; dragged or focused, `ink-muted`. The Mac's workbench now resizes too (380–760, kept on this Mac). When it is open but has no page to show (the page shown went away: the preview stopped, the browser or the phone was put down), it shows a **launcher** instead of a blank or closing on its own: **这里会出现什么 / What shows up here**, the pages it can hold as clay tiles with their marks and when each appears (**它在文件夹里做事时 / When it works in a folder** · **它开了网页时 / When it serves a page** · **它用 Ducter 的浏览器时 / When it uses Ducter's browser** · **它用手机或模拟器时 / When it uses a phone or simulator** · **它做出图片、文件或链接时 / When it makes pictures, files or links**), a page that has something raised and openable. (8) **Two screens side by side:** when the Phone page has two screens (Ducter's phone and a simulator, or two simulators), the choice above them adds **并排 / Side by side**, showing both in one row, each in its own frame with its own label and driver line; kept per device. (9) **Not taken:** Lody's flat grey density and 26 px rows; its tile grid and unread dot; tab pills for several conversations; a third pane beside the conversation; resizing the rail (its clamp follows the window; todo if asked). | U ("按建议"), research/78 §3 and items 1, 1a, 2, 2a | `apps/desktop/internal/views/{task.go,onhandrail.go,onhandlist.go,byproject.go,workbench.go,benchlaunch.go,benchsim.go,app.go}`, `apps/desktop/internal/hints/{hints.go,onhand.go}`, `apps/desktop/main.go`, `apps/web/src/{wide.ts,main.tsx,onhand.tsx,board.tsx,workbench.tsx,seen.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `design/{ui.md,workbench.md,product-model.md,scenarios.md,todo.md}`, `docs/{on-hand.md,phone.md}` |

## 2026-11-13: The file viewer's open items (the author: "剩下的待办也都做了吧", D239's not-built list)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D242 | **D239's open items, built 2026-11-13 on the web (and so the iPhone and Android apps) and the Mac app; for the author's review: a PDF's pages drawn inside Ducter, media keys that expire, find in a long file, and the Mac's read-only views without a current line or MyGo's English "Copy".** *Who:* the developer reading a PDF report or searching a long log a task left; the consumer handed "the report" as a PDF; the fresh user who taps a PDF card and expects to read it, not to be told to save it. *Feel:* before, a PDF said "can't open this yet", a long log was scrolled by eye, and a link to a picture or file kept working forever wherever it ended up; after, the PDF's pages are simply there, ⌘F finds the word, and a link that leaks stops opening within a day, with nothing for the person to do. (1) **A PDF's pages** (`fileView` → `pdf`, `FilePDF`): one under the other, each a sheet of white paper (`--pdf-page` / `PDFPage`, the one fixed white: printed matter) on the sunken ground at the reading width; the head says **{n} 页 / {n} pages**. The web draws with pdf.js (Mozilla, Apache-2.0, `pdfjs-dist` 4.10.38: its prebuilt modules copied to `app/pdf.js` and `app/pdf.worker.js`, about 1.7 MB, loaded only when a PDF opens, `isEvalSupported: false`) from the bytes it fetched: Chromium's own reader won't run in a document served under the media CSP's `sandbox` (research/75). The Mac draws with Core Graphics (the engine Preview uses), called through purego as the app already reaches the system without cgo; MyGo has no PDF view (research/75 §5, VERIFIED by a test that draws a page). Pages are drawn as they come within about a screen of view and let go when they leave, so memory holds a few pages; at twice their width in points. A PDF that can't be drawn: **这个 PDF 在这里画不出来。存一份，用你自己的应用看。/ This PDF can't be drawn here. Save a copy and open it in your own app.** (the Mac keeps **在 Mac 上打开**). Alternative not taken: keep Open on this Mac only (no native path was missing). (2) **Expiring media keys.** `k` = `<exp>.<sig>`, an HMAC of `<id>.<exp>`, open for 24–30 hours (keys signed in one 6-hour window end together: one URL, one cache entry). The home signs every ref afresh as a frame leaves for a client (`fresh` in ws.ts `send`), so the Log keeps ids and whatever key it holds never decides, and old messages, reconnects, a first screen and a task's history all arrive with good keys; nothing in the Log is rewritten. A client holding keys past their time asks over its own connection: `media_keys {ids}` → `media_keys {mediaKeys}` (not `keys`: the saved frame's). Web (`media-fresh.ts`): the newest key per id, a stale one asked about before use, a failed picture or fetch asking once a minute at most and drawing again. Mac: the newest key per id in `protocol` (`MediaPath`), the picture cache keyed by id, failed pictures and the open file fetched again when keys come. **Migration:** a pre-D242 key (a bare HMAC of the id) opens for 7 days after the home's first expiring key (`media/.keys-expire`), then never: an old Mac app keeps its pictures across the update, and a link already in a browser's history dies within the week. The tunnel (D228) and any later relay carry the same requests; nothing changes with the path. (3) **Find in a long file:** ⌘F / Ctrl+F or the head's drawn magnifier (**查找 / Find**) in a text, a report or a table: a field pressed into the head (**在这个文件里找 / Find in this file**), **{i} / {n}** or **没找到 / No matches**, **上一个 / Previous** · **下一个 / Next** (Enter, ⌘G; ⇧ back); Esc closes the field before the file. Every match in the highlight's colours, the current one in the accent, scrolled to (across too). Case set aside, never across two lines, at most 5000 (`findAll` / `FindAll`, the same rules). The web marks with the CSS Custom Highlight API (the current match selected where a browser lacks it); the Mac marks the editor's text. MyGo's Markdown view has no ranges to mark, so on the Mac a report or a table is found in its text, and closing Find lays it out again; a PDF has no find yet. (4) **The Mac's read-only views:** the code view has no current line (`NoActiveLine`: nothing stands there); code blocks carry Ducter's own **拷贝 / Copy** (**已拷贝**) in the viewer and the conversation, not MyGo's "Copy code". | U (the author, 2026-11-13: "剩下的待办也都做了吧"), D239's list, research/75 §5 | `packages/protocol/src/{media.ts,index.ts,internal.ts}`, `packages/protocol/test/fileview.test.ts`, `packages/core/src/{media.ts,ws.ts,web.ts,assets.d.ts}`, `packages/core/test/media.test.ts`, `apps/web/{build.ts,package.json,demo-file.ts}`, `apps/web/src/{fileview.tsx,fileview-pdf.tsx,fileview-find.ts,media-fresh.ts,media.tsx,app.ts,i18n-fileview.ts}`, `apps/web/static/{app.css,app.js,pdf.js,pdf.worker.js}`, `apps/web/test/fileview-find.test.ts`, `apps/desktop/internal/{media/pdf_darwin.go,media/pdf_other.go,media/pdf_test.go,protocol/media.go,protocol/fileview.go,protocol/protocol.go,theme/theme.go,i18n/fileview.go,views/fileview.go,views/media.go,views/app.go}`, `design/{media.md,product-model.md,scenarios.md,todo.md}`, `docs/pictures.md`, `research/75-file-viewer.md` |

## 2026-11-13: A phone task never asks for adb; a connected phone is one tap away (the author, testing on their Pixel Fold with a Linux home: "发现 agent 要我手机连 usb/WiFi adb 我需要用户只需手机连上客户端就能被控制 这个我们没做到么？")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D247 | **Work that needs a phone, when the person's Android app is connected but 用这台手机 / Use this phone isn't on there, opens as a task; its first phone tool shows one card, **「{task}」要用你的 {phone}** · **在你的 {phone} 上打开「用这台手机」…** · **先不用 / Not now** · **去打开 / Turn it on**; Go, from any window, opens the guide on that phone, and once Android's switch is on the task goes on by itself. No task ever asks for adb, USB or wireless debugging, a cable or Developer options. Built 2026-11-13 on the home, the web client and the Mac app; checked through the real core with a fake phone link and by the partner eval; not yet on the author's Pixel.** *Who:* the consumer who says "用我的手机…" and has only installed the app; the developer testing their own phone, who must not be handed a debugging chore; the fresh user, who has never heard of an accessibility service. *Feel:* before, a task that asked them to turn on USB debugging (a chore, and a scare: a cable and a developer screen on their own phone); after, one card that says what it needs and one tap that takes them to the one-minute guide on the phone in their hand, and the work carrying on when it's on. (1) **How the adb request happened** (traced in code; hel's Log contents were not read, so which path ran is INFERRED): `needs.phone` was described to the partner as "Ducter's own phone, on the computer that has 用手机", so for "use my phone" on a home with neither it could open an ordinary task without `needs.phone`; that task (a harness on hel with a shell) had no phone tools and nothing in its brief about phones, and did what a developer would: asked for USB/Wi-Fi debugging to run adb. With `needs.phone` the loadout refused (`NO_PHONE`), and the partner's line named 用这台手机 but no phone and no tap: a dead end. No Ducter code tells the person about adb (its only adb drives Ducter's own emulator, D217). (2) **The partner** sets `needs.phone` for any work on a phone (theirs or Ducter's), even with none set up, and never mentions adb, debugging, a cable or Developer options (the tool's description; eval scenarios `own-phone-not-set-up-zh/en`). (3) **Routing:** no phone that can serve the task (none added, no Ducter's phone where it runs) and an Android app connected now (bridge 1+) → the task opens and binds that phone; its first phone tool posts the card (`handset: {kind: "setup"}`, no default) and tells the task to end its turn. Go: Android's switch already on → added at once, the loan starts; otherwise the home sends `handset_guide` to that phone's page, which opens **让任务能借用这台手机** there (D238 §6); when the app isn't open, at its next connection. When it is added, the task's loan starts with no second card: the tap on its card was the yes for that task. Not now: the task carries on without a phone (its phone tools refuse, it reports what it would do). (4) **No Android app connected:** nothing opens, and the partner hears one line naming the one thing to do: open Ducter on {phone} (paired, not open), update it (too old), or install Ducter on their Android phone and add 用这台手机 there, before 用手机 on a Mac; each ends "Never suggest adb…". (5) **Every task's brief** (`deviceRule`, all harnesses, every place): the user's phone is reached only through Ducter's `phone_*` tools; never ask for USB or wireless debugging, a cable or Developer options, never run adb against a phone of theirs, unless the brief itself asks for exactly that (the developer who types "adb install it"); without phone tools, report blocked in one line. The phone section of the brief now names both phones. (6) **Not built:** a notification on the phone when the card is tapped elsewhere and the app is in the background (an Android change; the guide opens when the app is next in front; todo); offering a paired phone whose app isn't open as a card that waits (it is a line for now). | U (the author's report), L (the call) | `packages/protocol/src/handset.ts`, `packages/core/src/{handset/index.ts,android/index.ts,loadout.ts,candidates.ts,core.ts}`, `packages/core/test/handset-setup.test.ts`, `packages/runner/src/brief.ts`, `packages/runner/test/phone-brief.test.ts`, `packages/partner/src/partner.ts`, `packages/partner/eval/scenarios.ts`, `apps/web/src/{handset.tsx,i18n-handset.ts}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{protocol/handset.go,views/components.go,i18n/handset.go}`, `design/{use-this-phone.md,product-model.md,scenarios.md,todo.md}`, `docs/phone.md` |

## 2026-11-13: The Android app on a real Pixel Fold (the author: "连接中的动画是紧缩在左侧的竖条 文字也断行 … Android 里说我们 app 不发送通知 不太对 · use this phone 没有 icon · 开启 use this phone 第一步应该是允许受限 第二步才是开启 accessibility")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D246 | **Polish of the Android app after the author's first run on a Pixel Fold: the connecting screen sits centred at its size; the app asks once for notifications when 用这台手机 is on and shows one, the loan's, with 停下; the + chip shows its picture; the Use-this-phone guide is two steps, allow restricted settings first, then turn Ducter on. Built 2026-11-13, checked on an Android 16 foldable emulator; for the author's check on the Pixel.** *Who:* the author on his own Pixel Fold first; then the consumer who sideloads the APK and meets Android's restricted-settings wall; the fresh user, for whom "allow, then switch on" is the order that makes sense. *Feel:* before, a squeezed partner and a broken word on the big screen, Android saying the app sends nothing, a bare text chip, and a guide whose step 1 looked like step 2; after, the partner standing in the middle of its half, App info listing **在用这台手机 / Using this phone**, the chip's clay phone, and a guide in the order a person expects. (1) **Connecting:** the app's own connecting screen is a phone-wide column centred in a full-size frame; the frame, not the column, takes the bars' and a book's fold padding (D182). Before, on an open Fold the column itself (480 dp at most) took the half-screen padding and kept a few dp, so the partner squeezed and **正在连接…** broke letter by letter. It settles in after 180 ms (rises 8 dp, fades in, a small overshoot; at once with animations off), so a page that opens from the cache never flashes it; one line, 14 sp. (2) **Notifications:** the app declared no `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` and made no channel, so Android 13+ said it "doesn't send notifications". Messages from the home still wait for push (D181 (4), UnifiedPush in todo); what the app does show now is the loan's: while a task borrows the phone, **{name} 正在用这台手机 / {name} is using this phone** with the task (or **做完以后，在卡片上点「好了」** in the person's turn) and **停下 / Stop**, silent, ongoing, the second place for Stop beside the pill (research/74 §4). One channel, **在用这台手机 / Using this phone** (**任务借用这台手机时一直显示，带「停下」。**), made at launch so App info lists it from the first run. **Asked once** (Android 13+), when it starts to matter: when 用这台手机 has just been turned on and the app comes back by itself, or at the next open of a phone where it already is; never at first launch, never again after an answer. No channel is made for what isn't sent. (3) **The chip's picture:** a **+** chip (look `add`) now shows its picture too, softened, on every card (D43: what it does is shown before it is on); before, + chips were words only, so **+ 用这台手机**, alone on the phone's card, had no picture. The accessibility service keeps the app's own icon in Android's list (checked: it shows). (4) **The guide's order:** **1 允许受限制的设置 / Allow restricted settings** (only where Android restricts a sideloaded Ducter), **2 打开 Ducter / Turn on Ducter**; step 2 opens only when step 1 is done. Android shows **Allow restricted settings** in App info only after the person has met the block once (research/74 §2.1; checked on the emulator: no ⋮ menu before, the item after), so step 1 has two parts shown under it: **在「无障碍」里碰一下 Ducter / Tap Ducter once in Accessibility** (Android's dialog; on 15+ **系统已拒绝向此应用授予访问权限 / App was denied access** with **关闭 / Close**) and **在应用信息里允许 / Allow it in App info**. Android 16 refuses an app the read of its own restricted-settings op (checked: `SecurityException`), so the app says what it can: not restricted on Android 12 or older, for a store install, or once Ducter's switch has been on on this install (allowing lasts until uninstall); likely restricted for an APK installed from a download; unknown otherwise; where the op is readable (older Android) it also says whether the block was met. Where the app can't tell, the guide moves on by the person's visits and step 2 offers **「Ducter」还是灰的？回到第 1 步 / Ducter still greyed out? Back to step 1**. Steps tick themselves as before. | U (the author's report from his Pixel Fold), research/74 | `apps/android` (`Screens.kt`, `Pebble.kt`, `Notify.kt`, `Handset.kt`, `HandsetPill.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `Store.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml`, `res/drawable/ic_note_handset.xml`, strings), `apps/web/src/{handset.tsx,i18n-handset.ts,controls.tsx}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/test/handset-guide.test.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/handset.ts`, `design/{use-this-phone,android,product-model,todo}.md`, `design/prototypes/use-this-phone.html`, `docs/phone.md` |

## 2026-11-13: Screens a task drives, the way people expect them (the author: "按建议 go" on bringing Rakazo's computer-use behaviours into Ducter, in Ducter's look)

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D248 | **A screen a task drives is calm and honest about cost: looking never wakes it, a reconnect never flashes, the person's turn never cuts a step in half, and what it shows under the screen is what the task did, never what it read. Built 2026-11-13 for Ducter's phone on the home, the web and the Mac, with the brief rule and the 30-minute awake window; teach by showing is designed, not built. Checked by tests and headless screenshots; not yet on a real phone.** *Who:* the developer who opens a phone task's workbench to look (before, that booted a 3 GB emulator), the consumer glancing at a task that books for them, the fresh user who sees a phone screen beside a task for the first time. *Feel:* before, a black box that said 正在打开手机… on every reopen, a 接手 that could land mid-tap, nothing that said what it had done; after, a dozing phone with 叫醒, the last picture held while the new one fades in, "这一步做完就轮到你", and a short list of effects (好用, 顺滑, 赏心悦目, 放心; 惊喜: the phone dozes like the laptop does). *First time:* nothing to learn: the asleep art, one button. (1) **Asleep, never woken by looking:** a watch view of Ducter's phone when it isn't running is **asleep**: no hold, no start, no frames; it opens by itself when a task starts the phone; only **叫醒 / Wake** starts it (`phone_wake`). The art is the asleep devices' (D77, D81): the phone dozing under its knitted blanket, breathing at 4.8 s, with **Ducter 的手机睡着了 / Ducter's phone is asleep**, **看看不会叫醒它。叫醒要几秒钟，会用 {Mac} 的电和内存。** (with a task: **任务一用到它，它就会醒。**). Chosen over black glass, moon, "Zzz", a power symbol and words alone (research/82 §4.1: 5/5 LLM-proxy raters read it as asleep with Wake, 0/5 broken; black glass 2/5 broken). The device card, a card's own turn and Try it are explicit and still start it. Ducter's browser already never started from a watch (D190). The phone's card says **醒着 / 睡着了** (was 已开机 / 已关机). (2) **Smooth reconnects:** each client keeps the last frame per phone in memory; a view that opens again starts on it, a little quieter, with **正在重新连上… / Reconnecting…**; the new stream fades in over it (360 ms; none with reduced motion), the screen keeping its shape. (3) **Whose turn:** **接手 / My turn** while a phone tool is in flight waits for that step: the button reads **这一步做完就轮到你 / Yours after this step**, the line **它在做这一步，做完就停下来等你。**, and the turn is the person's when the step ends (8 s at most); tapping it again lets it be. **还给它 / Hand it back** stays one tap; **停下 / Stop** is never locked. The chip stays D214's **它在用 / 你在用** with its clay bead (raters: 4.6/5 clear, 0/5 alarmed; a red "AI is controlling" banner alarmed 5/5); it now follows the phone's turn on the web's Phone page (it was fixed to "it's driving") and is idle while asleep. (4) **它做了什么 / What it did:** the home keeps per task, in memory only, the last eight taps, typings, keys, swipes and apps opened (the screen's own labels, never typed text, never a look or a picture: proactive restraint); a watch shows the newest five under the screen: **点了「{label}」 · 在「{label}」里输入了文字 · 按了{key} · 滑动了屏幕 · 打开了 {app}**, each with a small drawn mark. (5) **A picture, not the person's turn:** the task brief for phone and app work says: when the person only needs to see something (a QR code, a confirmation number), `phone_screenshot` into the report's `show` (D206 shows it as a picture), with a default; their own turn only when their hands are needed; in a Mac app, say which window. (6) **The 30-minute window:** a request for the person's own turn on the phone (`phone_ask_user`) keeps the phone awake 30 minutes so the page is still there; then the task lets go and it may sleep; opening the card wakes it. (7) **Teach by showing, designed:** **做给它看 / Show it how** (raters: clear, never bossy; 教它一次 read as bossy 2/4) in the phone's live view: the person does it once in their turn while it watches; password fields never recorded; the result is a draft **技能 / Skill** (an existing word; 例行 read as repeating 4/4) with **试一次 / Try it once**, **留着 / Keep it**, **定期做 / Make it a routine** (D114's proposal card, never silent). Not built: it needs a recorder of the person's inputs, a model pass and the skills page (todo). (8) **Not built:** a reminder after about 4 hours waiting on the person (Ducter has no reminder for an open card; it belongs with the attention forecast); a live view for 用电脑 on a worker Mac; the dozing phone as clay raster art (the prompt is in `design/art/assets`; the image provider didn't answer this session, so a drawn SVG in the clay's colours ships meanwhile); human raters. | L (the author's "按建议 go"), research/77, research/82 | `packages/core/src/android/{live,tools,index}.ts`, `packages/core/src/ws.ts`, `packages/protocol/src/phone-use.ts`, `packages/runner/src/brief.ts`, `apps/web/src/{phone-use.tsx,workbench-screens.ts,workbench.tsx,i18n-phoneuse.ts}`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/{phoneuse,screenpresence}.go,store/phoneuse.go,protocol/{phoneuse,protocol}.go,i18n/phoneuse.go}`, `design/{computer-use,phone-use,workbench,product-model,scenarios,todo}.md`, `docs/`, `research/82-computer-use-presence.md` |

## 2026-11-13: Proactive that notices what dots noticed (the author, comparing a day of dots with Ducter: "不仅我主动问才说，根本没主动 ai，哪怕我主动问，效果也很差 … 这里对我们来说很重要")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D243 | **Restraint is about what is said, never about what is read: service notices reach triage; a trip coming up, a service the person uses and a correction are finds of their own; what can't wait has its own room and ripens with time; the home keeps the person's zone; "anything for me?" is answered from the same detectors. Built 2026-11-13 with fakes and a replay of the day (research/81); for the author's review; not yet live on hel.** *Who:* the heavy developer with production systems, travel and many tools' notices (the author); the consumer with trips and bills; the fresh user with one Gmail. *Feels:* before, "it reads my mail and tells me nothing"; after, 放心 (the edges are minded) and 惊喜 (it joined the flight with the database maintenance). *First time:* nothing new to set up. (1) **Stage 1** passes a bulk or no-reply mail to triage when its subject or sender says it may be a booking, a renewal or a billing change, maintenance, an outage, a failing job, a security alert, a shutdown, a delivery or boarding (`NOTICE_TERMS`), at most 60 a day (`NOTICE_DAY_MAX`; the rest are counted in Left out); a receipt's words alone and Gmail's Promotions never pass. (2) **Triage** gains `service` (maintenance, outage, incident, failing, security, shutdown, resolved; a window, an effect, prod) and `billing` (starts charging, price up, payment failed, over limit, trial ends); the ledger keeps two flights or two windows from one mail; a flight or train in the calendar is a booking, never a meeting. (3) **Three kinds** (ten in all): **快到了 / Coming up** (a flight or train within 36 h: when, from where, when to be there; urgent within 30 h), **你用的服务 / A service you use** (production maintenance within 48 h joined with the trip that day; an outage in the last 12 h; a job failing twice in three days or once on production; a shutdown within six weeks; a security alert, below the bar unless the model keeps it; a building's notice stays below the bar), **之前说的有变 / An update to what I told you** (a later notice that undoes or changes a find already said, said at once: a find keeps its topic and facts); money changing joins **快到期 · 要花钱**. (4) **The bar:** the informing kinds need no next step; the judge's rubric says what the cost is per kind (a person waiting is the cost; a trip or a window is worth a heads-up), sees typed details, and never writes about itself. (5) **Speaking:** urgent finds have their own room of 3 a day (`NOW_MAX`), no longer inside the note's 3; the detectors run every half hour, not only when mail arrives; with no quiet hours set, a heads-up that can wait six hours waits for 08:00 (23:00–08:00) in the person's zone. 只说急的 keeps no morning note. (6) **The zone:** the home keeps the zone the person's app last said in its hello and uses it for the note, "today" and the calendar when no quiet hours are set (hel ran in UTC). (7) **The partner:** a `noticed` tool (the same detectors, ranked, said or not, How I know, the next step) and a persona rule: asked what is worth knowing, `noticed` first, at most four, time-bound first, one line each, never what it read, checked or hadn't told them. Replay (`bun packages/partner/eval/proactive/day.ts`): 每天最多 3 件 1/7 → 7/7 in time, 0 false alarms; 只说急的 1/7 → 5/7 (the two left are a shutdown called off before it was urgent: correctly never said). Tradeoff: up to 60 more cheap-model reads a day per source (≤ ~0.75M tokens a month, inside the 2M budget). Alternative: per-item model judgement instead of detectors (`todo.md`, needs the author's labelled week). | the author, 2026-11-13; research/81 | `connectors.md` §1.3, §4, §5; `product-model.md`; `docs/accounts.md`; `packages/core/src/proactive/`; `packages/partner/src/persona.ts`; web and Mac `kind.*` |

## Superseded
- **D165 §10 stage 1: "Mailing lists, no-reply senders and bulk categories are left out"** (all of them, unread). Superseded by D243 (1): those whose subject or sender says they may need the person go to triage, up to 60 a day.
- **D165 §5: "Now … counts within the cap"** and the seven kinds. Superseded by D243 (5) and (3): "now" has its own room of 3; ten kinds.
- **D230 (6) "quiet hours off hold nothing"**, for heads-ups. Superseded by D243 (5): with no quiet hours, a heads-up that can wait six hours waits for 08:00; what is due sooner still goes.
- **D165: "the person's zone: their quiet hours', else the server's"**. Superseded by D243 (6): else the zone their app last said, then the server's.

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D245 | **A routine fires robustly, and "when something changes" is watched without a model. Ducter's routine model stays (proposal card, budget, review date); only its firing changes. Built 2026-11-13; checked by fake-clock tests, not yet in a live run.** *Who:* all three. The consumer has a "good morning" routine and a home that sleeps. The developer has hourly watchers whose account gets signed out overnight. The fresh user says "whenever a new file shows up in ~/Downloads/invoices, rename it" and never hears the words cron or trigger. *Feel:* before, a run after a restart said "good morning" at 3 pm, a signed-out account failed every tick until the routine paused itself for "3 runs in a row didn't work", a catch-up just after a restart could be skipped because the runner wasn't wired yet, and a watched folder cost a model look. After, a run knows it is late; a hiccup is retried once; a sign-in problem is said once and fixes itself when the person signs in; nothing is spent until something changes. (1) **Late:** a run that starts 15 min or more after its time gets a line in its brief: "This run started late: it was due {time} and started {time} ({n} min late) … adapt anything that depends on the time of day, skip steps that no longer make sense, and mention the delay when it matters". The user's own routines (D185) get it too, after their words. The run's line on the shelf says **晚了 12 分钟 / 12 min late** (from 90 min on, **晚了 n 小时 / n h late**). (2) **One safe retry:** a failed run is tried once more, 12 min later, if all three hold: it had not acted, it was not cancelled by the person or stopped by its budget, and the next firing is more than 30 min away. *Acted* means one of these appeared on the run's task before it failed: a tool call other than a pure read (`Read`, `Glob`, `Grep`, `LS`, `WebFetch`, `WebSearch`, `TodoWrite`, Ducter's `report_to_main`, `checkpoint`, `log_read`, `log_search`, `memory_search`, `task_read`); a report that posted something (progress, needs input, blocked); or a question from it. A run that never opened (the runner threw) has not acted. A retried failure doesn't count toward "3 in a row"; the retry's outcome does. The shelf says **{time}再试一次 / Tries again {time}**. (3) **Account errors space the routine out:** these are signed out, a key refused, or the allowance or plan used up. Ducter reads them from the task's failure reason or report, loosely, in en and zh (`troubleOf`, INFERRED from harness and CLI errors). The routine is tried again by itself after 5 min, doubling up to 24 h. While the firing is still worth running (min(period / 2, 12 h) after its time) it is that same firing, if it hadn't acted. Times that come up meanwhile fold into the next try, so an hourly routine never fails every hour. The first such error stays quiet. At the second in a row the partner says once, in one line, what to do (**暂时跑不了：要重新登录 / Can't run for now: needs signing in again**, or **…额度用完了 / …out of allowance**, on the shelf with **{time}再试一次**). The first run that works clears it, and the partner says so in a few words. Account errors count toward "3 in a row" only once the spacing has reached 24 h. Resuming a routine clears it. (4) **One catch-up:** D114's coalescing stays (one late run within min(period / 2, 12 h), else one skip). A retry, a try after an account error and a catch-up are one pending firing, never two runs. The startup gap is fixed: a firing that finds the runner not yet wired (right after a restart) waits 10 s and tries again within its window, instead of being recorded as skipped. A condition routine's watch that is still armed across a restart is kept, not doubled (before, every restart left one more watcher looking). (5) **When something changes (a model-free watch):** a condition routine may carry `watch` instead of a check or event. It watches exactly one of four things: files (names, sizes and modification times; a folder's direct entries; this computer only for now); a reading command under the check rules; a URL (one GET through the check rules' curl); or an app's reading tool, through Ducter's own look (`role: "watch"`, writes refused). Optional `path`, `key` and `fields` pick the list that matters and the fields that name an item, so a fetch time doesn't count and the run hears what is **new, updated, gone** (as untrusted data). The first look only learns what is there; a later look that differs starts one run, and the watch re-arms from what it saw, across restarts. A look that errors is never a change; it backs off as checks do. The proposal card's dry run says what it sees now ("sees 2 now"). Its **花多少 / Cost** row says honestly **只在有变化时用你的 AI 额度；平时看一眼不花 / Uses your AI allowance only when something changed; looking costs nothing**. A check routine without a judge says **只在条件满足时用你的 AI 额度；平时看一眼不花 / …only when it holds…**; a judge still costs a little per change, so it keeps the old line. On screen it is never "cron", "trigger" or "watch": the routine's name and its condition in words say what it waits for, as on New task's **等条件满足 / When something happens**. (6) **An unsure check asks rather than acts** (rakazo §8.6). A watch whose look can't be trusted (three or more items all gone at once) starts no run. Instead it puts one card: **例行「{name}」这次看到的跟平时很不一样（…），可能是没看对。要照样做一次吗？/ The routine "{name}" saw something unusual this time (…); it may not have looked right. Run it anyway?**, with Go **照样做 / Run it anyway** and default Hold. The watch rests until the person answers: Go runs it and lets the watch learn afresh; Hold watches on from what it saw before, so a source that comes back is no change. Every partner run's brief also says "If you are unsure whether a step is wanted or safe, don't do it: ask in your report instead". (7) **Partner tool:** `routine_propose`'s trigger description gains the watch shape. `routine_list` adds a run's lateness and retry time and a routine's account trouble. A fresh eval was run (D175), with a new scenario `routine-change-en`. *Runner-up rejected:* a separate trigger kind for changes. A watch is a condition the code can decide, and folding it into `condition` keeps one shelf card, one proposal card and one waiter path. *Not done:* rakazo's "post No changes" (silence is success); a watch's files on a worker; the routine-proposal guidance line "a repeating job carries out the user's lasting request each time" (a persona change of its own). | U ("按建议", 2026-11-13), research/77 §4, §8.2, §8.3, §8.6 | `packages/core/src/{routines.ts,waiters.ts,work.ts,core.ts}`, `packages/protocol/src/{index.ts,watch.ts,checks-run.ts}`, `packages/partner/src/{partner.ts,render.ts}`, `packages/partner/eval/scenarios.ts`, `packages/core/test/routine-runs.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/{work.tsx,i18n.ts}`, `apps/desktop/internal/{views/work.go,protocol/protocol.go,i18n/i18n.go}`, `design/{product-model.md,flows/B-kinds-of-work.md,scenarios.md,todo.md}`, `docs/waiting-and-routines.md` |

## Superseded
- **D114's "It pauses itself after 3 failed runs", counting every failed run, and "Missed firings coalesce into one late run … else one recorded skip", with a firing that found the runner not ready recorded as skipped.** Superseded by D245 (2)–(4): a retried failure and an account error before the spacing reaches 24 h don't count; a firing waits for the runner within its window.
- **The proposal card's one Cost line "每次用一点你的 AI 额度 / A little of your AI allowance each time" for every routine.** Superseded by D245 (5): a watch or a judge-free check says it spends only when something changed or holds.

## 2026-11-13: Bridge against the real Mem (the author: "the first real trial ran end to end … S1 1/4, S2 1/4, S3 1/3, X1 1/3; diagnose and fix")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D249 | **Bridge reads Mem as it really answers (nmem 0.10.118, Mem Cloud, VERIFIED 2026-11-13): a message names no sender and no parent, so in the user's "Only me" place every sender is one of their own agents, an agent that wrote is reached again by `reply` on its latest message, and silence after such a send is never nagged about when an unnamed own agent has written since. Built and run live: S1 6/6, S3 3/3, X1 3/3, S2 4/5; the fifth (the answer linked to the question) waits on Mem (B17).** *Who:* the heavy multi-agent developer first (a terminal Claude Code beside Ducter), and the consumer user whose other AI tools write to Ducter. *Feel:* before, the first real run muted the terminal's request ("another agent", not the user's), sent the user's question to an unrelated agent of theirs whose mailbox no longer exists ("Claude 收尾协调", 409, outcome unknown) and then offered "再发一次"; after, the request is the user's own and gets its card, the question reaches exactly the agent that wrote, and nothing is ever re-sent. (1) **Own place, own agents.** When `open` names no member and the saved context's Space is `personal-<its custodian>` (read locally with `agents context show`), the message is the user's own; anywhere else an unnamed sender stays someone else's (the safe side). `reply_to` is the message's own reply handle, never a parent; only a different one, or `in_reply_to`, links a reply. (2) **Named for the user** as **你的另一个 AI / your other AI agent**; the model is told it is one of the user's own and to call it by what it says it is. An asker card from it names 另一个 AI / other AI agent as the agent (the clients' template "你的 %s 问你" leaves a space before a Chinese name: todo). (3) **Reaching the one that wrote.** `bridge_contacts` returns `wroteLately` (up to 3 recent incoming messages, newest first, each with its first words) ahead of Mem's named cards, with a note to send to the newest when the user means an agent that has written, and to use a named card only when the user used that very name. Sending to one goes by `reply` on that message (Mem routes it to its sender); the op stays the user's question (no `inReplyTo`), so follow-through still applies. The send card adds **（就是发来「…」的那个）** so the tap confirms the recipient. (4) **Never a replacement.** `bridge_send` refuses while an earlier message to the same contact is `sending` or `unknown` ("Ducter is still finding out whether your earlier message went out"). Mem's 409 `cloud_agent_original_selection_unavailable` stays unknown and is recovered by inspect → resume on the same original (D106). (5) **Unlinked answers.** Because Mem links no reply, a sent question to an unnamed own agent counts as maybe-answered once such an agent writes after it; follow-through stays silent then (restraint over a wrong nag). (6) **A request for work is work**: the X1 hint now says a goal, fix or build request is asked as Go = open a task, never as the user's call (live: haiku turned S1 into an asker card). (7) **The fake answers in the real shapes** by default (`NMEM_FAKE_SHAPE=named` keeps the old one for the partner eval's teammates), plus `agents context show` and the 409. The live driver waits for in-flight sends before checking and checks X1's answer as a reply to the asking message. *Not changed:* the persona and every tool name and description (fingerprint unchanged; the bridge eval scenarios re-run: 4/4 with one judge re-run on result length). | Live trial ×3 on the author's Mac, raw nmem output under `ducter-evidence/bridge/raw-2026-11-13T07-{27-26,44-31,51-45,56-28}*`; research/66 §7 | `packages/partner/src/bridge.ts`, `bridge-out.ts`, `partner.ts`, `render.ts`; `eval/fake-bridge-nmem.ts`, `eval/bridge-live.ts`; `test/bridge-released.test.ts`; `design/bridge.md` "Try it live"; product-model glossary; research/66 §7; todo |

- **D108's assumption that a sender's member comes with each message** (`isMine` from the message's member alone). Superseded by D249 (1): with no member on the message, the place decides.

## 2026-11-13: The default is proactive (the author: "默认策略需要有主动 ai 哈")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D250 | **A home that never chose how it hears about things is on 每天最多 3 件 / Up to 3 a day, not 不用主动找我: connecting an account is consent to read it, and restraint lives in what is said (D243), not in whether it looks. The first-add question stays, with its default shown and silence meaning yes. 不再需要 to 早上那条还要吗？ goes to 只说急的 (never off), kept as its reason, and the morning note is offered back once when the notes got materially better. Built 2026-11-13; checked by fake-clock tests and the replay of the day (7/7), not yet live.** *Who:* the fresh user who connects Gmail and never touches a chip (before: nothing ever came, the feature looked absent; after: a morning message with up to 3 things, the chips under it); the consumer who expects dots' "it just tells me"; the developer whose home went quiet after a 不再需要 said to notes that, before D243, couldn't notice his flight. *Feel:* 惊喜 the first morning, 放心 because the first note says how it works and the chips are right there, 好用 because nothing had to be set. *First time:* adding the account; nothing else. (1) **The default.** Settings report `proactive: "three"` with `proactiveBy: "default"` when never chosen; the chip row shows 每天最多 3 件 selected with **你还没选过，先这样：早上把值得说的放在一条里，最多 3 件；急的随时说。 / You haven't chosen yet, so for now: one note each morning with up to 3 things; anything urgent right away.** Settings › 通知 shows the row only once there is an account. (2) **The first add.** The first-look offer stays (§11); watching starts at once (new mail only, from now); the quiet 60-day look back waits half an hour for the offer's answer, so a yes runs the look the person is waiting for first; a choice made elsewhere lets it go at once, silence lets it go after the half hour. What can't wait may be said "now" meanwhile, as on any day. (3) **The cadence question stays** (after the first look, or after Not now), with 每天最多 3 件 already selected; the default is not an answer, any chip is (even the one already on). A home that never saw the question (connected without the offer, or before D250) gets it once, under its first morning message on the default. (4) **不再需要 → 只说急的, kept as `keep_note`.** Chosen over a weekly or "when it matters" morning note: it is what the person literally said; after D243 只说急的 still carries a trip within 30 hours, production maintenance, money that changes tomorrow, a correction, a person who asked again; "anything for me?" is answered from the same finds in every mode; and a new rung would add a fifth chip and a concept for an ambiguous signal (three unread mornings may mean the lock screen was enough). The mode before is kept for the way back. (5) **Offered back once.** `NOTES_REV` (1 = D243) marks a material change in what the notes can notice. A home on 只说急的 whose source is `keep_note`, or unknown with 不再需要 in its Log, and whose rev is older, hears once, the next time the person writes: **现在我能提前告诉你航班、维护、扣费这些了。要每天早上说几件吗？ / I can now tell you about flights, maintenance and billing changes ahead of time. Want a few each morning?** with **好 / Yes** · **不用 / No thanks** (ask `note_again`). No answer keeps 只说急的; 好 restores the mode before (else 每天最多 3 件), 不用 keeps 只说急的; both are a choice, never offered again. A choice in Settings before then cancels it; a mode the person chose is never re-offered. Before D250 the source wasn't kept, so a home that chose 只说急的 in Settings after a 不再需要 hears the one line too (accepted: one line, default hold). (6) **The source is kept from now on:** `proactiveBy` = `default` · `chosen` (any client's chips, a re-offer answered) · `keep_note`. Alternative: keep "silence = nothing watches" and make the first-add question louder (stronger consent, but the fresh user who never answers gets nothing, which is the failure the author named). | the author, 2026-11-13 | `connectors.md` §3, §5, §8, §11, §13, §15; `product-model.md`; `scenarios.md` K6, K15; `docs/accounts.md`, `docs/settings.md`; `todo.md`; `packages/protocol/src/proactive.ts`; `packages/core/src/settings.ts`, `packages/core/src/proactive/engine.ts`; web and Mac chips, `note_again` |

- **D165 §3 "Never watch without asking. Connecting is not consent to watch", §11 (4) "If the person says nothing, nothing watches: the chip stays 不用主动找我 until they answer", §15 (4) "Watching starts only after the one question (no answer = only when asked)", and the protocol's "不用主动找我 … also the state before the person answered".** Superseded by D250 (1)–(3): never chosen is 每天最多 3 件; the question stays, with its default shown.

## 2026-11-13: Which phone (the author, from their phone, about their own Pixel Fold; the task said "还是没连上用户的真机：phone 工具这次控制的仍是模拟器")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D251 | **Phone work says which phone: `needs.phone` is `"mine"` (the person's own phone: their apps, accounts, messages), `"ducter"` (Ducter's own clean phone, 用手机), or `"any"` (either; the `true` of before D251 means the same). Work for the person's own phone never falls to Ducter's phone: their Android phone connected without 用这台手机 gets D247's card even where Ducter's phone could do it; 先不用 is told plainly and reports blocked, and may only ask whether Ducter's phone would do. Built 2026-11-13, checked by core tests through the real core and the partner eval (haiku); not yet live on hel.** *What happened (hel, 0.0.48, read-only):* the partner opened `{browse, phone: true}`; the Pixel Fold was paired and connected but not added (Android's switch was on, a separate bug fixed on main the same day: a phone whose switch is on is now added when its app says hello); D247's card appears only when nothing else can serve, and Ducter's emulator on the author's Mac could, so the task drove the emulator ("About emulated device") and reported blocked. *Who:* the consumer who says 用我的手机 and means their WeChat (before: a stranger's phone with none of their accounts, a confusing report; after: their own phone asked for, or one card to turn it on); the developer who says "try our app on Android" (Ducter's clean phone, never their own); the fresh user (unchanged: one card, one switch). *Feel:* 放心, the task never pretends a test phone is theirs. *First time:* they just ask. (1) **The partner picks which** (task_open's `needs.phone`, a string enum): mine for 我的手机, 我的微信, their orders, "on my Pixel"; ducter for 在安卓上试我们的 App, an APK, a fresh phone; any when an app anyone can open does; **unsure: mine** (a wrong mine costs one card; a wrong ducter does the work in an account that isn't theirs). (2) **Routing:** mine: their added phone (D238), else their connected Android phone with the setup card (D247) whatever else exists, else nothing opens (`MINE_NO_PHONE`: install Ducter on their Android phone; Ducter's phone only as a question when the work needs none of their accounts); bound for the task's life, it never falls back; with their phone gone mid-task the tools say so (`HANDSET_MINE_NONE`) and it reports blocked. ducter: Ducter's phone only (D217, D234), never the person's; none: `DUCTER_NO_PHONE` (用手机 on a Mac). any: unchanged (D238, D247). (3) **先不用 / 不借 on mine:** `HANDSET_MINE_NO`: no phone of theirs, report blocked; it may ask, with waiting as the default, whether Ducter's phone would do when the work needs none of their accounts; it never switches by itself. (4) **The task knows which phone it has:** the brief's phone section opens with it (mine: their own phone, never another; ducter: Ducter's own emulator, not theirs, none of their accounts but what they signed in to there; any: phone_look says which), and every `phone_look` starts with one line, **Phone: {Pixel Fold}, the user's own phone…** or **Phone: Ducter's own phone, an Android emulator on {Mac}. It is not the user's own phone…**. Alternative: keep `phone: true` and always prefer the person's connected phone (no new word for the partner, but "try our app on Android" would then borrow the person's own phone and its accounts, and a test install on it). | the author, 2026-11-13 | `use-this-phone.md` §3, §3.2; `phone-use.md` §5; `scenarios.md`; `docs/phone.md`; `todo.md`; `packages/protocol/src/loadout.ts` (`whichPhone`); `packages/core/src/loadout.ts`, `candidates.ts`, `android/index.ts`, `android/tools.ts`, `handset/index.ts`; `packages/runner/src/brief.ts`; `packages/partner/src/partner.ts` (task_open, `parseNeeds`); eval `which-phone-*` |

## Superseded
- **D238 §3's "`needs.phone` work uses the person's own phone when one is added; Ducter's phone on its computer otherwise" as the rule for every phone task, and D247's "the card only when no phone can serve it" for work on the person's own apps.** Superseded by D251 (2): it holds for `"any"` only; `"mine"` never uses Ducter's phone, `"ducter"` never the person's.

## 2026-11-13: The book comes back on the Pixel Fold (the author, Android app 0.0.48, home 0.0.49/0.0.50: "为啥我现在 Android 打开 fold 上竟然没有两栏布局了？")

| # | Decision | Source | Reflected in |
|---|---|---|---|
| D252 | **On an open foldable the book is always the layout: the conversation left of the fold, On hand (or the task opened beside it) right; the app reads the fold against the page as it is laid out now, and On hand never takes the whole screen on a book.** *Who:* the author on his Pixel Fold, and anyone who opens a foldable from the cover screen. *Feel:* before, unfolding with Ducter open left one 640 column lying across the crease, with no way back but folding again or leaving the app; after, the two sides open like a book every time. (1) **The cause (Android app, since D182):** the app worked out where the fold lies on the page only when Android reported a posture. Unfolding reports the inner screen's fold while the web view still has the cover screen's width, so the fold seemed to lie past the page's edge and came out as none (`window.ducterFold = null`); nothing read it again once the web view grew. Opening the app already unfolded worked, which is why it was missed. Reproduced on the emulator's Pixel Fold (open on the cover screen, unfold: `ducterFold` null at 841 CSS px). **Fix:** the app keeps Android's fold and reads it against the web view again whenever the web view's size or place changes (`MainActivity.readFold`), so unfolding, turning and resizing all land on the right split; checked: unfold → book, half-open → book, turned while half-open → tabletop, turned flat → one column, upright again → book. (2) **On hand at the whole screen on a book (amends D184 (5), keeps D244):** D216 made the whole-screen On hand one list on a 960 measure, and the book rules for D184's four lanes stopped matching anything, so on a book that list lay across the crease. The book already is On hand beside the conversation, so on a book On hand never takes the whole screen (its arrows are not shown, ⌘2 opens it beside the conversation, `#onhand/full` shows the book). An opened task still can: it stands in the second half with On hand as a rail in the first (D244), and Esc from it goes back to the book. The dead lane rules are gone. *Built 2026-11-13; checked on the Android 16 emulator's Pixel Fold; for the author's check on the Pixel (needs the app from this release: the fix is in the app, not the page).* | U (the author's Pixel Fold) | `apps/android` (`MainActivity.kt`, `CoreTest.kt`), `apps/web/src/fold.ts`, `apps/web/static/app.css`, `apps/web/test/{fold,fill}.test.ts`, `apps/web/demo-onhand.ts` (prints a pair link), `design/{android,ui}.md`, `docs/phone.md` |

## Superseded
- **D184 (5)'s "on a book fold On hand at the whole screen splits at the fold, what needs you on the first half and the other lanes on the second"**, and D244's "a task opened from the whole-screen On hand" on a book. Superseded by D252 (2): on a book On hand never takes the whole screen; a task opened at the whole screen (its own arrows) stands beside the rail.
